local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer

if tostring(game.PlaceId) ~= "142823291" then
	player:Kick("Invalid game")
	return
end

do
	local isPriv = false
	local ok, psId = pcall(function() return game.PrivateServerId end)
	if ok and type(psId) == "string" and psId ~= "" then
		isPriv = true
	else
		local ok2, vip = pcall(function()
			local rf = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
			rf = rf and rf:FindFirstChild("Extras")
			rf = rf and rf:FindFirstChild("IsVIPServer")
			if rf and rf:IsA("RemoteFunction") then return rf:InvokeServer() end
			return false
		end)
		if ok2 and vip == true then isPriv = true end
	end
	if isPriv then
		task.delay(1, function()
			pcall(function() player:Kick("Private server detected") end)
		end)
	end
end

do
	local passKey = ...
	local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local KEY_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/313ae/AeonHUBSCRIPT/main/keys.json"
	local BIND_WEBHOOK_URL = "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1549579107241562242/BHb6FlKUdgC9wix-EW0B3NzMPlfSd_Ggy09HbUcXbKxsTEkD_FNyDfm_2chNm0HdHaWx"
	local EXEC_WEBHOOK_URL = "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1549577678208765993/wZhv1Qhr58DN45zMOjkE-oZrtXVxvMta6ixggQEUShOzm7m4HhejLZVUJdfI75qPwsMa"

	local function sha256hex(msg)
		local K = {
			0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
			0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
			0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
			0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
			0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
			0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
			0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
			0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2,
		}
		local h0, h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7 =
			0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a, 0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19
		local ml = #msg * 8
		msg = msg .. string.char(0x80)
		msg = msg .. string.rep(string.char(0), (56 - (#msg % 64)) % 64)
		local len = ""
		for i = 7, 0, -1 do
			len = len .. string.char(math.floor(ml / (256 ^ i)) % 256)
		end
		msg = msg .. len
		local w = {}
		for chunk = 1, #msg, 64 do
			for i = 0, 15 do
				local o = chunk + i * 4
				local b1, b2, b3, b4 = string.byte(msg, o, o + 3)
				w[i + 1] = ((b1 * 256 + b2) * 256 + b3) * 256 + b4
			end
			for j = 17, 64 do
				local x, y = w[j - 15], w[j - 2]
				local s0 = bit32.bxor(bit32.bxor(bit32.rrotate(x, 7), bit32.rrotate(x, 18)), bit32.rshift(x, 3))
				local s1 = bit32.bxor(bit32.bxor(bit32.rrotate(y, 17), bit32.rrotate(y, 19)), bit32.rshift(y, 10))
				w[j] = (w[j - 16] + s0 + w[j - 7] + s1) % 4294967296
			end
			local a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h = h0, h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7
			for j = 1, 64 do
				local S1 = bit32.bxor(bit32.bxor(bit32.rrotate(e, 6), bit32.rrotate(e, 11)), bit32.rrotate(e, 25))
				local ch = bit32.bxor(bit32.band(e, f), bit32.band(bit32.bnot(e), g))
				local t1 = (h + S1 + ch + K[j] + w[j]) % 4294967296
				local S0 = bit32.bxor(bit32.bxor(bit32.rrotate(a, 2), bit32.rrotate(a, 13)), bit32.rrotate(a, 22))
				local maj = bit32.bxor(bit32.bxor(bit32.band(a, b), bit32.band(a, c)), bit32.band(b, c))
				local t2 = (S0 + maj) % 4294967296
				h = g; g = f; f = e; e = (d + t1) % 4294967296; d = c; c = b; b = a; a = (t1 + t2) % 4294967296
			end
			h0 = (h0 + a) % 4294967296; h1 = (h1 + b) % 4294967296
			h2 = (h2 + c) % 4294967296; h3 = (h3 + d) % 4294967296
			h4 = (h4 + e) % 4294967296; h5 = (h5 + f) % 4294967296
			h6 = (h6 + g) % 4294967296; h7 = (h7 + h) % 4294967296
		end
		return string.format("%08x%08x%08x%08x%08x%08x%08x%08x", h0, h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7)
	end

	local function normKey(s)
		local t = tostring(s or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
		return t:upper()
	end
	local function getHWID()
		local ok, id = pcall(function() return gethwid() end)
		if ok and type(id) == "string" and #id > 0 then return id end
		ok, id = pcall(function() return get_hwid() end)
		if ok and type(id) == "string" and #id > 0 then return id end
		return nil
	end
	local function fetchKeys()
		local ok, body = pcall(function()
			return game:HttpGet(KEY_URL .. "?t=" .. tostring(os.time()))
		end)
		if not ok or type(body) ~= "string" then return nil end
		local ok2, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(body) end)
		if ok2 and type(data) == "table" then return data end
		return nil
	end
	local function postWebhook(url, content)
		local reqFn = nil
		pcall(function() reqFn = (syn and syn.request) or http_request or request end)
		if type(url) ~= "string" or #url == 0 or type(reqFn) ~= "function" then return end
		if type(content) ~= "string" or #content == 0 then return end
		pcall(function()
			reqFn({
				Url = url,
				Method = "POST",
				Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
				Body = HttpService:JSONEncode({ content = content }),
			})
		end)
	end
	local function logExec(key)
		task.spawn(function()
			local hw = getHWID() or "no-hwid"
			local who = player.Name
			pcall(function()
				local d = player.DisplayName
				if type(d) == "string" and d ~= "" and d ~= who then who = who .. " (" .. d .. ")" end
			end)
			postWebhook(EXEC_WEBHOOK_URL, "EXEC " .. key .. " | " .. who .. " | " .. hw .. " | " .. tostring(os.time()))
		end)
	end
	local function bindHWID(keyHash, hwid, fullKey)
		task.spawn(function()
			local who = player.Name
			pcall(function()
				local d = player.DisplayName
				if type(d) == "string" and d ~= "" and d ~= who then who = who .. " (" .. d .. ")" end
			end)
			postWebhook(BIND_WEBHOOK_URL, "BIND " .. keyHash .. " | " .. hwid .. " | " .. who .. " | " .. fullKey .. " | " .. tostring(os.time()))
		end)
	end
	local function checkKey(key)
		if #key < 8 then return "bad", nil end
		local db = fetchKeys()
		if not db then return "netfail", nil end
		local h = sha256hex(key)
		local rec = db[h]
		if type(rec) ~= "table" then return "bad", nil end
		if rec.banned then return "banned", rec end
		if type(rec.expires) == "number" and rec.expires < os.time() then return "expired", rec end
		local hwid = getHWID()
		if hwid then
			local hwidHash = sha256hex(hwid)
			local stored = rec.hwidHash or rec.hwid
			if type(stored) == "string" and #stored > 0 then
				local cmp = stored
				if #stored ~= 64 then cmp = sha256hex(stored) end
				if cmp ~= hwidHash then return "hwid_mismatch", rec end
			end
		end
		return "ok", rec
	end


	local loaderKey = nil
	local function grab(cand)
		if type(cand) == "string" and normKey(cand) ~= "" then
			loaderKey = loaderKey or cand
		end
	end
	grab(passKey)
	pcall(function()
		if typeof(getgenv) == "function" then
			local g = getgenv()
			if type(g) == "table" then grab(g.Key) grab(g.key) end
		end
	end)
	pcall(function() grab(_G.Key) grab(_G.key) end)
	pcall(function() if type(shared) == "table" then grab(shared.Key) end end)
	local rawKey = nil
	if loaderKey and normKey(loaderKey) ~= "" then
		rawKey = loaderKey
	end
	if not rawKey then
		player:Kick("Invalid key")
		return
	end
	local key = normKey(rawKey)
	local st, rec = "netfail", nil
	for _ = 1, 3 do
		st, rec = checkKey(key)
		if st ~= "netfail" then break end
		task.wait(4)
	end
	if st == "ok" then
		local hwid = getHWID()
		local keyHash = sha256hex(key)
		local emptyHash = rec and ((rec.hwidHash == nil and rec.hwid == nil) or rec.hwidHash == "" or rec.hwid == "")
		if hwid and rec and emptyHash then
			bindHWID(keyHash, hwid, key)
		end
		logExec(key)
	elseif st == "bad" then
		player:Kick("Invalid key")
		return
	elseif st == "banned" then
		player:Kick("Key banned")
		return
	elseif st == "expired" then
		player:Kick("Your key has been expired")
		return
	elseif st == "hwid_mismatch" then
		player:Kick("HWID mismatch")
		return
	elseif st == "netfail" then
		player:Kick("Can't reach key server, try again")
		return
	end
end
_G.pgui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
pcall(function() if not isfolder("AeonHub") then makefolder("AeonHub") end end)
pcall(function() if not isfolder("AeonHub/AeonHubBin") then makefolder("AeonHub/AeonHubBin") end end)
pcall(function() if not isfolder("AeonHub/AeonHubMM2configs") then makefolder("AeonHub/AeonHubMM2configs") end end)
pcall(function() for _, n in ipairs({ "theme.txt", "mm2data_kroxn.lua", "emotes.json", "spotify_token.json" }) do
		if isfile(n) then
			local ok, data = pcall(readfile, n)
			if ok then pcall(writefile, "AeonHub/AeonHubBin/" .. n, data) end
			pcall(delfile, n)
		end
	end
	for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(".")) do
		local nm = tostring(f):match("([^/\\]+)$")
		if nm and nm:match("^spotcov_%d+%.png$") then pcall(delfile, tostring(f)) end
	end
	if isfile("AeonHub/autoload.txt") then
		local ok, old = pcall(readfile, "AeonHub/autoload.txt")
		if ok and type(old) == "string" then
			local nm = old:gsub("%s+$", "")
			if nm ~= "" then pcall(writefile, "AeonHub/AeonHubMM2configs/" .. nm .. "autoload.txt", nm) end
		end
		pcall(delfile, "AeonHub/autoload.txt")
	end
end)

function _G.__MM2_Tg(key, set, get, track, knob)
	_G.__MM2_Toggles[key] = { set = set, get = get, track = track, knob = knob }
end
_G.__MM2_Vals = {}

function _G.__MM2_Val(key, set, get, min, max)
	_G.__MM2_Vals[key] = { set = set, get = get, min = min, max = max }
end
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LIME = Color3.fromRGB(163, 255, 89)
_G.BG_DARK = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
local BG_DARKER = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 24)
local TEXT_PRIMARY = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
local TEXT_SECONDARY = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
local BORDER = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
local EASE_Q = Enum.EasingStyle.Quad
local TWEEN_FAST = TweenInfo.new(0.15, EASE_Q)
local TWEEN_POP = TweenInfo.new(0.25, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local TWEEN_EASE = TweenInfo.new(0.2, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
local OFF_STATE = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
local LIGHT_TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
local PURE_WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local THEMES = { Lime = { accent = Color3.fromRGB(163, 255, 89) }, Blue = { accent = Color3.fromRGB(89, 163, 255) }, Orange = { accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 163, 89) },
	Rose = { accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 89, 163) }, Red = { accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 77, 77) },
	Cat = { accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 79, 147), accent2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 128, 173), light = true }, }
local selectedTheme = "Lime"
pcall(function() local saved = readfile("AeonHub/AeonHubBin/theme.txt")
	if THEMES[saved] then selectedTheme = saved end
end)
local acc = (THEMES[selectedTheme] or THEMES.Lime).accent
local C_L_WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local C_L_SIDEBAR = Color3.fromRGB(250, 248, 250)
local C_L_BORDER = Color3.fromRGB(245, 201, 220)
local C_L_HOVER = Color3.fromRGB(255, 224, 237)
local C_L_PANEL = Color3.fromRGB(255, 247, 251)
local C_L_CARD = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 246)
local C_L_SEL = Color3.fromRGB(255, 217, 233)
local C_L_DIVIDER = Color3.fromRGB(241, 221, 230)
local C_T_PRIM = Color3.fromRGB(32, 32, 42)
local C_T_SEC = Color3.fromRGB(119, 116, 129)
local C_10 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 10)
local C_80 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
local LIGHT_BG_PAIRS = {
	{ _G.BG_DARK, C_L_WHITE }, { BG_DARKER, C_L_SIDEBAR }, { BORDER, C_L_BORDER }, { OFF_STATE, C_L_HOVER },
	{ C_10, C_L_WHITE }, { C_80, C_L_DIVIDER }, { Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35), C_L_HOVER }, { Color3.new(0, 0, 0), C_L_WHITE },
	{ TEXT_SECONDARY, C_T_PRIM }, { Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100), C_T_SEC },
}
local LIGHT_TXT_PAIRS = {
	{ TEXT_PRIMARY, C_T_PRIM }, { TEXT_SECONDARY, C_T_PRIM }, { LIGHT_TEXT, C_T_PRIM }, { PURE_WHITE, C_T_PRIM },
	{ Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120), C_T_PRIM }, { Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100), C_T_PRIM },
}
local LIGHT_STROKE_PAIRS = { { BORDER, C_L_BORDER }, { C_80, C_L_DIVIDER } }

local function __invPairs(list)
	local r = {}
	for _, e in ipairs(list) do r[#r + 1] = { e[2], e[1] } end
	return r
end
local DARK_BG_PAIRS = __invPairs(LIGHT_BG_PAIRS)
local DARK_TXT_PAIRS = __invPairs(LIGHT_TXT_PAIRS)
local DARK_STROKE_PAIRS = __invPairs(LIGHT_STROKE_PAIRS)

local function __pairGet(list, c)
	for _, e in ipairs(list) do if __MM2_ColEq(e[1], c) then return e[2] end end
	return nil
end

local function __swCol(on)
	local th = THEMES[selectedTheme]
	if th and th.light then return on and th.accent or C_L_HOVER end
	return on and acc or OFF_STATE
end

local function __knobCol(on)
	local th = THEMES[selectedTheme]
	if th and th.light then return on and C_L_WHITE or C_T_PRIM end
	return on and BG_DARKER or TEXT_SECONDARY
end

local function __headCol()
	local th = THEMES[selectedTheme]
	if th and th.light then return th.accent2 or th.accent end
	return acc
end
local __aenv = getfenv(1) or _G
__aenv.__MM2_BaseTxt = function() local th = THEMES[selectedTheme] return (th and th.light) and C_T_PRIM or TEXT_PRIMARY end
__aenv.__MM2_LightTxt = function() local th = THEMES[selectedTheme] return (th and th.light) and C_T_PRIM or LIGHT_TEXT end
__aenv.__MM2_WhiteTxt = function() local th = THEMES[selectedTheme] return (th and th.light) and C_T_PRIM or PURE_WHITE end
__aenv.__MM2_SecTxt = function() local th = THEMES[selectedTheme] return (th and th.light) and C_T_PRIM or TEXT_SECONDARY end
__aenv.__MM2_ColEq = function(a, b) return math.abs(a.R - b.R) < 0.002 and math.abs(a.G - b.G) < 0.002 and math.abs(a.B - b.B) < 0.002 end
__aenv.__MM2_CatTxt = function(c) return __MM2_ColEq(c, C_T_PRIM) or __MM2_ColEq(c, C_T_SEC) end
__aenv.__MM2_CatCol = function(c)
	return __MM2_CatTxt(c) or __MM2_ColEq(c, C_L_WHITE) or __MM2_ColEq(c, C_L_SIDEBAR) or __MM2_ColEq(c, C_L_BORDER) or __MM2_ColEq(c, C_L_HOVER) or __MM2_ColEq(c, C_L_PANEL) or __MM2_ColEq(c, C_L_CARD) or __MM2_ColEq(c, C_L_SEL) or __MM2_ColEq(c, C_L_DIVIDER)
end
__aenv.__MM2_BlockPaint = function(track, knob, lbl, blocked, on, instant)
	local th = THEMES[selectedTheme]
	local light = th and th.light
	local trackCol = blocked and (light and Color3.fromRGB(225, 216, 226) or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)) or __swCol(on)
	local knobCol = blocked and (light and C_T_SEC or Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)) or __knobCol(on)
	local knobPos = blocked and UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2) or (on and UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2))
	if lbl then lbl.TextColor3 = blocked and __MM2_SecTxt() or __MM2_BaseTxt() end
	if instant then
		track.BackgroundColor3 = trackCol
		knob.BackgroundColor3 = knobCol
		knob.Position = knobPos
	else
		TweenService:Create(track, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = trackCol }):Play()
		TweenService:Create(knob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = knobPos, BackgroundColor3 = knobCol }):Play()
	end
end
_G.__MM2_BlockedSyncs = {}
_G.__MM2_BlockSyncSets = _G.__MM2_BlockSyncSets or {}
table.insert(_G.__MM2_BlockSyncSets, _G.__MM2_BlockedSyncs)
pcall(function() local pgL = _G.pgui
	local otherGui = false
	if pgL then
		for _, d in ipairs(pgL:GetChildren()) do
			if d.Name == "AeonHub" and d:IsA("ScreenGui") and d:FindFirstChild("MainFrame") then otherGui = true end
		end
	end
	if otherGui then return end
	if pgL then
		for _, d in ipairs(pgL:GetChildren()) do
			if d.Name == "FovGui" then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
		end
	end
	for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
		if d:IsA("Folder") and d:GetAttribute("Owned") then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
	end
end)

local function __mk(cls, props)
	local o = Instance.new(cls)
	for i = 1, #props, 2 do
		o[props[i]] = props[i + 1]
	end
	o:SetAttribute("Owned", true)
	return o
end

local function __c(p, r) return __mk("UICorner", {"CornerRadius", r, "Parent", p}) end

local function __s(p, col, th, border) return __mk("UIStroke", {"Color", col, "Thickness", th or 1, "ApplyStrokeMode", border and Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border or Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Contextual, "Parent", p}) end

local function __sw(row, y, kb)
	local t = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, y), "BackgroundColor3", OFF_STATE, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", row})
	__c(t, UDim.new(1, 0))
	local kp = {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), "BackgroundColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY}
	if kb ~= nil then kp[#kp + 1] = "BorderSizePixel" kp[#kp + 1] = kb end
	kp[#kp + 1] = "Parent" kp[#kp + 1] = t
	local k = __mk("Frame", kp)
	__c(k, UDim.new(1, 0))
	return t, k
end

local function __sl(p, y, fill, col)
	local t = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 6), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y), "BackgroundColor3", OFF_STATE, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", p})
	__c(t, UDim.new(1, 0))
	local f = __mk("Frame", {"Size", UDim2.new(fill, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundColor3", col or LIME, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", t})
	__c(f, UDim.new(1, 0))
	local k = __mk("Frame", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12), "Position", UDim2.new(fill, -6, 0.5, -6), "BackgroundColor3", col or LIME, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", t})
	__c(k, UDim.new(1, 0))
	return t, f, k
end

local function __knob(t, k, on)
	TweenService:Create(t, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(on) }):Play()
	TweenService:Create(k, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = on and UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(on) }):Play()
	pcall(function() if _G.__MM2_ThemeMarkDirty then _G.__MM2_ThemeMarkDirty() end end)
end

local function __optFade(list, t, ti)
	for _, c in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
		if c:IsA("TextButton") then
			local lbl = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
			if lbl then
				if ti then TweenService:Create(lbl, ti, { TextTransparency = t }):Play() else lbl.TextTransparency = t end
			end
		end
	end
end

local function __optClear(list)
	for _, c in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
		if c:IsA("TextButton") then pcall(function() c:Destroy() end) end
	end
end
local sgui = __mk("ScreenGui", {"Name", "AeonHub", "ResetOnSpawn", false, "DisplayOrder", 100, "ZIndexBehavior", Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling, "Parent", _G.pgui})

local function __catElev(root, light)
	for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
		pcall(function()
			local n = d.Name
			if d:IsA("Frame") and n == "Sidebar" then
				if d:GetAttribute("BaseBG") == nil then d:SetAttribute("BaseBG", d.BackgroundTransparency) end
				d.BackgroundTransparency = light and 0.35 or d:GetAttribute("BaseBG")
				return
			end
			if n == "ToggleButton" then
				if d:GetAttribute("BaseBG2") == nil then d:SetAttribute("BaseBG2", d.BackgroundTransparency) end
				d.BackgroundTransparency = light and 0 or d:GetAttribute("BaseBG2")
				return
			end
			if d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton") or d:IsA("ScrollingFrame") or d:IsA("TextLabel") or (d:IsA("Frame") and (n == "Indicator" or (n:sub(-4) == "List" and n:sub(-12) ~= "DropdownList"))) then
				local bt = d.BackgroundTransparency
				if bt < 0.97 then
					if d:GetAttribute("BaseBG2") == nil then d:SetAttribute("BaseBG2", bt) end
					d.BackgroundTransparency = light and 0.45 or d:GetAttribute("BaseBG2")
				end
				return
			end
			if d:IsA("Frame") and (n:sub(-3) == "Box" or n:sub(-4) == "Card") then
				if d:GetAttribute("BaseBG") == nil then d:SetAttribute("BaseBG", d.BackgroundTransparency) end
				d.BackgroundTransparency = light and 0.4 or d:GetAttribute("BaseBG")
				local uc = d:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
				if uc then
					if uc:GetAttribute("BaseR") == nil then uc:SetAttribute("BaseR", uc.CornerRadius.Offset) end
					uc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, light and 12 or uc:GetAttribute("BaseR"))
				end
				local st = d:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
				if st then
					if st:GetAttribute("BaseT") == nil then st:SetAttribute("BaseT", st.Thickness) end
					st.Thickness = light and 2 or st:GetAttribute("BaseT")
				end
				local shName = "Elev_" .. n
				local sh = d.Parent and d.Parent:FindFirstChild(shName)
				if light then
					if (d.ZIndex or 1) > 0 then
						local sz = UDim2.new(d.Size.X.Scale, d.Size.X.Offset + 4, d.Size.Y.Scale, d.Size.Y.Offset + 7)
						local ps = UDim2.new(d.Position.X.Scale, d.Position.X.Offset + 2, d.Position.Y.Scale, d.Position.Y.Offset + 6)
						if not sh then
							sh = __mk("Frame", {"Name", shName, "Size", sz, "Position", ps, "BackgroundColor3", Color3.fromRGB(2, 2, 2), "BackgroundTransparency", 0.9, "ZIndex", (d.ZIndex or 1) - 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "AnchorPoint", Vector2.new(0, 0), "Parent", d.Parent})
							__c(sh, UDim.new(0, 14))
						else
							sh.Size = sz
							sh.Position = ps
						end
					end
				elseif sh then
					sh:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

local function applyTheme(name)
	local t = THEMES[name] or THEMES.Lime

	local function isAccent(c)
		for _, th in pairs(THEMES) do
			if __MM2_ColEq(c, th.accent) then return true end
		end
		return false
	end
	acc = t.accent
	local bgMap = t.light and LIGHT_BG_PAIRS or DARK_BG_PAIRS
	local txtMap = t.light and LIGHT_TXT_PAIRS or DARK_TXT_PAIRS
	local strokeMap = t.light and LIGHT_STROKE_PAIRS or DARK_STROKE_PAIRS
	local roots = { sgui }
	pcall(function()
		for _, g in ipairs(_G.pgui:GetChildren()) do
			if g ~= sgui and g.Name == "AeonHub" and g:IsA("ScreenGui") and g:FindFirstChild("MainFrame") then roots[#roots + 1] = g end
		end
	end)
	for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
		for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
			pcall(function()
				if t.light then
					if (d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox")) and d:GetAttribute("BaseTxtC") == nil then
						local tc = d.TextColor3
						if not __MM2_CatTxt(tc) and not isAccent(tc) then d:SetAttribute("BaseTxtC", tc) end
					end
					if d.Name == "TabIcon" and d:GetAttribute("BaseImgC") == nil then
						local ic = d.ImageColor3
						if not __MM2_CatCol(ic) and ic ~= THEMES.Cat.accent2 then d:SetAttribute("BaseImgC", ic) end
					end
					if d:IsA("Frame") and (d.Name:sub(-3) == "Box" or d.Name:sub(-4) == "Card") and d:GetAttribute("BaseBGC") == nil then
						local bc = d.BackgroundColor3
						if not __MM2_CatCol(bc) then d:SetAttribute("BaseBGC", bc) end
					end
				end
				local nb = __pairGet(bgMap, d.BackgroundColor3) if nb then d.BackgroundColor3 = nb end
				local nb2 = __pairGet(bgMap, d.BorderColor3) if nb2 then d.BorderColor3 = nb2 end
				if isAccent(d.BackgroundColor3) then d.BackgroundColor3 = t.accent end
				if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then
					if not t.light and __MM2_CatTxt(d.TextColor3) then
						local btc = d:GetAttribute("BaseTxtC")
						if btc then d.TextColor3 = btc end
					end
					local nt = __pairGet(txtMap, d.TextColor3) if nt and not isAccent(d.BackgroundColor3) then d.TextColor3 = nt end
					if t.light and not isAccent(d.TextColor3) and not isAccent(d.BackgroundColor3) then d.TextColor3 = C_T_PRIM end
					if not t.light and not isAccent(d.TextColor3) then local c = d.TextColor3 if (c.R + c.G + c.B) * 255 / 3 <= 60 then d.TextColor3 = TEXT_PRIMARY end end
					if isAccent(d.TextColor3) then d.TextColor3 = t.accent end
					if d.Parent and d.Parent.Name == "ToggleButton" then d.TextColor3 = t.accent2 or t.accent end
				end
				if t.light and d:IsA("Frame") then
					local n = d.Name
					if n:sub(-3) == "Box" then d.BackgroundColor3 = C_L_PANEL
					elseif n:sub(-4) == "Card" then d.BackgroundColor3 = C_L_CARD
					end
				end
				if not t.light and d:IsA("Frame") and d.Name:sub(-3) == "Box" and __MM2_ColEq(d.BackgroundColor3, C_L_PANEL) then d.BackgroundColor3 = d:GetAttribute("BaseBGC") or BG_DARKER end
				if not t.light and d:IsA("Frame") and d.Name:sub(-4) == "Card" and __MM2_ColEq(d.BackgroundColor3, C_L_CARD) then d.BackgroundColor3 = d:GetAttribute("BaseBGC") or BG_DARKER end
				if t.light and d:IsA("TextButton") and __MM2_ColEq(d.BackgroundColor3, C_L_SIDEBAR) then d.BackgroundColor3 = C_L_HOVER d:SetAttribute("LightBtn", true) end
				if not t.light and d:IsA("TextButton") and d:GetAttribute("LightBtn") then d.BackgroundColor3 = BG_DARKER d:SetAttribute("LightBtn", nil) end
			end)
			pcall(function()
				if not t.light and d.Name == "TabIcon" then
					local bic = d:GetAttribute("BaseImgC")
					if bic then d.ImageColor3 = bic end
				end
				if isAccent(d.ImageColor3) then d.ImageColor3 = t.accent end
				if d.Name == "TabIcon" and t.accent2 then d.ImageColor3 = t.accent2 end
			end)
			pcall(function() if d:IsA("UIStroke") then
					if isAccent(d.Color) then d.Color = t.accent end
					local ns = __pairGet(strokeMap, d.Color) if ns then d.Color = ns end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
		pcall(__catElev, root, t.light)
	end
	pcall(function() local on = t.light == true
		for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
			for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("UIGradient") and d.Name:sub(1, 7) == "CatGrad" then
					d.Enabled = on
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	pcall(function() for _, set in ipairs(_G.__MM2_BlockSyncSets or {}) do
			for _, f in pairs(set) do pcall(f, true) end
		end end)
end
if _G.__mm2Gen == nil then _G.__mm2Gen = 0 end
_G.__mm2Gen = _G.__mm2Gen + 1
local MY_GEN = _G.__mm2Gen
_G.__MM2_RT = _G.__MM2_RT or {}
_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN] = { conns = {}, uis = {}, keys = {}, cleanup = {},
	toggles = _G.__MM2_Toggles, vals = _G.__MM2_Vals, blocked = _G.__MM2_BlockedSyncs, gui = sgui }
_G.__MM2_UISConns = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].uis
_G.__MM2_Conns = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].conns
_G.__MM2_ConnKeys = { "__speedk", "__flyk", "__jumpk", "__noclipk", "__hclick", "__FVL_AIMCONN", "__FVL_VELCONN", "__FVL_W1", "__FVL_W2", "__FVL_CIRCLECONN",
	"__animrs", "__guiders", "__infostat", "__showc", "__skinstuck", "__throwc", "__MM2_DeadPoll", }
__aenv.__MM2_OtherGui = function(except)
	for _, rt in pairs(_G.__MM2_RT or {}) do
		local g = rt.gui
		if g and g ~= except and g.Parent then return true end
	end
	local pg = _G.pgui
	if pg then
		for _, g in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
			if g ~= except and g.Name == "AeonHub" and g:IsA("ScreenGui") and g:FindFirstChild("MainFrame") then return true end
		end
	end
	return false
end
_G.__MM2_CC = _G.__MM2_CC or {}
_G.__MM2_CCSave = function(part)
	if type(part) == "Instance" and _G.__MM2_CC[part] == nil then
		_G.__MM2_CC[part] = part.CanCollide
	end
end

local function __track(list, sig, fn)
	local c = sig:Connect(function(...) if not sgui.Parent then return end
		return fn(...)
	end)
	table.insert(list, c)
	return c
end

function _G.__MM2_Conn(sig, fn)
	local rt = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN]
	return __track(rt and rt.conns or {}, sig, fn)
end

function _G.__MM2_UIS(sig, fn)
	local rt = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN]
	return __track(rt and rt.uis or {}, sig, fn)
end

function _G.__MM2_ConnOwn(rt, sig, fn)
	if not rt then return sig:Connect(fn) end
	local g = rt.gui
	local c = sig:Connect(function(...) if g and not g.Parent then return end return fn(...) end)
	table.insert(rt.conns, c)
	return c
end

function _G.__MM2_ParkConns(rt, other)
	if not rt then return end
	_G.__MM2_OrphanConns = _G.__MM2_OrphanConns or {}
	for _, tbl in ipairs({ rt.uis, rt.conns, rt.keys }) do
		for _, c in ipairs(tbl) do
			if other then table.insert(_G.__MM2_OrphanConns, c)
			else pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
		end
	end
	rt.uis, rt.conns, rt.keys = {}, {}, {}
	_G.__MM2_UISConns = rt.uis
	_G.__MM2_Conns = rt.conns
end

function _G.__MM2_FlushOrphans()
	local o = _G.__MM2_OrphanConns
	if type(o) ~= "table" then return end
	for _, c in ipairs(o) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
	_G.__MM2_OrphanConns = {}
end

local function __sweepConns()
	local rt = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN]
	if not rt then return end
	local other = __MM2_OtherGui(sgui)
	_G.__MM2_ParkConns(rt, other)
	if not other then _G.__MM2_FlushOrphans() end
end
if not __MM2_OtherGui(sgui) then
	pcall(function() for _, k in ipairs(_G.__MM2_ConnKeys) do
			local c = _G[k]
			if c then pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
			_G[k] = nil
		end
		if type(_G.__gsupp) == "table" then
			for _, c in ipairs(_G.__gsupp) do pcall(function() c:Enable() end) end
		end
		_G.__gsupp = nil
		for _, k in ipairs({ "__FVL_AIMLEAD", "__FVL_WBLEAD", "__FVL_WBUP", "__FVL_YOFF", "__FVL_VDAMP", "__FVL_OFIX", "__FVL_BALLSHOW", "__FVL_PINGS", "__FVL_REPG", "__FVL_JDELAY", "__FVL_JMISS", "__FVL_JTOKEN", "__FVL_JSEQ" }) do
			_G[k] = nil
		end
	end)
end
pcall(function() _G.__MM2_FPDH = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight end)

local function alive() return sgui ~= nil and sgui.Parent ~= nil end
local ded = false
local liveConns = {}

local function __live(sig, fn)
	local c = sig:Connect(function(...) if ded or not sgui.Parent then return end
		return fn(...)
	end)
	table.insert(liveConns, c)
	return c
end
sgui.Destroying:Connect(function() for _, c in ipairs(liveConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
	table.clear(liveConns)
end)
sgui.Destroying:Connect(function()
	local rt = _G.__MM2_RT and _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN]
	if not rt then return end
	local other = __MM2_OtherGui(sgui)
	_G.__MM2_ParkConns(rt, other)
	if not other then _G.__MM2_FlushOrphans() end
	for i = 1, #rt.cleanup do pcall(rt.cleanup[i]) end
	rt.cleanup = {}
	for i = #(_G.__MM2_BlockSyncSets or {}), 1, -1 do
		if _G.__MM2_BlockSyncSets[i] == rt.blocked then table.remove(_G.__MM2_BlockSyncSets, i) end
	end
	_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN] = nil
end)

		if lchH then
			for _, n in ipairs({ "Farm Gyro", "Farm Velocity", "PredBall" }) do

			if humH then pcall(function() humH.PlatformStand = false end) end
		end

			if not alive() then return end

			if ch then
				for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
					local n = d.Name
					if d:IsA("BillboardGui") and (n == "NameESP" or n == "GunNameESP" or n:sub(1, 12) == "BearTrapESP_") then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
					if d:IsA("Highlight") and (n == "GunESP" or n == "NameESP" or n == "InnocentESP" or n == "SheriffESP" or n:sub(1, 12) == "InnocentESP_" or n:sub(1, 12) == "SheriffESP_" or n:sub(1, 11) == "MurderESP_") then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
					if d:GetAttribute("Owned") then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
				end
			end
			task.wait()
		end
		_G.__silent = false
		_G.__hideb = nil
		_G.__lightsave = nil
	end)
end

			pcall(function() o:Destroy() end)
		end
	end
end)

		local function sweep(container)

			for _, d in ipairs(desc) do

							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		sweep(_G.pgui)
	end)

end

local guiVisible = false

local function showToggleButton()
	if toggleButton.Visible then return end
	if mainFrame.Visible then return end
	if _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN] and _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].hideAll then return end
	toggleButton.Visible = true
	toggleButton.Position = _G.BUTTON_POS
	toggleButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	logo.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	logo.TextSize = 0
	TweenService:Create(toggleButton, TweenInfo.new(1, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Size = _G.BUTTON_FULL_SIZE
	}):Play()
	TweenService:Create(logo, TweenInfo.new(1, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Size = _G.LOGO_FULL_SIZE, TextSize = 34
	}):Play()
end
introFrame.Destroying:Connect(showToggleButton)
task.delay(10, function() if introFrame and introFrame.Parent then return end
	showToggleButton()
end)
do
	local dragging, dragInput, dragStart, startPos
	toggleButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging = true
			dragStart = input.Position
			startPos = toggleButton.Position
			input.Changed:Connect(function() if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
					dragging = false
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	toggleButton.InputChanged:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragInput = input
		end
	end)
	__live(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
		if dragging and input == dragInput then
			local delta = input.Position - dragStart
			toggleButton.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
		end
	end)
end
_G.FULL_SIZE = UDim2.new(0, 460, 0, 330)
local savedPos = UDim2.new(0.5, -230, 0.5, -165)
do
	local clickStart
	local pressStartTime
	local SMALL_SIZE = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

	local function buttonCenter()
		return UDim2.new( toggleButton.Position.X.Scale, toggleButton.Position.X.Offset, toggleButton.Position.Y.Scale, toggleButton.Position.Y.Offset
		)
	end
	toggleButton.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() clickStart = { x = toggleButton.Position.X.Offset, y = toggleButton.Position.Y.Offset }
		pressStartTime = os.clock()
	end)
	toggleButton.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() if not clickStart then return end
		local pos = toggleButton.Position
		local moved = math.abs(pos.X.Offset - clickStart.x) + math.abs(pos.Y.Offset - clickStart.y)
		local quickPress = pressStartTime and os.clock() - pressStartTime < 0.25
		clickStart = nil
		pressStartTime = nil
		if quickPress then
			local shrink = TweenService:Create(toggleButton, TweenInfo.new(0.08, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
				Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 44)
			})
			shrink:Play()
			TweenService:Create(logo, TweenInfo.new(0.08, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30), TextSize = 26
			}):Play()
			shrink.Completed:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(toggleButton, TweenInfo.new(0.1, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Size = _G.BUTTON_FULL_SIZE
			}):Play()
				TweenService:Create(logo, TweenInfo.new(0.1, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Size = _G.LOGO_FULL_SIZE, TextSize = 34
				}):Play()
			end)
		end
		if moved >= 10 then return end
		guiVisible = not guiVisible
		if guiVisible then
			mainFrame.Visible = true
			mainFrame.Size = SMALL_SIZE
			mainFrame.Position = buttonCenter()
			local openTween = TweenService:Create(mainFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Size = _G.FULL_SIZE, Position = savedPos
			})
			openTween:Play()
		else
			local closeTween = TweenService:Create(mainFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.25, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
				Size = SMALL_SIZE, Position = buttonCenter()
			})
			closeTween:Play()
			closeTween.Completed:Connect(function() if not guiVisible then
					mainFrame.Visible = false
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
end

		}):Play()

		}):Play()

	end

local pgs = {}
_G.tabs = {"Info", "Main", "Combat", "Visuals", "Warp", "Misc", "Settings"}
_G.tabButtons = {}
_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].tabButtons = _G.tabButtons
local activeIndicator = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "ActiveIndicator", "Size", UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundColor3", LIME, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ZIndex", 3, "Parent", frame})
_G.tabIndex = { Info = 1, Main = 2, Combat = 3, Visuals = 4, Warp = 5, Misc = 6, Settings = 7 }

local function setActiveTab(name, animate)
	if animate == nil then animate = true end
	for tabName, button in pairs(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].tabButtons) do
		if button and button.Parent then
			local label = button:FindFirstChild("Label")
			if label then
				local th = THEMES[selectedTheme]
				if tabName == name then
					label.TextColor3 = th.accent
					label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				else
					label.TextColor3 = (th and th.light) and C_T_PRIM or TEXT_SECONDARY
					label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local idx = _G.tabIndex[name] or 1
	local targetPos = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, (idx - 1) * 36)
	if animate then
		TweenService:Create(activeIndicator, TweenInfo.new(0.22, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = targetPos
		}):Play()
	else
		activeIndicator.Position = targetPos
	end
	for pageName, page in pairs(pgs) do
		if page and page.Parent then page.Visible = (pageName == name) end
	end
end

for i, name in ipairs(_G.tabs) do
	local tabButton = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", name, "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "LayoutOrder", i, "Parent", side})
	__mk("Frame", {"Name", "Indicator", "Size", UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, 0), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", tabButton})
	local label = __mk("TextLabel", {"Name", "Label", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", name, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", tabButton})
	label:SetAttribute("BaseTxtC", TEXT_SECONDARY)
	do
		local ids = { Warp = "rbxassetid://81965649414258", Info = "rbxassetid://97524596514958", Settings = "rbxassetid://89108848194312", Visuals = "rbxassetid://136446745324324",
			Main = "rbxassetid://96578643683773", Misc = "rbxassetid://118445136783717", Combat = "rbxassetid://138073064178308" }
		local id = ids[name]
		if id then
			__mk("ImageLabel", {"Name", "TabIcon", "Size", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -9), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Image", id, "ImageColor3", LIME, "Parent", tabButton})
			label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 0)
			label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -38, 1, 0)
			label.ClipsDescendants = true
		end
	end
	_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].tabButtons[name] = tabButton
	tabButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if name == "Info" and not pgs["Info"] then return end
		if name == "Info" and _G.__infoTint then _G.__infoTint() end
		setActiveTab(name)
	end)
end

	local themeListStroke = __mk("UIStroke", {"Color", BORDER, "Thickness", 1, "ApplyStrokeMode", Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, "Transparency", 1, "Parent", themeList})
	local setThemeOpen
	for i, optName in ipairs({ "Lime", "Blue", "Orange", "Rose", "Red", "Cat" }) do
		local opt = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, (i - 1) * 32), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", themeList})
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", optName, "Font", Enum.Font.Gotham, "TextSize", 12, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", opt})
		opt.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectedTheme = optName
			themeDropdownLabel.Text = "Theme: " .. optName
			applyTheme(optName)
			pcall(function() writefile("AeonHub/AeonHubBin/theme.txt", optName) end)
			setThemeOpen(false)
		end)
	end
	setThemeOpen = function(open)
		if not alive() then return end
		themeDropdownOpen = open

		if open then

			local th = (themeDropdown.Position.Y.Offset + themeList.Position.Y.Offset) + themeList.Size.Y.Offset + 8 - settingsBox.Position.Y.Offset

			local push = th - 173

			_G.__MM2_CfgPush = push

			local cfgD = page:FindFirstChild("ConfigDivider")

			local cfgL = page:FindFirstChild("ConfigLoadButton")
			local cfgR = page:FindFirstChild("ConfigRefreshButton")
			local cfgS = page:FindFirstChild("ConfigSaveButton")
			local cfgX = page:FindFirstChild("ConfigDeleteButton")
			local cfgO = page:FindFirstChild("ConfigOverwriteButton")
			local cfgAS = page:FindFirstChild("ConfigAutoSetButton")
			local cfgAC = page:FindFirstChild("ConfigAutoClearButton")

			if cfgC then TweenService:Create(cfgC, TweenInfo.new(0.1, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 268 + push) }):Play() end
			if cfgB then TweenService:Create(cfgB, TweenInfo.new(0.1, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 283 + push) }):Play() end
			if cfgN then TweenService:Create(cfgN, TweenInfo.new(0.1, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 324 + push + csh) }):Play() end

			if cfgS then TweenService:Create(cfgS, TweenInfo.new(0.1, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 406 + push + csh) }):Play() end

				if cfgL then TweenService:Create(cfgL, TweenInfo.new(0.18, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.In), { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 365 + csh) }):Play() end
				local cfgR = page:FindFirstChild("ConfigRefreshButton")

	_G.__MM2_MenuKeyApply = function(nm)
		local ok, kc = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[nm] end)
		if ok and kc and menuKeybind then
			menuKey = kc
			menuKeybind.Text = nm
		end
	end
	menuKeybind.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() capturingMenuKey = true
		captureArmedAt = os.clock()
		capturePrevText = menuKeybind.Text
		menuKeybind.Text = "..."
	end)
	_G.__MM2_UIS(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gpe) if not alive() then return end
		if ded then return end
		if gpe then return end
		if capturingMenuKey and os.clock() - captureArmedAt > 15 then
			capturingMenuKey = false
			if capturePrevText then menuKeybind.Text = capturePrevText end
		end
		if capturingMenuKey then
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
				menuKey = input.KeyCode
				local nm = tostring(input.KeyCode):gsub("Enum.KeyCode.", "")
				_G.__MM2_MenuKeyName = nm
				local short = { LeftControl = "LCtrl", RightControl = "RCtrl", LeftShift = "LShift", RightShift = "RShift", LeftAlt = "LAlt", RightAlt = "RAlt", CapsLock = "Caps", Space = "Spc", Backspace = "Bspc" }
				nm = short[nm] or nm
				if #nm > 5 then nm = nm:sub(1, 5) end
				menuKeybind.Text = nm
				menuKeybind.TextColor3 = __MM2_BaseTxt()
			end
			capturingMenuKey = false
			return
		end
		if menuKey and input.KeyCode == menuKey then
			local hrt = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN]
			if hrt then hrt.hideAll = not hrt.hideAll end
			if hrt and hrt.hideAll then
				mainFrame.Visible = false
				toggleButton.Visible = false
			else
				guiVisible = true
				mainFrame.Visible = true
				mainFrame.Size = _G.FULL_SIZE
				mainFrame.Position = savedPos
				toggleButton.Visible = true
				toggleButton.Size = _G.BUTTON_FULL_SIZE
				logo.Size = _G.LOGO_FULL_SIZE
				logo.TextSize = 34
			end
		end
	end)

	local function setGunsEnabled(state)
		pcall(function() local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
			local ch = lp and lp.Character
			if ch then
				for _, t in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do
					if t:IsA("Tool") and (t.Name == "Gun" or t.Name == "Revolver") then
						pcall(function() t.Enabled = state end)
					end
				end
			end
			local bp = lp and lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
			if bp then
				for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
					if t:IsA("Tool") and (t.Name == "Gun" or t.Name == "Revolver") then
						pcall(function() t.Enabled = state end)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end

							for _, c in ipairs(gg) do pcall(function() c:Enable() end) end
						end
					end)
				end
				pcall(function() setGunsEnabled(true) end)
				pcall(function() if _G.__FVL_UNSUPPRESS then _G.__FVL_UNSUPPRESS() end
				end)

				pcall(function() if espf and espf.Parent then espf:Destroy() end end)
				pcall(function() sgui:Destroy() end)
				if __MM2_OtherGui(sgui) then return end
				pcall(function() for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
						if d:GetAttribute("Owned") then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
					end
				end)
				pcall(function() local lpu = Players.LocalPlayer
					local chu = lpu and lpu.Character
					if chu then
						for _, n in ipairs({ "Farm Gyro", "Farm Velocity", "PredBall" }) do
							local o = chu:FindFirstChild(n)
							if o then pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
						end
					end
					local camU = workspace.CurrentCamera
					if camU then
						local b = camU:FindFirstChild("HitboxBall")
						if b then pcall(function() b:Destroy() end) end
					end
				end)

					if S0.FogColor ~= nil then pcall(function() L0.FogColor = S0.FogColor end) end

						pcall(function() local lpu = Players.LocalPlayer
							local chu = lpu and lpu.Character
							if not chu then return end
							for _, n in ipairs({ "Farm Gyro", "Farm Velocity", "HitboxBall" }) do
								local o = chu:FindFirstChild(n)
								if o then pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
							end
							local camU = workspace.CurrentCamera
							if camU then
								local b = camU:FindFirstChild("HitboxBall")
								if b then pcall(function() b:Destroy() end) end
							end
						end)
					end)
				end)
				pcall(function() local t = _G.__draws
					if type(t) == "table" then
						for _, d in ipairs(t) do pcall(function() d:Remove() end) end
					end
					_G.__draws = nil
				end)
				pcall(function() task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.3)
						if espf and espf.Parent then return end
						local ps = game:GetService("Players")
						for _, plr in ipairs(ps:GetPlayers()) do
							local ch = plr.Character
							if ch then
								task.wait()
								for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
									local n = d.Name
									if d:IsA("BillboardGui") and (n == "NameESP" or n == "GunNameESP" or n:sub(1, 12) == "BearTrapESP_") then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
									if d:IsA("Highlight")
										and (n == "GunESP" or n == "NameESP" or n == "InnocentESP" or n == "SheriffESP" or n == "MurderESP" or n:sub(1, 12) == "InnocentESP_" or n:sub(1, 12) == "SheriffESP_" or n:sub(1,
											11) == "MurderESP_") then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end)
				pcall(function() local lpU = Players.LocalPlayer
					local chU = lpU and lpU.Character
					local humU = chU and chU:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if humU then
						pcall(function() humU.WalkSpeed = 16 end)
						pcall(function() humU.JumpPower = 50 end)
						pcall(function() humU.UseJumpPower = false end)
						pcall(function() humU.JumpHeight = 7.2 end)
						pcall(function() humU.PlatformStand = false end)
					end
					if chU then
						for _, d in ipairs(chU:GetDescendants()) do
							if d:IsA("BodyVelocity") or d:IsA("BodyGyro") then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
						end
					end
				end)
				pcall(function() workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom end)
				pcall(function() if espf and espf.Parent then espf:Destroy() end end)
				pcall(function() sgui:Destroy() end)
				pcall(function() local rt = _G.__MM2_RT and _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN]
					if rt then rt.cleanup = {} end
					_G.__lightsave = nil
					_G.__silent = false
					_G.__hideb = nil
					_G.__MM2_Spots = nil
					_G.__draws = nil
				end)
			end)
		end
	end)
	__mk("Frame", {"Name", "ConfigDivider", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 1), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 231), "BackgroundColor3", Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80), "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", page})
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Name", "ConfigHeader", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 14), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 239), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", "Config", "Parent", page})
	local configBox = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "ConfigBox", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 271), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 268), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ZIndex", 0, "Parent", page})
	__c(configBox, UDim.new(0, 8))
	__s(configBox, BORDER, 1, true)
	local cfgDrop = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "ConfigDropdown", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 283), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", page})
	__s(cfgDrop, BORDER, 1, true)
	local cfgDropLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Config: ...", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", cfgDrop})
	local cfgNameBox = __mk("TextBox", {"Name", "ConfigNameBox", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 324), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "PlaceholderText", "Config name...", "PlaceholderColor3", Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100), "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "ClearTextOnFocus", false, "Parent", page})
	__s(cfgNameBox, BORDER, 1, true)
	__mk("UIPadding", {"PaddingLeft", UDim.new(0, 12), "PaddingRight", UDim.new(0, 12), "Parent", cfgNameBox})
	local cfgLoadBtn = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "ConfigLoadButton", "Size", UDim2.new(0.5, -20, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 365), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", page})
	__s(cfgLoadBtn, BORDER, 1, true)
	local cfgLoadBtnLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Load", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", cfgLoadBtn})
	local cfgRefresh = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "ConfigRefreshButton", "Size", UDim2.new(0.5, -21, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0.5, 3, 0, 365), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", page})
	__s(cfgRefresh, BORDER, 1, true)
	local cfgRefreshLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Refresh", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", cfgRefresh})
	local cfgSave = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "ConfigSaveButton", "Size", UDim2.new(0.5, -20, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 406), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", page})
	__s(cfgSave, BORDER, 1, true)
	local cfgSaveLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Save", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", cfgSave})
	local cfgDelete = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "ConfigDeleteButton", "Size", UDim2.new(0.5, -21, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0.5, 3, 0, 406), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", page})
	__s(cfgDelete, BORDER, 1, true)
	local cfgDeleteLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Delete", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", cfgDelete})
	local cfgOver = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "ConfigOverwriteButton", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 447), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", page})
	__s(cfgOver, BORDER, 1, true)
	local cfgOverLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Overwrite", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", cfgOver})
	local cfgAutoSet = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "ConfigAutoSetButton", "Size", UDim2.new(0.5, -20, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 488), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", page})
	__s(cfgAutoSet, BORDER, 1, true)
	local cfgAutoSetLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Set as auto-load", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", cfgAutoSet})
	local cfgAutoClr = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "ConfigAutoClearButton", "Size", UDim2.new(0.5, -21, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0.5, 3, 0, 488), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", page})
	__s(cfgAutoClr, BORDER, 1, true)
	local cfgAutoClrLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Clear auto-load", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", cfgAutoClr})
	local CFG_DIR = "AeonHub/AeonHubMM2configs"
	local cfgHttp = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local cfgOpts = {}
	local cfgTimes = {}
	local cfgOpen = false
	local cfgSelected = nil
	local cfgToggleAlias = { autokill = "antifling", spectate = "spamfling" }
	local cfgOpenList
	cfgDrop.ZIndex = 10
	local cfgArrow = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "v", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "Parent", cfgDrop})
	local cfgList = __mk("ScrollingFrame", {"Name", "ConfigList", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 41), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "ZIndex", 10, "ScrollBarThickness", 0, "Visible", false, "Parent", cfgDrop})
	local cfgListStroke = __mk("UIStroke", {"Color", BORDER, "Thickness", 1, "ApplyStrokeMode", Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, "Transparency", 1, "Parent", cfgList})

	local function cfgClean(name)
		name = tostring(name or ""):gsub("[^%w _%-]", "")
		name = name:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
		if #name > 32 then name = name:sub(1, 32) end
		return name
	end

	local function cfgPopName(n)
		n = tostring(n or "")
		if #n > 16 then n = n:sub(1, 16) .. "..." end
		return n
	end

	local function cfgToast(sub)
		task.spawn(function() if _G.__MM2_Toast then pcall(_G.__MM2_Toast, "Config", sub, "CfgWarn") end end)
	end

	local function cfgFlash(label)
		if not label then return end
		TweenService:Create(label, TWEEN_FAST, { TextColor3 = __headCol() }):Play()
		task.delay(1.2, function() if label.Parent then pcall(function() TweenService:Create(label, TweenInfo.new(0.3, EASE_Q), { TextColor3 = __MM2_LightTxt() }):Play() end) end
		end)
	end

	local function cfgScan()
		cfgOpts = {}
		cfgTimes = {}
		pcall(function() for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(CFG_DIR)) do
				local n = tostring(f):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
				if n then
					table.insert(cfgOpts, n)
					local t = 0
					pcall(function() local d = cfgHttp:JSONDecode(readfile(CFG_DIR .. "/" .. n .. ".json"))
						if type(d) == "table" and type(d.t) == "number" then t = d.t end
					end)
					cfgTimes[n] = t
				end
			end
		end)
		table.sort(cfgOpts, function(a, b) local ta = cfgTimes[a] or 0
			local tb = cfgTimes[b] or 0
			if ta ~= tb then return ta < tb end
			return a < b
		end)
		if cfgSelected then
			local found = false
			for _, n in ipairs(cfgOpts) do
				if n == cfgSelected then found = true break end
			end
			if not found then
				cfgSelected = nil
				cfgDropLabel.Text = "Config: ..."
			end
		end
	end

	local function cfgAutoName()
		local found = nil
		pcall(function() for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(CFG_DIR)) do
				local base = tostring(f):match("([^/\\]+)$")
				local n = base and base:match("^(.+)autoload%.txt$")
				if n then found = n end
			end
		end)
		return found
	end

	local function cfgClearAuto()
		pcall(function() for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(CFG_DIR)) do
				local base = tostring(f):match("([^/\\]+)$")
				if base and base:match("^.+autoload%.txt$") then pcall(delfile, tostring(f)) end
			end
		end)
	end

	local function cfgSetAuto(name)
		cfgClearAuto()
		pcall(function() writefile(CFG_DIR .. "/" .. name .. "autoload.txt", name) end)
	end
	_G.__MM2_CfgAutoGet = cfgAutoName

	local function cfgApply(name)
		local ok, data = pcall(function() return cfgHttp:JSONDecode(readfile(CFG_DIR .. "/" .. name .. ".json")) end)
		if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" then return false end
		local order = { "rain", "skybox", "noRain", "hitbox", "showhitbox" }
		local seen = {}

		local function apply(key)
			key = cfgToggleAlias[key] or key
			local e = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].toggles[key]
			if not e or seen[key] then return end
			seen[key] = true
			local want = data.toggles and data.toggles[key]
			if type(want) ~= "boolean" then return end
			pcall(e.set, want)
			if e.track then
				e.track.BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(want)
				if e.knob then
					e.knob.Position = want and UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2)
					e.knob.BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(want)
				end
			end
		end
		for _, k in ipairs(order) do apply(k) end
		if type(data.toggles) == "table" then
			for k in pairs(data.toggles) do apply(k) end
		end
		if type(data.values) == "table" and type(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].vals) == "table" then
			for k, v in pairs(data.values) do
				local e = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].vals[k]
				if e and type(v) == "number" and v == v then
					if e.min and v < e.min then v = e.min end
					if e.max and v > e.max then v = e.max end
					pcall(e.set, v)
				end
			end
		end
		if type(data.skybox) == "string" and _G.__MM2_CfgSkyboxApply then pcall(_G.__MM2_CfgSkyboxApply, data.skybox) end
		if type(data.anim) == "string" and _G.__MM2_AnimChoiceApply then pcall(_G.__MM2_AnimChoiceApply, data.anim) end
		if type(data.aimTarget) == "string" and _G.__MM2_AimTargetApply then pcall(_G.__MM2_AimTargetApply, data.aimTarget) end
		if type(data.menuKey) == "string" and _G.__MM2_MenuKeyApply then pcall(_G.__MM2_MenuKeyApply, data.menuKey) end
		return true
	end
	_G.__MM2_CfgLoad = cfgApply
	local cfgRender
	_G.__MM2_CfgAutoLoad = function(name)
		if cfgApply(name) then
			cfgSelected = name
			cfgDropLabel.Text = "Config: " .. name
			cfgScan()
			cfgRender()
		end
	end

	local function cfgWrite(name)
		local toggles = {}
		for k, e in pairs(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].toggles) do
			local ok, v = pcall(e.get)
			if ok then toggles[k] = v and true or false end
		end
		local values = {}
		if type(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].vals) == "table" then
			for k, e in pairs(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].vals) do
				local ok, v = pcall(e.get)
				if ok and type(v) == "number" and v == v then values[k] = v end
			end
		end
		local created = os.time()
		pcall(function() local old = cfgHttp:JSONDecode(readfile(CFG_DIR .. "/" .. name .. ".json"))
			if type(old) == "table" and type(old.t) == "number" then created = old.t end
		end)
		return pcall(function() writefile(CFG_DIR .. "/" .. name .. ".json", cfgHttp:JSONEncode({ v = 2, t = created, toggles = toggles, values = values, skybox = _G.__MM2_SkyChoiceName, anim = _G.__MM2_AnimChoiceName, aimTarget = _G.aimTarget, menuKey = _G.__MM2_MenuKeyName }))
		end)
	end
	cfgRender = function()
		__optClear(cfgList)
		for i, name in ipairs(cfgOpts) do
			local opt = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, (i - 1) * 32), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Name", "Cfg" .. i, "ZIndex", 11, "Parent", cfgList})
			__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", name, "Font", Enum.Font.Gotham, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", (name == cfgSelected) and acc or TEXT_SECONDARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", opt})
			opt.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() cfgApply(name)
				cfgSelected = name
				cfgDropLabel.Text = "Config: " .. name
				cfgOpenList(false)
			end)
		end
		cfgList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #cfgOpts * 32)
	end
	local cfgCard = page:FindFirstChild("ConfigBox")

	local function cfgSetShift(shift, ti)
		_G.__MM2_CfgShift = shift
		local push = _G.__MM2_CfgPush or 0
		ti = ti or TWEEN_POP

		local function mv(o, xs, xo, base)
			if o then TweenService:Create(o, ti, { Position = UDim2.new(xs, xo, 0, base + push + shift) }):Play() end
		end
		mv(cfgNameBox, 0, 18, 324)
		mv(cfgLoadBtn, 0, 18, 365)
		mv(cfgRefresh, 0.5, 3, 365)
		mv(cfgSave, 0, 18, 406)
		mv(cfgDelete, 0.5, 3, 406)
		mv(cfgOver, 0, 18, 447)
		mv(cfgAutoSet, 0, 18, 488)
		mv(cfgAutoClr, 0.5, 3, 488)
		if cfgCard then TweenService:Create(cfgCard, ti, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 268 + push), Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 271 + shift) }):Play() end
		TweenService:Create(page, ti, { CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 660 + shift) }):Play()
	end
	cfgOpenList = function(open)
		if open then cfgScan() end
		cfgOpen = open
		cfgArrow.Rotation = open and 180 or 0
		local has = open and #cfgOpts > 0
		page.ScrollingEnabled = not has
		if has then
			cfgRender()
			local h = math.min(#cfgOpts * 32, 96)
			cfgList.BackgroundTransparency = 0
			cfgListStroke.Transparency = 0
			cfgList.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
			cfgList.Visible = true
			TweenService:Create(cfgList, TWEEN_POP, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h) }):Play()
			cfgSetShift(h + 6, TWEEN_POP)
		else
			TweenService:Create(cfgList, TWEEN_EASE, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0) }):Play()
			task.delay(0.2, function() if not cfgOpen then cfgList.Visible = false end end)
			cfgSetShift(0, TWEEN_EASE)
		end
	end
	cfgDrop.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() cfgOpenList(not cfgOpen) end)
	cfgLoadBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() cfgFlash(cfgLoadBtnLabel)
		local name = cfgSelected
		local typed = cfgClean(cfgNameBox.Text)
		if typed ~= "" then
			local exists = false
			pcall(function() exists = isfile(CFG_DIR .. "/" .. typed .. ".json") end)
			if exists then name = typed end
		end
		if not name then cfgToast("Failed") return end
		if cfgApply(name) then
			cfgToast("Loaded " .. cfgPopName(name))
			cfgSelected = name
			cfgDropLabel.Text = "Config: " .. name
			cfgScan()
			cfgRender()
		else
			cfgToast("Failed")
		end
	end)
	cfgRefresh.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() cfgFlash(cfgRefreshLabel)
		cfgScan()
		cfgRender()
		cfgToast("Succesfully refreshed")
	end)
	cfgSave.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() cfgFlash(cfgSaveLabel)
		local name = cfgClean(cfgNameBox.Text)
		if name == "" then cfgToast("Failed") return end
		if not cfgWrite(name) then cfgToast("Failed") return end
		cfgToast("Saved " .. cfgPopName(name))
		cfgNameBox.Text = ""
		cfgSelected = name
		cfgDropLabel.Text = "Config: " .. name
		cfgScan()
		cfgRender()
		if cfgOpen then cfgList.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, cfgList.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - cfgList.Size.Y.Offset)) end
	end)
	cfgDelete.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() cfgFlash(cfgDeleteLabel)
		if not cfgSelected then cfgToast("Failed") return end
		cfgToast("Deleted " .. cfgPopName(cfgSelected))
		pcall(function() if isfile(CFG_DIR .. "/" .. cfgSelected .. ".json") then delfile(CFG_DIR .. "/" .. cfgSelected .. ".json") end end)
		pcall(function() if isfile(CFG_DIR .. "/" .. cfgSelected .. "autoload.txt") then delfile(CFG_DIR .. "/" .. cfgSelected .. "autoload.txt") end end)
		cfgSelected = nil
		cfgDropLabel.Text = "Config: ..."
		cfgScan()
		cfgRender()
	end)
	cfgOver.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() cfgFlash(cfgOverLabel)
		if cfgSelected then cfgWrite(cfgSelected) cfgToast("Overwrited " .. cfgPopName(cfgSelected)) else cfgToast("Failed") end
	end)
	cfgAutoSet.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() cfgFlash(cfgAutoSetLabel)
		if cfgSelected then
			cfgSetAuto(cfgSelected)
			cfgRender()
			cfgToast("Set " .. cfgPopName(cfgSelected))
		else
			cfgToast("Failed")
		end
	end)
	cfgAutoClr.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() cfgFlash(cfgAutoClrLabel)
		local an = cfgAutoName()
		cfgClearAuto()
		cfgRender()
		cfgToast(an and ("Clear " .. cfgPopName(an)) or "Cleared auto-load")
	end)
	pgs[name] = page
end

local function hasTool(p, ...)
	local names = { ... }
	local char = p.Character
	if char then
		for _, c in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("Tool") then
				for _, n in ipairs(names) do
					if c.Name == n then return true end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local bp = p:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	if bp then
		for _, c in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("Tool") then
				for _, n in ipairs(names) do
					if c.Name == n then return true end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return false
end
do
	local page = __mk("ScrollingFrame", {"Name", "VisualsPage", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Visible", false, "CanvasSize", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 580), "ScrollingDirection", Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, "ElasticBehavior", Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never, "ScrollBarThickness", 0, "Parent", inner})
	pgs["Visuals"] = page
	__mk("UIPadding", {"PaddingTop", UDim.new(0, 12), "PaddingLeft", UDim.new(0, 18), "PaddingRight", UDim.new(0, 18), "PaddingBottom", UDim.new(0, 18), "Parent", page})
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", "Player", "Parent", page})
	local innocentESPEnabled = false
	local ihl = {}

	local function innocentHasTool(p, name)
		local char = p.Character
		if char and char:FindFirstChild(name) then return true end
		local bp = p:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		if bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name) then return true end
		for _, v in ipairs(char and char:GetDescendants() or {}) do
			if v.Name:lower():find(name:lower()) and (v:IsA("Tool") or v:IsA("BasePart")) then return true end
		end
		for _, v in ipairs(bp and bp:GetDescendants() or {}) do
			if v.Name:lower():find(name:lower()) and (v:IsA("Tool") or v:IsA("BasePart")) then return true end
		end
		return false
	end
	local gpdDead = nil
	pcall(function() local rsD = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
		local rm = rsD:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
		local ex = rm and rm:FindFirstChild("Extras")
		gpdDead = ex and ex:FindFirstChild("GetPlayerData") or nil
	end)
	_G.__MM2_DeadCache = _G.__MM2_DeadCache or {}
	if not (_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN] and _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].deadPoll and _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].deadPoll.Connected) then
		_G.__MM2_DeadPoll = _G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then return end
			if ded then return end
			if os.clock() - (_G.__MM2_DeadT or 0) < 2 then return end
			_G.__MM2_DeadT = os.clock()
			local cache = _G.__MM2_DeadCache
			if type(cache) ~= "table" then return end
			if not gpdDead then
				pcall(function() local rsR = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
					local rmR = rsR:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
					local exR = rmR and rmR:FindFirstChild("Extras")
					gpdDead = exR and exR:FindFirstChild("GetPlayerData") or nil
				end)
				if not gpdDead then return end
			end
			local okD, resD = pcall(function() return gpdDead:InvokeServer() end)
			if not okD or type(resD) ~= "table" then return end
			local raD = false
			pcall(function() if _G.__MM2_RoundActive then raD = _G.__MM2_RoundActive() end end)
			if raD and not _G.__MM2_RoundWas then
				local lt0 = _G.__MM2_DeadLatch
				if type(lt0) == "table" then
					for k in pairs(lt0) do lt0[k] = nil end
				end
			end
			_G.__MM2_RoundWas = raD
			local stage, staged = {}, 0
			for _, plD in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				local ent = resD[plD.Name]
				if type(ent) == "table" then
					local deadD = (ent.Dead == true) or (ent.Killed == true)
					stage[plD] = deadD
					staged = staged + 1
					local lt = _G.__MM2_DeadLatch
					if type(lt) ~= "table" then lt = {} _G.__MM2_DeadLatch = lt end
					if deadD then
						lt[plD] = plD.Character
					elseif lt[plD] ~= nil and lt[plD] ~= plD.Character then
						lt[plD] = nil
					end
				end
			end
			if staged == 0 then
				for k in pairs(cache) do cache[k] = nil end
				return
			end
			for k in pairs(cache) do cache[k] = nil end
			for k, v in pairs(stage) do cache[k] = v end
		end)
		if _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN] then _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].deadPoll = _G.__MM2_DeadPoll end
	end
	local roundMod = nil

	local function serverPlayerData(name)
		if not roundMod then
			pcall(function() for _, d in ipairs(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):GetDescendants()) do
					if d.Name == "CurrentRoundClient" and d:IsA("ModuleScript") then
						roundMod = d
						break
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		if not roundMod then return nil end
		local entry = nil
		pcall(function() local tbl = require(roundMod)
			if type(tbl) == "table" and type(tbl.PlayerData) == "table" then
				entry = tbl.PlayerData[name]
			end
		end)
		if type(entry) ~= "table" then return nil end
		return entry
	end
	local DEAD_WORDS = { dead = true, killed = true, eliminated = true, corpse = true, spectating = true, deadbody = true, deadmechanic = true, }
	local memo = {}
	local memoT = 0

	local function entryFor(p)
		local now = os.clock()
		if now - memoT > 0.25 then
			memoT = now
			memo = {}
		end
		local cached = memo[p]
		if cached == false then return nil end
		if cached ~= nil then return cached end
		local e = serverPlayerData(p.Name)
		memo[p] = e or false
		return e
	end

	local function roundTablePopulated()
		if not roundMod then return false end
		local populated = false
		pcall(function() local tbl = require(roundMod)
			if type(tbl) == "table" and type(tbl.PlayerData) == "table" then
				for _ in pairs(tbl.PlayerData) do populated = true break end
			end
		end)
		return populated
	end

	local function serverDead(p)
		local result = false
		local e = entryFor(p)
		if e then
			if e.Dead == true or e.Killed == true then return true end
			if e.Alive == false then return true end
			for _, key in ipairs({ "Status", "State", "Role", "Dead", "Killed" }) do
				local raw = e[key]
				if type(raw) == "string" then
					local norm = raw:lower():gsub("%s+", "")
					if DEAD_WORDS[norm] then return true end
				end
			end
		elseif roundTablePopulated() then
			result = true
		end
		return result
	end
	local LIVE_ROLES = { innocent = true, murderer = true, sheriff = true, hero = true, zombie = true, survivor = true, }

	local function serverRoleLive(p)
		if not roundMod then return true end
		local e = entryFor(p)
		if not e then return false end
		local raw = e.Role
		if type(raw) ~= "string" then return false end
		local ok, norm = pcall(function() return raw:lower():gsub("%s+", "") end)
		if not ok or not norm then return false end
		return LIVE_ROLES[norm] == true
	end

	local function liveZombie(p)
		local z = false
		pcall(function() local c = p.Character
			if not c or c.Parent ~= workspace then return end
			local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not h or h.Health <= 0 then return end
			if c:GetAttribute("Zombie") == true then z = true return end
			local bp = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
			if c:FindFirstChild("Knife") or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Knife")) then z = true end
		end)
		return z
	end

	local function innocentIsDead(p)
		local dead = false
		pcall(function() local char = p.Character
			local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not char or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then dead = true end
		end)
		if dead then return true end
		if liveZombie(p) then return false end
		local attr = nil
		pcall(function() attr = p:GetAttribute("Dead") end)
		if attr == true then return true end
		if serverDead(p) then return true end
		if type(_G.__MM2_DeadCache) == "table" then
			local srv = _G.__MM2_DeadCache[p]
			if srv == true then return true end
		end
		local lt = _G.__MM2_DeadLatch
		if type(lt) == "table" then
			local ch = lt[p]
			if ch ~= nil and ch == p.Character then return true end
		end
		return false
	end

	local function isInvisibleChar(char)
		local parts = 0
		local hid = 0
		for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") then
				parts = parts + 1
				local t = 0
				pcall(function() t = d.Transparency end)
				if t >= 0.9 then hid = hid + 1 end
				if parts > 30 then break end
			end
		end
		return parts > 0 and hid == parts
	end

	local function hasVisibleBody(char)
		local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
		local torso = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
		if not head and not torso then return false end

		local function vis(inst)
			if not inst then return false end
			local t = 1
			pcall(function() t = inst.Transparency end)
			return t < 0.9
		end
		return vis(head) or vis(torso)
	end

	local function bodyHidden(char)
		if not char then return true end
		local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
		local torso = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
		if not head and not torso then return true end
		local vis, saw = false, false
		for _, part in ipairs({ head, torso }) do
			if part then
				saw = true
				local t = 1
				pcall(function() t = part.Transparency end)
				if t < 0.9 then vis = true end
			end
		end
		return saw and not vis
	end

	local function hasExactTool(p, ...)
		return hasTool(p, ...)
	end

	local function hasknife(p)
		return hasExactTool(p, "Knife")
	end

	local function hasGun(p)
		return hasExactTool(p, "Gun", "Revolver")
	end

	local function serverRole(name)
		if not roundMod then
			pcall(function() for _, d in ipairs(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):GetDescendants()) do
					if d.Name == "CurrentRoundClient" and d:IsA("ModuleScript") then
						roundMod = d
						break
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		if roundMod then
			local ok, role = pcall(function() local tbl = require(roundMod)
				if type(tbl) == "table" and type(tbl.PlayerData) == "table" then
					local e = tbl.PlayerData[name]
					if type(e) == "table" and type(e.Role) == "string" then return e.Role end
				end
				return nil
			end)
			if ok then return role end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function normRole(r)
		if type(r) ~= "string" then return nil end
		local ok, res = pcall(function() return r:lower():gsub("%s+", "") end)
		if ok then return res end
		return nil
	end

	local function isInnocentRole(p)
		local r = nil
		pcall(function() r = serverRole(p.Name) end)
		if r == nil then return false end
		local rn = normRole(r)
		return rn == "innocent" or rn == "survivor"
	end

	local function detectRole(p)
		local zb = nil
		pcall(function() local c = p.Character
			if c then zb = c:GetAttribute("Zombie") end
		end)
		if zb == true then return "Murderer" end
		if hasknife(p) then return "Murderer" end
		if hasGun(p) then return "Sheriff" end
		local r = serverRole(p.Name)
		local rn = normRole(r)
		if rn == "zombie" then return "Murderer" end
		if rn == "murderer" then return "Murderer" end
		if rn == "sheriff" or rn == "hero" then return "Sheriff" end
		return nil
	end
	_G.__MM2_DetectRole = detectRole

	local function innocentRole(p)
		local d = detectRole(p)
		if d == "Murderer" or d == "Sheriff" then return "Armed" end
		return nil
	end

	local function roundActive()
		local known = false
		local hasMod = false
		pcall(function() for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				local r = serverRole(plr.Name)
				if roundMod then hasMod = true end
				local rn = normRole(r)
				if rn == "murderer" or rn == "sheriff" or rn == "hero" or rn == "innocent"
					or rn == "zombie" or rn == "survivor" then known = true break end
			end
		end)
		if not hasMod then return true end
		return known
	end
	_G.__MM2_RoundActive = roundActive
	_G.__MM2_IsDead = innocentIsDead

	local function espCharOk(p)
		local char = p.Character
		if not char or not char.Parent then return nil end
		if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char) ~= p then return nil end
		local humCheck = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not humCheck or humCheck.Health <= 0 then return nil end
		if char.Parent ~= workspace then return nil end
		if not char:IsA("Model") then return nil end
		if isInvisibleChar(char) then return nil end
		if not hasVisibleBody(char) then return nil end
		if not char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			and not char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
			and not char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
			and not char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) then return nil end
		return char
	end
	do
		local function innocentAdd(p)
			if ded then return end
			if not alive() then return end
			if not roundActive() then return end
			if p == player then return end
			if innocentIsDead(p) then return end
			if innocentHasTool(p, "Knife") or innocentHasTool(p, "Gun") or innocentHasTool(p, "Revolver") then return end
			if innocentRole(p) then return end
			if not isInnocentRole(p) then return end
			local char = espCharOk(p)
			if not char then return end
			if ihl[p] then return end
			local hl = __mk("Highlight", {"Name", "InnocentESP_" .. MY_GEN .. "_" .. p.Name, "FillTransparency", 1, "OutlineTransparency", 0, "OutlineColor", Color3.fromRGB(50, 210, 70), "DepthMode", Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop, "Adornee", char})
			local okParent = pcall(function() hl.Parent = espf end)
			if not okParent or hl.Parent ~= espf then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) return end
			ihl[p] = hl
		end

		local function innocentRemove(p)
			local hl = ihl[p]
			if hl then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) ihl[p] = nil end
			local char = p.Character
			if char then
				for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("Highlight") and (d.Name == "InnocentESP_" .. MY_GEN or d.Name == "InnocentESP") then
						pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local function innocentClear()
			local ps = {}
			for p, _ in pairs(ihl) do ps[#ps + 1] = p end
			for _, p in ipairs(ps) do innocentRemove(p) end
		end
		local syncing = {}

		local function innocentSync(p)
			if syncing[p] then return end
			syncing[p] = true
			pcall(function() local hl = ihl[p]
				if hl and hl.Parent and hl.Adornee == p.Character and not innocentIsDead(p) and roundActive() and isInnocentRole(p) and not innocentRole(p) then return end
				innocentRemove(p)
				if innocentESPEnabled then innocentAdd(p) end
			end)
			syncing[p] = nil
		end

		local function innocentApply()
			for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do innocentSync(plr) end
		end
		local roundResyncAt = 0

		local function scheduleRoundResync()
			local t = os.clock()
			roundResyncAt = t
			task.delay(2, function() if roundResyncAt ~= t then return end
				if innocentESPEnabled then innocentApply() end
			end)
		end

		local function iesp(state)
			innocentESPEnabled = state
			if state then innocentApply() else innocentClear() end
		end

		local function innocentTrack(p)
			_G.__MM2_Conn(p.CharacterAdded, function() if not alive() then return end
				if innocentESPEnabled then
					task.wait(0.5)
					innocentSync(p)
				end
			end)
			_G.__MM2_Conn(p:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"), function() if not innocentESPEnabled then return end
				innocentSync(p)
			end)
		end
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do innocentTrack(plr) end
		_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerAdded, function(plr) if not alive() then return end
			innocentTrack(plr)
			if innocentESPEnabled and plr.Character then
				innocentSync(plr)
			end
		end)
		_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(plr) if not alive() then return end innocentRemove(plr) end)
		pcall(function() player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Dead"):Connect(function() if innocentESPEnabled then
					innocentApply()
				end
			end)
		end)
		pcall(function() local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
			local remotes = rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
			local gp = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
			local ev = gp and gp:FindFirstChild("PlayerDataChanged")
			if ev then
				_G.__MM2_Conn(ev.OnClientEvent, function() if not innocentESPEnabled then return end
					innocentApply()
					scheduleRoundResync()
				end)
			end
		end)
		do
			local lastSync = 0
			_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then return end
				if not innocentESPEnabled then return end
				if os.clock() - lastSync < 1 then return end
				lastSync = os.clock()
				innocentApply()
			end)
		end
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 38), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Innocent ESP", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local innocentTrackUI, innocentKnob = __sw(page, 38)
		_G.__MM2_Tg("innocent", iesp, function() return innocentESPEnabled end, innocentTrackUI, innocentKnob)
