		innocentTrackUI.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() iesp(not innocentESPEnabled)
			__knob(innocentTrackUI, innocentKnob, innocentESPEnabled)
		end)
	end
	local sheriffOn = false
	local shl = {}
	do
		local function sheriffAdd(p)
			if ded then return end
			if not alive() then return end
			if not roundActive() then return end
			if p == player then return end
			if innocentIsDead(p) then return end
			local srvRole = nil
			pcall(function() srvRole = detectRole(p) end)
			if srvRole == "Murderer" then return end
			if srvRole == nil then
				if not (hasGun(p) and not hasknife(p)) then return end
			elseif srvRole ~= "Sheriff" then return end
			local char = espCharOk(p)
			if not char then return end
			if shl[p] then return end
			local hl = __mk("Highlight", {"Name", "SheriffESP_" .. MY_GEN .. "_" .. p.Name, "FillTransparency", 1, "OutlineTransparency", 0, "OutlineColor", Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 255), "DepthMode", Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop, "Adornee", char})
			local okParent = pcall(function() hl.Parent = espf end)
			if not okParent or hl.Parent ~= espf then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) return end
			shl[p] = hl
		end

		local function sheriffRemove(p)
			local hl = shl[p]
			if hl then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) shl[p] = nil end
			local char = p.Character
			if char then
				for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("Highlight") and (d.Name == "SheriffESP_" .. MY_GEN or d.Name == "SheriffESP") then
						pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local function sheriffClear()
			local ps = {}
			for p, _ in pairs(shl) do ps[#ps + 1] = p end
			for _, p in ipairs(ps) do sheriffRemove(p) end
		end
		local ssyncing = {}

		local function sheriffSync(p)
			if ssyncing[p] then return end
			ssyncing[p] = true
			pcall(function() local hl = shl[p]
				if hl and hl.Parent and hl.Adornee == p.Character and not innocentIsDead(p) and roundActive() and detectRole(p) == "Sheriff" then return end
				sheriffRemove(p)
				if sheriffOn then sheriffAdd(p) end
			end)
			ssyncing[p] = nil
		end

		local function sheriffApply()
			for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do sheriffSync(plr) end
		end

		local function sesp(state)
			sheriffOn = state
			if state then sheriffApply() else sheriffClear() end
		end

		local function sheriffTrack(p)
			_G.__MM2_Conn(p.CharacterAdded, function() if not alive() then return end
				if sheriffOn then
					task.wait(0.5)
					sheriffSync(p)
				end
			end)
			_G.__MM2_Conn(p:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"), function() if not sheriffOn then return end
				sheriffSync(p)
			end)
		end
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do sheriffTrack(plr) end
		_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerAdded, function(plr) if not alive() then return end
			sheriffTrack(plr)
			if sheriffOn and plr.Character then
				sheriffSync(plr)
			end
		end)
		_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(plr) if not alive() then return end sheriffRemove(plr) end)
		pcall(function() local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
			local remotes = rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
			local gp = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
			local ev = gp and gp:FindFirstChild("PlayerDataChanged")
			if ev then
				_G.__MM2_Conn(ev.OnClientEvent, function() if not sheriffOn then return end
					sheriffApply()
				end)
			end
		end)
		do
			local lastSync = 0
			_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then return end
				if not sheriffOn then return end
				if os.clock() - lastSync < 1 then return end
				lastSync = os.clock()
				sheriffApply()
			end)
		end
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 68), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Sheriff ESP", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local sheriffTrackUI, sheriffKnob = __sw(page, 68)
		_G.__MM2_Tg("sheriff", sesp, function() return sheriffOn end, sheriffTrackUI, sheriffKnob)
		sheriffTrackUI.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() sesp(not sheriffOn)
			__knob(sheriffTrackUI, sheriffKnob, sheriffOn)
		end)
	end
	local mon = false
	local mhl = {}
	do
		local function murderAdd(p)
			if ded then return end
			if not alive() then return end
			if not roundActive() then return end
			if p == player then return end
			if innocentIsDead(p) then return end
			local srvRoleM = nil
			pcall(function() srvRoleM = detectRole(p) end)
			if srvRoleM == "Sheriff" then return end
			if srvRoleM == nil then
				if not hasknife(p) then return end
			elseif srvRoleM ~= "Murderer" then return end
			local char = espCharOk(p)
			if not char then return end
			if mhl[p] then return end
			local hl = __mk("Highlight", {"Name", "MurderESP_" .. MY_GEN .. "_" .. p.Name, "FillTransparency", 1, "OutlineTransparency", 0, "OutlineColor", Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50), "DepthMode", Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop, "Adornee", char})
			local okParent = pcall(function() hl.Parent = espf end)
			if not okParent or hl.Parent ~= espf then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) return end
			mhl[p] = hl
		end

		local function murderRemove(p)
			local hl = mhl[p]
			if hl then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) mhl[p] = nil end
			local char = p.Character
			if char then
				for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("Highlight") and (d.Name == "MurderESP_" .. MY_GEN or d.Name == "MurderESP") then
						pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local function murderClear()
			local ps = {}
			for p, _ in pairs(mhl) do ps[#ps + 1] = p end
			for _, p in ipairs(ps) do murderRemove(p) end
		end
		local msyncing = {}

		local function murderSync(p)
			if msyncing[p] then return end
			msyncing[p] = true
			pcall(function() local hl = mhl[p]
				if hl and hl.Parent and hl.Adornee == p.Character and not innocentIsDead(p) and roundActive() and detectRole(p) == "Murderer" then return end
				murderRemove(p)
				if mon then murderAdd(p) end
			end)
			msyncing[p] = nil
		end

		local function murderApply()
			for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do murderSync(plr) end
		end
		do
			_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.RenderStepped, function() if not alive() then return end
				local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
				if not cam then return end
				for _, hl in ipairs(espf:GetChildren()) do
					if hl:IsA("Highlight") then
						local show = true
						pcall(function() local ad = hl.Adornee
							local rp = nil
							if ad and ad:IsA("Model") then
								rp = ad:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or ad:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or ad:FindFirstChild("Torso")
							elseif ad and ad:IsA("BasePart") then
								rp = ad
							end
							if rp then
								local rel = cam.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(rp.Position)
								show = rel.Z < 0
							end
						end)
						pcall(function() if hl.Enabled ~= show then hl.Enabled = show end
						end)
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		local function mesp(state)
			mon = state
			if state then murderApply() else murderClear() end
		end

		local function murderTrack(p)
			_G.__MM2_Conn(p.CharacterAdded, function() if not alive() then return end
				if mon then
					task.wait(0.5)
					murderSync(p)
				end
			end)
			_G.__MM2_Conn(p:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"), function() if not mon then return end
				murderSync(p)
			end)
		end
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do murderTrack(plr) end
		_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerAdded, function(plr) if not alive() then return end
			murderTrack(plr)
			if mon and plr.Character then
				murderSync(plr)
			end
		end)
		_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(plr) if not alive() then return end murderRemove(plr) end)
		pcall(function() local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
			local remotes = rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
			local gp = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
			local ev = gp and gp:FindFirstChild("PlayerDataChanged")
			if ev then
				_G.__MM2_Conn(ev.OnClientEvent, function() if not mon then return end
					murderApply()
				end)
			end
		end)
		do
			local lastSync = 0
			_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then return end
				if not mon then return end
				if os.clock() - lastSync < 1 then return end
				lastSync = os.clock()
				murderApply()
			end)
		end
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 98), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Murderer ESP", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local murderTrackUI, murderKnob = __sw(page, 98)
		_G.__MM2_Tg("murder", mesp, function() return mon end, murderTrackUI, murderKnob)
		murderTrackUI.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() mesp(not mon)
			__knob(murderTrackUI, murderKnob, mon)
		end)
	end
	do
		local lastStraySweep = 0
		_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then return end
			if os.clock() - lastStraySweep < 5 then return end
			lastStraySweep = os.clock()
			for _, t in ipairs({ ihl, shl, mhl }) do
				for p, hl in pairs(t) do
					local bad = false
					pcall(function() if not hl or hl.Parent ~= espf then bad = true end
					end)
					if bad then
						pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end)
						t[p] = nil
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	local boxOn = false
	local boxes = {}
	local hasDrawing = typeof(Drawing) == "table" and typeof(Drawing.new) == "function"

	local function boxColor(p)
		local h = mhl[p]
		if h and h.Parent then return Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50) end
		h = shl[p]
		if h and h.Parent then return Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 255) end
		h = ihl[p]
		if h and h.Parent then return Color3.fromRGB(50, 210, 70) end
		local _dr = nil
		pcall(function() _dr = detectRole(p) end)
		if _dr == "Murderer" then return Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50) end
		if _dr == "Sheriff" then return Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 255) end
		return Color3.fromRGB(50, 210, 70)
	end
	do
		local function boxAdd(p)
			if not alive() then return end
			if p == player then return end
			if not hasDrawing then return end
			if boxes[p] then return end
			if innocentIsDead(p) then return end
			if not serverRoleLive(p) then return end
			local char = p.Character
			if not char then return end
			if isInvisibleChar(char) or not hasVisibleBody(char) then return end
			local ok, box = pcall(Drawing.new, "Square")
			if not ok or not box then return end
			box.Visible = false
			box.Color = boxColor(p)
			box.Thickness = 1
			box.Filled = false
			box.Transparency = 1
			boxes[p] = box
			pcall(function() _G.__draws = _G.__draws or {} table.insert(_G.__draws, box) end)
		end

		local function boxRemove(p)
			local box = boxes[p]
			if box then pcall(function() box:Remove() end) boxes[p] = nil end
		end

		local function boxSync(p)
			if ded then return end
			boxRemove(p)
			if boxOn then boxAdd(p) end
		end

		local function boxApply()
			for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do boxSync(plr) end
		end

		local function boxClear()
			local ps = {}
			for p, _ in pairs(boxes) do ps[#ps + 1] = p end
			for _, p in ipairs(ps) do boxRemove(p) end
		end

		local function besp(state)
			boxOn = state
			if state then boxApply() else boxClear() end
		end

		local function boxTrack(p)
			_G.__MM2_Conn(p.CharacterAdded, function() if not alive() then return end
				if boxOn then
					task.wait(0.5)
					boxSync(p)
				end
			end)
			_G.__MM2_Conn(p:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"), function() if not boxOn then return end
				boxSync(p)
			end)
		end
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do boxTrack(plr) end
		_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerAdded, function(plr) if not alive() then return end
			boxTrack(plr)
			if boxOn and plr.Character then
				boxSync(plr)
			end
		end)
		_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(plr) if not alive() then return end boxRemove(plr) end)
		pcall(function() local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
			local remotes = rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
			local gp = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
			local ev = gp and gp:FindFirstChild("PlayerDataChanged")
			if ev then
				_G.__MM2_Conn(ev.OnClientEvent, function() if boxOn then boxApply() end
				end)
			end
		end)
		do
			local lastBoxSync = 0
			_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then return end
				if not boxOn then return end
				if os.clock() - lastBoxSync < 3 then return end
				lastBoxSync = os.clock()
				boxApply()
			end)
		end
		_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.RenderStepped, function() if not alive() then return end
			if not boxOn then
				for _, box in pairs(boxes) do box.Visible = false end
				return
			end
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if not cam then return end
			local viewSize = cam.ViewportSize
			for p, box in pairs(boxes) do
				local visible = false
				if p ~= player and not innocentIsDead(p) then
					local char = p.Character
					if char and bodyHidden(char) then char = nil end
					local rootPart = char
					and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart"))
					if rootPart then
						local frontOk = false
						pcall(function() local topP = char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Head")
							topP = (topP and topP.Position or rootPart.Position) + Vector3.new(0, 4.8, 0)
							local botP = rootPart.Position - Vector3.new(0, 4.8, 0)
							local rt = cam.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(topP)
							local rb = cam.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(botP)
							if rt.Z < 0 and rb.Z < 0 then frontOk = true end
						end)
						if not frontOk then rootPart = nil end
					end
					if rootPart then
						local torso = char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Head")
						local topPos = (torso and torso.Position or rootPart.Position) + Vector3.new(0, 4.8, 0)
						local bottomPos = rootPart.Position - Vector3.new(0, 4.8, 0)
						local top, topOnScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(topPos)
						local bottom, botOnScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(bottomPos)
						if topOnScreen or botOnScreen then
							local screenSize = math.max(bottom.Y - top.Y, 10)
							local boxWidth = screenSize * 0.65
							if boxWidth < viewSize.X and screenSize < viewSize.Y then
								local px = math.clamp(top.X - boxWidth / 2, 0, viewSize.X - boxWidth)
								local py = math.clamp(top.Y, 0, viewSize.Y - screenSize)
								px, py = math.floor(px + 0.5), math.floor(py + 0.5)
								boxWidth, screenSize = math.floor(boxWidth + 0.5), math.floor(screenSize + 0.5)
								box.Size = Vector2.new(boxWidth, screenSize)
								box.Position = Vector2.new(px, py)
								box.Color = boxColor(p)
								visible = true
							end
						end
					end
				end
				box.Visible = visible
			end
		end)
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 128), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Box ESP", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local boxTrackUI, boxKnob = __sw(page, 128)
		_G.__MM2_Tg("box", besp, function() return boxOn end, boxTrackUI, boxKnob)
		boxTrackUI.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() besp(not boxOn)
			__knob(boxTrackUI, boxKnob, boxOn)
		end)
	end
	local nameOn = false
	local nameGuis = {}
	do
		local function nameRemove(p)
			local g = nameGuis[p]
			if g then pcall(function() g:Destroy() end) nameGuis[p] = nil end
		end

		local function nameAdd(p)
			if ded then return end
			if not alive() then return end
			if p == player then return end
			if innocentIsDead(p) then return end
			if nameGuis[p] then return end
			if not serverRoleLive(p) then return end
			local char = p.Character
			if not char then return end
			if isInvisibleChar(char) or not hasVisibleBody(char) then return end
			local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
			or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
			if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end
			local bg = __mk("BillboardGui", {"Name", "NameESP", "Size", UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 20), "StudsOffsetWorldSpace", Vector3.new(0, -5.6, 0), "AlwaysOnTop", true, "Adornee", hrp})
			local okBg = pcall(function() bg.Parent = char end)
			if not okBg or bg.Parent ~= char then pcall(function() bg:Destroy() end) return end
			__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", p.Name, "TextColor3", boxColor(p), "TextStrokeTransparency", 0, "TextStrokeColor3", Color3.new(0, 0, 0), "Font", Enum.Font.Arcade, "TextSize", 15, "Parent", bg})
			nameGuis[p] = bg
		end

		local function nameSync(p)
			nameRemove(p)
			if nameOn then nameAdd(p) end
		end

		local function nameApply()
			for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do nameSync(plr) end
		end

		local function nameClear()
			local ps = {}
			for p, _ in pairs(nameGuis) do ps[#ps + 1] = p end
			for _, p in ipairs(ps) do nameRemove(p) end
		end

		local function nesp(state)
			nameOn = state
			if state then nameApply() else nameClear() end
		end

		local function nameTrack(p)
			_G.__MM2_Conn(p:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"), function() if nameOn then nameSync(p) end end)
			_G.__MM2_Conn(p.CharacterAdded, function() if not alive() then return end
				task.wait(0.5)
				if nameOn then nameSync(p) end
			end)
		end
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do nameTrack(plr) end
		do
			local nameSyncT = 0
			_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then return end
				if not nameOn then return end
				if os.clock() - nameSyncT < 1 then return end
				nameSyncT = os.clock()
				nameApply()
			end)
		end
		_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerAdded, function(plr) if not alive() then return end
			nameTrack(plr)
			task.wait(0.5)
			if nameOn then nameSync(plr) end
		end)
		_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(plr) if not alive() then return end nameRemove(plr) end)
		pcall(function() local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
			local remotes = rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
			local gp = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
			local ev = gp and gp:FindFirstChild("PlayerDataChanged")
			if ev then
				_G.__MM2_Conn(ev.OnClientEvent, function() if not nameOn then return end
					for p, g in pairs(nameGuis) do
						local lbl = g:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
						if lbl then lbl.TextColor3 = boxColor(p) end
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
		do
			_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then return end
				if not nameOn then return end
				for p, g in pairs(nameGuis) do
					if g and g.Parent then
						local char = p.Character
						local show = not innocentIsDead(p) and not bodyHidden(char)
						g.Enabled = show
						if show then
							local lbl = g:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
							if lbl then
								local ok, col = pcall(boxColor, p)
								if ok and col then pcall(function() lbl.TextColor3 = col end) end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 158), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Name ESP", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local nameTrackUI, nameKnob = __sw(page, 158)
		_G.__MM2_Tg("name", nesp, function() return nameOn end, nameTrackUI, nameKnob)
		nameTrackUI.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() nesp(not nameOn)
			__knob(nameTrackUI, nameKnob, nameOn)
		end)
	end
	do
		local visualsBox = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "VisualsBox", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 16, 0, 166), "Position", UDim2.new(0, -8, 0, 25), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ZIndex", 0, "Parent", page})
		__c(visualsBox, UDim.new(0, 8))
		__s(visualsBox, BORDER, 1)
		__mk("Frame", {"Name", "ObjectiveDivider", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 206), "BackgroundColor3", Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80), "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", page})
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 214), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", "Objective", "Parent", page})
		local gunPanel = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "GunBox", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 16, 0, 136), "Position", UDim2.new(0, -8, 0, 243), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ZIndex", 0, "Parent", page})
		__c(gunPanel, UDim.new(0, 8))
		__s(gunPanel, BORDER, 1)
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 256), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Gun ESP", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local gunTrack, gunKnob = __sw(page, 256)
		local gunOn = false
		local gunHighlight = nil
		local gunNameGui = nil
		local gunNameOn = false
		local gunDistOn = false
		local gunTarget = nil

		local function findGunDrop()
			local found = nil
			pcall(function() for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
					if d.Name == "GunDrop" then
						found = d
						break
					end
					if d.Name == "Gun" and d:IsA("Tool") then
						local par = d.Parent
						if par and not par:IsA("Backpack") and not par:IsA("Player") and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(par) then
							found = d
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			return found
		end
		local gunNameRemove

		local function gunRemove()
			if gunHighlight then pcall(function() gunHighlight:Destroy() end) gunHighlight = nil end
			gunNameRemove()
			for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("Highlight") and d.Name == "GunESP" then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
			end
		end
		gunNameRemove = function()
			if gunNameGui then pcall(function() gunNameGui:Destroy() end) gunNameGui = nil end
			gunTarget = nil
			for _, d in ipairs(espf:GetChildren()) do
				if d:IsA("BillboardGui") and d.Name == "GunNameESP" then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
			end
		end

		local function gunNameCreate(target)
			gunNameRemove()
			if not alive() then return end
			if not target then return end
			gunTarget = target
			local glbl = __mk("BillboardGui", {"Name", "GunNameESP", "Size", UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 20), "StudsOffsetWorldSpace", Vector3.new(0, -2.5, 0), "AlwaysOnTop", true, "Adornee", target})
			local okG = pcall(function() glbl.Parent = espf end)
			if okG and glbl.Parent == espf then
				__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Gun", "TextColor3", Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50), "TextStrokeTransparency", 0, "TextStrokeColor3", Color3.new(0, 0, 0), "Font", Enum.Font.Arcade, "TextSize", 15, "Parent", glbl})
				gunNameGui = glbl
			else
				pcall(function() glbl:Destroy() end)
			end
		end

		local function gunApply()
			gunRemove()
			if not gunOn then return end
			local drop = findGunDrop()
			if not drop then return end
			local target = drop
			if drop:IsA("Tool") then
				target = drop:FindFirstChild("Handle") or drop:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) or drop
			end
			local hl = __mk("Highlight", {"Name", "GunESP", "FillTransparency", 1, "OutlineTransparency", 0, "OutlineColor", Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50), "DepthMode", Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop, "Adornee", target})
			local okP = pcall(function() hl.Parent = espf end)
			if okP and hl.Parent == espf then
				gunHighlight = hl
			else
				pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end)
			end
			gunTarget = target
			if gunNameOn or gunDistOn then gunNameCreate(target) end
		end

		local function gesp(state)
			gunOn = state
			if state then gunApply() else gunRemove() end
		end
		pcall(function() _G.__MM2_Conn(workspace.DescendantAdded, function(d) if not gunOn then return end
				if d.Name == "GunDrop" or (d.Name == "Gun" and d:IsA("Tool")) then
					task.wait(0.5)
					if gunOn then gunApply() end
				end
			end)
		end)
		pcall(function() _G.__MM2_Conn(workspace.DescendantRemoving, function(d) if not gunOn then return end
				if d.Name == "GunDrop" or (d.Name == "Gun" and d:IsA("Tool")) then
					gunApply()
				end
			end)
		end)
		do
			local lastGun = 0
			_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then return end
				if not gunOn then return end
				if os.clock() - lastGun < 3 then return end
				lastGun = os.clock()
				gunApply()
			end)
		end
		_G.__MM2_Tg("gun", gesp, function() return gunOn end, gunTrack, gunKnob)
		gunTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() gesp(not gunOn)
			__knob(gunTrack, gunKnob, gunOn)
		end)
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 286), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Gun Name ESP", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local gunNameTrack, gunNameKnob = __sw(page, 286)

		local function gnesp(state)
			gunNameOn = state
			if state then
				if gunOn and alive() then
					local drop = findGunDrop()
					if drop then
						local target = drop
						if drop:IsA("Tool") then
							target = drop:FindFirstChild("Handle") or drop:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) or drop
						end
						gunNameCreate(target)
					end
				end
			else
				if not gunDistOn then gunNameRemove() end
			end
		end
		_G.__MM2_Tg("gunName", gnesp, function() return gunNameOn end, gunNameTrack, gunNameKnob)
		gunNameTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() gnesp(not gunNameOn)
			__knob(gunNameTrack, gunNameKnob, gunNameOn)
		end)
		do
			local lastGunDist = 0
			_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then return end
				if not gunDistOn then return end
				if os.clock() - lastGunDist < 0.5 then return end
				lastGunDist = os.clock()
				local g = gunNameGui
				if g and g.Parent and gunTarget and gunTarget.Parent then
					local lbl = g:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
					local char = player.Character
					local hrp = char
					and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
					if lbl and hrp then
						local tp = nil
						pcall(function() if gunTarget:IsA("BasePart") then tp = gunTarget.Position
							elseif gunTarget:IsA("Model") then tp = gunTarget:GetPivot().Position end
						end)
						if tp then
							local d = math.floor((tp - hrp.Position).Magnitude + 0.5)
							pcall(function() lbl.Text = "Gun | " .. tostring(d) end)
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 316), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Gun Distance ESP", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local gunDistTrack, gunDistKnob = __sw(page, 316)

		local function gdesp(state)
			gunDistOn = state
			if state then
				if gunOn and alive() then
					local drop = findGunDrop()
					if drop then
						local target = drop
						if drop:IsA("Tool") then
							target = drop:FindFirstChild("Handle") or drop:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) or drop
						end
						gunNameCreate(target)
					end
				end
			else
				if not gunNameOn then
					gunNameRemove()
				else
					local g = gunNameGui
					if g then
						local lbl = g:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
						if lbl then pcall(function() lbl.Text = "Gun" end) end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		_G.__MM2_Tg("gunDist", gdesp, function() return gunDistOn end, gunDistTrack, gunDistKnob)
		gunDistTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() gdesp(not gunDistOn)
			__knob(gunDistTrack, gunDistKnob, gunDistOn)
		end)
		local btrapOn = false
		local btrapGui = {}

		local function btrapMatch(o)
			if o.Name ~= "Trap" then return false end
			local p = o.Parent
			if not p then return false end
			local cls = p.ClassName
			if cls ~= "Workspace" and cls ~= "Folder" and cls ~= "Model" then return false end
			return o:IsA("BasePart") or o:IsA("Model")
		end

		local function btrapAdd(o)
			if not btrapOn then return end
			if not btrapMatch(o) then return end
			if o:IsA("Model") and not o:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart") then return end
			if btrapGui[o] then return end
			local target = o
			if o:IsA("Model") then
				target = o:FindFirstChild("TrapVisual") or o:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart") or o
				if not (target:IsA("BasePart") or target:IsA("Model")) then return end
			end
			local gui = __mk("BillboardGui", {"Name", "BearTrapESP_" .. MY_GEN, "Size", UDim2.new(0, 160, 0, 18), "StudsOffset", Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), "AlwaysOnTop", true, "Adornee", target})
			local lbl = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Trap", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 12, "TextColor3", Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40), "TextStrokeColor3", Color3.new(0, 0, 0), "TextStrokeTransparency", 0, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, "Parent", gui})
			local okp = pcall(function() gui.Parent = espf end)
			if okp and gui.Parent == espf then
				btrapGui[o] = { gui = gui, lbl = lbl, target = target }
			else
				pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
			end
		end

		local function btrapUpdate()
			local ch = player.Character
			local hrp = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not hrp then return end
			local origin = hrp.Position
			for o, e in pairs(btrapGui) do
				local pos = nil
				pcall(function()
					local t = e.target or o
					pos = (t:IsA("BasePart") and t.Position or t:GetPivot().Position)
				end)
				if pos then
					e.lbl.Text = "Trap | " .. math.floor((pos - origin).Magnitude + 0.5)
				end
			end
		end

		local function btrapClear()
			for _, e in pairs(btrapGui) do pcall(function() e.gui:Destroy() end) end
			btrapGui = {}
		end

		local function btrapScan()
			for o, e in pairs(btrapGui) do
				local bad = false
				pcall(function() if not o.Parent then bad = true end
					if not o:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then bad = true end
				end)
				if bad then
					pcall(function() e.gui:Destroy() end)
					btrapGui[o] = nil
				end
			end
			if not btrapOn then return end
			for _, o in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do btrapAdd(o) end
		end

		local function btrapesp(state)
			btrapOn = state
			pcall(function() if _G.__btrapc then _G.__btrapc:Disconnect() _G.__btrapc = nil end end)
			pcall(function() if _G.__btrapd then _G.__btrapd:Disconnect() _G.__btrapd = nil end end)
			if state then
				btrapScan()
				pcall(function() _G.__btrapc = _G.__MM2_Conn(workspace.DescendantAdded, function(d) if not alive() then return end
						btrapAdd(d)
					end)
				end)
				local lastB = 0
				_G.__btrapd = _G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then return end
					local now = os.clock()
					if now - lastB >= 1 then
						lastB = now
						btrapScan()
					end
					if now - (_G.__btrapLT or 0) >= 0.15 then
						_G.__btrapLT = now
						btrapUpdate()
					end
				end)
			else
				btrapClear()
			end
		end
		_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup or {}
		table.insert(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup, function() pcall(btrapClear) end)
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 346), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Bear trap ESP", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local btrapTrack, btrapKnob = __sw(page, 346)
		_G.__MM2_Tg("btrap", btrapesp, function() return btrapOn end, btrapTrack, btrapKnob)
		btrapTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() btrapesp(not btrapOn)
			__knob(btrapTrack, btrapKnob, btrapOn)
		end)
	end
end
do
	local page = __mk("ScrollingFrame", {"Name", "MiscPage", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Visible", false, "CanvasSize", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "ScrollingDirection", Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, "ElasticBehavior", Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never, "ScrollBarThickness", 0, "Parent", inner})
	pgs["Misc"] = page

	local function refreshMiscCanvas()
		local bottom = 0
		for _, c in ipairs(page:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("GuiObject") and c.Visible then
				local b = c.Position.Y.Offset + c.Size.Y.Offset
				if b > bottom then bottom = b end
			end
		end
		page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, bottom + 331)
	end
	_G.__MM2_Conn(page:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"), function() if page.Visible then task.defer(refreshMiscCanvas) task.delay(0.5, refreshMiscCanvas) end
	end)
	task.delay(1, refreshMiscCanvas)
	__mk("UIPadding", {"PaddingTop", UDim.new(0, 12), "PaddingLeft", UDim.new(0, 18), "PaddingRight", UDim.new(0, 18), "PaddingBottom", UDim.new(0, 18), "Parent", page})
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", "Mobility", "Parent", page})
	do
		local speedOn = false
		local speedValue = 50
		local speedKey = nil
		local capturingSpeedKey = false

		local function applySpeed()
			if not alive() then return end
			local char = player.Character
			local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if hum then
				pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = speedOn and speedValue or 16 end)
			end
		end
		_G.__MM2_Conn(player.CharacterAdded, function() if not alive() then return end
			task.wait(0.5)
			if speedOn then applySpeed() end
		end)
		local miscBox = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "MiscBox", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 16, 0, 193), "Position", UDim2.new(0, -8, 0, 25), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ZIndex", 0, "Parent", page})
		__c(miscBox, UDim.new(0, 8))
		__s(miscBox, BORDER, 1)
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 38), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Speed", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local speedKeybind = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "SpeedKeybind", "Size", UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 24), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -79, 0, 38), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "AutoButtonColor", false, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 10, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Text", "...", "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "ClipsDescendants", true, "Parent", page})
		__c(speedKeybind, UDim.new(0, 8))
		__s(speedKeybind, Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50), 1, true)
		local speedTrack, speedKnob = __sw(page, 38)

		local function setSpeed(state)
			if _G.watermarkSpdNameLabel then pcall(function() _G.watermarkSpdNameLabel.TextColor3 = state and acc or __MM2_BaseTxt()
					if watermarkSpeedKeyLabel then watermarkSpeedKeyLabel.TextColor3 = state and acc or __MM2_WhiteTxt() end
				end) end
			speedOn = state
			if state then applySpeed() else applySpeed() end
		end
		_G.__MM2_Tg("speed", setSpeed, function() return speedOn end, speedTrack, speedKnob)
		speedTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setSpeed(not speedOn)
			__knob(speedTrack, speedKnob, speedOn)
		end)
		speedKeybind.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() capturingSpeedKey = true
			speedKeybind.Text = "..."
		end)
		_G.__speedk = _G.__MM2_Conn(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gpe) if gpe then return end
			if capturingSpeedKey then
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
					speedKey = input.KeyCode
					local nm = tostring(input.KeyCode):gsub("Enum.KeyCode.", "")
					local short = { LeftControl = "LCtrl", RightControl = "RCtrl", LeftShift = "LShift", RightShift = "RShift", LeftAlt = "LAlt", RightAlt = "RAlt", CapsLock = "Caps", Space = "Spc", Backspace = "Bspc" }
					nm = short[nm] or nm
					if #nm > 5 then nm = nm:sub(1, 5) end
					speedKeybind.Text = nm
					_G.speedKeyName = nm
					if watermarkSpeedKeyLabel then
						pcall(function() watermarkSpeedKeyLabel.Text = "[<font size=\"13\">" .. nm .. "</font>]" end)
					end
					speedKeybind.TextColor3 = __MM2_BaseTxt()
				end
				capturingSpeedKey = false
				return
			end
			if speedKey and input.KeyCode == speedKey then
				setSpeed(not speedOn)
				__knob(speedTrack, speedKnob, speedOn)
			end
		end)
		local speedSliderBar, speedSliderFill, speedSliderKnob = __sl(page, 71, 0.167, LIME)
		local speedValueLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 64), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "50/300", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 10, "TextColor3", LIME, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, "Parent", page})
		local draggingSpeed = false

		local function speedApply(v)
			local n = tonumber(v) or 50
			if n ~= n then n = 50 end
			speedValue = math.clamp(math.floor(n + 0.5), 0, 300)
			local pct = speedValue / 300
			speedSliderFill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
			speedSliderKnob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, -6, 0.5, -6)
			speedValueLabel.Text = tostring(speedValue) .. "/300"
			if speedOn then applySpeed() end
		end
		_G.__MM2_Val("speed", speedApply, function() return speedValue end, 0, 300)

		local function speedset(x)
			local absSize = speedSliderBar.AbsoluteSize.X
			if absSize <= 0 then return end
			local pct = math.clamp((x - speedSliderBar.AbsolutePosition.X) / absSize, 0, 1)
			speedApply(math.floor(pct * 300 + 0.5))
		end
		speedSliderBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				draggingSpeed = true
				speedset(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)
		__live(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
			if draggingSpeed
				and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
				speedset(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)
		__live(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				draggingSpeed = false
			end
		end)
	end
	do
		local flyValue = 50
		local flyOn = false
		local flyVel, flyGyro, flyConn
		local flyKeys = {}
		local FLY_MIN, FLY_MAX = 0, 300
		local flyCleanupDone = false

		local function stopFly()
			if flyConn then flyConn:Disconnect() flyConn = nil end
			if flyVel then pcall(function() flyVel:Destroy() end) flyVel = nil end
			if flyGyro then pcall(function() flyGyro:Destroy() end) flyGyro = nil end
			local char = player.Character
			local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if hum then hum.PlatformStand = false end
		end
		if not flyCleanupDone then
			pcall(function() _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup or {}
				table.insert(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup, function() pcall(stopFly) end)
				flyCleanupDone = true
			end)
		end

		local function startFly()
			if not alive() then return end
			local char = player.Character
			local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not hrp or not hum then return end
			hum.PlatformStand = true
			flyVel = __mk("BodyVelocity", {"MaxForce", Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9), "Velocity", Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), "Parent", hrp})
			flyGyro = __mk("BodyGyro", {"MaxTorque", Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9), "P", 9e4, "CFrame", hrp.CFrame, "Parent", hrp})
			flyConn = _G.__MM2_ConnOwn(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN], RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then flyConn:Disconnect() return end
				if not flyOn then return end
				local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
				if not cam or not hrp.Parent then return end
				local move = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				if flyKeys[Enum.KeyCode.W] then move = move + cam.CFrame.LookVector end
				if flyKeys[Enum.KeyCode.S] then move = move - cam.CFrame.LookVector end
				if flyKeys[Enum.KeyCode.D] then move = move + cam.CFrame.RightVector end
				if flyKeys[Enum.KeyCode.A] then move = move - cam.CFrame.RightVector end
				if flyKeys[Enum.KeyCode.E] then move = move + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
				if flyKeys[Enum.KeyCode.Q] then move = move - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
				if move.Magnitude > 0 then move = move.Unit * flyValue else move = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end
				flyVel.Velocity = move
				flyGyro.CFrame = cam.CFrame
			end)
		end
		_G.__MM2_Conn(player.CharacterAdded, function() if not alive() then return end
			task.wait(0.5)
			if flyOn then stopFly() startFly() end
		end)
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 87), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Flight", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local flightKeybind = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "FlightKeybind", "Size", UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 24), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -79, 0, 87), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "AutoButtonColor", false, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 10, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Text", "...", "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "ClipsDescendants", true, "Parent", page})
		__c(flightKeybind, UDim.new(0, 8))
		__s(flightKeybind, Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50), 1, true)
		local flightTrack, flightKnob = __sw(page, 87)

		local function setFlyOn(state)
			if _G.watermarkFltNameLabel then pcall(function() _G.watermarkFltNameLabel.TextColor3 = state and acc or __MM2_BaseTxt()
					if watermarkFlightKeyLabel then watermarkFlightKeyLabel.TextColor3 = state and acc or __MM2_WhiteTxt() end
				end) end
			flyOn = state
			if state then
				TweenService:Create(flightTrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(true) }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(flightKnob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(true) }):Play()
				startFly()
			else
				TweenService:Create(flightTrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(false) }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(flightKnob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(false) }):Play()
				stopFly()
			end
		end
		_G.__MM2_Tg("fly", setFlyOn, function() return flyOn end, flightTrack, flightKnob)
		flightTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setFlyOn(not flyOn) end)
		local flyBoundKey = nil
		local bindingFly = false
		flightKeybind.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() bindingFly = true
			flightKeybind.Text = "..."
		end)
		_G.__flyk = _G.__MM2_Conn(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gpe) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
				flyKeys[input.KeyCode] = true
			end
			if bindingFly then
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
					flyBoundKey = input.KeyCode
					local nm = tostring(input.KeyCode):gsub("Enum.KeyCode.", "")
					local short = { LeftControl = "LCtrl", RightControl = "RCtrl", LeftShift = "LShift", RightShift = "RShift", LeftAlt = "LAlt", RightAlt = "RAlt", CapsLock = "Caps", Space = "Spc", Backspace = "Bspc" }
					nm = short[nm] or nm
					if #nm > 5 then nm = nm:sub(1, 5) end
					flightKeybind.Text = nm
					_G.flightKeyName = nm
					if watermarkFlightKeyLabel then
						pcall(function() watermarkFlightKeyLabel.Text = "[<font size=\"13\">" .. nm .. "</font>]" end)
					end
					flightKeybind.TextColor3 = __MM2_BaseTxt()
				end
				bindingFly = false
				if not flyBoundKey then flightKeybind.Text = "..." end
				return
			end
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
				and flyBoundKey
				and input.KeyCode == flyBoundKey
				and not gpe then
				setFlyOn(not flyOn)
			end
		end)
		_G.__MM2_UIS(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
				flyKeys[input.KeyCode] = nil
			end
		end)
		local flightSliderBar, flightSliderFill, flightSliderKnob = __sl(page, 120, 0.167, LIME)
		local flightValueLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 113), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "50/300", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 10, "TextColor3", LIME, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, "Parent", page})
		local draggingFlight = false

		local function flightApply(v)
			local n = tonumber(v) or 50
			if n ~= n then n = 50 end
			flyValue = math.clamp(math.floor(n + 0.5), FLY_MIN, FLY_MAX)
			local pct = (flyValue - FLY_MIN) / (FLY_MAX - FLY_MIN)
			flightSliderFill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
			flightSliderKnob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, -6, 0.5, -6)
			flightValueLabel.Text = tostring(flyValue) .. "/300"
		end
		_G.__MM2_Val("flyspeed", flightApply, function() return flyValue end, FLY_MIN, FLY_MAX)

		local function updateFlightSlider(x)
			local absSize = flightSliderBar.AbsoluteSize.X
			if absSize <= 0 then return end
			local pct = math.clamp((x - flightSliderBar.AbsolutePosition.X) / absSize, 0, 1)
			flightApply(math.floor(FLY_MIN + pct * (FLY_MAX - FLY_MIN) + 0.5))
		end
		flightSliderBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				draggingFlight = true
				updateFlightSlider(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)
		__live(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
			if draggingFlight
				and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
				updateFlightSlider(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)
		__live(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				draggingFlight = false
			end
		end)
	end
	do
		local jumpValue = 50
		local jumpOn = false
		local JUMP_MIN, JUMP_MAX = 0, 300

		local function applyJump()
			if not alive() then return end
			local char = player.Character
			local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if hum then
				hum.UseJumpPower = true
				hum.JumpPower = jumpOn and jumpValue or 50
			end
		end
		_G.__MM2_Conn(player.CharacterAdded, function() if not alive() then return end
			task.wait(0.5)
			if jumpOn then applyJump() end
		end)
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 136), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Jump Power", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local jumpKeybind = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "JumpKeybind", "Size", UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 24), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -79, 0, 136), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "AutoButtonColor", false, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 10, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Text", "...", "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "ClipsDescendants", true, "Parent", page})
		__c(jumpKeybind, UDim.new(0, 8))
		__s(jumpKeybind, Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50), 1, true)
		local jumpTrack, jumpKnob = __sw(page, 136)

		local function setJumpOn(state)
			if _G.watermarkJmpNameLabel then pcall(function() _G.watermarkJmpNameLabel.TextColor3 = state and acc or __MM2_BaseTxt()
					if watermarkJumpKeyLabel then watermarkJumpKeyLabel.TextColor3 = state and acc or __MM2_WhiteTxt() end
				end) end
			jumpOn = state
			if state then
				TweenService:Create(jumpTrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(true) }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(jumpKnob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(true) }):Play()
				applyJump()
			else
				TweenService:Create(jumpTrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(false) }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(jumpKnob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(false) }):Play()
				applyJump()
			end
		end
		_G.__MM2_Tg("jump", setJumpOn, function() return jumpOn end, jumpTrack, jumpKnob)
		jumpTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setJumpOn(not jumpOn) end)
		local jumpBoundKey = nil
		local bindingJump = false
		jumpKeybind.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() bindingJump = true
			jumpKeybind.Text = "..."
		end)
		_G.__jumpk = _G.__MM2_Conn(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gpe) if bindingJump then
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
					jumpBoundKey = input.KeyCode
					local nm = tostring(input.KeyCode):gsub("Enum.KeyCode.", "")
					local short = { LeftControl = "LCtrl", RightControl = "RCtrl", LeftShift = "LShift", RightShift = "RShift", LeftAlt = "LAlt", RightAlt = "RAlt", CapsLock = "Caps", Space = "Spc", Backspace = "Bspc" }
					nm = short[nm] or nm
					if #nm > 5 then nm = nm:sub(1, 5) end
					jumpKeybind.Text = nm
					_G.jumpKeyName = nm
					if watermarkJumpKeyLabel then
						pcall(function() watermarkJumpKeyLabel.Text = "[<font size=\"13\">" .. nm .. "</font>]" end)
					end
					jumpKeybind.TextColor3 = __MM2_BaseTxt()
				end
				bindingJump = false
				if not jumpBoundKey then jumpKeybind.Text = "..." end
				return
			end
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
				and jumpBoundKey
				and input.KeyCode == jumpBoundKey
				and not gpe then
				setJumpOn(not jumpOn)
			end
		end)
		local jumpSliderBar, jumpSliderFill, jumpSliderKnob = __sl(page, 169, 0.167, LIME)
		local jumpValueLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 162), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "50/300", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 10, "TextColor3", LIME, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, "Parent", page})
		local draggingJump = false

		local function jumpApply(v)
			local n = tonumber(v) or 50
			if n ~= n then n = 50 end
			jumpValue = math.clamp(math.floor(n + 0.5), JUMP_MIN, JUMP_MAX)
			local pct = (jumpValue - JUMP_MIN) / (JUMP_MAX - JUMP_MIN)
			jumpSliderFill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
			jumpSliderKnob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, -6, 0.5, -6)
			jumpValueLabel.Text = tostring(jumpValue) .. "/300"
			if jumpOn then applyJump() end
		end
		_G.__MM2_Val("jump", jumpApply, function() return jumpValue end, JUMP_MIN, JUMP_MAX)

		local function updateJumpSlider(x)
			local absSize = jumpSliderBar.AbsoluteSize.X
			if absSize <= 0 then return end
			local pct = math.clamp((x - jumpSliderBar.AbsolutePosition.X) / absSize, 0, 1)
			jumpApply(math.floor(JUMP_MIN + pct * (JUMP_MAX - JUMP_MIN) + 0.5))
		end
		jumpSliderBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				draggingJump = true
				updateJumpSlider(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)
		__live(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
			if draggingJump
				and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
				updateJumpSlider(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)
		__live(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				draggingJump = false
			end
		end)
	end
	do
		local noclipOn = false
		local noclipConn = nil
		local noclipSaved = {}
		local noclipParts = {}
		local noclipChar = nil
		local noclipAdded = nil

		local function applyNoclip(char)
			if not alive() then return end
			if not char then return end
			if noclipChar ~= char then
				noclipChar = char
				noclipParts = {}
				for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("BasePart") then noclipParts[#noclipParts + 1] = d end
				end
				pcall(function() if noclipAdded then noclipAdded:Disconnect() end
					noclipAdded = char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(newPart) if newPart:IsA("BasePart") then noclipParts[#noclipParts + 1] = newPart end
					end)
				end)
			end
			for i = 1, #noclipParts do
				local part = noclipParts[i]
				if part and part.Parent then
					if noclipSaved[part] == nil then
						noclipSaved[part] = part.CanCollide
						if _G.__MM2_CCSave then _G.__MM2_CCSave(part) end
					end
					if part.CanCollide then part.CanCollide = false end
				else
					noclipParts[i] = nil
				end
			end
		end

		local function restoreNoclip()
			for part, was in pairs(noclipSaved) do
				if part and part.Parent then
					part.CanCollide = was
				end
			end
			noclipSaved = {}
			pcall(function() if noclipAdded then noclipAdded:Disconnect() end
			end)
			noclipAdded = nil
			noclipParts = {}
			noclipChar = nil
		end
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 185), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Noclip", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local noclipKeybind = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "NoclipKeybind", "Size", UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 24), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -79, 0, 185), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "AutoButtonColor", false, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 10, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Text", "...", "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "ClipsDescendants", true, "Parent", page})
		__c(noclipKeybind, UDim.new(0, 8))
		__s(noclipKeybind, Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50), 1, true)
		local noclipTrack, noclipKnob = __sw(page, 185)

		local function setNoclipOn(state)
			if _G.watermarkNcpNameLabel then pcall(function() _G.watermarkNcpNameLabel.TextColor3 = state and acc or __MM2_BaseTxt()
					if watermarkNoclipKeyLabel then watermarkNoclipKeyLabel.TextColor3 = state and acc or __MM2_WhiteTxt() end
				end) end
			noclipOn = state
			if noclipConn then noclipConn:Disconnect() noclipConn = nil end
			if state then
				TweenService:Create(noclipTrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(true) }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(noclipKnob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(true) }):Play()
				local char = player.Character
				if char then
					noclipSaved = {}
					applyNoclip(char)
					noclipConn = _G.__MM2_ConnOwn(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN], RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then pcall(function() noclipConn:Disconnect() end) return end applyNoclip(char) end)
				end
			else
				TweenService:Create(noclipTrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(false) }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(noclipKnob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(false) }):Play()
				restoreNoclip()
			end
		end
		_G.__MM2_Conn(player.CharacterAdded, function(char) if not alive() then return end
			noclipSaved = {}
			if noclipOn then
				task.wait(0.5)
				if player.Character == char then
					applyNoclip(char)
					if noclipConn then noclipConn:Disconnect() end
					noclipConn = _G.__MM2_ConnOwn(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN], RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then pcall(function() noclipConn:Disconnect() end) return end applyNoclip(char) end)
				end
			end
		end)
		_G.__MM2_Tg("noclip", setNoclipOn, function() return noclipOn end, noclipTrack, noclipKnob)
		noclipTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setNoclipOn(not noclipOn) end)
		_G.noclipBoundKey = nil
		_G.bindingNoclip = false
		noclipKeybind.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() _G.bindingNoclip = true
			noclipKeybind.Text = "..."
		end)
		_G.__noclipk = _G.__MM2_Conn(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gpe) if _G.bindingNoclip then
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
					_G.noclipBoundKey = input.KeyCode
					local nm = tostring(input.KeyCode):gsub("Enum.KeyCode.", "")
					local short = { LeftControl = "LCtrl", RightControl = "RCtrl", LeftShift = "LShift", RightShift = "RShift", LeftAlt = "LAlt", RightAlt = "RAlt", CapsLock = "Caps", Space = "Spc", Backspace = "Bspc" }
					nm = short[nm] or nm
					if #nm > 5 then nm = nm:sub(1, 5) end
					noclipKeybind.Text = nm
					_G.noclipKeyName = nm
					if watermarkNoclipKeyLabel then
						pcall(function() watermarkNoclipKeyLabel.Text = "[<font size=\"13\">" .. nm .. "</font>]" end)
					end
					noclipKeybind.TextColor3 = __MM2_BaseTxt()
				end
				_G.bindingNoclip = false
				if not _G.noclipBoundKey then noclipKeybind.Text = "..." end
				return
			end
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
				and _G.noclipBoundKey
				and input.KeyCode == _G.noclipBoundKey
				and not gpe then
				setNoclipOn(not noclipOn)
			end
		end)
	end
	__mk("Frame", {"Name", "FunDivider", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 233), "BackgroundColor3", Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80), "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", page})
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 241), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", "Fun", "Parent", page})
	local funBox = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "FunBox", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 16, 0, 362), "Position", UDim2.new(0, -8, 0, 270), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ZIndex", 0, "Parent", page})
	_G.funBoxCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	_G.funBoxCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
	_G.funBoxCorner.Parent = funBox
	_G.funBoxStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	_G.funBoxStroke.Color = BORDER
	_G.funBoxStroke.Thickness = 1
	_G.funBoxStroke.Parent = funBox
	local teleportListH = 0
	_G.skyListH = 0
	local rainOn, skyboxOn = false, false
	local skyboxTrack, skyboxKnob, rainTrack, rainKnob

	local function refreshExclusion()
		for _, set in ipairs(_G.__MM2_BlockSyncSets or {}) do
			for _, f in pairs(set) do pcall(f) end
		end
	end

	local function refreshFunBox(animate)
		local bottom = 632
		if teleportListH > 0 then bottom = math.max(bottom, 324 + teleportListH + 13) end
		if _G.skyListH > 0 then bottom = math.max(bottom, 365 + _G.skyListH + 13) end
		local h = bottom - 270
		if animate then
			TweenService:Create(funBox, TWEEN_POP, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 16, 0, h) }):Play()
		else
			funBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 16, 0, h)
		end
	end
	do
		local teleportTarget = nil
		local funDropdownOpen = false
		local funDropdown = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "FunDropdown", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 283), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", page})
		__s(funDropdown, BORDER, 1, true)
		local funDropdownLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Teleport: ...", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 12, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", funDropdown})
		local funDropdownArrow = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "v", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "Parent", funDropdown})
		local funDropdownList = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "FunDropdownList", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 324), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ClipsDescendants", true, "Visible", false, "ZIndex", 5, "Parent", page})
		local funDropdownListStroke = __s(funDropdownList, BORDER, 1, true)

		local function setTeleportListGhost(ghost)
			local t = ghost and 1 or 0
			funDropdownList.BackgroundTransparency = t
			funDropdownListStroke.Transparency = t
			__optFade(funDropdownList, t)
		end

		local function animateTeleportList(open, count)
			if open then
				funDropdownList.Visible = true
				setTeleportListGhost(true)
				TweenService:Create(funDropdownList, TWEEN_POP, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, count * 32), BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(funDropdownListStroke, TWEEN_POP, { Transparency = 0 }):Play()
				__optFade(funDropdownList, 0, TWEEN_POP)
			else
				TweenService:Create(funDropdownList, TWEEN_EASE, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(funDropdownListStroke, TWEEN_EASE, { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
				__optFade(funDropdownList, 1, TWEEN_EASE)
				task.delay(0.22, function() if not funDropdownOpen then funDropdownList.Visible = false end
				end)
			end
			task.delay(0.3, function() refreshMiscCanvas() end)
		end

		local function refreshTeleportList()
			for _, c in ipairs(funDropdownList:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("TextButton") then pcall(function() c:Destroy() end) end
			end
			local n = 0
			for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if plr ~= player then
					n = n + 1
					local opt = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, (n - 1) * 32), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", funDropdownList})
					__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", plr.Name, "Font", Enum.Font.Gotham, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", (teleportTarget == plr) and acc or TEXT_SECONDARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", opt})
					opt.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() teleportTarget = plr
						funDropdownLabel.Text = "Teleport: " .. plr.Name:upper()
						local char = player.Character
						local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local tchar = plr.Character
						local thrp = tchar and tchar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if hrp and thrp then
							pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = thrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0, 2) end)
						end
						funDropdownOpen = false
						teleportListH = 0
						animateTeleportList(false, 0)
						refreshFunBox(true)
						funDropdownArrow.Rotation = 0
						funDropdownArrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0)
						refreshTeleportList()
					end)
				end
			end
			if funDropdownOpen then
				funDropdownList.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, n * 32)
			end
		end
		funDropdown.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() funDropdownOpen = not funDropdownOpen
			if funDropdownOpen then refreshTeleportList() end
			local count = 0
			for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if plr ~= player then count = count + 1 end
			end
			teleportListH = funDropdownOpen and (count * 32) or 0
			animateTeleportList(funDropdownOpen, count)
			refreshFunBox(true)
			funDropdownArrow.Rotation = funDropdownOpen and 180 or 0
			funDropdownArrow.Position = funDropdownOpen and UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1) or UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0)
		end)

		local function refreshTeleportCount()
			if not funDropdownOpen then return end
			do
				local n = 0
				for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if plr ~= player then n = n + 1 end end
				teleportListH = n * 32
			end
			refreshTeleportList() refreshMiscCanvas() refreshFunBox(true)
		end
		_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerAdded, function() if not alive() then return end
			refreshTeleportCount()
		end)
		_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(plr) if not alive() then return end
			if teleportTarget == plr then
				teleportTarget = nil
				funDropdownLabel.Text = "Teleport: ..."
			end
			refreshTeleportCount()
		end)
	end
	do
		skyboxOn = false
		local skyboxChoice
		local skyDropdownLabel
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Name", "CustomSkyboxLbl", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 370), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Custom skybox", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		skyboxTrack = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 370), "BackgroundColor3", OFF_STATE, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", page})
		__c(skyboxTrack, UDim.new(1, 0))
		skyboxKnob = __mk("Frame", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), "BackgroundColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Parent", skyboxTrack})
		__c(skyboxKnob, UDim.new(1, 0))
		local applySkybox, removeCustomSky

		local function setCustomSkybox(state)
			if state and rainOn then return end
			skyboxOn = state
			if state then
				if skyboxChoice then applySkybox(skyboxChoice)
				elseif skyClockSaved == nil then
					pcall(function() local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
						skyClockSaved = lighting.ClockTime
					end)
				end
			else
				removeCustomSky()
			end
			refreshExclusion()
			for _, set in ipairs(_G.__MM2_BlockSyncSets or {}) do
				for _, f in pairs(set) do pcall(f) end
			end
		end
		_G.__MM2_Tg("skybox", setCustomSkybox, function() return skyboxOn end, skyboxTrack, skyboxKnob)
		local skyLbl = page:FindFirstChild("CustomSkyboxLbl")

		local skySync = function(instant)
			__MM2_BlockPaint(skyboxTrack, skyboxKnob, skyLbl, rainOn and true or false, skyboxOn, instant)
		end
		_G.__MM2_BlockedSyncs["skybox"] = skySync
		skySync(true)
		skyboxTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if rainOn then skySync() refreshExclusion() return end
			setCustomSkybox(not skyboxOn)
			__knob(skyboxTrack, skyboxKnob, skyboxOn)
		end)
