						Cyan = { SkyboxBk = "rbxassetid://134992490496115", SkyboxDn = "rbxassetid://134253426516129", SkyboxFt = "rbxassetid://89320882090607",
							SkyboxLf = "rbxassetid://78161747802269", SkyboxRt = "rbxassetid://115639031905206", SkyboxUp = "rbxassetid://78627754992642" },
							Hades = { SkyboxBk = "rbxassetid://101109101929646", SkyboxDn = "rbxassetid://119785170451641", SkyboxFt = "rbxassetid://102369704169780",
								SkyboxLf = "rbxassetid://83161570226284", SkyboxRt = "rbxassetid://124328307114323", SkyboxUp = "rbxassetid://114052428323695" },
								Space = { SkyboxBk = "rbxassetid://80966534717269", SkyboxDn = "rbxassetid://113226673570712", SkyboxFt = "rbxassetid://100258759870572",
									SkyboxLf = "rbxassetid://116635782634340", SkyboxRt = "rbxassetid://96519294007841", SkyboxUp = "rbxassetid://114999232476842" },
									PinkSunrise = { SkyboxBk = "rbxassetid://122725998548778", SkyboxDn = "rbxassetid://79450614504452", SkyboxFt = "rbxassetid://99397487569175",
										SkyboxLf = "rbxassetid://83952742293028", SkyboxRt = "rbxassetid://118204656561495", SkyboxUp = "rbxassetid://93852884824086" }, }
		local skyClockSaved = nil
		local skyLightThemes = { NeonSky = { amb = Color3.fromRGB(125, 122, 170), out = Color3.fromRGB(122, 122, 175), top = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 255), bot = Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 230), eds = 1 }, Night = { amb = Color3.fromRGB(118, 126, 168), out = Color3.fromRGB(115, 124, 172), top = Color3.fromRGB(150, 168, 255), bot = Color3.fromRGB(128, 148, 235), eds = 1 }, LightPink = { amb = Color3.fromRGB(172, 142, 158), out = Color3.fromRGB(172, 145, 162), top = Color3.fromRGB(255, 198, 240), bot = Color3.fromRGB(232, 172, 222), eds = 1 },
			Spooky = { amb = Color3.fromRGB(100, 150, 110), out = Color3.fromRGB(95, 145, 105), top = Color3.fromRGB(140, 255, 160), bot = Color3.fromRGB(70, 140, 90), eds = 1 },
			Cyan = { amb = Color3.fromRGB(55, 120, 145), out = Color3.fromRGB(50, 115, 140), top = Color3.fromRGB(120, 235, 255), bot = Color3.fromRGB(40, 110, 135), eds = 1 },
			Hades = { amb = Color3.fromRGB(140, 62, 42), out = Color3.fromRGB(135, 58, 40), top = Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 90), bot = Color3.fromRGB(150, 50, 30), eds = 1 },
			Space = { amb = Color3.fromRGB(72, 62, 145), out = Color3.fromRGB(68, 58, 140), top = Color3.fromRGB(150, 130, 255), bot = Color3.fromRGB(55, 45, 120), eds = 1 },
			PinkSunrise = { amb = Color3.fromRGB(200, 178, 198), out = Color3.fromRGB(196, 174, 194), top = Color3.fromRGB(180, 222, 250), bot = Color3.fromRGB(255, 198, 220), eds = 1 } }
		local savedAtmo = {}
		local savedSkies = nil
		local skyMode, skyNightTaken = nil, nil
		local skyNightCC = nil
		removeCustomSky = function()
			local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
			pcall(function() local mine = lighting:FindFirstChild("Sky")
				if mine then mine:Destroy() end
			end)
			if savedSkies then
				pcall(function() for _, s in ipairs(savedSkies) do
						if s then s.Parent = lighting end
					end
				end)
				savedSkies = nil
			end
			pcall(function() lighting.FogStart = 0
				lighting.FogEnd = 200
				lighting.ClockTime = 0
				lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(157, 157, 157)
				lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(157, 157, 157)
				lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
				lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
				lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
			end)
			skyClockSaved = nil
			for _, e in ipairs(savedAtmo) do
				pcall(function() e.obj.Density = e.Density
					e.obj.Offset = e.Offset
					e.obj.Color = e.Color
					e.obj.Decay = e.Decay
					e.obj.Glare = e.Glare
					e.obj.Haze = e.Haze
				end)
			end
			savedAtmo = {}
			pcall(function() local na = lighting:FindFirstChild("SkyAtmo")
				if na then na:Destroy() end
			end)
			if skyNightTaken then
				pcall(function() if lighting.Ambient == skyNightTaken.nAmb then lighting.Ambient = skyNightTaken.oldAmb end
					if lighting.OutdoorAmbient == skyNightTaken.nOut then lighting.OutdoorAmbient = skyNightTaken.oldOut end
					if lighting.ColorShift_Top == skyNightTaken.nTop then lighting.ColorShift_Top = skyNightTaken.oldTop end
					if lighting.ColorShift_Bottom == skyNightTaken.nBot then lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = skyNightTaken.oldBot end
				end)
				for _, e in ipairs(skyNightTaken.atmo) do
					pcall(function() e.obj.Density = e.OD
						e.obj.Offset = e.OO
						e.obj.Glare = e.OG
						e.obj.Haze = e.OH
					end)
				end
				skyNightTaken = nil
			end
			if skyNightCC then pcall(function() skyNightCC:Destroy() end) skyNightCC = nil end
			skyMode = nil
		end
		applySkybox = function(name)
			local preset = skyPresets[name]
			if not preset then return end
			for _, id in pairs(preset) do
				if not id or id == "" then
					skyDropdownLabel.Text = "Skybox: UNAVAILABLE"
					return
				end
			end
			local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
			removeCustomSky()
			if skyClockSaved == nil then
				skyClockSaved = lighting.ClockTime
			end
			if lighting.FogEnd < 90000 then
				pcall(function() lighting.FogStart = 0
					lighting.FogEnd = 100000
				end)
			end
			local atmoTheme = nil
			if name == "NeonSky" then atmoTheme = { d = 0.3, o = 0, c = Color3.fromRGB(140, 90, 200), de = Color3.fromRGB(110, 70, 170), g = 0.3, h = 2 }
			elseif name == "Night" then atmoTheme = { d = 0.25, o = 0, c = Color3.fromRGB(180, 190, 220), de = Color3.fromRGB(120, 130, 170), g = 0.2, h = 2 }
			elseif name == "LightPink" then atmoTheme = { d = 0.3, o = 0, c = Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 200), de = Color3.fromRGB(230, 120, 170), g = 0.3, h = 2 }
			elseif name == "Spooky" then atmoTheme = { d = 0.3, o = 0, c = Color3.fromRGB(115, 185, 130), de = Color3.fromRGB(70, 120, 85), g = 0.25, h = 2 }
			elseif name == "Cyan" then atmoTheme = { d = 0.32, o = 0, c = Color3.fromRGB(80, 190, 220), de = Color3.fromRGB(35, 110, 140), g = 0.2, h = 2 }
			elseif name == "Hades" then atmoTheme = { d = 0.34, o = 0, c = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 90), de = Color3.fromRGB(170, 55, 30), g = 0.35, h = 2 }
			elseif name == "Space" then atmoTheme = { d = 0.34, o = 0, c = Color3.fromRGB(120, 110, 235), de = Color3.fromRGB(55, 45, 130), g = 0.2, h = 2 }
			elseif name == "PinkSunrise" then atmoTheme = { d = 0.28, o = 0, c = Color3.fromRGB(240, 190, 215), de = Color3.fromRGB(160, 190, 225), g = 0.25, h = 2 } end
			for _, a in ipairs(lighting:GetChildren()) do
				if a:IsA("Atmosphere") then
					savedAtmo[#savedAtmo + 1] = { obj = a, Density = a.Density, Offset = a.Offset, Color = a.Color, Decay = a.Decay, Glare = a.Glare, Haze = a.Haze }
					pcall(function() if atmoTheme then
							a.Density = atmoTheme.d
							a.Offset = atmoTheme.o
							a.Color = atmoTheme.c
							a.Decay = atmoTheme.de
							a.Glare = atmoTheme.g
							a.Haze = atmoTheme.h
						else
							a.Density = 0
						end
					end)
				end
			end
			if atmoTheme and not lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere") then
				pcall(function() local na = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
					na.Name = "SkyAtmo"
					na.Density = atmoTheme.d
					na.Offset = atmoTheme.o
					na.Color = atmoTheme.c
					na.Decay = atmoTheme.de
					na.Glare = atmoTheme.g
					na.Haze = atmoTheme.h
					na.Parent = lighting
				end)
			end
			pcall(function() for _, s in ipairs(lighting:GetChildren()) do
					if s:IsA("Sky") and not s:GetAttribute("Owned") then
						savedSkies = savedSkies or {}
						savedSkies[#savedSkies + 1] = s
						s.Parent = nil
					end
				end
			end)
			local sky = __mk("Sky", {"Name", "Sky", "CelestialBodiesShown", false})
			for prop, id in pairs(preset) do
				pcall(function() sky[prop] = id end)
			end
			sky.Parent = lighting
			local th = skyLightThemes[name]
			if th then
				pcall(function() if not lighting:FindFirstChild("LightCC") then
						lighting.Ambient = th.amb
						lighting.OutdoorAmbient = th.out
						lighting.ColorShift_Top = th.top
						lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = th.bot
					end
					lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = th.eds
				end)
			end
			pcall(function() if lighting:FindFirstChild("LightCC") then
					lighting.ColorShift_Top = PURE_WHITE
					lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
				end
			end)
			skyDropdownLabel.Text = "Skybox: " .. name:upper()
		end
		pcall(function() _G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
				if not skyboxOn then return end
				local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
				if lighting.ClockTime ~= 14 then lighting.ClockTime = 14 end
				local th = skyLightThemes[skyboxChoice]
				if th and not lighting:FindFirstChild("LightCC") then
					pcall(function() if lighting.Ambient ~= th.amb then lighting.Ambient = th.amb end
						if lighting.OutdoorAmbient ~= th.out then lighting.OutdoorAmbient = th.out end
						if lighting.ColorShift_Top ~= th.top then lighting.ColorShift_Top = th.top end
						if lighting.ColorShift_Bottom ~= th.bot then lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = th.bot end
						lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = th.eds
					end)
				end
				local isNight = lighting.ClockTime < 6 or lighting.ClockTime >= 18
				local nm = isNight and "night" or "day"
				if nm ~= skyMode then
					skyMode = nm
					if skyNightTaken then
						pcall(function() if lighting.Ambient == skyNightTaken.nAmb then lighting.Ambient = skyNightTaken.oldAmb end
							if lighting.OutdoorAmbient == skyNightTaken.nOut then lighting.OutdoorAmbient = skyNightTaken.oldOut end
							if lighting.ColorShift_Top == skyNightTaken.nTop then lighting.ColorShift_Top = skyNightTaken.oldTop end
							if lighting.ColorShift_Bottom == skyNightTaken.nBot then lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = skyNightTaken.oldBot end
						end)
						for _, e in ipairs(skyNightTaken.atmo) do
							pcall(function() e.obj.Density = e.OD
								e.obj.Offset = e.OO
								e.obj.Glare = e.OG
								e.obj.Haze = e.OH
							end)
						end
						skyNightTaken = nil
						if skyNightCC then pcall(function() skyNightCC:Destroy() end) skyNightCC = nil end
					end
				end
				if isNight then
					if not skyNightTaken then
						skyNightTaken = { oldAmb = lighting.Ambient, oldOut = lighting.OutdoorAmbient, oldTop = lighting.ColorShift_Top, oldBot = lighting.ColorShift_Bottom, nAmb = Color3.fromRGB(38, 42, 58), nOut = Color3.fromRGB(54, 60, 80), nTop = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), nBot = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), atmo = {} }
					end
					pcall(function() if lighting.Ambient ~= skyNightTaken.nAmb then lighting.Ambient = skyNightTaken.nAmb end
						if lighting.OutdoorAmbient ~= skyNightTaken.nOut then lighting.OutdoorAmbient = skyNightTaken.nOut end
						if lighting.ColorShift_Top ~= skyNightTaken.nTop then lighting.ColorShift_Top = skyNightTaken.nTop end
						if lighting.ColorShift_Bottom ~= skyNightTaken.nBot then lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = skyNightTaken.nBot end
					end)
					pcall(function() for _, a in ipairs(lighting:GetChildren()) do
							if a:IsA("Atmosphere") and not a:GetAttribute("Owned") then
								local taken = nil
								for _, e in ipairs(skyNightTaken.atmo) do if e.obj == a then taken = e end end
								if taken then
									if a.Density ~= 0.08 then a.Density = 0.08 end
									if a.Haze ~= 0 then a.Haze = 0 end
									if a.Glare ~= 0 then a.Glare = 0 end
									if a.Offset ~= 0 then a.Offset = 0 end
								else
									skyNightTaken.atmo[#skyNightTaken.atmo + 1] = { obj = a, OD = a.Density, OO = a.Offset, OG = a.Glare, OH = a.Haze }
									a.Density = 0.08 a.Haze = 0 a.Glare = 0 a.Offset = 0
								end
							end
						end
					end)
					if not (skyNightCC and skyNightCC.Parent) then
						skyNightCC = __mk("ColorCorrectionEffect", {"Name", "SkyNightCC", "Brightness", 0.08, "Contrast", 0.02, "Parent", lighting})
					end
				end
			end) end)
		do
			task.spawn(function()
				while skyboxOn and alive() do
					pcall(function() local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
						if not alive() then return end
						if lighting.ClockTime ~= 14 then lighting.ClockTime = 14 end
						local th = skyLightThemes[skyboxChoice]
						if th and not lighting:FindFirstChild("LightCC") then
							if lighting.Ambient ~= th.amb then lighting.Ambient = th.amb end
							if lighting.OutdoorAmbient ~= th.out then lighting.OutdoorAmbient = th.out end
							if lighting.ColorShift_Top ~= th.top then lighting.ColorShift_Top = th.top end
							if lighting.ColorShift_Bottom ~= th.bot then lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = th.bot end
							lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = th.eds
						end
					end)
					task.wait(0.05)
				end
			end)
		end
		skyboxChoice = nil
		_G.__MM2_SkyChoiceName = nil
		local skyDropdownOpen = false
		local skyDropdown = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "SkyDropdown", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 324), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", page})
		__s(skyDropdown, BORDER, 1, true)
		skyDropdownLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Skybox: ...", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 12, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", skyDropdown})
		local skyDropdownArrow = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "v", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "Parent", skyDropdown})
		local skyDropdownList = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "SkyDropdownList", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 365), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ClipsDescendants", true, "Visible", false, "ZIndex", 5, "Parent", page})
		local skyDropdownListStroke = __s(skyDropdownList, BORDER, 1, true)
		local skyOptions = { "NeonSky", "Night", "LightPink", "Spooky", "Cyan", "Hades", "Space", "PinkSunrise" }

		local function setSkyListGhost(ghost)
			local t = ghost and 1 or 0
			skyDropdownList.BackgroundTransparency = t
			skyDropdownListStroke.Transparency = t
			__optFade(skyDropdownList, t)
		end

		local function animateSkyList(open, count)
			if open then
				skyDropdownList.Visible = true
				setSkyListGhost(true)
				TweenService:Create(skyDropdownList, TWEEN_POP, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, count * 32), BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(skyDropdownListStroke, TWEEN_POP, { Transparency = 0 }):Play()
				__optFade(skyDropdownList, 0, TWEEN_POP)
			else
				TweenService:Create(skyDropdownList, TWEEN_EASE, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(skyDropdownListStroke, TWEEN_EASE, { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
				__optFade(skyDropdownList, 1, TWEEN_EASE)
				task.delay(0.22, function() if not skyDropdownOpen then skyDropdownList.Visible = false end
				end)
			end
			task.delay(0.3, function() refreshMiscCanvas() end)
		end

		local function refreshSkyList()
			for _, c in ipairs(skyDropdownList:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("TextButton") then pcall(function() c:Destroy() end) end
			end
			for i, optName in ipairs(skyOptions) do
				local opt = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, (i - 1) * 32), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", skyDropdownList})
				__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", optName, "Font", Enum.Font.Gotham, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", (skyboxChoice == optName) and acc or TEXT_SECONDARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", opt})
				opt.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() skyboxChoice = optName
					_G.__MM2_SkyChoiceName = optName
					skyDropdownLabel.Text = "Skybox: " .. optName:upper()
					if skyboxOn then applySkybox(optName) end
					_G.skyListH = 0
					animateSkyList(false, 0)
					refreshFunBox(true)
					skyDropdownArrow.Rotation = 0
					skyDropdownArrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0)
					refreshSkyList()
				end)
			end
			if skyDropdownOpen then
				skyDropdownList.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, #skyOptions * 32)
			end
		end
		refreshSkyList()
		_G.__MM2_CfgSkyboxApply = function(name)
			if skyPresets[name] then
				skyboxChoice = name
				_G.__MM2_SkyChoiceName = name
				skyDropdownLabel.Text = "Skybox: " .. name:upper()
				if skyboxOn then applySkybox(name) end
			end
		end
		skyDropdown.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() skyDropdownOpen = not skyDropdownOpen
			if skyDropdownOpen then refreshSkyList() end
			_G.skyListH = skyDropdownOpen and (#skyOptions * 32) or 0
			animateSkyList(skyDropdownOpen, #skyOptions)
			refreshFunBox(true)
			skyDropdownArrow.Rotation = skyDropdownOpen and 180 or 0
			skyDropdownArrow.Position = skyDropdownOpen and UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1) or UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0)
		end)
		local satOn = false
		local satValue = 50
		local satEffect = nil

		local function getSatEffect()
			if satEffect and satEffect.Parent then return satEffect end
			local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
			local found = lighting:FindFirstChild("Saturation")
			if found and found:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") then
				satEffect = found
				return satEffect
			end
			local fx = __mk("ColorCorrectionEffect", {"Name", "Saturation", "Enabled", false, "Saturation", 0, "Parent", lighting})
			satEffect = fx
			return satEffect
		end

		local function applySat()
			local fx = getSatEffect()
			if fx then
				fx.Enabled = satOn
				pcall(function() fx.Saturation = satValue / 50 - 1 end)
			end
		end
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 400), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Color saturation", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local satTrack, satKnob = __sw(page, 400)

		local function setSatOn(state)
			satOn = state
			__knob(satTrack, satKnob, state)
			applySat()
		end
		_G.__MM2_Tg("sat", setSatOn, function() return satOn end, satTrack, satKnob)
		satTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setSatOn(not satOn) end)
		local satSliderBar, satSliderFill, satSliderKnob = __sl(page, 433, 0.5, LIME)
		local satValueLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 426), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "50/100", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 10, "TextColor3", LIME, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, "Parent", page})
		local draggingSat = false

		local function satApply(v)
			local n = tonumber(v) or 50
			if n ~= n then n = 50 end
			satValue = math.clamp(math.floor(n + 0.5), 0, 100)
			local pct = satValue / 100
			satSliderFill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
			satSliderKnob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, -6, 0.5, -6)
			satValueLabel.Text = tostring(satValue) .. "/100"
			applySat()
		end
		_G.__MM2_Val("sat", satApply, function() return satValue end, 0, 100)

		local function updateSatSlider(x)
			local absSize = satSliderBar.AbsoluteSize.X
			if absSize <= 0 then return end
			local pct = math.clamp((x - satSliderBar.AbsolutePosition.X) / absSize, 0, 1)
			satApply(math.floor(pct * 100 + 0.5))
		end
		satSliderBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				draggingSat = true
				updateSatSlider(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)
		__live(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
			if draggingSat
				and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
				updateSatSlider(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)
		__live(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				draggingSat = false
			end
		end)
	end
	do
		rainOn = false
		local RAIN_SOUND_ID = "rbxassetid://1516791621"
		local rain3DFolder = nil
		local rainBeams = {}
		local rainBeamTick = 0
		local rainActive = 2000
		local rainActivePrev = 2000
		local rainEmaDt = 0.016
		_G.lastPerfCheck = 0
		_G.rainBuilding = false

		local function buildRain3D()
			if _G.rainBuilding then return end
			if rain3DFolder and rain3DFolder.Parent then return end
			pcall(function() if rain3DFolder then rain3DFolder:Destroy() end end)
			rainBeams = {}
			rain3DFolder = __mk("Folder", {"Name", "Rain3D", "Parent", workspace})
			_G.rainBuilding = true
			for i = 1, 2000 do
				local top = Instance.new("Attachment")
				local bot = Instance.new("Attachment")
				top.Parent = rain3DFolder
				bot.Parent = rain3DFolder
				local b = __mk("Beam", {"Attachment0", top, "Attachment1", bot, "Width0", 0.06, "Width1", 0.06, "Color", ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 145)), "Transparency", NumberSequence.new(0.55), "FaceCamera", true, "Enabled", false, "Parent", rain3DFolder})
				rainBeams[i] = { top = top, bot = bot, beam = b, x = 0, y = 0, z = 0, speed = math.random(80, 120), init = false }
				if i % 250 == 0 then task.wait() end
			end
			if rain3DFolder and not rain3DFolder.Parent then rain3DFolder = nil end
			_G.rainBuilding = false
		end
		local rainSound = nil
		local rainSound2 = nil

		local function stopRainSound()
			if rainSound then
				pcall(function() rainSound:Stop() end)
				pcall(function() rainSound:Destroy() end)
				rainSound = nil
			end
			if rainSound2 then
				pcall(function() rainSound2:Stop() end)
				pcall(function() rainSound2:Destroy() end)
				rainSound2 = nil
			end
		end

		local function startRainSound()
			if RAIN_SOUND_ID == "" then return end
			stopRainSound()
			local s = __mk("Sound", {"Name", "Rain", "SoundId", RAIN_SOUND_ID, "Looped", true, "Volume", noRainSoundOn and 0 or 0.5, "Parent", game:GetService("SoundService")})
			pcall(function() s:Play() end)
			local myS = s
			rainSound = s
			task.delay(3, function() if not rainOn or not alive() then return end
				if rainSound ~= myS then return end
				pcall(function() if rainSound2 then rainSound2:Destroy() end end)
				rainSound2 = nil
				local s2 = __mk("Sound", {"Name", "RainEcho", "SoundId", RAIN_SOUND_ID, "Looped", true, "Volume", noRainSoundOn and 0 or 0.5, "Parent", game:GetService("SoundService")})
				pcall(function() s2:Play() end)
				rainSound2 = s2
			end)
		end
		local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
		local splashParams = RaycastParams.new()
		splashParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

		local function spawnSplash()
			local char = player.Character
			local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not hrp then return end
			local ox = math.random(-250, 250) / 10
			local oz = math.random(-250, 250) / 10
			local origin = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(ox, 30, oz)
			local res = nil
			local filter = { char }
			for attempt = 1, 4 do
				splashParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter
				local r = nil
				pcall(function() r = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -70, 0), splashParams) end)
				if not r then break end
				local transp = 0
				pcall(function() transp = r.Instance.Transparency end)
				if transp < 1 then res = r break end
				filter[#filter + 1] = r.Instance
			end
			if not res then return end
			local att = __mk("Attachment", {"WorldPosition", res.Position})
			local okP = pcall(function() att.Parent = workspace.Terrain end)
			if not okP then pcall(function() att:Destroy() end) return end
			local pe = __mk("ParticleEmitter", {"Rate", 0, "EmissionDirection", Enum.NormalId.Top, "SpreadAngle", Vector2.new(35, 35), "Speed", NumberRange.new(7, 13), "Acceleration", Vector3.new(0, -45, 0), "Size", NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.35), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.1) }), "Transparency", NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) }), "Color", ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(170, 200, 230)), "Lifetime", NumberRange.new(0.25, 0.45), "Parent", att})
			pcall(function() pe:Emit(3) end)
			Debris:AddItem(att, 2)
		end
		local splashAcc = 0
		local lastSoundCheck = 0
		_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt) if not alive() then return end
			if not rainOn then return end
			if RAIN_SOUND_ID ~= "" and os.clock() - lastSoundCheck > 2 then
				lastSoundCheck = os.clock()
				local okPlay = false
				pcall(function() okPlay = rainSound ~= nil and rainSound.IsPlaying end)
				if not okPlay then startRainSound() end
			end
			do
				local l = game:GetService("Lighting")
				pcall(function() l.FogStart = 0
					l.FogEnd = 100000
				end)
			end
			rainEmaDt = rainEmaDt * 0.95 + dt * 0.05
			if os.clock() - _G.lastPerfCheck > 2 then
				_G.lastPerfCheck = os.clock()
				if rainEmaDt > 0.03 and rainActive > 500 then
					rainActive = rainActive - 250
				elseif rainEmaDt < 0.018 and rainActive < 2000 then
					rainActive = rainActive + 250
				end
			end
			splashAcc = splashAcc + math.min(dt, 0.1)
			local splashBudget = 0
			while splashAcc >= 0.02 and splashBudget < 3 do
				splashBudget = splashBudget + 1
				splashAcc = splashAcc - 0.02
				spawnSplash()
			end
			if splashAcc > 0.06 then splashAcc = 0.06 end
		end)
		_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.RenderStepped, function(dt) if not alive() then return end
			if not rainOn then return end
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if not cam then return end
			if not rain3DFolder or not rain3DFolder.Parent then
				pcall(function() buildRain3D() end)
			end
			if rain3DFolder and rain3DFolder.Parent then
				local cp = cam.CFrame.Position
				local base = Vector3.new(math.floor(cp.X / 50) * 50, math.floor(cp.Y / 50) * 50, math.floor(cp.Z / 50) * 50)
				rainBeamTick = rainBeamTick + 1
				local stepDt = dt * 3
				if rainActivePrev ~= rainActive then
					for idx2, s2 in ipairs(rainBeams) do
						if idx2 > rainActive then pcall(function() s2.beam.Enabled = false end) end
					end
					rainActivePrev = rainActive
				end
				for idx, s in ipairs(rainBeams) do
					if idx <= rainActive then
						if not s.init then
							s.x = math.random(-2500, 2500) / 10
							s.z = math.random(-2500, 2500) / 10
							s.y = math.random(-300, 400) / 10
							s.init = true
							s.top.WorldPosition = Vector3.new(base.X + s.x, base.Y + s.y, base.Z + s.z)
							s.bot.WorldPosition = Vector3.new(base.X + s.x + 0.6, base.Y + s.y - 6, base.Z + s.z)
							s.beam.Enabled = true
						end
						if (idx + rainBeamTick) % 3 == 0 then
							s.y = s.y - s.speed * stepDt
							if s.y < -30 then
								s.y = 40
								s.x = math.random(-2500, 2500) / 10
								s.z = math.random(-2500, 2500) / 10
							end
							s.top.WorldPosition = Vector3.new(base.X + s.x, base.Y + s.y, base.Z + s.z)
							s.bot.WorldPosition = Vector3.new(base.X + s.x + 0.6, base.Y + s.y - 6, base.Z + s.z)
							s.beam.Enabled = true
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		local rainLbl = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 449), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Cinematic rain", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		rainTrack = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 449), "BackgroundColor3", OFF_STATE, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", page})
		__c(rainTrack, UDim.new(1, 0))
		rainKnob = __mk("Frame", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), "BackgroundColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Parent", rainTrack})
		__c(rainKnob, UDim.new(1, 0))

		local rainSync = function(instant)
			__MM2_BlockPaint(rainTrack, rainKnob, rainLbl, skyboxOn and true or false, rainOn, instant)
		end
		_G.__MM2_BlockedSyncs["rain"] = rainSync
		rainSync(true)
		local rainAtmoSaved = nil
		local rainFogSaved = nil
		local RAIN_SKY = { SkyboxBk = "rbxassetid://125066713749479", SkyboxDn = "rbxassetid://123524263518656", SkyboxFt = "rbxassetid://73374270267092", SkyboxLf = "rbxassetid://138534931141529",
			SkyboxRt = "rbxassetid://107060664203851", SkyboxUp = "rbxassetid://103739852573345" }
		local rainCloudsSaved = nil
		local rainClockSaved = nil
		local rainSunSaved = nil

		local function applyRainSky()
			local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
			local atmo = lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
			if atmo then
				rainAtmoSaved = { obj = atmo, Density = atmo.Density, Offset = atmo.Offset, Color = atmo.Color, Decay = atmo.Decay, Glare = atmo.Glare, Haze = atmo.Haze }
				pcall(function() atmo.Density = 0.3
					atmo.Offset = 0.1
					atmo.Color = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 125)
					atmo.Decay = Color3.fromRGB(100, 105, 115)
					atmo.Glare = 0
					atmo.Haze = 1
				end)
			else
				local na = __mk("Atmosphere", {"Name", "RainAtmo", "Density", 0.3, "Offset", 0.1, "Color", Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 125), "Decay", Color3.fromRGB(100, 105, 115), "Glare", 0, "Haze", 1})
				pcall(function() na.Parent = lighting end)
			end
			pcall(function() local old = lighting:FindFirstChild("RainSky")
				if old then old:Destroy() end
			end)
			if not rainSunSaved then rainSunSaved = {} end
			rainSunSaved.skies = {}
			pcall(function() for _, s in ipairs(lighting:GetChildren()) do
					if s:IsA("Sky") and not s:GetAttribute("Owned") then
						rainSunSaved.skies[#rainSunSaved.skies + 1] = s
						s.Parent = nil
					end
				end
			end)
			local rs = __mk("Sky", {"Name", "RainSky", "CelestialBodiesShown", false})
			for prop, id in pairs(RAIN_SKY) do
				pcall(function() rs[prop] = id end)
			end
			pcall(function() rs.Parent = lighting end)
			local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
			if terrain then
				local okE, wasE = pcall(function() return terrain.CloudsEnabled end)
				local okC, wasC = pcall(function() return terrain.CloudColor end)
				rainCloudsSaved = { obj = terrain, enabled = (okE and wasE == true), color = (okC and wasC) }
				pcall(function() terrain.CloudsEnabled = true end)
				pcall(function() terrain.CloudColor = Color3.fromRGB(105, 105, 110) end)
			end
			pcall(function() local old = lighting:FindFirstChild("RainBloom")
				if old then old:Destroy() end
			end)
			local bloom = __mk("BloomEffect", {"Name", "RainBloom", "Intensity", 0.8, "Size", 32, "Threshold", 0.85})
			pcall(function() bloom.Parent = lighting end)
			rainClockSaved = { clock = lighting.ClockTime, bright = lighting.Brightness }
			pcall(function() lighting.ClockTime = 0
				lighting.Brightness = 1.5
			end)
			pcall(function() local old = lighting:FindFirstChild("RainCC")
				if old then old:Destroy() end
			end)
			local rcc = __mk("ColorCorrectionEffect", {"Name", "RainCC", "Brightness", -0.05, "Contrast", 0.1, "Saturation", -0.1, "TintColor", Color3.fromRGB(170, 180, 210)})
			pcall(function() rcc.Parent = lighting end)
			pcall(function() local cc = lighting:FindFirstChild("LightCC")
				if cc then
					if not rainSunSaved then rainSunSaved = {} end
					rainSunSaved.cc = cc
					rainSunSaved.tint = cc.TintColor
					cc.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(205, 205, 212)
				end
			end)
			if not rainSunSaved then rainSunSaved = {} end
			rainSunSaved.raysAll = {}
			pcall(function() for _, r in ipairs(lighting:GetChildren()) do
					if r:IsA("SunRaysEffect") then
						rainSunSaved.raysAll[#rainSunSaved.raysAll + 1] = { obj = r, enabled = r.Enabled }
						r.Enabled = false
					end
				end
			end)
			if not rainSunSaved then rainSunSaved = {} end
			rainSunSaved.ambient = lighting.Ambient
			rainSunSaved.outdoor = lighting.OutdoorAmbient
			rainSunSaved.top = lighting.ColorShift_Top
			rainSunSaved.bottom = lighting.ColorShift_Bottom
			rainSunSaved.envDiff = lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale
			rainSunSaved.expo = lighting.ExposureCompensation
			pcall(function() lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 75)
				lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 115)
				lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 205)
				lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 125)
				lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0.5
				lighting.ExposureCompensation = -0.25
			end)
			rainSunSaved.envSpec = lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale
			pcall(function() lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 1
			end)
		end

		local function removeRainSky()
			local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
			if rainSunSaved then
				pcall(function() if rainSunSaved.skies then
						for _, s in ipairs(rainSunSaved.skies) do
							if s then s.Parent = lighting end
						end
					end
				end)
				pcall(function() if rainSunSaved.cc and rainSunSaved.cc.Parent then
						rainSunSaved.cc.TintColor = rainSunSaved.tint
					end
				end)
				pcall(function() if rainSunSaved.raysAll then
						for _, e in ipairs(rainSunSaved.raysAll) do
							if e.obj and e.obj.Parent then
								e.obj.Enabled = e.enabled
							end
						end
					end
				end)
				pcall(function() if rainSunSaved.ambient and lighting.Ambient == Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 75) then lighting.Ambient = rainSunSaved.ambient end
					if rainSunSaved.outdoor and lighting.OutdoorAmbient == Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 115) then lighting.OutdoorAmbient = rainSunSaved.outdoor end
					if rainSunSaved.top and lighting.ColorShift_Top == Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 205) then lighting.ColorShift_Top = rainSunSaved.top end
					if rainSunSaved.bottom and lighting.ColorShift_Bottom == Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 125) then lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = rainSunSaved.bottom end
					if rainSunSaved.envDiff ~= nil
						and lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale == 0.5 then lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = rainSunSaved.envDiff end
					if rainSunSaved.expo ~= nil
						and lighting.ExposureCompensation == -0.25 then lighting.ExposureCompensation = rainSunSaved.expo end
				end)
				pcall(function() if rainSunSaved.envSpec ~= nil
						and lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale == 1 then lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = rainSunSaved.envSpec end
				end)
				rainSunSaved = nil
			end
			local rs = lighting:FindFirstChild("RainSky")
			if rs then pcall(function() rs:Destroy() end) end
			local rb = lighting:FindFirstChild("RainBloom")
			if rb then pcall(function() rb:Destroy() end) end
			local rc = lighting:FindFirstChild("RainCC")
			if rc then pcall(function() rc:Destroy() end) end
			if rainFogSaved then
				pcall(function() if lighting.FogStart == 0 and lighting.FogEnd == 100000 then
						lighting.FogStart = rainFogSaved.near
						lighting.FogEnd = rainFogSaved.far
					end
				end)
				rainFogSaved = nil
			end
			if rainClockSaved then
				local cc2, br2
				pcall(function() cc2 = lighting.ClockTime
					br2 = lighting.Brightness
				end)
				if cc2 == 0 then
					pcall(function() lighting.ClockTime = rainClockSaved.clock
					end)
				end
				if br2 == 1.5 then
					pcall(function() lighting.Brightness = rainClockSaved.bright
					end)
				end
				rainClockSaved = nil
			end
			if rainAtmoSaved then
				local aobj = rainAtmoSaved.obj
				if aobj and aobj.Parent ~= nil then
					pcall(function() aobj.Density = rainAtmoSaved.Density
						aobj.Offset = rainAtmoSaved.Offset
						aobj.Color = rainAtmoSaved.Color
						aobj.Decay = rainAtmoSaved.Decay
						aobj.Glare = rainAtmoSaved.Glare
						aobj.Haze = rainAtmoSaved.Haze
					end)
				end
				rainAtmoSaved = nil
			else
				local na = lighting:FindFirstChild("RainAtmo")
				if na then pcall(function() na:Destroy() end) end
			end
			if rainCloudsSaved then
				if rainCloudsSaved.enabled ~= nil then
					pcall(function() rainCloudsSaved.obj.CloudsEnabled = rainCloudsSaved.enabled end)
				end
				if rainCloudsSaved.color ~= nil then
					pcall(function() rainCloudsSaved.obj.CloudColor = rainCloudsSaved.color end)
				end
				rainCloudsSaved = nil
			end
		end
		local glossSaved = {}
		local glossDone = {}
		local glossConn = nil
		local glassyGlossOn = false

		local function isGlossable(part)
			if not part:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
			local m = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			if m and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m) then return false end
			return true
		end

		local function glossPart(part)
			if not isGlossable(part) then return end
			if glossDone[part] then return end
			glossDone[part] = true
			glossSaved[#glossSaved + 1] = { part = part, mat = part.Material, ref = part.Reflectance }
			pcall(function() part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
				part.Reflectance = 0.5
			end)
		end

		local function applyGloss()
			for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("BasePart") then glossPart(d) end
			end
			if glossConn then glossConn:Disconnect() end
			glossConn = _G.__MM2_ConnOwn(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN], workspace.DescendantAdded, function(d) if glassyGlossOn then
					task.defer(function() glossPart(d) end)
				end
			end)
		end
		local glossCleanupDone = false

		local function removeGloss()
			if glossConn then glossConn:Disconnect() glossConn = nil end
			for _, e in ipairs(glossSaved) do
				pcall(function() if e.part and e.part.Parent then
						e.part.Material = e.mat
						e.part.Reflectance = e.ref
					end
				end)
			end
			glossSaved = {}
			glossDone = {}
		end
		if not glossCleanupDone then
			pcall(function() _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup or {}
				table.insert(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup, function() pcall(function() removeGloss() end) end)
				glossCleanupDone = true
			end)
		end
		local noRainSoundTrack, noRainSoundKnob, noRainSoundLbl
		local orangeLights = {}
		local orangeLightFix = {}
		local ORANGE_LIGHT = Color3.fromRGB(236, 88, 0)
		local orangeConn = nil
		local orangeDebounce = false
		local orangeRunId = 0
		local greenLights = {}
		local greenLightFix = {}
		local GREEN_LIGHT = Color3.fromRGB(76, 187, 23)
		local greenConn = nil
		local greenDebounce = false
		local greenRunId = 0

		local function attachFixedOrangeLight(l)
			pcall(function() if not l or not l:IsA("Light") then return end
				local already = false
				for _, f in ipairs(orangeLightFix) do
					if f.light == l then already = true break end
				end
				if already then return end
				orangeLightFix[#orangeLightFix + 1] = { light = l, color = l.Color, bright = l.Brightness, enabled = l.Enabled, }
				l.Color = ORANGE_LIGHT
				if type(l.Brightness) == "number" then l.Brightness = math.max(l.Brightness, 3) end
				l.Enabled = true
			end)
		end

		local function attachFixedGreenLight(l)
			pcall(function() if not l or not l:IsA("Light") then return end
				local already = false
				for _, f in ipairs(greenLightFix) do
					if f.light == l then already = true break end
				end
				if already then return end
				greenLightFix[#greenLightFix + 1] = { light = l, color = l.Color, bright = l.Brightness, enabled = l.Enabled, }
				l.Color = GREEN_LIGHT
				if type(l.Brightness) == "number" then l.Brightness = math.max(l.Brightness, 3) end
				l.Enabled = true
			end)
		end

		local function unpaintGreen()
			greenRunId = greenRunId + 1
			if greenConn then greenConn:Disconnect() greenConn = nil end
			for _, l in ipairs(greenLights) do
				pcall(function() if l then l:Destroy() end end)
			end
			greenLights = {}
			for _, f in ipairs(greenLightFix) do
				pcall(function() local l = f.light
					if l and l.Parent then
						l.Color = f.color
						l.Brightness = f.bright
						l.Enabled = f.enabled
					end
				end)
			end
			greenLightFix = {}
			_G.__MM2_GreenSpots = nil
		end

		local function unpaintOrange()
			orangeRunId = orangeRunId + 1
			if orangeConn then orangeConn:Disconnect() orangeConn = nil end
			for _, l in ipairs(orangeLights) do
				pcall(function() if l then l:Destroy() end end)
			end
			orangeLights = {}
			for _, f in ipairs(orangeLightFix) do
				pcall(function() local l = f.light
					if l and l.Parent then
						l.Color = f.color
						l.Brightness = f.bright
						l.Enabled = f.enabled
					end
				end)
			end
			orangeLightFix = {}
			_G.__MM2_Spots = nil
		end
		local spotFaces = { Enum.NormalId.Top, Enum.NormalId.Bottom, Enum.NormalId.Front, Enum.NormalId.Back, Enum.NormalId.Left, Enum.NormalId.Right }

		local function attachFixedGreen(part)
			pcall(function() if part:FindFirstChild("GreenSpot") then return end
				for _, f in ipairs(spotFaces) do
					local sl = __mk("SpotLight", {"Name", "GreenSpot", "Color", GREEN_LIGHT, "Brightness", 3, "Range", 30, "Angle", 90, "Face", f, "Shadows", false, "Parent", part})
					greenLights[#greenLights + 1] = sl
					_G.__MM2_GreenSpots = _G.__MM2_GreenSpots or {}
					table.insert(_G.__MM2_GreenSpots, sl)
				end
			end)
		end

		local function attachFixedOrange(part)
			pcall(function() if part:FindFirstChild("Spot") then return end
				for _, f in ipairs(spotFaces) do
					local sl = __mk("SpotLight", {"Name", "Spot", "Color", Color3.fromRGB(236, 88, 0), "Brightness", 3, "Range", 30, "Angle", 90, "Face", f, "Shadows", false, "Parent", part})
					orangeLights[#orangeLights + 1] = sl
					_G.__MM2_Spots = _G.__MM2_Spots or {}
					table.insert(_G.__MM2_Spots, sl)
				end
			end)
		end

		local greenFixedPaths = { { "Factory", "Base", "Map", 453 }, { "Factory", "Base", "Map", 61, 14 }, { "Factory", "Base", "Map", 1897 }, { "Factory", "Base", "Map", 1886 },
			{ "Hotel", "Base", "Map", "Parts", "Kitchen", "DiningRoom", "Exhaust", 4 }, { "BioLab", "Decoration_Regular", "Tubes", 2, 3 }, }

		local function greenResolveFixed()
			if not rainOn or ded then return end
			for _, path in ipairs(greenFixedPaths) do
				pcall(function() local cur = workspace
					for i = 1, #path do
						local seg = path[i]
						if type(seg) == "number" then
							cur = cur:GetChildren()[seg]
						else
							cur = cur:FindFirstChild(seg)
						end
						if not cur then return end
					end
					if cur and cur:IsA("BasePart") then
						attachFixedGreen(cur)
					elseif cur and cur:IsA("Light") then
						attachFixedGreenLight(cur)
					end
				end)
			end
		end

		local function orangeResolveFixed()
			if not rainOn or ded then return end
			for _, path in ipairs(orangeFixedPaths) do
				pcall(function() local cur = workspace
					for i = 1, #path do
						local seg = path[i]
						if type(seg) == "number" then
							cur = cur:GetChildren()[seg]
						else
							cur = cur:FindFirstChild(seg)
						end
						if not cur then return end
					end
					if cur and cur:IsA("BasePart") then
						attachFixedOrange(cur)
					elseif cur and cur:IsA("Light") then
						attachFixedOrangeLight(cur)
					end
				end)
			end
		end

		local function paintGreen()
			unpaintGreen()
			greenResolveFixed()
			greenConn = _G.__MM2_ConnOwn(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN], workspace.DescendantAdded, function() if not rainOn or ded then return end
				if greenDebounce then return end
				greenDebounce = true
				task.delay(2, function() greenDebounce = false
					greenResolveFixed()
				end)
			end)
			greenRunId = greenRunId + 1
			local myId = greenRunId
			task.spawn(function() while myId == greenRunId and alive() do
					task.wait(10)
					if not rainOn then break end
					greenResolveFixed()
				end
			end)
		end

		local function paintOrange()
			unpaintOrange()
			orangeResolveFixed()
			orangeConn = _G.__MM2_ConnOwn(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN], workspace.DescendantAdded, function() if not rainOn or ded then return end
				if orangeDebounce then return end
				orangeDebounce = true
				task.delay(2, function() orangeDebounce = false
					orangeResolveFixed()
				end)
			end)
			orangeRunId = orangeRunId + 1
			local myId = orangeRunId
			task.spawn(function() while myId == orangeRunId and alive() do
					task.wait(10)
					if not rainOn then break end
					orangeResolveFixed()
				end
			end)
		end
		local rainCleanupDone = false

		local function setRainOn(state)
			if state and skyboxOn then return end
			if state and not rainFogSaved then
				local lg = game:GetService("Lighting")
				rainFogSaved = { near = lg.FogStart, far = lg.FogEnd }
			end
			rainOn = state
			if state then
				rainActive = 2000
				rainActivePrev = 2000
				buildRain3D()
				applyRainSky()
				paintOrange()
				paintGreen()
				startRainSound()
			else
				stopRainSound()
				removeRainSky()
				unpaintOrange()
				unpaintGreen()
				pcall(function() if rain3DFolder then rain3DFolder:Destroy() end end)
				rainBeams = {}
			end
			__knob(rainTrack, rainKnob, state)
			for _, set in ipairs(_G.__MM2_BlockSyncSets or {}) do
				for _, f in pairs(set) do pcall(f) end
			end
			if not rainCleanupDone then
				pcall(function() _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup or {}
					table.insert(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup, function() pcall(function() setRainOn(false) end) end)
					rainCleanupDone = true
				end)
			end
			refreshExclusion()
		end
		_G.__MM2_Tg("rain", setRainOn, function() return rainOn end, rainTrack, rainKnob)
		rainTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if skyboxOn then rainSync() refreshExclusion() return end
			setRainOn(not rainOn)
		end)
		do
			local glassyOn = false
			__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 479), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Glassy effect", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
			local glassyTrack, glassyKnob = __sw(page, 479)

			local function setGlassyOn(state)
				glassyOn = state
				glassyGlossOn = state
				if state then
					applyGloss()
				else
					removeGloss()
				end
				__knob(glassyTrack, glassyKnob, state)
			end
			_G.__MM2_Tg("glassy", setGlassyOn, function() return glassyOn end, glassyTrack, glassyKnob)
			glassyTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setGlassyOn(not glassyOn) end)
		end
		noRainSoundLbl = __mk("TextLabel", {"Name", "NoRainSoundLbl", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 509), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "No rain sound", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		noRainSoundTrack = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 509), "BackgroundColor3", OFF_STATE, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Active", false, "Parent", page})
		__c(noRainSoundTrack, UDim.new(1, 0))
		noRainSoundKnob = __mk("Frame", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), "BackgroundColor3", Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100), "Parent", noRainSoundTrack})
		__c(noRainSoundKnob, UDim.new(1, 0))
		noRainSoundOn = false

		local function setNoRainSound(state)
			if not rainOn then return end
			noRainSoundOn = state
			if rainSound then
				pcall(function() rainSound.Volume = state and 0 or 0.5 end)
			end
			if rainSound2 then
				pcall(function() rainSound2.Volume = state and 0 or 0.5 end)
			end
			__knob(noRainSoundTrack, noRainSoundKnob, state)
		end

		local noRainSync = function(instant)
			local blocked = not rainOn
			if blocked then
				noRainSoundOn = false
				if rainSound then pcall(function() rainSound.Volume = 0.5 end) end
				if rainSound2 then pcall(function() rainSound2.Volume = 0.5 end) end
			end
			__MM2_BlockPaint(noRainSoundTrack, noRainSoundKnob, noRainSoundLbl, blocked, noRainSoundOn, instant)
		end
		_G.__MM2_BlockedSyncs["noRain"] = noRainSync
		_G.__MM2_Tg("noRain", setNoRainSound, function() return noRainSoundOn end, noRainSoundTrack, noRainSoundKnob)
		noRainSoundTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if not rainOn then
				noRainSync()
				return
			end
			setNoRainSound(not noRainSoundOn)
		end)
		noRainSync(true)
	end
	do
		local shadowOn = false
		local shadowSaved = nil

		local function applyShadow()
			local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
			shadowSaved = { Tech = lighting.Technology, Global = lighting.GlobalShadows, Soft = lighting.ShadowSoftness }
			pcall(function() lighting.Technology = Enum.Technology.Future
				lighting.GlobalShadows = true
				lighting.ShadowSoftness = 0
			end)
		end

		local function removeShadow()
			local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
			if shadowSaved then
				pcall(function() lighting.Technology = shadowSaved.Tech
					lighting.GlobalShadows = shadowSaved.Global
					lighting.ShadowSoftness = shadowSaved.Soft
				end)
				shadowSaved = nil
			end
		end
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 539), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Realistic shadow", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local shadowTrack, shadowKnob = __sw(page, 539)

		local function setShadowOn(state)
			shadowOn = state
			if state then
				applyShadow()
			else
				removeShadow()
			end
			__knob(shadowTrack, shadowKnob, state)
		end
		_G.__MM2_Tg("shadow", setShadowOn, function() return shadowOn end, shadowTrack, shadowKnob)
		shadowTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setShadowOn(not shadowOn) end)
	end
	do
		local lightOn = false
		local lightSaved = nil

		local function applyLight()
			local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
			lightSaved = { Tech = lighting.Technology, ambient = lighting.Ambient, outdoor = lighting.OutdoorAmbient, top = lighting.ColorShift_Top, bottom = lighting.ColorShift_Bottom }
			local ct = lighting.ClockTime
			local dayLit = ct >= 6 and ct < 18.5
			pcall(function() if dayLit and not lighting:FindFirstChild("RainSky") then
					lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(105, 105, 110)
					lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 165)
				end
				if dayLit and not lighting:FindFirstChild("Sky") then
					lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 215)
					lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 135)
				end
			end)
			pcall(function() lighting.Technology = Enum.Technology.Future
			end)
			pcall(function() local old = lighting:FindFirstChild("Bloom")
				if old then old:Destroy() end
			end)
			local bloom = __mk("BloomEffect", {"Name", "Bloom", "Intensity", 0.35, "Size", 20, "Threshold", 1})
			pcall(function() bloom.Parent = lighting end)
			pcall(function() local old = lighting:FindFirstChild("LightCC")
				if old then old:Destroy() end
			end)
			local cc = __mk("ColorCorrectionEffect", {"Name", "LightCC", "Contrast", 0.12, "Saturation", 0.08})
			pcall(function() cc.Parent = lighting end)
			if lighting:FindFirstChild("RainSky") then
				pcall(function() cc.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(205, 205, 212) end)
			end
		end

		local function removeLight()
			local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
			for _, n in ipairs({ "Bloom", "LightCC" }) do
				pcall(function() local o = lighting:FindFirstChild(n)
					if o then o:Destroy() end
				end)
			end
			if lightSaved then
				pcall(function() lighting.Technology = lightSaved.Tech
				end)
				pcall(function() if lightSaved.ambient and lighting.Ambient == Color3.fromRGB(105, 105, 110) then lighting.Ambient = lightSaved.ambient end
					if lightSaved.outdoor and lighting.OutdoorAmbient == Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 165) then lighting.OutdoorAmbient = lightSaved.outdoor end
					if lightSaved.top and (lighting.ColorShift_Top == Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 215) or lighting.ColorShift_Top == PURE_WHITE) then lighting.ColorShift_Top = lightSaved.top end
					if lightSaved.bottom and (lighting.ColorShift_Bottom == Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 135) or lighting.ColorShift_Bottom == Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)) then lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = lightSaved.bottom end
				end)
				lightSaved = nil
			end
		end
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 569), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Realistic lighting", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
		local lightTrack, lightKnob = __sw(page, 569)

		local function setLightOn(state)
			lightOn = state
			if state then
				applyLight()
			else
				removeLight()
			end
			__knob(lightTrack, lightKnob, state)
		end
		_G.__MM2_Tg("light", setLightOn, function() return lightOn end, lightTrack, lightKnob)
		lightTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setLightOn(not lightOn) end)
	end
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 599), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Motion blur", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", page})
	local blurTrack, blurKnob = __sw(page, 599)
	local blurOn = false
	local blurAmt, angSmooth, blurVel, blurLast, blurCam, blurEff, blurFade, blurFadeLvl = 0, 0, 0, nil, nil, nil, -1, 0

	local function ensureBlur()
		if not blurEff or not blurEff.Parent then
			local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
			local old = lighting:FindFirstChild("MotionBlur")
			if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end
			blurEff = __mk("DepthOfFieldEffect", {"Name", "MotionBlur", "NearIntensity", 0, "FarIntensity", 0, "FocusDistance", 14, "InFocusRadius", 8, "Parent", lighting})
		end
		return blurEff
	end

	local function setBlurOn(state)
		blurOn = state
		if state then
			ensureBlur()
		else
			blurOn = false
			angSmooth = 0
			blurLast = nil
		end
		__knob(blurTrack, blurKnob, state)
	end
	_G.__MM2_Tg("mblur", setBlurOn, function() return blurOn end, blurTrack, blurKnob)
	blurTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setBlurOn(not blurOn) end)
	_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.RenderStepped, function(rdt)
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local dt = math.min((rdt or 0.016), 0.1)
		if blurOn then
			if not cam then return end
			if cam ~= blurCam then blurCam = cam blurLast = nil angSmooth = 0 blurVel = 0 blurFade = -1 end
			local now = cam.CFrame
			local char = player.Character
			local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local focus = 14
			if root then focus = math.max(2, (cam.CFrame.Position - root.Position).Magnitude) end
			local eff = ensureBlur()
			local base = math.clamp(focus * 0.55, 5, 14)
			eff.FocusDistance = focus
			local ang = 0
			if blurLast and dt > 0 then
				local l0, l1 = blurLast.LookVector, now.LookVector
				local yawd = math.atan2(l0.X, l0.Z) - math.atan2(l1.X, l1.Z)
				if yawd > 3.14159265358979 then yawd = yawd - 6.28318530717959 elseif yawd < -3.14159265358979 then yawd = yawd + 6.28318530717959 end
				yawd = math.abs(yawd) / dt
				if yawd > 35 then yawd = 35 end
				local p0 = math.asin(math.clamp(l0.Y, -1, 1))
				local p1 = math.asin(math.clamp(l1.Y, -1, 1))
				local pitchd = math.abs(p1 - p0) / dt
				if pitchd > 20 then pitchd = 20 end
				ang = yawd * 1.25 + pitchd
			end
			local mv = 0
			if root then
				local spd = 0
				pcall(function() spd = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude end)
				spd = math.clamp(spd / 24, 0, 1)
				mv = spd * spd * (3 - 2 * spd) * 0.3
			end
			angSmooth = angSmooth + (ang - angSmooth) * math.min(1, dt * 8)
			local target = math.clamp((angSmooth - 1.2) / 4.2, 0, 1)
			target = target * target * (3 - 2 * target)
			target = math.clamp(target + mv, 0, 1)
			if blurFade >= 0 then
				if ang > 1.5 or mv > blurAmt + 0.005 then
					blurFade = -1
				elseif blurFade >= 0.7 then
					blurFade = -1
					blurAmt = 0
					blurVel = 0
				else
					blurFade = blurFade + dt
					local kk = math.min(1, blurFade / 0.7)
					local k = kk * kk * (3 - 2 * kk)
					blurAmt = blurFadeLvl * (1 - k)
					blurVel = -blurFadeLvl * 6 * kk * (1 - kk) / 0.7
				end
			end
			if blurFade < 0 then
				if ang < 0.5 and mv < 0.6 * blurAmt and blurAmt > 0.05 then
					blurFade = 0
					blurFadeLvl = blurAmt
				elseif target > blurAmt + 0.005 then
					local steps = dt > 0.05 and math.ceil(dt / 0.05) or 1
					local sdt = dt / steps
					for i = 1, steps do
						blurVel = blurVel + (169 * (target - blurAmt) - 26 * blurVel) * sdt
						blurAmt = blurAmt + blurVel * sdt
						if blurAmt < 0 then blurAmt = 0 blurVel = 0 elseif blurAmt > 1 then blurAmt = 1 blurVel = 0 end
					end
				else
					blurVel = blurVel + (25 * (target - blurAmt) - 10 * blurVel) * dt
					blurAmt = blurAmt + blurVel * dt
					if blurAmt < 0 then blurAmt = 0 blurVel = 0 end
				end
			end
			eff.NearIntensity = blurAmt * 0.32
			eff.FarIntensity = blurAmt * 0.9
			eff.InFocusRadius = base * (1 - 0.55 * blurAmt)
			blurLast = now
		elseif blurEff then
			if blurFade < 0 then
				blurFade = 0
				blurFadeLvl = math.max(blurAmt, 0)
			end
			if blurFade >= 0.7 then
				blurFade = -1
				blurAmt = 0
				blurVel = 0
				pcall(function() blurEff:Destroy() end)
				blurEff = nil
			else
				blurFade = blurFade + dt
				local kk = math.min(1, blurFade / 0.7)
				local k = kk * kk * (3 - 2 * kk)
				blurAmt = blurFadeLvl * (1 - k)
				blurVel = 0
				pcall(function() blurEff.FarIntensity = math.max(blurAmt, 0) * 0.9 blurEff.NearIntensity = math.max(blurAmt, 0) * 0.32 end)
			end
		else
			blurAmt = 0
			blurVel = 0
		end
	end)
end
local mpage = __mk("ScrollingFrame", {"Name", "MainPage", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Visible", true, "CanvasSize", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 800), "ScrollingDirection", Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, "ElasticBehavior", Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never, "ScrollBarThickness", 0, "Parent", inner})
pgs["Main"] = mpage

__mk("TextLabel", {"Name", "PlaceholderHeader", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", "General", "LayoutOrder", 1, "Parent", mpage})
