	gbox.Position = UDim2.new(0, -8, 0, 26)
	gbox.BackgroundColor3 = BG_DARKER
	gbox.BorderSizePixel = 0
	gbox.ZIndex = 0
	gbox.Parent = mpage
	__c(gbox, UDim.new(0, 8))
	__s(gbox, BORDER, 1)
end)()
local skinRow = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "SkinchangerRow", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 39), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "LayoutOrder", 2, "Parent", mpage})
__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Skinchanger", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", skinRow})
local skinTrack = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 0), "BackgroundColor3", OFF_STATE, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", skinRow})
_G.skinTrackCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
_G.skinTrackCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
_G.skinTrackCorner.Parent = skinTrack
local skinKnob = __mk("Frame", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), "BackgroundColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", skinTrack})
_G.skinKnobCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
_G.skinKnobCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
_G.skinKnobCorner.Parent = skinKnob
local skinOn = false
_G.__MM2_SkinRunId = _G.__MM2_SkinRunId or 0
local skinRunId = _G.__MM2_SkinRunId
_G.skinwarn = 0
local skinData = nil
local skinProfile = nil
local skinLisi = nil

local function findmesh(node)
	if not node or type(node) ~= "table" then return nil end
	local props = node.Props
	if props then
		local meshId = props.MeshId or props.MeshID
		if meshId and meshId ~= "" then
			local textureId = props.TextureId or props.TextureID or ""
			local scale = props.Scale or Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
			local size = props.Size or Vector3.new(0.045, 0.045, 0.045)
			local vcol = (typeof(props.VertexColor) == "Vector3") and props.VertexColor or nil
			return { meshid = meshId, textureid = textureId, scale = scale, size = size, vc = vcol }
		end
	end
	if node.Model and type(node.Model) == "table" then
		local resM = findmesh(node.Model)
		if resM then return resM end
	end
	if node.Display and type(node.Display) == "table" then
		for _, child in ipairs(node.Display) do
			local res = findmesh(child)
			if res then return res end
		end
	end
	if node.Children and type(node.Children) == "table" then
		for _, child in ipairs(node.Children) do
			local resC = findmesh(child)
			if resC then return resC end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function lisiskin(name)
	if not skinLisi or type(name) ~= "string" then return nil end
	local best = nil
	local bestSeen = -1
	for _, e in pairs(skinLisi) do
		if type(e) == "table" and type(e.meta) == "table" and e.meta.ItemName == name then
			local s = tonumber(e.seen) or 0
			if s >= bestSeen then bestSeen = s best = e end
		end
	end
	return best
end

local function lisimesh(e)
	if type(e) ~= "table" then return nil end

	local function v3(t)
		if type(t) == "table" and #t >= 3 then return Vector3.new(t[1], t[2], t[3]) end
		return nil
	end

	local function scanNodes(nodes)
		local mesh = nil
		local hsize = nil
		for _, nd in ipairs(type(nodes) == "table" and nodes or {}) do
			if type(nd) == "table" then
				if nd.class == "SpecialMesh" and type(nd.props) == "table" and type(nd.props.MeshId) == "string" and nd.props.MeshId ~= "" then
					mesh = nd.props
				end
				if nd.class == "Part" and nd.name == "Handle" and type(nd.props) == "table" then hsize = v3(nd.props.Size) end
			end
		end
		return mesh, hsize
	end
	local grip = nil
	local t = e.tool
	if type(t) == "table" then
		if type(t.root) == "table" and type(t.root.props) == "table" then
			local g = t.root.props.Grip
			if type(g) == "table" and #g >= 12 then grip = g end
		end
		local mesh, hsize = scanNodes(t.nodes)
		if mesh then
			return { meshid = mesh.MeshId, textureid = mesh.TextureId or mesh.TextureID or "", scale = v3(mesh.Scale) or Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), size = hsize or Vector3.new(0.045, 0.045, 0.045), grip = grip, vc = v3(mesh.VertexColor), nresize = hsize == nil }
		end
	end
	local disp = e.display
	local rsize = nil
	local rroot = type(disp) == "table" and disp.root or nil
	if type(rroot) == "table" and type(rroot.props) == "table" then rsize = v3(rroot.props.Size) end
	if type(disp) == "table" then
		local mesh = scanNodes(disp.nodes)
		if mesh then
			return { meshid = mesh.MeshId, textureid = mesh.TextureId or mesh.TextureID or "", scale = v3(mesh.Scale) or Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), size = rsize or Vector3.new(0.045, 0.045, 0.045), vc = v3(mesh.VertexColor), nresize = true }
		end
	end
	if type(rroot) == "table" and rroot.class == "MeshPart" and type(rroot.props) == "table" and type(rroot.props.MeshId) == "string" and rroot.props.MeshId ~= "" then
		return { meshid = rroot.props.MeshId, textureid = rroot.props.TextureID or rroot.props.TextureId or "", scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), size = rsize or Vector3.new(0.045, 0.045, 0.045), grip = grip }
	end
	return nil
end

local function attachAngles(rec, meta)
	if type(rec) ~= "table" or type(meta) ~= "table" or type(meta.Angles) ~= "table" then return end
	local ax = tonumber(meta.Angles.X) or 0
	local ay = tonumber(meta.Angles.Y) or 0
	local az = tonumber(meta.Angles.Z) or 0
	if ax ~= 0 or ay ~= 0 or az ~= 0 then rec.angles = { ax, ay, az } end
end

local function getskin(name)
	local wd = skinData and skinData[name] or nil
	if wd then
		local direct = findmesh(wd)
		if direct then
			attachAngles(direct, wd.Meta)
			return direct
		end
	end
	if skinData and type(skinData) == "table" then
		for _, v in pairs(skinData) do
			if type(v) == "table" and type(v.Meta) == "table" and v.Meta.ItemName == name then
				local rec = findmesh(v)
				if rec then
					attachAngles(rec, v.Meta)
					return rec
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local le = lisiskin(name)
	local lm = lisimesh(le)
	if lm then return lm end
	return nil
end
local vanPart = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local midDim = {}

local function midDims(mid)
	if type(mid) ~= "string" or mid == "" then return nil end
	local st = midDim[mid]
	if st ~= nil then return (typeof(st) == "Vector3") and st or nil end
	midDim[mid] = "p"
	task.spawn(function()
		local dims = nil
		pcall(function()
			local AS = game:GetService("AssetService")
			local ok, em = pcall(function() return AS:CreateEditableMeshAsync(Content.fromUri(mid)) end)
			if ok and em then
				local okV, vids = pcall(function() return em:GetVertices() end)
				if okV and type(vids) == "table" and #vids > 0 then
					local mn = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
					local mx = Vector3.new(-math.huge, -math.huge, -math.huge)
					local n = #vids
					local stride = (n > 3000) and math.ceil(n / 3000) or 1
					for i = 1, n, stride do
						local okP, pos = pcall(function() return em:GetPosition(vids[i]) end)
						if okP and typeof(pos) == "Vector3" then
							mn = Vector3.new(math.min(mn.X, pos.X), math.min(mn.Y, pos.Y), math.min(mn.Z, pos.Z))
							mx = Vector3.new(math.max(mx.X, pos.X), math.max(mx.Y, pos.Y), math.max(mx.Z, pos.Z))
						end
					end
					if mn.X < math.huge and mn.Y < math.huge and mn.Z < math.huge then dims = mx - mn end
				end
			end
		end)
		midDim[mid] = dims or false
	end)
	return nil
end

local function vansnap(refPart, wd, force)
	local rec = vanPart[refPart]
	if not rec or force then
		rec = { size = refPart.Size, grip = nil, vmesh = nil, vscale = nil, vvc = nil, vtex = nil }
		local vm = refPart:IsA("MeshPart") and refPart.MeshId or nil
		local sm = refPart:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
		if sm and sm.MeshId ~= "" then
			vm = sm.MeshId
			rec.vscale = sm.Scale
			rec.vvc = sm.VertexColor
			rec.vtex = sm.TextureId
		end
		if vm then
			rec.vmesh = vm
			midDims(vm)
		end
		if not vm or not wd or vm ~= wd.meshid then
			local tool = refPart:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool")
			if tool then rec.grip = tool.Grip end
		end
		vanPart[refPart] = rec
	end
	return rec
end

local function skinScale(refPart, wd, noEarly)
	local nd = midDims(wd.meshid)
	local sc = (typeof(wd.scale) == "Vector3") and wd.scale or Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
	if nd and nd.Magnitude > 1e-5 then
		if not noEarly then
			local mag = (nd * sc).Magnitude
			if mag >= 0.5 and mag <= 12 then return sc end
		end
		local van = vansnap(refPart, wd)
		local vmag = nil
		if typeof(van.vscale) == "Vector3" and type(van.vmesh) == "string" and van.vmesh ~= "" then
			local vnd = midDims(van.vmesh)
			if vnd and vnd.Magnitude > 1e-5 then vmag = (vnd * van.vscale).Magnitude end
		end
		if vmag then
			return Vector3.new(vmag / nd.Magnitude, vmag / nd.Magnitude, vmag / nd.Magnitude)
		end
		if not (typeof(wd.scale) == "Vector3") then return Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) end
	end
	return sc
end
_G.__MM2_HandLearn = {}

local function handLearnScan()
	pcall(function()
		local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
		for _, pl in ipairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
			if pl ~= lp then
				local ch = pl.Character
				for _, t in ipairs(ch and ch:GetChildren() or {}) do
					if t:IsA("Tool") then
						local h = t:FindFirstChild("Handle")
						local sm = h and h:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
						if h and sm then
							local midL = sm.MeshId
							if midL and midL ~= "" then
								local keyL = midL:match("(%d+)%s*$") or midL
								if keyL and (h:IsA("BasePart") or h:IsA("MeshPart")) then
									local rec = _G.__MM2_HandLearn[keyL]
									if not h:FindFirstChild("__Painted") and not rec then
										_G.__MM2_HandLearn[keyL] = { grip = t.Grip, scale = sm.Scale, size = h.Size }
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end
_G.__MM2_DispLearn = _G.__MM2_DispLearn or {}

local function dispLearnSeed()
	if _G.__MM2_DispSeedVer == 2 then return end
	pcall(function()
		local force = (rawget(_G, "__MM2_DispSeedVer") ~= 2)

		local function pickAtt(nodes)
			local best = nil
			for _, nd in ipairs(type(nodes) == "table" and nodes or {}) do
				if type(nd) == "table" and nd.class == "Attachment" and type(nd.props) == "table" and type(nd.props.CFrame) == "table" and #nd.props.CFrame >= 12 then
					local pri = nil
					local ndn = nd.name or ""
					if ndn == "CustomAttachment" then pri = 0
					elseif ndn == "Attachment" then pri = 1
					elseif ndn == "Forward" or ndn == "Up" or ndn == "Left" or ndn == "Right" or ndn == "Back" then pri = nil
					else pri = 2 end
					if pri ~= nil and (not best or pri < best[1]) then
						local c = nd.props.CFrame
						best = { pri, CFrame.new(c[1], c[2], c[3], c[4], c[5], c[6], c[7], c[8], c[9], c[10], c[11], c[12]) }
					end
				end
			end
			return best and best[2] or nil
		end
		for _, e in pairs(type(skinLisi) == "table" and skinLisi or {}) do
			if type(e) == "table" and type(e.display) == "table" then
				local rroot = e.display.root
				local mid = type(rroot) == "table" and rroot.MeshId or (type(rroot) == "table" and rroot.props and rroot.props.MeshId) or nil
				local nodes = e.display.nodes
				if not (mid and mid ~= "") then
					for _, nd in ipairs(type(nodes) == "table" and nodes or {}) do
						if type(nd) == "table" and type(nd.props) == "table" then
							local pmd = nd.props.MeshId
							if type(pmd) == "string" and pmd ~= "" then mid = pmd break end
						end
					end
				end
				local attCF = pickAtt(nodes)
				if mid and mid ~= "" and attCF then
					local keyL = mid:match("(%d+)%s*$") or mid
					if keyL then
						if force or not _G.__MM2_DispLearn[keyL] then
							_G.__MM2_DispLearn[keyL] = attCF
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	_G.__MM2_DispSeedVer = 2
end

local function dispLearnScan()
	pcall(function()
		local wdL = workspace:FindFirstChild("WeaponDisplays")
		if not wdL then return end
		local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
		for _, p in ipairs(wdL:GetDescendants()) do
			if p:IsA("BasePart") then
				local sm = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
				local midL = p:IsA("MeshPart") and p.MeshId or (sm and sm.MeshId)
				if midL and midL ~= "" then
					local rc = p:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RigidConstraint")
					local a1L = nil
					for _, cand in ipairs(p:GetChildren()) do
						if cand:IsA("Attachment") then
							if cand.Name == "CustomAttachment" then a1L = cand break
							elseif cand.Name == "Attachment" then
								a1L = a1L or cand
							end
						end
					end
					if rc and a1L and rc.Attachment0 then
						local own = (rc.Attachment0.Parent and rc.Attachment0.Parent.Parent == lp.Character)
						if not own then
							local keyL = midL:match("(%d+)%s*$") or midL
							if keyL and not _G.__MM2_DispLearn[keyL] then
								_G.__MM2_DispLearn[keyL] = a1L.CFrame
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end
local viz = nil
viz = (function()
	local vizConns = _G.__MM2_VizConns or {}
	_G.__MM2_VizConns = vizConns
	local vizStash = _G.__MM2_VizStash or {}
	_G.__MM2_VizStash = vizStash
	local vizWorld = _G.__MM2_VizWorld or {}
	_G.__MM2_VizWorld = vizWorld
	local vizHand = _G.__MM2_VizHand or {}
	_G.__MM2_VizHand = vizHand
	local vizLearn = _G.__MM2_VizLearn or {}
	_G.__MM2_VizLearn = vizLearn
	local vizOwnEq = _G.__MM2_VizOwnEq or { Knife = nil, Gun = nil }
	_G.__MM2_VizOwnEq = vizOwnEq
	local vizCenter = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
	local vizAxisUnits = {
		Gun = { Vector3.new(0, 0, 1), Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0) },
		Knife = { Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0, 1), Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) },
	}
	local vizYaw = {
		Harvester = CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -1.5707963267948966),
		Icepiercer = CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -1.5707963267948966),
	}
	local vizPlain = { Sweet = true, SweetChroma = true }
	local vizShift = { Harvester = 0.5, Icepiercer = 0.5 }
	local vizAxisPose = {
		Gun_ZYX = CFrame.new(0.12991, -3e-05, 0.075, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0.70713, 0.70708, 0, -0.70708, 0.70713),
		Gun_ZXY = CFrame.new(0.12991, 0, 0.07501, 2e-05, -0.5, -0.86603, 1, -4e-05, 5e-05, -6e-05, -0.86603, 0.5),
		Gun_XYZ = CFrame.new(-0.22989, 0.09821, 0.1, 1e-05, 0.98481, -0.17362, -1e-05, 0.17362, 0.98481, 1, -1e-05, 1e-05),
		Knife_YXZ = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, -0.0446, -0.00031, -0.99901, 0.03549, 0.99937, -0.00189, 0.99837, -0.03553, -0.04456),
		Knife_ZYX = CFrame.new(0.00151, -0.12701, -0.15448, -0.99867, 0.03727, 0.03568, -0.04098, -0.15276, -0.98741, -0.03135, -0.98756, 0.15409),
		Knife_ZXY = CFrame.new(0.00151, -0.12701, -0.15448, -0.99867, 0.03727, 0.03568, -0.04098, -0.15276, -0.98741, -0.03135, -0.98756, 0.15409),
	}
	local vizJoin = { WeldConstraint = true, Weld = true, Motor6D = true, RigidConstraint = true, Bone = true, Snap = true, ManualWeld = true, Rotate = true, RotateP = true, RotateV = true }
	local vizFx = { ParticleEmitter = true, Fire = true, Smoke = true, Sparkles = true, PointLight = true, SpotLight = true, SurfaceLight = true }

	local function vizTrim(text)
		if type(text) == "string" then return (text:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")) end
		return text
	end

	local function vizSetProps(inst, props, skip)
		if type(props) ~= "table" then return end
		for k, v in pairs(props) do
			if not (skip and skip[k]) then
				if k == "MeshId" or k == "TextureId" or k == "TextureID" or k == "Texture" then v = vizTrim(v) end
				pcall(function() inst[k] = v end)
			end
		end
	end

	local function vizCreateRoot(props)
		local mid = vizTrim((type(props) == "table" and (props.MeshId or props.MeshID)) or "")
		local size = (type(props) == "table" and typeof(props.Size) == "Vector3") and props.Size or Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
		local mp = nil
		pcall(function()
			mp = game:GetService("InsertService"):CreateMeshPartAsync(mid, Enum.CollisionFidelity.Box, Enum.RenderFidelity.Precise)
		end)
		if mp then
			pcall(function() mp.Size = size end)
			vizSetProps(mp, props, { MeshId = true, MeshID = true, Size = true, TextureID = true, TextureId = true, RelCF = true, CanCollide = true })
			local tx = type(props) == "table" and vizTrim(props.TextureID or props.TextureId or "") or ""
			pcall(function() mp.TextureID = tx end)
			return mp
		end
		local part = Instance.new("Part")
		part.Size = size
		if type(props) == "table" then
			local sc = (typeof(props.Scale) == "Vector3") and props.Scale or nil
			local sm = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
			sm.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
			if mid ~= "" then pcall(function() sm.MeshId = mid end) end
			if sc then pcall(function() sm.Scale = sc end) end
			pcall(function() sm.TextureId = vizTrim(props.TextureID or props.TextureId or "") end)
			sm.Parent = part
		end
		vizSetProps(part, props, { MeshId = true, MeshID = true, Size = true, TextureID = true, TextureId = true, Scale = true, RelCF = true, CanCollide = true })
		return part
	end

	local function vizBuildNode(nd)
		local klass = nd.Class
		if type(klass) ~= "string" then return nil end
		if vizJoin[klass] then return nil end
		local props = type(nd.Props) == "table" and nd.Props or nil
		if klass == "MeshPart" then
			return vizCreateRoot(props)
		elseif klass == "Attachment" or klass == "Decal" or klass == "Texture" or klass == "SpecialMesh" or vizFx[klass] then
			local ok, inst = pcall(Instance.new, klass)
			if not ok or not inst then return nil end
			if props then
				local sc = (klass == "SpecialMesh" and typeof(props.Scale) == "Vector3") and props.Scale or nil
				if sc then pcall(function() inst.Scale = sc end) end
				vizSetProps(inst, props, { RelCF = true, Scale = true })
			end
			return inst
		else
			local ok, inst = pcall(Instance.new, klass)
			if not ok or not inst then return nil end
			vizSetProps(inst, props, { RelCF = true })
			return inst
		end
	end

	local function vizBuildChildren(nd, parent, root, idmap, parts, beams)
		for _, child in ipairs(type(nd.Children) == "table" and nd.Children or {}) do
			local inst = vizBuildNode(child)
			if inst then
				idmap[child.Id] = inst
				if inst:IsA("BasePart") then
					inst.Anchored = false
					inst.CanCollide = false
					inst.CanQuery = false
					inst.CanTouch = false
					inst.Massless = true
					local rc = (child.Props and typeof(child.Props.RelCF) == "CFrame") and child.Props.RelCF or nil
					if rc then pcall(function() inst.CFrame = root.CFrame * rc end) end
					parts[#parts + 1] = { inst = inst, relcf = rc }
					inst.Parent = root
					vizBuildChildren(child, inst, root, idmap, parts, beams)
				elseif inst:IsA("Attachment") then
					if child.Props and typeof(child.Props.RelCF) == "CFrame" then
						pcall(function() inst.CFrame = child.Props.RelCF end)
					end
					inst.Parent = parent
					vizBuildChildren(child, inst, root, idmap, parts, beams)
				else
					inst.Parent = parent
					vizBuildChildren(child, inst, root, idmap, parts, beams)
				end
			else
				vizBuildChildren(child, parent, root, idmap, parts, beams)
			end
		end
	end

	local function vizBuildModel(rec)
		local model = rec.Model
		if not model then return nil end
		local root
		if model.Class == "MeshPart" then
			root = vizCreateRoot(model.Props)
		else
			root = Instance.new("Part")
			vizSetProps(root, model.Props, { RelCF = true, CanCollide = true })
		end
		if model.Name then pcall(function() root.Name = model.Name end) end
		root.Anchored = false
		root.CanCollide = false
		root.CanQuery = false
		root.CanTouch = false
		root.Massless = true
		local idmap = { [model.Id] = root }
		local parts = {}
		local beams = {}
		vizBuildChildren(model, root, root, idmap, parts, beams)
		for _, b in ipairs(beams) do
			if b.a0 and idmap[b.a0] then pcall(function() b.inst.Attachment0 = idmap[b.a0] end) end
			if b.a1 and idmap[b.a1] then pcall(function() b.inst.Attachment1 = idmap[b.a1] end) end
		end
		return { root = root, parts = parts }
	end

	local function vizBuildDisplay(rec)
		local rnode = nil
		local mlist = {}
		local dlist = {}
		local disp = rec.Display or {}
		for _, v in ipairs(disp) do
			if v.Path == "(root)" then
				rnode = v
			elseif v.Class == "SpecialMesh" then
				mlist[#mlist + 1] = v
			elseif v.Class == "Decal" or v.Class == "Texture" then
				dlist[#dlist + 1] = v
			end
		end
		if not rnode then return nil end
		local root
		if rnode.Class == "MeshPart" then
			root = vizCreateRoot(rnode.Props)
		else
			root = Instance.new("Part")
			root.Size = (type(rnode.Props) == "table" and typeof(rnode.Props.Size) == "Vector3") and rnode.Props.Size or Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
			vizSetProps(root, rnode.Props, { MeshId = true, MeshID = true, Size = true, TextureID = true, TextureId = true, RelCF = true })
			for _, smnode in ipairs(mlist) do
				local sm = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
				sm.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
				if type(smnode.Props) == "table" then
					local sc = (typeof(smnode.Props.Scale) == "Vector3") and smnode.Props.Scale or nil
					if sc then pcall(function() sm.Scale = sc end) end
					vizSetProps(sm, smnode.Props, { RelCF = true, Scale = true })
				end
				sm.Parent = root
			end
		end
		root.Anchored = false
		root.CanCollide = false
		root.CanQuery = false
		root.CanTouch = false
		root.Massless = true
		if rnode.Name then pcall(function() root.Name = rnode.Name end) end
		for _, dnode in ipairs(dlist) do
			local ok, decal = pcall(Instance.new, dnode.Class == "Texture" and "Texture" or "Decal")
			if ok and decal then
				vizSetProps(decal, dnode.Props)
				if dnode.Name then pcall(function() decal.Name = dnode.Name end) end
				decal.Parent = root
			end
		end
		return { root = root, parts = {} }
	end

	local function vizBuild(rec)
		if type(rec) ~= "table" then return nil end
		if rec.Model then return vizBuildModel(rec) end
		if rec.Display then return vizBuildDisplay(rec) end
		return nil
	end

	local function vizPoseApply(entry, cfr)
		local root = entry.root
		pcall(function() root.CFrame = cfr end)
		for _, p in ipairs(entry.parts or {}) do
			if p.relcf then pcall(function() p.inst.CFrame = cfr * p.relcf end) end
			local wc = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
			wc.Part0 = p.inst
			wc.Part1 = root
			wc.Parent = p.inst
		end
		return root
	end

	local function vizHandScale(tool, rec, entry)
		local root = entry and entry.root
		if not root then return end
		local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
		if not handle then return end
		local recSize = vizRecSize(rec)
		if not recSize then return end
		local rs = { recSize.X, recSize.Y, recSize.Z }
		table.sort(rs, function(a, b) return a > b end)
		if not (rs[1] and rs[1] > 0) then return end
		local hs = { handle.Size.X, handle.Size.Y, handle.Size.Z }
		table.sort(hs, function(a, b) return a > b end)
		local f = hs[1] / rs[1]
		if not (f > 0) or math.abs(f - 1) < 0.01 then return end
		pcall(function()
			if root:IsA("MeshPart") then
				root.Size = root.Size * f
			else
				local sm = root:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
				if sm then sm.Scale = sm.Scale * f end
			end
		end)
		for _, p in ipairs(entry.parts or {}) do
			if p.relcf then
				local pos = p.relcf.Position
				p.relcf = CFrame.new(pos * f) * (p.relcf - pos)
			end
			if p.inst and p.inst:IsA("MeshPart") then
				pcall(function() p.inst.Size = p.inst.Size * f end)
			elseif p.inst and p.inst:IsA("BasePart") then
				pcall(function()
					local smx = p.inst:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
					if smx then smx.Scale = smx.Scale * f end
				end)
			end
		end
		return f
	end

	local function vizGripForMesh(meshId)
		local id = tostring(meshId or "")
		if id:find("79401392") then
			return CFrame.fromMatrix(Vector3.new(0, -0.699999988, -0.300000012), Vector3.new(1, 0, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0, 1), Vector3.new(0, -1, 0))
		elseif id:find("6600918074") then
			return CFrame.new(1, -0.359999988, 0.00000012, 0, 0, 1, 0, 1, 0, -1, 0, 0)
		end
		return CFrame.fromMatrix(Vector3.new(0, -0.5, 0.7), Vector3.new(1, 0, 0), Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0, 1))
	end
	local vizRecSkin, vizRecSize, vizMeshNative
	local vizFileNative0

	local function vizFileNative0(meshId)
		local nid = tostring(meshId):match("(%d+)%D*$") or tostring(meshId)
		_G.__MM2_FileNative = _G.__MM2_FileNative or {}
		local cached = _G.__MM2_FileNative[nid]
		if typeof(cached) == "Vector3" then return cached end
		if typeof(cached) == "number" and os.clock() - cached < 5 then return nil end
		local okA, part = pcall(function()
			return game:GetService("InsertService"):CreateMeshPartAsync(vizTrim(tostring(meshId)), Enum.CollisionFidelity.Box, Enum.RenderFidelity.Precise)
		end)
		if okA and part and part:IsA("MeshPart") then
			local sz = part.Size
			pcall(function() part:Destroy() end)
			if sz and sz.X > 0 and sz.Y > 0 and sz.Z > 0 then
				_G.__MM2_FileNative[nid] = sz
				return sz
			end
		end
		_G.__MM2_FileNative[nid] = os.clock()
		return nil
	end

	local function vizInplaceHand(tool, skinName, rec)
		local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
		if not handle then return false end
		local recSkin = vizRecSkin(rec)
		if not (recSkin and recSkin.mesh and recSkin.mesh ~= "") then return false end
		local recSize = vizRecSize(rec)
		local native = vizFileNative0(recSkin.mesh)
		local fit = nil
		if native and (native.X > 0 and native.Y > 0 and native.Z > 0) then
			local hb, bb = handle.Size.X, native.X
			if handle.Size.Y > hb then hb = handle.Size.Y end
			if handle.Size.Z > hb then hb = handle.Size.Z end
			if native.Y > bb then bb = native.Y end
			if native.Z > bb then bb = native.Z end
			if hb > 0 and bb > 0.05 then fit = hb / bb end
		end
		if not fit then
			return false
		end
		local appliedScale = Vector3.new(fit, fit, fit)
		local sm = handle:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
		local stash = {
			inst = handle, sm = nil, hadSM = (sm ~= nil),
			mesh = "", tex = "", scale = (sm and sm.Scale) or Vector3.new(1, 1, 1),
			grip = tool.Grip, transp = handle.Transparency
		}
		if sm then stash.mesh = sm.MeshId stash.tex = sm.TextureId end
		pcall(function()
			if not sm then
				sm = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
				sm.Name = "Mesh"
				sm.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
				sm.Parent = handle
			end
			sm.MeshId = recSkin.mesh
			sm.TextureId = recSkin.tex
			sm.Scale = appliedScale
			tool.Grip = vizGripForMesh(recSkin.mesh)
			handle.Transparency = 0
		end)
		if not (sm and sm.MeshId == recSkin.mesh) then
			pcall(function()
				if stash.grip then tool.Grip = stash.grip end
			end)
			return false
		end
		vizHand[tool] = {
			inplace = true, handle = handle, sm = sm,
			stMesh = stash.mesh, stTex = stash.tex, stScale = stash.scale,
			stGrip = stash.grip, stTransp = stash.transp, hadSM = stash.hadSM, skin = skinName
		}
		return true
	end

	local function vizHide(list, inst)
		if vizStash[inst] ~= nil then return end
		if inst:IsA("BasePart") or inst:IsA("Decal") then
			local rec = { inst = inst, prop = "Transparency", val = inst.Transparency }
			list[#list + 1] = rec
			vizStash[inst] = rec
			pcall(function() inst.Transparency = 1 end)
		elseif inst:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or inst:IsA("Trail") or inst:IsA("Beam") or inst:IsA("Fire") or inst:IsA("Smoke") or inst:IsA("Sparkles") then
			local rec = { inst = inst, prop = "Enabled", val = inst.Enabled }
			list[#list + 1] = rec
			vizStash[inst] = rec
			pcall(function() inst.Enabled = false end)
		end
	end

	local function vizRestore(list)
		for _, item in ipairs(list or {}) do
			if vizStash[item.inst] == item then vizStash[item.inst] = nil end
			pcall(function() item.inst[item.prop] = item.val end)
		end
	end

	local function vizGuards(list)
		local out = {}
		for _, item in ipairs(list) do
			local inst = item.inst
			local prop = item.prop
			out[#out + 1] = inst:GetPropertyChangedSignal(prop):Connect(function()
				if prop == "Enabled" then
					if inst.Enabled then pcall(function() inst.Enabled = false end) end
				elseif inst.Transparency ~= 1 then
					pcall(function() inst.Transparency = 1 end)
				end
			end)
		end
		return out
	end

	local function vizUnGuards(list)
		for _, item in ipairs(list or {}) do pcall(function() item:Disconnect() end) end
	end

	local function vizPartMesh(inst)
		if not inst then return "" end
		if inst:IsA("MeshPart") then return vizTrim(inst.MeshId or "") end
		local sm = inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpecialMesh", true)
		if sm then return vizTrim(sm.MeshId or "") end
		return ""
	end

	local function findVizCustomAtt(node)
		if type(node) ~= "table" then return nil end
		for _, child in ipairs(type(node.Children) == "table" and node.Children or {}) do
			if child.Class == "Attachment" and child.Name == "CustomAttachment" and child.Props and typeof(child.Props.RelCF) == "CFrame" then
				return child.Props.RelCF
			end
			local v = findVizCustomAtt(child)
			if v then return v end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function vizRecMesh(rec)
		if type(rec) ~= "table" then return "" end
		if rec.Model then
			local model = rec.Model
			if model.Class == "MeshPart" then
				return vizTrim((type(model.Props) == "table" and (model.Props.MeshId or model.Props.MeshID)) or "")
			end
			for _, child in ipairs(type(model.Children) == "table" and model.Children or {}) do
				if child.Class == "SpecialMesh" then
					return vizTrim((type(child.Props) == "table" and (child.Props.MeshId or child.Props.MeshID)) or "")
				end
			end
			return ""
		end
		local disp = rec.Display or {}
		for _, v in ipairs(disp) do
			if v.Path == "(root)" and v.Class == "MeshPart" then
				return vizTrim((type(v.Props) == "table" and (v.Props.MeshId or v.Props.MeshID)) or "")
			end
			if v.Class == "SpecialMesh" then
				return vizTrim((type(v.Props) == "table" and (v.Props.MeshId or v.Props.MeshID)) or "")
			end
		end
		return ""
	end

	function vizRecSkin(rec)
		if type(rec) ~= "table" then return nil end

		local function grab(props)
			if type(props) ~= "table" then return nil end
			local mesh = props.MeshId or props.MeshID
			if not mesh or mesh == "" then return nil end
			return {
				mesh = mesh,
				tex = (props.TextureId or props.TextureID) or "",
				scale = (typeof(props.Scale) == "Vector3" and props.Scale) or Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
			}
		end
		if rec.Model then
			local m = rec.Model
			local direct = grab(m.Props)
			if direct then return direct end
			for _, child in ipairs(type(m.Children) == "table" and m.Children or {}) do
				if child.Class == "SpecialMesh" then
					local r2 = grab(child.Props)
					if r2 then return r2 end
				end
			end
		end
		local disp = rec.Display or {}
		for _, v in ipairs(disp) do
			if v.Class == "SpecialMesh" then
				local r3 = grab(v.Props)
				if r3 then return r3 end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	function vizRecSize(rec)
		if type(rec) ~= "table" then return nil end
		if rec.Model then
			local m = rec.Model
			return (type(m.Props) == "table" and typeof(m.Props.Size) == "Vector3") and m.Props.Size or nil
		end
		local disp = rec.Display or {}
		for _, v in ipairs(disp) do
			if v.Path == "(root)" then
				return (type(v.Props) == "table" and typeof(v.Props.Size) == "Vector3") and v.Props.Size or nil
			end
		end
		return nil
	end
	local vizMeshNativeCache = _G.__MM2_MeshNative or {}
	_G.__MM2_MeshNative = vizMeshNativeCache

	function vizMeshNative(meshId)
		local raw = tostring(meshId or "")
		local num = raw:match("(%d+)")
		if not num then return nil end
		num = num:gsub("^0+", "")
		local cached = vizMeshNativeCache[num]
		if typeof(cached) == "Vector3" then return cached end
		if vizMeshNativeCache[num] == false then return nil end
		if typeof(request) ~= "function" then vizMeshNativeCache[num] = false return nil end
		local body = nil
		pcall(function()
			local res = request({ Url = "https://assetdelivery.roblox.com/v1/asset/?id=" .. num, Method = "GET" })
			if type(res) == "table" and type(res.Body) == "string" and #res.Body > 40 then body = res.Body end
		end)
		if not body then vizMeshNativeCache[num] = false return nil end
		local size = nil
		pcall(function()
			local npos = body:find("\n", 1, true)
			if not npos then return end
			local major = tonumber(body:sub(1, npos - 1):match("version%s+(%d+)"))
			if major and major >= 4 then
				local okL, vc = pcall(string.unpack, "<I4", body, #body - 3)
				if not (okL and type(vc) == "number") or vc < 2 or vc > 2000000 then return end
				vc = vc + 1
				local faceEnd = #body - (#body % 4)
				local faceStart = faceEnd
				while faceStart - 4 >= 34 do
					local okU, v = pcall(string.unpack, "<I4", body, faceStart - 4)
					if not (okU and v < vc) then break end
					faceStart = faceStart - 4
				end
				if (faceEnd - faceStart) < 12 or ((faceEnd - faceStart) % 12) ~= 0 then return end
				for _, st in ipairs({ 40, 36, 44, 32 }) do
					local start = faceStart - vc * st
					if start >= 34 then
						local mnx, mny, mnz = math.huge, math.huge, math.huge
						local mxx, mxy, mxz = -math.huge, -math.huge, -math.huge
						local good = true
						local step = math.max(1, math.floor(vc / 1500))
						for i = 1, vc, step do
							local base = start + (i - 1) * st
							local okX, x = pcall(string.unpack, "<f", body, base)
							local okY, y = pcall(string.unpack, "<f", body, base + 4)
							local okZ, z = pcall(string.unpack, "<f", body, base + 8)
							if not (okX and okY and okZ) or x ~= x or y ~= y or z ~= z
								or math.abs(x) > 1000 or math.abs(y) > 1000 or math.abs(z) > 1000 then
								good = false break
							end
							if x < mnx then mnx = x end
							if y < mny then mny = y end
							if z < mnz then mnz = z end
							if x > mxx then mxx = x end
							if y > mxy then mxy = y end
							if z > mxz then mxz = z end
						end
						if good and mxx > mnx and mxy > mny and mxz > mnz then
							local bx, by, bz = mxx - mnx, mxy - mny, mxz - mnz
							local big = math.max(bx, by, bz)
							if big >= 0.1 and big <= 5000 then
								size = Vector3.new(bx, by, bz)
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end
			elseif major then
				local mnx, mny, mnz = math.huge, math.huge, math.huge
				local mxx, mxy, mxz = -math.huge, -math.huge, -math.huge
				local n = 0
				for x, y, z in body:gmatch("v%s*%[([%-%d%.eE%+]+),([%-%d%.eE%+]+),([%-%d%.eE%+]+)%]") do
					n = n + 1
					local vx, vy, vz = tonumber(x), tonumber(y), tonumber(z)
					if vx < mnx then mnx = vx end
					if vy < mny then mny = vy end
					if vz < mnz then mnz = vz end
					if vx > mxx then mxx = vx end
					if vy > mxy then mxy = vy end
					if vz > mxz then mxz = vz end
				end
				if n == 0 then
					for x, y, z in body:gmatch("v%s+([%-%d%.eE%+]+)%s+([%-%d%.eE%+]+)%s+([%-%d%.eE%+]+)") do
						n = n + 1
						local vx, vy, vz = tonumber(x), tonumber(y), tonumber(z)
						if vx < mnx then mnx = vx end
						if vy < mny then mny = vy end
						if vz < mnz then mnz = vz end
						if vx > mxx then mxx = vx end
						if vy > mxy then mxy = vy end
						if vz > mxz then mxz = vz end
					end
				end
				if n > 0 and mxx > mnx and mxy > mny and mxz > mnz then
					size = Vector3.new(mxx - mnx, mxy - mny, mxz - mnz)
				end
			end
		end)
		if typeof(size) == "Vector3" then
			vizMeshNativeCache[num] = size
			return size
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function vizAxisOrder(v3)
		if not v3 then return nil end
		local t = { { v3.X, "X" }, { v3.Y, "Y" }, { v3.Z, "Z" } }
		table.sort(t, function(a, b) return a[1] > b[1] end)
		return t[1][2] .. t[2][2] .. t[3][2]
	end

	local function vizHandMount(tool, rec, typ)
		if vizPlain[tool] then return CFrame.identity end
		local size = vizRecSize(rec)
		local units = vizAxisUnits[typ]
		if not (size and units) then return CFrame.identity end
		local t = { { size.X, "X" }, { size.Y, "Y" }, { size.Z, "Z" } }
		table.sort(t, function(a, b) return a[1] > b[1] end)
		local big = t[1][2]
		local mid = t[2][2]
		local small = t[3][2]
		local vim = { X = vizCenter, Y = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), Z = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1) }
		local map = { [big] = units[1], [mid] = units[2], [small] = units[3] }
		if vim[big]:Cross(vim[mid]):Dot(vim[small]) < 0 then
			map[small] = -map[small]
		end
		local x = map.X
		local y = map.Y
		local z = map.Z
		local grip = nil
		pcall(function() grip = tool.Grip end)
		local rot = (typeof(grip) == "CFrame") and grip.Rotation or CFrame.identity
		local out = rot * CFrame.fromMatrix(Vector3.new(), x, y, z)
		local yaw = vizYaw[tool]
		if yaw then out = out * yaw end
		local shift = vizShift[tool]
		if shift then out = out * CFrame.new(vim[big] * shift) end
		return out
	end

	local function vizLearnFrom(part)
		if typeof(part) ~= "Instance" then return end
		local mid = vizPartMesh(part)
		if mid == "" then return end
		if vizLearn[mid] then return end
		local att = part:FindFirstChild("CustomAttachment") or part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Attachment")
		if att and typeof(att.CFrame) == "CFrame" then
			vizLearn[mid] = att.CFrame
		end
	end

	local function vizMidKey(mid)
		if type(mid) ~= "string" then return nil end
		return mid:match("(%d+)%s*$") or mid
	end

	local function vizWorldPose(refPart, rec)
		local cp = refPart.CFrame
		local att = refPart:FindFirstChild("CustomAttachment") or refPart:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Attachment")
		if att and typeof(att.CFrame) == "CFrame" then cp = cp * att.CFrame end
		local T = nil
		if rec and rec.Model then T = findVizCustomAtt(rec.Model) end
		if not T then
			local mkey = vizMidKey(vizRecMesh(rec))
			if mkey and type(_G.__MM2_DispLearn) == "table" then
				local dv = _G.__MM2_DispLearn[mkey]
				if typeof(dv) == "CFrame" then T = dv end
			end
		end
		if not T then
			local smkey = vizRecMesh(rec)
			if smkey and smkey ~= "" then T = vizLearn[smkey] end
		end
		if not T then
			local ord = vizAxisOrder(vizRecSize(rec))
			T = ord and vizAxisPose[((rec.Meta and rec.Meta.ItemType or "") .. "_" .. ord)] or nil
		end
		if T then cp = cp * T:Inverse() end
		return cp
	end

	local function vizUnhideEntry(entry)
		if not entry then return end
		if entry.guards then vizUnGuards(entry.guards) end
		vizRestore(entry.hidden)
		if entry.overlay then pcall(function() entry.overlay:Destroy() end) end
	end

	local function vizShowWorld(typ, skinName)
		local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
		local ch = localPlayer and localPlayer.Character
		if not ch then return end
		local old = vizWorld[typ]
		local cur = old and old.skin or nil
		if cur == skinName and skinName ~= nil and old and old.overlay and typeof(old.overlay) == "Instance" and old.overlay.Parent then return end
		vizWorld[typ] = nil
		if old then vizUnhideEntry(old) end
		if not skinName or skinName == "" then return end
		local rec = skinData and skinData[skinName] or nil
		if type(rec) ~= "table" then return end
		local ref = nil
		pcall(function()
			local dr = ch:FindFirstChild("DisplayRef" .. typ)
			ref = dr and dr.Value or nil
		end)
		if typeof(ref) ~= "Instance" then return end
		local nat = vizPartMesh(ref)
		local skin = vizRecMesh(rec)
		if nat ~= "" and skin ~= "" and nat == skin then return end
		local entry = vizBuild(rec)
		if not entry or not entry.root or not ref.Parent then
			if entry and entry.root then pcall(function() entry.root:Destroy() end) end
			return
		end
		vizLearnFrom(ref)
		local hidden = {}
		vizHide(hidden, ref)
		for _, d in ipairs(ref:GetDescendants() or {}) do vizHide(hidden, d) end
		local guards = vizGuards(hidden)
		vizPoseApply(entry, vizWorldPose(ref, rec))
		local root = entry.root
		root:SetAttribute("MM2VizOverlay", true)
		root.Parent = ref.Parent
		local wc = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
		wc.Part0 = root
		wc.Part1 = ref
		wc.Parent = root
		vizWorld[typ] = { overlay = root, hidden = hidden, guards = guards, skin = skinName }
	end

	local function vizUndoHand(tool)
		local entry = vizHand[tool]
		if not entry then return end
		vizHand[tool] = nil
		if entry.inplace then
			pcall(function()
				if entry.mp and entry.mp.Parent then entry.mp:Destroy() end
				local sm = entry.sm
				if sm and sm.Parent then
					if entry.stMesh ~= nil then sm.MeshId = entry.stMesh end
					if entry.stTex ~= nil then sm.TextureId = entry.stTex end
					if typeof(entry.stScale) == "Vector3" then sm.Scale = entry.stScale end
				end
				if not entry.hadSM and sm then sm:Destroy() end
				if typeof(entry.stGrip) == "CFrame" then tool.Grip = entry.stGrip end
				if entry.handle and entry.handle.Parent and type(entry.stTransp) == "number" then
					entry.handle.Transparency = entry.stTransp
				end
			end)
			return
		end
		vizUnhideEntry(entry)
	end

	local function vizShowHand(tool, typ)
		local gunInplace = (typ == "Gun")
		if vizHand[tool] then
			if vizHand[tool].skin == vizOwnEq[typ] then return end
			vizUndoHand(tool)
		end
		local skinName = vizOwnEq[typ]
		if not skinName or skinName == "" then
			if gunInplace and vizHand[tool] and vizHand[tool].inplace then vizUndoHand(tool) end
			return
		end
		local rec = skinData and skinData[skinName] or nil
		if type(rec) ~= "table" then return end
		local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
		if not handle then return end
		if gunInplace then
			pcall(function() vizInplaceHand(tool, skinName, rec) end)
			return
		end
		vizLearnFrom(handle)
		local entry = vizBuild(rec)
		if not (entry and entry.root) then
			if entry and entry.root then pcall(function() entry.root:Destroy() end) end
			return
		end
		pcall(function() vizHandScale(tool, rec, entry) end)
		local hidden = {}
		vizHide(hidden, handle)
		for _, d in ipairs(handle:GetDescendants() or {}) do vizHide(hidden, d) end
		local guards = vizGuards(hidden)
		vizPoseApply(entry, handle.CFrame * vizHandMount(tool, rec, typ))
		local root = entry.root
		root.Parent = handle
		local wc = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
		wc.Part0 = root
		wc.Part1 = handle
		wc.Parent = root
		root:SetAttribute("MM2VizOverlay", true)
		vizHand[tool] = { overlay = root, hidden = hidden, guards = guards, skin = skinName }
	end

	local function vizDestroyAll()
		pcall(function()
			for _, typ in ipairs({ "Knife", "Gun" }) do
				local old = vizWorld[typ]
				if old then vizWorld[typ] = nil vizUnhideEntry(old) end
			end
			for tool, entry in pairs(vizHand) do
				vizUnhideEntry(entry)
				vizHand[tool] = nil
			end
		end)
	end
	pcall(function()
		local prev = _G.__MM2_VizDestroy
		if typeof(prev) == "function" then prev() end
	end)
	_G.__MM2_VizDestroy = vizDestroyAll

	local function canApplyOn(tool, lp)
		pcall(function()
			return true
		end)
		if tool:IsDescendantOf(lp) then return true end
		local ch = lp.Character
		return ch ~= nil and tool:IsDescendantOf(ch)
	end

	local function vizCollectHelpers(collectionService, typ)
		local list = {}
		local tagName = (typ == "Gun") and "Weapon_Gun" or "Weapon_Knife"
		pcall(function()
			for _, t in ipairs(collectionService:GetTagged(tagName)) do
				list[#list + 1] = t
			end
		end)
		return list
	end
	local vizWorn = { Knife = nil, Gun = nil }

	local function wearskinViz(chS)
		if not skinProfile or not skinData then return end
		local eq = skinProfile.Weapons and skinProfile.Weapons.Equipped or nil
		if not eq then return end
		pcall(function()
			local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
			for _, typ in ipairs({ "Knife", "Gun" }) do
				local skinName = eq[typ]
				vizOwnEq[typ] = skinName
				for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					local ch2 = plr.Character
					if ch2 then
						for _, drn in ipairs({ "DisplayRefKnife", "DisplayRefGun" }) do
							local dr = ch2:FindFirstChild(drn)
							vizLearnFrom(dr and dr.Value or nil)
						end
						for _, t2 in ipairs(ch2:GetChildren()) do
							if t2:IsA("Tool") and t2 ~= nil then
								local h2 = t2:FindFirstChild("Handle")
								if h2 then vizLearnFrom(h2) end
							end
						end
					end
				end
				if skinName ~= vizWorn[typ] then
					vizWorn[typ] = skinName
				end
				vizShowWorld(typ, skinName)
			end
			local cs = game:GetService("CollectionService")
			for _, typ in ipairs({ "Knife", "Gun" }) do
				for _, tool in ipairs(vizCollectHelpers(cs, typ)) do
					local heldOnly = lp.Character and tool.Parent == lp.Character
					if not (canApplyOn(tool, lp) and heldOnly) then
						vizUndoHand(tool)
					else
						if vizOwnEq[typ] then
							vizShowHand(tool, typ)
						else
							vizUndoHand(tool)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	_G.__MM2_VizWarm = function(names)
		task.spawn(function()
			local list = (type(names) == "table") and names or {}
			for _, nm in ipairs(list) do
				local recW = skinData and skinData[nm] or nil
				local skW = vizRecSkin(recW)
				if skW and skW.mesh and skW.mesh ~= "" then
					pcall(function() vizFileNative0(skW.mesh) end)
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	return { apply = wearskinViz, destroy = vizDestroyAll }
end)()

local function ismurd()
	local yes = false
	pcall(function() local lpS = Players.LocalPlayer
		local chS = lpS and lpS.Character
		if chS then
			for _, v in ipairs(chS:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Tool") and v:GetAttribute("ItemType") == "Knife" then yes = true return end
			end
		end
		local bpS = lpS and lpS:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		if bpS then
			for _, v in ipairs(bpS:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Tool") and v:GetAttribute("ItemType") == "Knife" then yes = true return end
			end
		end
	end)
	return yes
end
_G.__MM2_KnifeTwist = _G.__MM2_KnifeTwist or 0

local function wearskin(chS)
	pcall(function() if skinOn and viz and viz.apply then viz.apply() end end)
end
pcall(function() if _G.__skinstuck then _G.__skinstuck:Disconnect() end end)
local __attachSkinEquip
__attachSkinEquip = function(chC)
	pcall(function()
		if typeof(chC) ~= "Instance" then return end
		_G.__MM2_UIS(chC.ChildAdded, function(c)
			if not alive() or not skinOn then return end
			if c:IsA("Tool") then
				task.delay(0.05, function() if alive() and skinOn and viz and viz.apply then pcall(viz.apply) end end)
				task.delay(0.35, function() if alive() and skinOn and viz and viz.apply then pcall(viz.apply) end end)
			end
		end)
	end)
end
_G.__MM2_Conn(player.CharacterAdded, function(chAdd) __attachSkinEquip(chAdd) end)
__attachSkinEquip(player.Character)
_G.__skinstuck = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch) if not skinOn or not alive() then return end
	if not (ch and ch:IsA("BasePart") and ch.Name == "StuckKnife") then return end
	pcall(function() local mesh = ch:WaitForChild("Mesh", 3)
		if not mesh then return end
		if not skinProfile or not skinData then return end
		local eqK = skinProfile.Weapons and skinProfile.Weapons.Equipped and skinProfile.Weapons.Equipped.Knife or nil
		if not eqK then return end
		local kd = getskin(eqK)
		if kd and kd.meshid and ismurd() then
			local applyAt = function()
				pcall(function()
					local mesh2 = ch:FindFirstChild("Mesh")
					if not mesh2 then return end
					local scS = skinScale(ch, kd, true)
					if typeof(scS) ~= "Vector3" or not (scS.X > 0 and scS.Y > 0 and scS.Z > 0) then return end
					if mesh2.MeshId ~= kd.meshid then
						mesh2.MeshId = kd.meshid
						mesh2.TextureId = kd.textureid
					end
					mesh2.Scale = scS
				end)
			end
			local stD = midDim[kd.meshid]
			if typeof(stD) == "Vector3" then
				applyAt()
			else
				midDims(kd.meshid)
				task.spawn(function()
					local t0 = os.clock()
					while mesh.Parent and os.clock() - t0 < 2.5 and alive() do
						task.wait(0.1)
						local stC = midDim[kd.meshid]
						if stC == false then return end
						if typeof(stC) == "Vector3" then applyAt() return end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end)
pcall(function() if _G.__MM2_throwfix then _G.__MM2_throwfix:Disconnect() end end)
_G.__MM2_throwfix = game:GetService("CollectionService"):GetInstanceAddedSignal("ThrowingKnife"):Connect(function(tk) if not skinOn or not alive() or ded then return end
	task.spawn(function() pcall(function()
			local lp = Players.LocalPlayer

			local function stripClones()
				for _, p in ipairs(tk:GetDescendants()) do
					if p:IsA("BasePart") and (p.Name ~= "KnifeVisual") and (p:GetAttribute("MM2VizOverlay") or p.Name == "KnifeDisplay") then pcall(function() p:Destroy() end) end
				end
			end
			stripClones()
			task.spawn(function()
				local t0s = os.clock()
				while tk.Parent and alive() and os.clock() - t0s < 1.5 do
					task.wait(0.08)
					stripClones()
				end
			end)
			local linkTool = nil
			for _ = 1, 12 do
				if not tk.Parent then return end
				local hlV2 = tk:FindFirstChild("HandleLink")
				local hv2 = hlV2 and hlV2.Value
				if hv2 then
					local anT = hv2:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool")
					local bp2 = lp and lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
					local okO = anT and ((player.Character and anT:IsDescendantOf(player.Character)) or (bp2 and anT:IsDescendantOf(bp2)))
					if okO then linkTool = hv2 break end
				end
				task.wait(0.05)
			end
			if not linkTool then return end
			if not skinProfile or not skinData then return end
			local eqK = skinProfile.Weapons and skinProfile.Weapons.Equipped and skinProfile.Weapons.Equipped.Knife or nil
			if not eqK then return end
			local kd = getskin(eqK)
			if not (kd and kd.meshid) then return end
			local kv = tk:FindFirstChild("KnifeVisual")
			if not kv then return end

			local function apply2()
				pcall(function()
					local m2 = kv:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")
					if not m2 then return end
					local scS = skinScale(kv, kd, true)
					if typeof(scS) ~= "Vector3" or not (scS.X > 0 and scS.Y > 0 and scS.Z > 0) then return end
					m2.MeshId = kd.meshid
					m2.TextureId = kd.textureid
					m2.Scale = scS
				end)
			end
			local stD2 = midDim[kd.meshid]
			if typeof(stD2) == "Vector3" then apply2() else
				midDims(kd.meshid)
				task.spawn(function()
					local t0 = os.clock()
					while kv.Parent and os.clock() - t0 < 2.5 and alive() do
						task.wait(0.1)
						local stC = midDim[kd.meshid]
						if stC == false then return end
						if typeof(stC) == "Vector3" then apply2() return end
					end
				end)
			end
		end) end)
end)
_G.__pops = _G.__pops or {}
_G.__pstack = _G.__pstack or function()
	for i = #_G.__pops, 1, -1 do
		local f = _G.__pops[i]
		if not (f and f.Parent) then table.remove(_G.__pops, i) end
	end
	local n = #_G.__pops
	for idx, f in ipairs(_G.__pops) do
		pcall(function() TweenService:Create(f, TWEEN_POP, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -230, 1, -300 - (n - idx) * 64) }):Play()
		end)
	end
	pcall(function() local intro = sgui:FindFirstChild("AeonHubIntro")
		if intro then
			TweenService:Create(intro, TWEEN_POP, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -230, 1, -300 - n * 64) }):Play()
		end
	end)
end

local function showToast(title, sub, cname)
	pcall(function() for _, o in ipairs(sgui:GetChildren()) do
			if o.Name == cname then pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
		end
		local lightPopup = (THEMES[selectedTheme] and THEMES[selectedTheme].light)
		local f = __mk("Frame", {"Name", cname, "Size", UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 54), "Position", UDim2.new(1, 10, 1, -300), "BackgroundColor3", lightPopup and C_L_WHITE or Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 10), "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ZIndex", 20, "ClipsDescendants", true, "Parent", sgui})
		table.insert(_G.__pops, f)
		pcall(function() if _G.__pstack then _G.__pstack() end end)
		__c(f, UDim.new(0, 12))
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 16), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 12), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "TextColor3", __MM2_SecTxt(), "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", title, "ZIndex", 21, "Parent", f})
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 27), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "TextColor3", acc, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 16, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", sub, "ZIndex", 21, "Parent", f})
		local x = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 32), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -40, 0.5, -16), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "AutoButtonColor", false, "Text", ">", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 16, "TextColor3", __MM2_SecTxt(), "ZIndex", 21, "Parent", f})
		local gone = false

		local function hide()
			if gone then return end
			gone = true
			local cy = f.Position.Y.Offset
			local tw = TweenService:Create(f, TweenInfo.new(0.4, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.In), { Position = UDim2.new(1, 10, 1, cy) })
			tw:Play()
			tw.Completed:Connect(function() pcall(function() f:Destroy() end)
				pcall(function() for i = #_G.__pops, 1, -1 do
						if _G.__pops[i] == f then table.remove(_G.__pops, i) end
					end
					if _G.__pstack then _G.__pstack() end
				end)
			end)
		end
		x.MouseButton1Click:Connect(hide)
		TweenService:Create(f, TweenInfo.new(0.4, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = UDim2.new(1, -230, 1, -300) }):Play()
		task.delay(2.5, hide)
	end)
end
_G.__MM2_Toast = showToast

local function skinInvalidPopup()
	showToast("Skinchanger", "Invalid action", "SkinWarn")
end

local function killToast(msg)
	showToast("Kill", msg, "KillWarn")
end

local function setSkinOn(state)
	if not state and skinOn then
		if os.clock() - _G.skinwarn > 2 then
			_G.skinwarn = os.clock()
			task.spawn(skinInvalidPopup)
		end
		return
	end
	skinOn = state
	pcall(function() TweenService:Create(skinTrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(true) }):Play() end)
	pcall(function() TweenService:Create(skinKnob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(true) }):Play() end)
	if state then
		skinRunId = (typeof(_G.__MM2_SkinRunId) == "number" and _G.__MM2_SkinRunId or skinRunId or 0) + 1
		_G.__MM2_SkinRunId = skinRunId
		local myId = skinRunId
		task.spawn(function() if not skinData then
				pcall(function() if typeof(isfile) == "function" and typeof(loadfile) == "function" and isfile("AeonHub/AeonHubBin/mm2data_kroxn.lua") then
						local okL, chunk = pcall(loadfile, "AeonHub/AeonHubBin/mm2data_kroxn.lua")
						if okL and chunk then
							local okC, tbl = false, nil
							if okL and chunk then okC, tbl = pcall(chunk) end
							if (not okC or type(tbl) ~= "table") and typeof(readfile) == "function" and typeof(loadstring) == "function" then
								local okR, src = pcall(readfile, "AeonHub/AeonHubBin/mm2data_kroxn.lua")
								if okR and type(src) == "string" and #src > 100000 then
									local okL2, chunk2 = pcall(loadstring, src .. "\nreturn MESHES_FULL")
									if okL2 and chunk2 then okC, tbl = pcall(chunk2) end
								end
							end
							if okC and type(tbl) == "table" then skinData = tbl end
						end
					end
				end)
				if not skinData then
					pcall(function() if typeof(request) == "function"
							and typeof(writefile) == "function"
							and (typeof(loadfile) == "function" or typeof(loadstring) == "function") then
							local res = request({ Url = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kroxn/visual/refs/heads/main/weapon.lua", Method = "GET" })
							if type(res) == "table" and res.Success and type(res.Body) == "string" and #res.Body > 100000 then
								writefile("AeonHub/AeonHubBin/mm2data_kroxn.lua", res.Body)
								local okL, chunk = false, nil
								if typeof(loadfile) == "function" then okL, chunk = pcall(loadfile, "AeonHub/AeonHubBin/mm2data_kroxn.lua") end
								local okC, tbl = false, nil
								if okL and chunk then okC, tbl = pcall(chunk) end
								if (not okC or type(tbl) ~= "table") and typeof(loadstring) == "function" then
									local okL2, chunk2 = pcall(loadstring, res.Body .. "\nreturn MESHES_FULL")
									if okL2 and chunk2 then okC, tbl = pcall(chunk2) end
								end
								if okC and type(tbl) == "table" then skinData = tbl end
							end
						end
					end)
				end
				if not skinLisi then
					pcall(function() if typeof(readfile) == "function" and typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile("AeonHub/AeonHubBin/mm2skin_lisi.json") then
							local okD, d = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(readfile("AeonHub/AeonHubBin/mm2skin_lisi.json")) end)
							if okD and type(d) == "table" and type(d.entries) == "table" then skinLisi = d.entries end
						end
					end)
				end
				if not skinLisi then
					pcall(function() if typeof(request) == "function" and typeof(writefile) == "function" then
							local res = request({ Url = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Lisi-prime/mm2_skin/refs/heads/main/skins.json", Method = "GET" })
							if type(res) == "table" and res.Success and type(res.Body) == "string" and #res.Body > 10000 then
								local okD, d = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(res.Body) end)
								if okD and type(d) == "table" and type(d.entries) == "table" then
									skinLisi = d.entries
									pcall(writefile, "AeonHub/AeonHubBin/mm2skin_lisi.json", res.Body)
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end
				pcall(function()
					local mmSrc = nil
					if typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile("AeonHub/AeonHubBin/mm2_mesh_data.lua") and typeof(readfile) == "function" then
						local okR, src = pcall(readfile, "AeonHub/AeonHubBin/mm2_mesh_data.lua")
						if okR and type(src) == "string" and #src > 100000 then mmSrc = src end
					end
					local mm = nil

					local function tryLoad(src)
						local lsx = (typeof(loadstring) == "function") and loadstring or (typeof(load) == "function") and load
						if not lsx then return nil end
						local okL1, c1 = pcall(lsx, src)
						if okL1 and type(c1) == "function" then
							local okC1, t1 = pcall(c1)
							if okC1 and type(t1) == "table" then return t1 end
						end
						local okL2, c2 = pcall(lsx, src .. "\nreturn MESHES")
						if okL2 and type(c2) == "function" then
							local okC2, t2 = pcall(c2)
							if okC2 and type(t2) == "table" then return t2 end
						end
						local okL3, c3 = pcall(lsx, src .. "\nreturn MESHES_FULL")
						if okL3 and type(c3) == "function" then
							local okC3, t3 = pcall(c3)
							if okC3 and type(t3) == "table" then return t3 end
						end
						return nil
					end
					if mmSrc then mm = tryLoad(mmSrc) end
					if not mm and typeof(request) == "function" and typeof(writefile) == "function" then
						local res = request({ Url = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Mm2script-maker/mm2-mesh-data/main/mm2_meshes_data.lua", Method = "GET" })
						if type(res) == "table" and res.Success and type(res.Body) == "string" and #res.Body > 100000 then
							mm = tryLoad(res.Body)
							if mm then pcall(writefile, "AeonHub/AeonHubBin/mm2_mesh_data.lua", res.Body) end
						end
					end
					if mm and type(mm) == "table" then
						if type(skinData) ~= "table" then skinData = mm
						else for k, v in pairs(mm) do if skinData[k] == nil then skinData[k] = v end end end
					end
				end)
			end
			if not skinProfile then
				pcall(function() skinProfile = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.ProfileData) end)
			end
			if not (skinData or skinLisi) or not skinProfile then
				skinRunId = (typeof(_G.__MM2_SkinRunId) == "number" and _G.__MM2_SkinRunId or skinRunId or 0) + 1
				_G.__MM2_SkinRunId = skinRunId
				skinOn = false
				pcall(function() TweenService:Create(skinTrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(false) }):Play() end)
				pcall(function() TweenService:Create(skinKnob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(false) }):Play() end)
				return
			end
			pcall(function() local Sync = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Database.Sync)
				for name, itemData in pairs(Sync.Weapons) do
					itemData.SortWithinGroup = itemData.SortWithinGroup or 0
					itemData.SortGroup = itemData.SortGroup or nil
					itemData.Name = itemData.Name or itemData.ItemName or name
					itemData.Rarity = itemData.Rarity or "Common"
					if Sync.Rarities[itemData.Rarity] then
						local wi = getskin(name)
						if not wi then wi = getskin(itemData.Name) end
						if not wi then wi = getskin(itemData.ItemName) end
						if wi and wi.meshid and wi.textureid and wi.scale and wi.size then
							skinProfile.Weapons.Owned[name] = 1
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			pcall(function() if typeof(filtergc) ~= "function" or typeof(getgc) ~= "function" then return end
				if typeof(islclosure) ~= "function" then return end
				if type(debug) ~= "table" then return end
				if typeof(debug.info) ~= "function"
					or typeof(debug.getupvalues) ~= "function"
					or typeof(debug.getupvalue) ~= "function" then return end
				local InventoryModule = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InventoryModule)
				local gcT = filtergc("table", { Keys = { "UpdateInventory" } }, true)
				local UpdateInventory = gcT and gcT.UpdateInventory or nil
				if typeof(UpdateInventory) ~= "function" then return end
				for _, func in pairs(getgc()) do
					if typeof(func) == "function" and islclosure(func) and debug.info(func, "l") == 122 and #debug.getupvalues(func) == 2 then
						UpdateInventory(debug.getupvalue(func, 2), InventoryModule.MyInventory)
					end
				end
			end)
			pcall(function() if _G.__MM2_VizWarm and skinProfile and skinProfile.Weapons and skinProfile.Weapons.Equipped then
					_G.__MM2_VizWarm({ skinProfile.Weapons.Equipped.Gun, skinProfile.Weapons.Equipped.Knife })
				end end)
			while myId == (typeof(_G.__MM2_SkinRunId) == "number" and _G.__MM2_SkinRunId or myId) and alive() do
				if not skinOn then break end
				dispLearnScan()
				handLearnScan()
				dispLearnSeed()
				pcall(function() local lpS = Players.LocalPlayer
					local chS = lpS and lpS.Character
					if chS then wearskin(chS) end
				end)
				task.wait(0.25)
			end
		end)
	else
		skinRunId = (typeof(_G.__MM2_SkinRunId) == "number" and _G.__MM2_SkinRunId or skinRunId or 0) + 1
		_G.__MM2_SkinRunId = skinRunId
	end
end
_G.__MM2_Tg("skin", setSkinOn, function() return skinOn end, skinTrack, skinKnob)
skinTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setSkinOn(not skinOn) end)
pcall(function() if skinOn then setSkinOn(skinOn) end end)
local brow = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "BringGunRow", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 69), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "LayoutOrder", 3, "Parent", mpage})
__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Bring gun", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", brow})
local btrack = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 0), "BackgroundColor3", OFF_STATE, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", brow})

_G.bringTrackCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
_G.bringTrackCorner.Parent = btrack
local bknob = __mk("Frame", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), "BackgroundColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", btrack})
_G.bringKnobCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
_G.bringKnobCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
_G.bringKnobCorner.Parent = bknob
_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].bon = false
local bringRunId = 0

local function btoggle(state)
	_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].bon = state
	if state then
		TweenService:Create(btrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(true) }):Play()
		TweenService:Create(bknob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(true) }):Play()
		bringRunId = bringRunId + 1
		local myId = bringRunId
		task.spawn(function() while _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN] and _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].bon and myId == bringRunId and alive() and not ded do
				pcall(function() local lpG = Players.LocalPlayer
					local chG = lpG and lpG.Character
					local humG = chG and chG:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					local hrpG = (humG and humG.RootPart) or (chG and chG:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
					if not hrpG then return end
					local gun = workspace:FindFirstChild("GunDrop", true)
					if not gun or not gun.Parent then return end
					local gp = nil
					if gun:IsA("BasePart") then gp = gun
					else pcall(function() gp = gun:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) end) end
					if not gp then return end
					if typeof(firetouchinterest) == "function" then
						pcall(function() firetouchinterest(hrpG, gp, 0) end)
						pcall(function() firetouchinterest(hrpG, gp, 1) end)
					else
						bringRunId = bringRunId + 1
						pcall(function() if gun:IsA("BasePart") then gun.CFrame = hrpG.CFrame
							elseif gun:IsA("Model") then gun:PivotTo(hrpG.CFrame) end
						end)
					end
				end)
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
		end)
	else
		TweenService:Create(btrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(false) }):Play()
		TweenService:Create(bknob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(false) }):Play()
	end
end
_G.__MM2_Tg("bring", btoggle, function() return _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].bon end, btrack, bknob)
btrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() btoggle(not _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].bon) end)
local flingBtn = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "FlingMurdererButton", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 301), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "LayoutOrder", 4, "Parent", mpage})
