local flabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Fling murderer", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 12, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, "Parent", flingBtn})
local flingin = false
_G.__setFling = function(s) flingin = s end
local fstart = 0

local function findmurder()
	local found = nil
	pcall(function() if espf and espf.Parent then
			for _, h in ipairs(espf:GetChildren()) do
				if h:IsA("Highlight") then
					local nm = h.Name:match("^MurderESP_%d+_(.+)$")
					if nm then
						local plr = Players:FindFirstChild(nm)
						if plr and plr ~= player and plr.Character then found = plr break end
						if found then
							local hh = found.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							if not hh or hh.Health <= 0 then found = nil end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	if not found then
		pcall(function() local cand, candRank = nil, 0
			for _, q in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if q ~= player and q.Character and q.Character.Parent == workspace then
					local hum2 = q.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if hum2 and hum2.Health > 0 and not (_G.__MM2_IsDead and _G.__MM2_IsDead(q)) then
						local rank = 0
						local srvRole = nil
						if _G.__MM2_DetectRole then pcall(function() srvRole = _G.__MM2_DetectRole(q) end) end
						if srvRole == "Murderer" then rank = 3 end
						if rank == 0 then
							if q.Character:FindFirstChild("Knife") then rank = 2 end
						end
						if rank == 0 then
							local bp = q:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
							if bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Knife") then rank = 1 end
						end
						if rank > candRank then cand, candRank = q, rank end
					end
				end
			end
			found = cand
		end)
	end
	return found
end
_G.__flingc = function(target)
	local lp0 = player
	local ch = lp0 and lp0.Character
	local hrp = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hrp then flingin = false return end
	local cFrame = hrp.CFrame
	local fallenPartsDestroyHeight = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
	local character = ch
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not humanoid then flingin = false return end
	local rootPart = humanoid.RootPart
	if not rootPart then flingin = false return end
	local health = humanoid.Health
	local character2 = target.Parent and target.Character or nil
	local humanoid2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local rootPart2 = humanoid2 and humanoid2.RootPart
	local head = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("Head")
	local accessory = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Accessory")
	accessory = accessory and accessory:FindFirstChild("Handle")
	if humanoid2 and humanoid2.Sit then
		flingin = false
		return
	end
	local tAlive = false
	pcall(function() if humanoid2 and humanoid2.Health > 0 then tAlive = true end
	end)
	if not tAlive then
		flingin = false
		return
	end
	if head then
		workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = head
	elseif accessory then
		workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = accessory
	elseif humanoid2 then
		workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid2
	end

	local function plant(part2, num2, num3)
		rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(part2.Position) * num2 * num3
		pcall(function() character:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(part2.Position) * num2 * num3)
		end)
		rootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(9000000000, 9000000000, 9000000000)
		rootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(9000000000, 9000000000, 9000000000)
	end

	local function spin(instance)
		local now = tick()
		local n = 0
		while true do
			if not (not rootPart or not humanoid2 or not flingin) then
				if instance.Velocity.Magnitude < 50 then
					n = n + 250
					local n2 = humanoid2.MoveDirection * instance.Velocity.Magnitude / 1.25
					local cframe = CFrame.Angles
					plant(instance, CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0) + n2, cframe(math.rad(n), 0, 0))
					task.wait()
					local n3 = humanoid2.MoveDirection * instance.Velocity.Magnitude / 1.25
					local cframe2 = CFrame.Angles
					plant(instance, CFrame.new(0, -0.5, 0) + n3, cframe2(math.rad(n), 0, 0))
					task.wait()
					local n4 = humanoid2.MoveDirection * instance.Velocity.Magnitude / 1.25
					local cframe3 = CFrame.Angles
					plant(instance, CFrame.new(0.75, 0.5, -0.75) + n4, cframe3(math.rad(n), 0, 0))
					task.wait()
					local n5 = humanoid2.MoveDirection * instance.Velocity.Magnitude / 1.25
					local cframe4 = CFrame.Angles
					plant(instance, CFrame.new(-0.75, -0.5, 0.75) + n5, cframe4(math.rad(n), 0, 0))
					task.wait()
					local moveDirection = humanoid2.MoveDirection
					local cframe5 = CFrame.Angles
					plant(instance, CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0) + moveDirection, cframe5(math.rad(n), 0, 0))
					task.wait()
					local moveDirection2 = humanoid2.MoveDirection
					local cframe6 = CFrame.Angles
					plant(instance, CFrame.new(0, -0.5, 0) + moveDirection2, cframe6(math.rad(n), 0, 0))
					task.wait()
				else
					local cframe = CFrame.Angles
					plant(instance, CFrame.new(0, 0.5, humanoid2.WalkSpeed), cframe(1.5707963267948966, 0, 0))
					task.wait()
					local cframe2 = CFrame.Angles
					plant(instance, CFrame.new(0, -0.5, -humanoid2.WalkSpeed), cframe2(0, 0, 0))
					task.wait()
					local cframe3 = CFrame.Angles
					plant(instance, CFrame.new(0, 0.5, humanoid2.WalkSpeed), cframe3(1.5707963267948966, 0, 0))
					task.wait()
					local cframe4 = CFrame.Angles
					plant(instance, CFrame.new(0, -0.5, 0), cframe4(1.5707963267948966, 0, 0))
					task.wait()
					local cframe5 = CFrame.Angles
					plant(instance, CFrame.new(0, -0.5, 0), cframe5(0, 0, 0))
					task.wait()
					local cframe6 = CFrame.Angles
					plant(instance, CFrame.new(0, -0.5, 0), cframe6(-1.5707963267948966, 0, 0))
					task.wait()
					local cframe7 = CFrame.Angles
					plant(instance, CFrame.new(0, -0.5, 0), cframe7(0, 0, 0))
					task.wait()
				end
				local tDead = false
				pcall(function() if not humanoid2 or humanoid2.Health <= 0 then tDead = true end
				end)
				if instance.Velocity.Magnitude > 300
					or instance.Parent ~= target.Character
					or target.Parent ~= Players
					or humanoid.Health <= 0
					or humanoid.Health < health
					or humanoid2
					and humanoid2.Sit
					or tDead
					or tick() > now + 2
					or not flingin
					or ded then
					break
				end
			else
				break
			end
		end
	end
	Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = (0/0)
	local bodyVelocity = __mk("BodyVelocity", {"Name", "SkidFlingBV", "Parent", rootPart, "Velocity", Vector3.zero, "MaxForce", Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)})
	humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
	pcall(function() if rootPart2 and head then
			if (rootPart2.CFrame.p - head.CFrame.p).Magnitude <= 5 then
				spin(rootPart2)
			else
				spin(head)
			end
		elseif rootPart2 then
			spin(rootPart2)
		elseif head then
			spin(head)
		elseif accessory then
			spin(accessory)
		end
	end)
	bodyVelocity:Destroy()
	humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, true)
	Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid
	if cFrame and not ded then
		pcall(function() for i = 1, 50 do
				rootPart.CFrame = cFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0)
				pcall(function() character:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(cFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0))
				end)
				humanoid:ChangeState("GettingUp")
				for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("BasePart") then
						child.Velocity = Vector3.zero
						child.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
					end
				end
				task.wait()
				if (rootPart.Position - cFrame.p).Magnitude < 25 then
					break
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = fallenPartsDestroyHeight
	flingin = false
	if ded then
		pcall(function() local humU2 = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if humU2 then
				Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = humU2
				Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			end
		end)
	end
end
flingBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(flabel, TWEEN_FAST, { TextColor3 = __headCol() }):Play()
	task.delay(1.5, function() pcall(function() TweenService:Create(flabel, TweenInfo.new(0.3, EASE_Q), { TextColor3 = __MM2_BaseTxt() }):Play() end) end)
	if flingin then
		if os.clock() - fstart < 10 then
			return
		end
		flingin = false
	end
	pcall(function() local lp = player
		local ch = lp and lp.Character
		local hrp = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hrp then return end
		local rOK, rAct = pcall(function() if _G.__MM2_RoundActive then return _G.__MM2_RoundActive() end return true end)
		if rOK and rAct == false then return end
		local target = findmurder()
		if not target then
			return
		end
		local tAliveC = false
		pcall(function() local tc = target and target.Character
			local th = tc and tc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if th and th.Health > 0 then tAliveC = true end
		end)
		if not tAliveC then flingin = false return end
		flingin = true
		fstart = os.clock()
		_G.__flingc(target)
	end)
end)
;(function() local kbtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	kbtn.Name = "KillAllButton"
	kbtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
	kbtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 140)
	kbtn.BackgroundColor3 = BG_DARKER
	kbtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	kbtn.Text = ""
	kbtn.AutoButtonColor = false
	kbtn.Parent = mpage
	__s(kbtn, BORDER, 1, true)
	local klabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Kill all", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 12, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, "Parent", kbtn})
	kbtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(klabel, TWEEN_FAST, { TextColor3 = __headCol() }):Play()
		task.delay(1.5, function() pcall(function() TweenService:Create(klabel, TweenInfo.new(0.3, EASE_Q), { TextColor3 = __MM2_BaseTxt() }):Play() end) end)
		local lpK = player
		local chK = lpK and lpK.Character
		if not chK then return end
		local isMurd = false
		for _, t in ipairs(chK:GetChildren()) do
			if t:IsA("Tool") and t.Name == "Knife" then isMurd = true end
		end
		if not isMurd then
			local bpK = lpK and lpK:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
			if bpK and bpK:FindFirstChild("Knife") then isMurd = true end
		end
		if not isMurd then
			if os.clock() - (_G.__killw or 0) > 2 then
				_G.__killw = os.clock()
				task.spawn(function() killToast("Must be murderer") end)
			end
			return
		end
		local fn = _G.__MM2_KillAll
		if not fn then
			task.spawn(function() killToast("Kill all: not ready") end)
			return
		end
		if fn(true) then
			task.spawn(function() killToast("Successful") end)
		else
			task.spawn(function() killToast("Knife not found") end)
		end
	end)
	;(function() _G.__mlay = { k = 0, f = 0 }
		_G.__mpApply = function()
			if not alive() then return end
			local ks = (_G.__mlay and _G.__mlay.k) or 0
			local fs = (_G.__mlay and _G.__mlay.f) or 0
			local base = _G.__mpBase
			if not base then return end
			local info = TWEEN_POP
			for _, n in ipairs({ "FlingDivider", "FlingHeader", "FlingBox", "FlingTargetDropdown", "FlingTargetList" }) do
				local el = mpage:FindFirstChild(n)
				local b = base[n]
				if el and b then TweenService:Create(el, info, { Position = UDim2.new(b.xs, b.xo, 0, b.yo + ks) }):Play() end
			end
			for _, n in ipairs({ "FlingMurdererButton", "FlingSheriffButton", "AntiFlingRow", "SpamFlingRow" }) do
				local el = mpage:FindFirstChild(n)
				local b = base[n]
				if el and b then TweenService:Create(el, info, { Position = UDim2.new(b.xs, b.xo, 0, b.yo + ks) }):Play() end
			end
			local fb = mpage:FindFirstChild("FlingBox")
			local bb = base["FlingBox"]
			local lb = base["FlingTargetList"]
			local fh = bb and bb.h or 0
			if fb and bb then
				if lb then
					local need = lb.yo + fs + 8 - bb.yo
					if need > fh then fh = need end
				end
				TweenService:Create(fb, info, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 16, 0, fh) }):Play()
			end
			for _, n in ipairs({ "FarmDivider", "FarmHeader", "CoinFarmRow", "FarmFlingRow", "FarmKillRow", "FarmHopRow", "FarmBox" }) do
				local el = mpage:FindFirstChild(n)
				local b = base[n]
				if el and b then
					local extra = math.max(0, (fh or 0) - 206)
					TweenService:Create(el, info, { Position = UDim2.new(b.xs, b.xo, 0, b.yo + ks + extra) }):Play()
				end
			end
			pcall(function() local cv = 800 + ks
				if fb and bb then
					local bot = bb.yo + fh
					if bot + 20 > cv then cv = bot + 20 end
				end
				local fhb = base["FarmHeader"]
				if fhb then
					local bot2 = fhb.yo + 14 + ks + math.max(0, (fh or 0) - 206)
					if bot2 + 20 > cv then cv = bot2 + 20 end
				end
				TweenService:Create(mpage, info, { CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, cv) }):Play()
			end)
		end

		local function ksync(n)
			if not alive() then return end
			local h = 121 + n
			if h < 163 then h = 163 end
			_G.__mlay.k = h - 163
			pcall(function() local gb = mpage:FindFirstChild("GeneralBox")
				if gb then TweenService:Create(gb, TWEEN_POP, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 16, 0, h) }):Play() end
			end)
			pcall(function() if _G.__mpApply then _G.__mpApply() end end)
		end
		local kdrbtn = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "KillTargetDropdown", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 99), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", mpage})
		__s(kdrbtn, BORDER, 1, true)
		local kdlabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Kill: ...", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", kdrbtn})
		local kdrarrow = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "v", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "Parent", kdrbtn})
		local kdrlist = __mk("ScrollingFrame", {"Name", "KillTargetList", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 139), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ScrollBarThickness", 0, "Visible", false, "ZIndex", 10, "Parent", mpage})
		local kdrstroke = __s(kdrlist, BORDER, 1, true)

		local function kdropghost(ghost)
			local t = ghost and 1 or 0
			kdrlist.BackgroundTransparency = t
			kdrstroke.Transparency = t
			__optFade(kdrlist, t)
		end
		local kdopen = false
		local kdsel = nil
		local openkdrop

		local function krefreshdrop()
			for _, c in ipairs(kdrlist:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("TextButton") then pcall(function() c:Destroy() end) end
			end
			local y = 0
			for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if p ~= player then
					local b = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", kdrlist})
					__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", p.DisplayName .. " (@" .. p.Name .. ")", "Font", Enum.Font.Gotham, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", (kdsel == p) and acc or TEXT_SECONDARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", b})
					local who = p
					b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() openkdrop(false)
						kdlabel.Text = "Kill: " .. string.sub(who.DisplayName, 1, 14)
						kdsel = who
						pcall(function() local lpK = player
							local chK = lpK and lpK.Character
							if not chK then return end
							local isMurd = false
							for _, t in ipairs(chK:GetChildren()) do
								if t:IsA("Tool") and t.Name == "Knife" then isMurd = true break end
							end
							if not isMurd then
								local bpK = lpK and lpK:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
								if bpK and bpK:FindFirstChild("Knife") then isMurd = true end
							end
							if not isMurd then
								if os.clock() - (_G.__killw or 0) > 2 then
									_G.__killw = os.clock()
									task.spawn(function() killToast("Must be murderer") end)
								end
								return
							end
							local fn = _G.__MM2_KillAll
							if not fn then
								task.spawn(function() killToast("Kill all: not ready") end)
								return
							end
							if fn(true, who) then
								task.spawn(function() killToast("Successful") end)
							else
								task.spawn(function() killToast("Knife not found") end)
							end
						end)
					end)
					y = y + 32
				end
			end
			kdrlist.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y)
			return y
		end
		openkdrop = function(open)
			kdopen = open
			kdrarrow.Rotation = open and 180 or 0
			if open then
				local yy = krefreshdrop() or 0
				kdrlist.Visible = true
				kdropghost(true)
				TweenService:Create(kdrlist, TWEEN_POP, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, yy), BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
				ksync(yy)
				TweenService:Create(kdrstroke, TWEEN_POP, { Transparency = 0 }):Play()
				__optFade(kdrlist, 0, TWEEN_POP)
			else
				TweenService:Create(kdrlist, TWEEN_EASE, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
				ksync(0)
				TweenService:Create(kdrstroke, TWEEN_EASE, { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
				__optFade(kdrlist, 1, TWEEN_EASE)
				task.delay(0.22, function() if not kdopen then kdrlist.Visible = false end end)
			end
		end
		kdrbtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() openkdrop(not kdopen) end)
		pcall(function() _G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerAdded, function() if alive() then if kdopen then ksync(krefreshdrop() or 0) else ksync(0) end end end)
			_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerRemoving, function() if alive() then if kdopen then ksync(krefreshdrop() or 0) else ksync(0) end end end)
		end)
	end)()
end)()

local function sherbuild()

	local function findsheriff()
		local found = nil
		pcall(function() if espf and espf.Parent then
				for _, h in ipairs(espf:GetChildren()) do
					if h:IsA("Highlight") then
						local nm = h.Name:match("^SheriffESP_%d+_(.+)$")
						if nm then
							local plr = Players:FindFirstChild(nm)
							if plr and plr ~= player and plr.Character then
								local hh = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
								if hh and hh.Health > 0 then found = plr end
							end
							if found then break end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		if not found then
			pcall(function() local cand, candRank = nil, 0
				for _, q in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					if q ~= player and q.Character and q.Character.Parent == workspace then
						local hum2 = q.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						if hum2 and hum2.Health > 0 and not (_G.__MM2_IsDead and _G.__MM2_IsDead(q)) then
							local rank = 0
							local srvRole = nil
							if _G.__MM2_DetectRole then pcall(function() srvRole = _G.__MM2_DetectRole(q) end) end
							if srvRole == "Sheriff" then rank = 3 end
							if rank == 0 then
								if q.Character:FindFirstChild("Gun") or q.Character:FindFirstChild("Revolver") then rank = 2 end
							end
							if rank == 0 then
								local bp = q:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
								if bp and (bp:FindFirstChild("Gun") or bp:FindFirstChild("Revolver")) then rank = 1 end
							end
							if rank > candRank then cand, candRank = q, rank end
						end
					end
				end
				found = cand
			end)
		end
		return found
	end
	local sherBtn = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "FlingSheriffButton", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 343), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "LayoutOrder", 5, "Parent", mpage})
	__s(sherBtn, BORDER, 1, true)
	local sherlabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Fling sheriff", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 12, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, "Parent", sherBtn})
	sherBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(sherlabel, TWEEN_FAST, { TextColor3 = __headCol() }):Play()
		task.delay(1.5, function() pcall(function() TweenService:Create(sherlabel, TweenInfo.new(0.3, EASE_Q), { TextColor3 = __MM2_BaseTxt() }):Play() end) end)
		if flingin then
			if os.clock() - fstart < 10 then return end
			flingin = false
		end
		pcall(function() local lp = player
			local ch = lp and lp.Character
			local hrp = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not hrp then return end
			local rOK, rAct = pcall(function() if _G.__MM2_RoundActive then return _G.__MM2_RoundActive() end return true end)
			if rOK and rAct == false then return end
			local target = findsheriff()
			if not target then
				return
			end
			local tAliveC = false
			pcall(function() local tc = target and target.Character
				local th = tc and tc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if th and th.Health > 0 then tAliveC = true end
			end)
			if not tAliveC then flingin = false return end
			flingin = true
			fstart = os.clock()
			_G.__flingc(target)
		end)
	end)
end
sherbuild()
;(function() local arow = Instance.new("Frame")
	arow.Name = "AntiFlingRow"
	arow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
	arow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 389)
	arow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	arow.BorderSizePixel = 0
	arow.Parent = mpage
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Anti-fling", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", arow})
	local atrack = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 0), "BackgroundColor3", acc, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", arow})
	__c(atrack, UDim.new(1, 0))
	local aknob = __mk("Frame", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", atrack})
	__c(aknob, UDim.new(1, 0))
	local aon = true

	local function atoggle(state)
		aon = state
		__knob(atrack, aknob, state)
	end
	pcall(function() if _G.__antifling then _G.__antifling:Disconnect() end end)
	_G.__antifling = _G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not aon or ded then return end
		pcall(function() local lpA = Players.LocalPlayer
			local chA = lpA and lpA.Character
			local hrpA = chA and chA:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not hrpA then return end
			for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if p ~= lpA and p.Character then
					for _, d in ipairs(p.Character:GetDescendants()) do
						if d:IsA("BasePart") and not d.Anchored and d.Velocity.Magnitude > 200 then
							if (d.Position - hrpA.Position).Magnitude < 30 then
								d.Velocity = Vector3.zero
								d.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
								d.Anchored = true
								task.delay(0.3, function() if d and d.Parent then d.Anchored = false end
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end)
	_G.__MM2_Tg("antifling", atoggle, function() return aon end, atrack, aknob)
	atrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() atoggle(not aon) end)
end)()
;(function() local sprow = Instance.new("Frame")
	sprow.Name = "SpamFlingRow"
	sprow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
	sprow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 419)
	sprow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	sprow.BorderSizePixel = 0
	sprow.Parent = mpage
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Spam fling", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", sprow})
	local sptrack, spknob = __sw(sprow, 0, 0)
	local spon = false

	local function sptoggle(state)
		spon = state
		__knob(sptrack, spknob, state)
	end
	task.spawn(function() while alive() do
			task.wait(1)
			if spon and not flingin and not ded then
				pcall(function() local tgt = _G.__flingt
					if not tgt or tgt.Parent ~= Players then return end
					local tch = tgt.Character
					local thum = tch and tch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if not tch or not thum or thum.Health <= 0 then return end
					local lp = player
					local ch = lp and lp.Character
					if not (ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) then return end
					flingin = true
					fstart = os.clock()
					_G.__flingc(tgt)
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
	_G.__MM2_Tg("spamfling", sptoggle, function() return spon end, sptrack, spknob)
	sptrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() sptoggle(not spon) end)
end)()
;(function() local fdiv = Instance.new("Frame")

	__mk("TextLabel", {"Name", "FlingHeader", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 217), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", "Fling", "Parent", mpage})

		fbox.Name = "FlingBox"

		fbox.Parent = mpage

		__s(fbox, BORDER, 1)

;(function() local farmdiv = Instance.new("Frame")
	farmdiv.Name = "FarmDivider"
	farmdiv.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
	farmdiv.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 472)
	farmdiv.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
	farmdiv.BorderSizePixel = 0
	farmdiv.Parent = mpage
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Name", "FarmHeader", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 480), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", "Farm", "Parent", mpage})
end)()
;(function() local farmbox = Instance.new("Frame")
	farmbox.Name = "FarmBox"
	farmbox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 16, 0, 146)

	__c(farmbox, UDim.new(0, 8))
	__s(farmbox, BORDER, 1)
end)()
;(function() local cfon = false
	local cftoggle = nil
	local cfrow = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "CoinFarmRow", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 524), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", mpage})
	local cflabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Auto farm", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", cfrow, "Active", true})
	cflabel.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			cftoggle(not cfon)
		end
	end)
	local cftrack, cfknob = __sw(cfrow, 0, 0)
	local cfgen = 0
	local cfskips, cffails = {}, {}
	local cfclip
	local cfparts = {}
	local cfcliplist = {}
	local cfclipchar = nil
	local cflastmap, cftarget, cftargett = nil, nil, 0
	local cfccsave, cfmapt = nil, 0
	local cfaimpos = nil
	local cfidle = false
	local cfbagt, cfbagv = 0, 0
	local cfdeadsv = nil
	local cfdied = false
	local cffull = false
	local cfflingT = 0
	local cfkillDone, cfkillT = false, 0
	local cfflingall = nil
	local cfbagAmt, cfbagCap, cfbagEv, cfroundLive = 0, 0, false, false

	local function cfskip(c)
		local t = cfskips[c]
		if not t then return false end
		if t < os.clock() then cfskips[c] = nil cffails[c] = nil return false end
		return true
	end

	local function cffail(c)
		local n = (cffails[c] or 0) + 1
		cffails[c] = n
		if n >= 2 then cfskips[c] = os.clock() + 20 end
	end
	local cftouch_last = 0

	local function cftouch_fire(coin)
		if type(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" then return end
		if not coin or not coin.Parent then return end
		local lp0 = Players.LocalPlayer
		local ch0 = lp0 and lp0.Character
		local hrp0 = ch0 and ch0:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hrp0 then return end
		local list = {}
		local seen = {}

		local function add(p)
			if p and not seen[p] and p:IsA("BasePart") and p:FindFirstChildOfClass("TouchTransmitter") then
				seen[p] = true
				list[#list + 1] = p
			end
		end
		add(coin)
		for _, v in ipairs(coin:GetChildren()) do add(v) end
		local par = coin.Parent
		if par then
			if par:IsA("BasePart") then add(par) end
			for _, v in ipairs(par:GetChildren()) do add(v) end
		end
		if #list == 0 then list[1] = coin end
		for _, p in ipairs(list) do
			pcall(firetouchinterest, hrp0, p, 0)
			pcall(firetouchinterest, hrp0, p, 1)
		end
	end

	local function cftouch(coin)
		local now = os.clock()
		if now - cftouch_last < 0.05 then return end
		cftouch_last = now
		cftouch_fire(coin)
	end
	local cfmag_seen = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

	local function cfmagnet(coin)
		if not coin or not coin.Parent then return end
		if coin:GetAttribute("Collected") or coin:GetAttribute("Delete") then return end
		local now = os.clock()
		local t = cfmag_seen[coin]
		if t and now - t < 0.35 then return end
		cfmag_seen[coin] = now
		cftouch_fire(coin)
	end

	local function cfbags()
		local ok, n = pcall(function() local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
			if not lp then return nil end
			local g = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
			local main = g and g:FindFirstChild("MainGUI")
			if not main then return nil end
			local cont = main:FindFirstChild("Game") and main.Game:FindFirstChild("CoinBags") and main.Game.CoinBags:FindFirstChild("Container")
			if not cont then
				local lob = main:FindFirstChild("Lobby")
				local dock = lob and lob:FindFirstChild("Dock")
				cont = dock and dock:FindFirstChild("CoinBags") and dock.CoinBags:FindFirstChild("Container")
			end
			if not cont then return nil end
			local pick = nil
			for _, b in ipairs(cont:GetChildren()) do
				if b:IsA("GuiObject") and b:FindFirstChild("CurrencyFrame") then
					if b.Visible then pick = b break
					elseif b.Name == "Coin" and not pick then pick = b end
				end
			end
			if not pick then return nil end
			local full = pick:FindFirstChild("Full")
			if full and full.Visible then return 999 end
			local cf0 = pick:FindFirstChild("CurrencyFrame")
			cf0 = cf0 and cf0:FindFirstChild("Icon")
			cf0 = cf0 and cf0:FindFirstChild("Coins")
			local tx = cf0 and cf0.Text or ""
			if string.lower(tx):find("full") or string.lower(tx):find("max") then return 999 end
			local num0, den0 = tx:match("^%s*(%d+)%s*/%s*(%d+)")
			if num0 and den0 and tonumber(den0) > 0 and tonumber(num0) >= tonumber(den0) then return 999 end
			local digits, mult = tx:match("^%s*(%d+%.?%d*)%s*([KkMmBb])")
			if digits then
				local k = string.lower(mult)
				local f = 1
				if k == "k" then f = 1000 elseif k == "m" then f = 1000000 elseif k == "b" then f = 1000000000 end
				return tonumber(digits) * f
			end
			return tonumber(tx) or 0
		end)
		if ok then return n end
		return nil
	end
	pcall(function() local ev = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
		ev = ev and ev:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
		ev = ev and ev:FindFirstChild("CoinCollected")
		if ev then _G.__MM2_Conn(ev.OnClientEvent, function(_, amt, cap)
				amt = tonumber(amt)
				cap = tonumber(cap)
				if amt then cfbagAmt = amt cfbagEv = true end
				if cap and cap > 0 then cfbagCap = cap end
			end) end
	end)

	local function cfclipset(on)
		if on then
			if cfclip then return end
			cfclip = _G.__MM2_ConnOwn(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN], RunService.Heartbeat, function() if ded then
					cfclipset(false)
					return
				end
				pcall(function() local ch = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer.Character
					if not ch then return end
					if cfclipchar ~= ch then
						cfclipchar = ch
						cfcliplist = {}
						for _, p in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
							if p:IsA("BasePart") then cfcliplist[#cfcliplist + 1] = p end
						end
					end
					for i = 1, #cfcliplist do
						local p = cfcliplist[i]
						if p and p.Parent then
							if p.CanCollide then
								cfparts[p] = true
								if _G.__MM2_CCSave then _G.__MM2_CCSave(p) end
								p.CanCollide = false
							end
						else
							cfcliplist[i] = nil
						end
					end
				end)
			end)
		else
			if cfclip then pcall(function() cfclip:Disconnect() end) cfclip = nil end
			for p in pairs(cfparts) do pcall(function() p.CanCollide = true end) end
			cfparts = {}
			cfcliplist = {}
			cfclipchar = nil
		end
	end

	local function cfprep(on)
		pcall(function() local ch = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer.Character
			if not ch then return end
			local ut = ch:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
			if ut then
				local a = ut:FindFirstChild("Farm Gyro")
				if a then a:Destroy() end
				local b = ut:FindFirstChild("Farm Velocity")
				if b then b:Destroy() end
			end
			local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if hum then hum.PlatformStand = on and true or false end
		end)
	end

	local function cfleave()
		pcall(function() if not cfaimpos then return end
			local ch0 = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer.Character
			local hrp0 = ch0 and ch0:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not hrp0 then return end
			local flat = (Vector3.new(cfaimpos.X, hrp0.Position.Y, cfaimpos.Z) - hrp0.Position).Magnitude
			if flat > 12 then return end
			if hrp0.Position.Y >= cfaimpos.Y + 5 then return end
			hrp0.Velocity = Vector3.zero
			hrp0.CFrame = CFrame.new(cfaimpos.X, cfaimpos.Y + 8, cfaimpos.Z)
		end)
	end

	local function cfpause()
		if not cfidle then
			cfidle = true
			cfleave()
			cfclipset(false)
		end
		cfprep(false)
	end

	local function hidebRun(fn)
		_G.__hideb = true
		local ok = pcall(fn)
		_G.__hideb = nil
		if not ok then
			task.delay(2, function() if _G.__hideb then _G.__hideb = nil end
			end)
		end
	end

	local function grabRemotes(t, out)
		if t and t:IsA("Tool") then
			local ef = t:FindFirstChild("Events")
			if ef then
				local ks = ef:FindFirstChild("KnifeStabbed")
				if ks and ks:IsA("RemoteEvent") and not out.sev then out.sev = ks end
				local ht = ef:FindFirstChild("HandleTouched")
				if ht and ht:IsA("RemoteEvent") and not out.tev then out.tev = ht end
			end
		end
	end
	_G.__MM2_Knife = function(equip, tool, allowRS, strict)
		local me = Players.LocalPlayer
		local ch = me and me.Character
		if not ch then return nil end
		if not (tool and tool:IsA("Tool") and tool.Parent) then
			tool = ch:FindFirstChild("Knife")
			if equip and not tool then
				local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local bp = me:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
				local kb = bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Knife")
				if hum and kb then
					pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(kb) end)
					local t0 = os.clock()
					while os.clock() - t0 < 1 do
						local cur = me.Character and me.Character:FindFirstChild("Knife")
						local ef = cur and cur:FindFirstChild("Events")
						if ef and ef:FindFirstChild("KnifeStabbed") and ef:FindFirstChild("HandleTouched") then tool = cur break end
						task.wait(0.05)
					end
					if not tool then tool = me.Character and me.Character:FindFirstChild("Knife") end
				end
			end
		end
		local out = {}
		grabRemotes(tool, out)
		if not strict then
			if not out.sev or not out.tev then
				for _, t in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do
					if t:IsA("Tool") and t.Name == "Knife" then grabRemotes(t, out) end
				end
			end
			if not out.sev or not out.tev then
				local bp = me:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
				if bp then
					for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
						if t:IsA("Tool") and t.Name == "Knife" then grabRemotes(t, out) end
					end
				end
			end
			if not out.sev or not out.tev then
				for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
						if d.Name == "KnifeStabbed" and not out.sev then out.sev = d end
						if d.Name == "HandleTouched" and not out.tev then out.tev = d end
					end
				end
			end
			if allowRS and (not out.sev or not out.tev) and os.clock() - (_G.__MM2_RSscan or 0) > 5 then
				_G.__MM2_RSscan = os.clock()
				pcall(function() local rs0 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
					for _, d in ipairs(rs0:GetDescendants()) do
						if d:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
							if d.Name == "KnifeStabbed" and not out.sev then out.sev = d end
							if d.Name == "HandleTouched" and not out.tev then out.tev = d end
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
		if not out.sev or not out.tev then return nil end
		return tool, out.sev, out.tev
	end
	_G.__MM2_KillAll = function(equip, only)
		local me = Players.LocalPlayer
		local sev, tev = select(2, _G.__MM2_Knife(equip))
		if not sev or not tev then return false end
		hidebRun(function() sev:FireServer()
			local list = only and { only } or Players:GetPlayers()
			for _, q in ipairs(list) do
				if q ~= me and q.Character then
					local hrp = q.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local hum = q.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
						tev:FireServer(hrp)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		return true
	end

	local function cfkillmurder()
		return _G.__MM2_KillAll(true) == true
	end
	local cfZmod, cfZmodT = nil, 0

	local function cfIsZombie(p)
		if not p then return false end
		local z = false
		pcall(function() local ch = p.Character
			if ch and ch:GetAttribute("Zombie") == true then z = true end
		end)
		if z then return true end
		local role = nil
		pcall(function()
			if not cfZmod or os.clock() - cfZmodT > 5 then
				cfZmod = nil
				local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
				local md = rs:FindFirstChild("Modules")
				md = md and md:FindFirstChild("CurrentRoundClient")
				if not md then
					for _, d in ipairs(rs:GetDescendants()) do
						if d.Name == "CurrentRoundClient" and d:IsA("ModuleScript") then md = d break end
					end
				end
				cfZmod = md
				cfZmodT = os.clock()
			end
			if cfZmod then
				local tbl = require(cfZmod)
				local e = type(tbl) == "table" and type(tbl.PlayerData) == "table" and tbl.PlayerData[p.Name]
				if type(e) == "table" and type(e.Role) == "string" then role = e.Role end
			end
		end)
		if type(role) ~= "string" then return false end
		local ok, rn = pcall(function() return role:lower():gsub("%s+", "") end)
		return ok and (rn == "zombie" or rn == "infected")
	end

	local function cfZombieRound()
		local zr = false
		pcall(function() local me = Players.LocalPlayer
			for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if pl ~= me and cfIsZombie(pl) then zr = true return end
			end
		end)
		return zr
	end

	local function cfkillrevenge()
		if not cfon then return end
		if os.clock() - (_G.__cfrevT or 0) < 3 then return end
		if not _G.__setFling or not _G.__flingc then return end
		local me = Players.LocalPlayer
		local killer = nil
		pcall(function() local md = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Modules")
			md = md and md:FindFirstChild("CurrentRoundClient")
			if md then
				local pd = require(md).PlayerData
				if type(pd) == "table" then
					for n, d in pairs(pd) do
						if n ~= me.Name and type(d) == "table" and d.Role == "Murderer" and not d.Dead then
							local q = Players:FindFirstChild(n)
							if q and not cfIsZombie(q) then killer = q break end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		if not killer then
			pcall(function() for _, q in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					if q ~= me and q.Character and not cfIsZombie(q) then
						local hasKnife = q.Character:FindFirstChild("Knife")
						if not hasKnife then
							local bp = q:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
							if bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Knife") then hasKnife = true end
						end
						if hasKnife then
							local hum = q.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							if hum and hum.Health > 0 then killer = q break end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		if not killer then return end
		_G.__cfrevT = os.clock()
		task.spawn(function() pcall(function() _G.__setFling(true) end)
			pcall(function() _G.__flingc(killer) end)
			pcall(function() _G.__setFling(false) end)
		end)
	end
	local cfCleanupDone = false
	local cfCoinReg = nil
	local cfRegAdded, cfRegRemoved = nil, nil

	local function cfRegBuild()
		cfCoinReg = {}
		pcall(function() for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if d.Name == "Coin_Server" and d:IsA("PVInstance") then cfCoinReg[d] = true end
			end
		end)
	end

	local function cfRegStart()
		cfRegBuild()
		if cfRegAdded then return end
		cfRegAdded = _G.__MM2_ConnOwn(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN], workspace.DescendantAdded, function(d) if cfCoinReg and d.Name == "Coin_Server" and d:IsA("PVInstance") then cfCoinReg[d] = true end
		end)
		cfRegRemoved = _G.__MM2_ConnOwn(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN], workspace.DescendantRemoving, function(d) if cfCoinReg then cfCoinReg[d] = nil end
		end)
	end

	local function cfRegStop()
		if cfRegAdded then pcall(function() cfRegAdded:Disconnect() end) end
		if cfRegRemoved then pcall(function() cfRegRemoved:Disconnect() end) end
		cfRegAdded, cfRegRemoved = nil, nil
		cfCoinReg = nil
	end

	local function cfstop()
		cfRegStop()
		cfgen = cfgen + 1
		cfprep(false)
		cfclipset(false)
		cftarget = nil
		cfleave()
		cfaimpos = nil
		cfidle = false
	end
	if not cfCleanupDone then
		pcall(function() _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup or {}
			table.insert(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup, function() pcall(cfstop, true) end)
			cfCleanupDone = true
		end)
	end

	local function cfstart()
		cfgen = cfgen + 1
		local gen = cfgen
		cfRegStart()
		cfclipset(true)
		task.spawn(function() while gen == cfgen and cfon do
				task.wait(2)
				pcall(function() if gen ~= cfgen or not cfon then return end
					local lpq = Players.LocalPlayer
					if not lpq then return end
					local remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
					local extras = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Extras")
					local gpd = extras and extras:FindFirstChild("GetPlayerData")
					if not gpd then cfdeadsv = nil return end
					local ok, res = pcall(function() return gpd:InvokeServer() end)
					if ok and type(res) == "table" then
						local ent = res[lpq.Name]
						if ent then cfdeadsv = (ent.Dead or ent.Killed) and true or false end
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
		task.spawn(function() while gen == cfgen and cfon do
				local cfdtt = task.wait()
				pcall(function() if gen ~= cfgen or not cfon then return end
					if not alive() then cfstop() return end
					local rtp0 = workspace:FindFirstChild("RoundTimerPart")
					local tm0 = 0
					if rtp0 then tm0 = tonumber(rtp0:GetAttribute("Time")) or 0 end
					if tm0 > 0 and not cfroundLive then
						cfroundLive = true
						cffull = false
						cfkillDone = false
						cfbagv = 0
						cfbagAmt = 0
						cfbagCap = 0
						cfbagEv = false
						cfbagt = 0
					elseif tm0 <= 0 then
						cfroundLive = false
					end
					local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
					local roundOk = true
					pcall(function() local md = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Modules")
						md = md and md:FindFirstChild("CurrentRoundClient")
						if md then
							local pd = require(md).PlayerData
							if type(pd) == "table" then
								local me = pd[lp.Name]
								if not me or not me.Role or me.Dead then roundOk = false end
							end
						end
					end)
					local deadLocal = false
					pcall(function() local lpc = Players.LocalPlayer
						local chc = lpc and lpc.Character
						local hc = chc and chc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						if hc and hc.Health <= 0 then deadLocal = true end
					end)
					if tm0 > 0 and (not roundOk or deadLocal) and os.clock() - cfflingT > 8 then
						cfflingT = os.clock()
						if cfflingall then task.spawn(cfflingall) end
					end
					if tm0 > 0 and roundOk and not deadLocal and cffull and _G.__fakOn and not cfkillDone and os.clock() - cfkillT > 2 then
						local isMurd = false
						pcall(function() local mm = Players.LocalPlayer
							local mc = mm and mm.Character
							if mc then
								for _, t in ipairs(mc:GetChildren()) do
									if t:IsA("Tool") and t.Name == "Knife" then isMurd = true break end
								end
								if not isMurd then
									local mb = mm:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
									if mb and mb:FindFirstChild("Knife") then isMurd = true end
								end
							end
						end)
						if isMurd then
							cfkillT = os.clock()
							if os.clock() - (_G.__cfkillPopT or 0) > 10 then
								_G.__cfkillPopT = os.clock()
								task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.7) if _G.__MM2_Toast then pcall(_G.__MM2_Toast, "Farm", "Killing all", "FarmWarn") end end)
							end
							task.spawn(function() if cfkillmurder() then
									cfkillDone = true
								elseif os.clock() - (_G.__cfkillWarnT or 0) > 10 then
									_G.__cfkillWarnT = os.clock()
									pcall(function() killToast("Kill all: knife not found") end)
								end
							end)
						end
					end
					local ch = lp and lp.Character
					local hrp = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local humL = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if not hrp then
						return
					end
					if cfdeadsv == true or not humL or humL.Health <= 0 then
						if cfon then cfdied = true end
						cfprep(false)
						cftarget = nil
						if cfon and os.clock() - cfflingT > 8 then
							cfflingT = os.clock()
							task.spawn(cfkillrevenge)
						end
						return
					end
					if cfdied and humL and humL.Health > 0 then
						cfdied = false
						task.spawn(cfkillrevenge)
					end
					local map = cflastmap
					local cc = cfccsave
					if os.clock() - cfmapt > 0.5 then
						cfmapt = os.clock()
						map = nil
						for _, m in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
							if m:FindFirstChild("CoinAreas") or m:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer") then map = m end
						end
						if not map then
							local cc0 = workspace:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer", true)
							if cc0 then map = cc0.Parent end
						end
						if map ~= cflastmap then
							cflastmap = map
							cfskips = {}
							cffails = {}
							cfaimpos = nil
							cfdied = false
							cfkillDone = false
							cc = map and map:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer", true)
							cfccsave = cc
						end
					end
					if tm0 > 0 then
						if not roundOk then
							cftarget = nil
							cfpause()
							return
						end
						if os.clock() - cfbagt > 0.4 then
							cfbagt = os.clock()
							if not cfbagEv then
								local v0 = cfbags()
								if v0 and v0 < 999 then cfbagv = v0 end
							end
						end
						if cfbagEv then cfbagv = cfbagAmt end
						local amt0 = cfbagv
						local cap0 = 999
						if cfbagEv and cfbagCap > 0 then cap0 = cfbagCap end
						if amt0 >= cap0 then
							if not cffull then task.spawn(function() if _G.__MM2_Toast then pcall(_G.__MM2_Toast, "Farm", "Full bag", "FarmWarn") end end) end
							cffull = true
							cfpause()
							if _G.__fafOn and cfflingall and os.clock() - cfflingT > 8 then
								cfflingT = os.clock()
								if cfZombieRound() then
									task.spawn(function() if _G.__MM2_Toast then pcall(_G.__MM2_Toast, "Warning", "Zombie round", "FarmWarn") end end)
								else
									task.spawn(cfflingall)
								end
							end
							cftarget = nil
							return
						end
						cffull = false
						cfkillDone = false
						local num4 = hrp
						if ch then
							local obj7 = nil
							local near = {}
							local bd = math.huge
							local tagged = nil
							pcall(function() tagged = game:GetService("CollectionService"):GetTagged("CoinVisual") end)
							if tagged then
								for _, c in ipairs(tagged) do
									if c and c.Parent and c:IsA("BasePart") and not c:GetAttribute("Collected") and not c:GetAttribute("Delete") and not cfskip(c) then
										local dd = (num4.Position - c.Position).Magnitude
										if dd < bd then bd = dd obj7 = c end
										if dd <= 8 then near[#near + 1] = c end
									end
								end
							end
							if not obj7 and cc then
								for _, c in ipairs(cc:GetChildren()) do
									if c.Name == "Coin_Server" and c:IsA("PVInstance") and not c:GetAttribute("Collected") and not c:GetAttribute("Delete") and not cfskip(c) then
										local dd = (num4.Position - c:GetPivot().Position).Magnitude
										if dd < bd then bd = dd obj7 = c end
										if dd <= 8 then near[#near + 1] = c end
									end
								end
							end
							if not obj7 and cfCoinReg then
								for c in pairs(cfCoinReg) do
									if c.Parent and not c:GetAttribute("Collected") and not c:GetAttribute("Delete") and not cfskip(c) then
										local dd = (num4.Position - c:GetPivot().Position).Magnitude
										if dd < bd then bd = dd obj7 = c end
										if dd <= 8 then near[#near + 1] = c end
									end
								end
							end
							for _, c in ipairs(near) do cfmagnet(c) end
							if num4 and obj7 then
								if cfidle then
									cfidle = false
									cfclipset(true)
								end
								local cfp = obj7:GetPivot().Position
								local cfcoin = Vector3.new(cfp.X, cfp.Y, cfp.Z)
								local cfaim = cfcoin - Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)
								cfaimpos = cfaim
								if (cfcoin - num4.Position).Magnitude <= 8 then
									cftouch(obj7)
								end
								if obj7 ~= cftarget then
									cftarget = obj7
									cftargett = os.clock()
									cfprep(true)
								end
								local cfdir = cfaim - num4.Position
								local cfdist = cfdir.Magnitude
								local cfnp = cfaim
								if cfdist > 0.1 then
									local cfst = cfdtt or 0
									if cfst > 0.2 then cfst = 0.2 end
									cfnp = num4.Position + cfdir.Unit * math.min(25 * cfst, cfdist)
								end
								pcall(function() num4.CFrame = CFrame.new(cfnp) * CFrame.Angles(-1.5707963267948966, 0, 0)
									num4.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
									num4.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
								end)
								if os.clock() - cftargett > 6 then
									cffail(obj7)
									cftarget = nil
									cftargett = os.clock()
								end
							elseif num4 then
								cftarget = nil
								cfpause()
							end
						else
							cftarget = nil
							cfpause()
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
	end
	cftoggle = function(state)
		if state and not alive() then return end
		cfon = state
		if state then
			TweenService:Create(cftrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(true) }):Play()
			TweenService:Create(cfknob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(true) }):Play()
			cfdeadsv = nil
			cffull = false
			cfkillDone = false
			cfkillT = 0
			cfbagv = 0
			cfbagAmt = 0
			cfbagCap = 0
			cfbagEv = false
			cfbagt = 0
			cfroundLive = false
			cfaimpos = nil
			cfidle = false
			cfflingT = 0
			cfdied = false
			if cflabel then cflabel.Text = "Auto farm" end
			local rtpx = workspace:FindFirstChild("RoundTimerPart")
			local tmx = rtpx and (tonumber(rtpx:GetAttribute("Time")) or 0) or 0
			if tmx <= 0 then
				task.spawn(function() if _G.__MM2_Toast then pcall(_G.__MM2_Toast, "Farm", "Waiting for round", "FarmWarn") end end)
			elseif cfZombieRound() then
				task.spawn(function() if _G.__MM2_Toast then pcall(_G.__MM2_Toast, "Warning", "Zombie round", "FarmWarn") end end)
			end
			cfstart()
		else
			TweenService:Create(cftrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(false) }):Play()
			TweenService:Create(cfknob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(false) }):Play()
			cfstop()
			if cflabel then pcall(function() cflabel.Text = "Auto farm" end) end
		end
	end
	cfflingall = function()
		if not _G.__setFling or not _G.__flingc then return end
		if cfZombieRound() then return end
		cftarget = nil
		cfprep(false)
		task.spawn(function() local function runfling()
				_G.__setFling(true)
				local me = Players.LocalPlayer
				local killer = nil
				pcall(function() local md = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Modules")
					md = md and md:FindFirstChild("CurrentRoundClient")
					if md then
						local pd = require(md).PlayerData
						if type(pd) == "table" then
							for name, info in pairs(pd) do
								if type(info) == "table" and info.Role == "Murderer" and not info.Dead then
									local pl = Players:FindFirstChild(name)
									if pl and pl ~= me and not cfIsZombie(pl) then killer = pl end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
				if not killer then
					pcall(function() for _, q in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
							if q ~= me and q.Character and q.Character.Parent == workspace and not cfIsZombie(q) then
								local hasKnife = q.Character:FindFirstChild("Knife")
								if not hasKnife then
									local bp = q:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
									if bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Knife") then hasKnife = true end
								end
								if hasKnife then
									local hum = q.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
									if hum and hum.Health > 0 then killer = q break end
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end
				if killer and cfon then
					task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.7) if _G.__MM2_Toast then pcall(_G.__MM2_Toast, "Farm", "Flinging", "FarmWarn") end end)
					pcall(function() _G.__flingc(killer) end)
				end
			end
			pcall(runfling)
			pcall(function() _G.__setFling(false) end)
		end)
	end
	_G.__MM2_Tg("cf", cftoggle, function() return cfon end, cftrack, cfknob)
	cftrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() cftoggle(not cfon) end)
end)()
;(function() local fafrow = Instance.new("Frame")
	fafrow.Name = "FarmFlingRow"
	fafrow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
	fafrow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 554)
	fafrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	fafrow.BorderSizePixel = 0
	fafrow.Parent = mpage
	local faflabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Fling after auto farm", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", fafrow, "Active", true})
	local fafOn, faftoggle
	faflabel.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			faftoggle(not fafOn)
		end
	end)
	local faftrack, fafknob = __sw(fafrow, 0, 0)
	fafOn = false
	_G.__fafOn = false
	faftoggle = function(state)
		fafOn = state
		_G.__fafOn = state
		__knob(faftrack, fafknob, state)
	end
	_G.__MM2_Tg("faf", faftoggle, function() return fafOn end, faftrack, fafknob)
	faftrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() faftoggle(not fafOn) end)
end)()
;(function() local fakrow = Instance.new("Frame")
	fakrow.Name = "FarmKillRow"
	fakrow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
	fakrow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 584)
	fakrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	fakrow.BorderSizePixel = 0
	fakrow.Parent = mpage
	local faklabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Kill all after auto farm", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", fakrow, "Active", true})
	local fakOn, faktoggle
	faklabel.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			faktoggle(not fakOn)
		end
	end)
	local faktrack, fakknob = __sw(fakrow, 0, 0)
	fakOn = false
	_G.__fakOn = false
	faktoggle = function(state)
		fakOn = state
		_G.__fakOn = state
		__knob(faktrack, fakknob, state)
	end
	_G.__MM2_Tg("fak", faktoggle, function() return fakOn end, faktrack, fakknob)
	faktrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() faktoggle(not fakOn) end)
end)()
;(function() local ahrow = Instance.new("Frame")
	ahrow.Name = "FarmHopRow"
	ahrow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
	ahrow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 614)
	ahrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	ahrow.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ahrow.Parent = mpage
	local ahlabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Auto-hop", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", ahrow, "Active", true})
	local ahOn, ahtoggle
	ahlabel.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			ahtoggle(not ahOn)
		end
	end)
	local ahtrack, ahknob = __sw(ahrow, 0, 0)
	ahOn = false
	local ahLastHop = 0

	local function ahCount()
		local n = 0
		pcall(function() n = #Players:GetPlayers() end)
		return n
	end

	local function ahHop()
		if os.clock() - ahLastHop < 30 then return end
		ahLastHop = os.clock()
		task.delay(1.5, function() pcall(function() if not ahOn or not alive() then return end
				if ahCount() >= 5 then return end
				task.spawn(function() if _G.__MM2_Toast then pcall(_G.__MM2_Toast, "Farm", "Server hopping", "FarmWarn") end end)
				game:GetService("TeleportService"):Teleport(game.PlaceId)
			end)
		end)
	end

	local function ahEval()
		if not ahOn or not alive() then return end
		local n = ahCount()
		if n < 5 then
			ahHop()
		end
	end
	ahtoggle = function(state)
		ahOn = state
		if state then
			TweenService:Create(ahtrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(true) }):Play()
			TweenService:Create(ahknob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(true) }):Play()
			ahEval()
		else
			TweenService:Create(ahtrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(false) }):Play()
			TweenService:Create(ahknob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(false) }):Play()
		end
	end
	_G.__MM2_Tg("ah", ahtoggle, function() return ahOn end, ahtrack, ahknob)
	ahtrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() ahtoggle(not ahOn) end)
	pcall(function() _G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerRemoving, function() if ahOn then ahEval() end
		end)
	end)
	task.spawn(function() while alive() do
			task.wait(30)
			if ahOn then ahEval() end
		end
	end)
end)()

local function dropbuild()
	local drbtn = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "FlingTargetDropdown", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 259), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", mpage})
	__s(drbtn, BORDER, 1, true)
	local drlabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Fling: ...", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", drbtn})
	local drarrow = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "v", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "Parent", drbtn})
	local drlist = __mk("ScrollingFrame", {"Name", "FlingTargetList", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 299), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ZIndex", 10, "ScrollBarThickness", 0, "Visible", false, "Parent", mpage})
	local drstroke = __s(drlist, BORDER, 1, true)

	local function dropghost(ghost)
		local t = ghost and 1 or 0
		drlist.BackgroundTransparency = t
		drstroke.Transparency = t
		__optFade(drlist, t)
	end
	local dopen = false
	local dsel = nil
	local opendrop

	local function refreshdrop()
		for _, c in ipairs(drlist:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("TextButton") then pcall(function() c:Destroy() end) end
		end
		local y = 0
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= player then
				local b = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", drlist})
				__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", p.DisplayName .. " (@" .. p.Name .. ")", "Font", Enum.Font.Gotham, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", (dsel == p) and acc or TEXT_SECONDARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "Parent", b})
				local who = p
				b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() opendrop(false)
					drlabel.Text = "Fling: " .. string.sub(who.DisplayName, 1, 14)
					dsel = who
					_G.__flingt = who
					pcall(function() if flingin then return end
						local lp = player
						local ch = lp and lp.Character
						local hrp = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if not hrp then return end
						local tch = who.Character
						local thrp = tch and tch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if not thrp then return end
						local hum = tch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
						local okGo = false
						pcall(function() ch:PivotTo(thrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)) okGo = true end)
						if not okGo then return end
						flingin = true
						fstart = os.clock()
						_G.__flingc(who)
					end)
				end)
				y = y + 32
			end
		end
		drlist.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y)
		return y
	end

	local function fsync(n)
		if not alive() then return end
		_G.__mlay.f = n
		pcall(function() if _G.__mpApply then _G.__mpApply() end end)
	end
	opendrop = function(open)
		dopen = open
		drarrow.Rotation = open and 180 or 0
		if open then
			local yy = refreshdrop() or 0
			drlist.Visible = true
			dropghost(true)
			TweenService:Create(drlist, TWEEN_POP, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, yy), BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
			fsync(yy)
			TweenService:Create(drstroke, TWEEN_POP, { Transparency = 0 }):Play()
			__optFade(drlist, 0, TWEEN_POP)
		else
			fsync(0)
			TweenService:Create(drlist, TWEEN_EASE, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
			TweenService:Create(drstroke, TWEEN_EASE, { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
			__optFade(drlist, 1, TWEEN_EASE)
			task.delay(0.22, function() if not dopen then drlist.Visible = false end end)
		end
	end
	drbtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() opendrop(not dopen) end)
	pcall(function() _G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerAdded, function() if dopen and alive() then fsync(refreshdrop() or 0) end end)
		_G.__MM2_Conn(Players.PlayerRemoving, function() if dopen and alive() then fsync(refreshdrop() or 0) end end)
	end)
end
dropbuild()
do
	local nb = {}
	for _, n in ipairs({ "FlingDivider", "FlingHeader", "FlingBox", "FlingTargetDropdown", "FlingTargetList",
		"FlingMurdererButton", "FlingSheriffButton", "AntiFlingRow", "SpamFlingRow", "FarmDivider", "FarmHeader", "CoinFarmRow", "FarmFlingRow", "FarmKillRow", "FarmHopRow", "FarmBox" }) do
			local el = mpage:FindFirstChild(n)
			if el and el:IsA("GuiObject") then nb[n] = { xs = el.Position.X.Scale, xo = el.Position.X.Offset, yo = el.Position.Y.Offset, h = el.Size.Y.Offset } end
		end
	_G.__mpBase = nb
end
local cpage = __mk("ScrollingFrame", {"Name", "CombatPage", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Visible", false, "CanvasSize", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 675), "ScrollingDirection", Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, "ElasticBehavior", Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never, "ScrollBarThickness", 0, "Parent", inner})
pgs["Combat"] = cpage
do
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Name", "AimHeader", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 14), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 12), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", "Aim", "LayoutOrder", 1, "Parent", cpage})
	__mk("Frame", {"Name", "CombatSpacer", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 5), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 36), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "LayoutOrder", 2, "Parent", cpage})
end
_G.aimTarget = _G.aimTarget or "Torso"
do
	local targetDropdownOpen = false
	local targetDropdown = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "TargetDropdown", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 51), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "ZIndex", 10, "LayoutOrder", 3, "Parent", cpage})
	__s(targetDropdown, BORDER, 1, true)
	local targetDropdownLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Target: " .. string.upper(_G.aimTarget or "Torso"), "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 12, "TextColor3", LIGHT_TEXT, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", targetDropdown})
	local targetDropdownArrow = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "v", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "Parent", targetDropdown})
	_G.__MM2_AimTargetApply = function(name)
		if name == "Head" or name == "Torso" then
			_G.aimTarget = name
			targetDropdownLabel.Text = "Target: " .. name:upper()
		end
	end
	local targetList = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "TargetList", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 41), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ClipsDescendants", true, "Visible", false, "ZIndex", 5, "Parent", targetDropdown})
	local targetListStroke = __s(targetList, BORDER, 1, true)
	local setTargetOpen
	for i, optName in ipairs({ "Head", "Torso" }) do
		local opt = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, (i - 1) * 32), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", targetList})
		__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", optName, "Font", Enum.Font.Gotham, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", (_G.aimTarget == optName) and acc or TEXT_SECONDARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", opt})
		opt.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() _G.aimTarget = optName
			targetDropdownLabel.Text = "Target: " .. optName:upper()
			for _, c in ipairs(targetList:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("TextButton") then
					local lbl = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
					if lbl then lbl.TextColor3 = (lbl.Text == optName) and acc or __MM2_SecTxt() end
				end
			end
			setTargetOpen(false)
		end)
	end
	setTargetOpen = function(open)
		targetDropdownOpen = open
		targetDropdownArrow.Rotation = open and 180 or 0

		local function setGhost(ghost)
			local t = ghost and 1 or 0
			targetList.BackgroundTransparency = t
			targetListStroke.Transparency = t
			__optFade(targetList, t)
		end
		if open then
			targetList.Visible = true
			setGhost(true)
			TweenService:Create(targetList, TWEEN_POP, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 64), BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
			TweenService:Create(targetListStroke, TWEEN_POP, { Transparency = 0 }):Play()
			__optFade(targetList, 0, TWEEN_POP)
		else
			TweenService:Create(targetList, TWEEN_EASE, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
			TweenService:Create(targetListStroke, TWEEN_EASE, { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
			__optFade(targetList, 1, TWEEN_EASE)
			task.delay(0.22, function() if not targetDropdownOpen then targetList.Visible = false end
			end)
		end
	end
	targetDropdown.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setTargetOpen(not targetDropdownOpen) end)
end
do
	local combatBox = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "CombatBox", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 201), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 35), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ZIndex", 0, "Parent", cpage})
	__c(combatBox, UDim.new(0, 8))
	__s(combatBox, BORDER, 1)
	pcall(function() local od = inner:FindFirstChild("HitboxDivider")
		if od then od:Destroy() end
		local oh = inner:FindFirstChild("HitboxHeader")
		if oh then oh:Destroy() end
	end)
end
local wb = false
_G.__MM2_Wbon = false
_G.__MM2_Wallon = false
do
	local hboxbg = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "HitboxBox", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 154), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 297), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ZIndex", 0, "Parent", cpage})
	__c(hboxbg, UDim.new(0, 8))
	__s(hboxbg, BORDER, 1)
	__mk("Frame", {"Name", "HitboxDivider", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 1), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 261), "BackgroundColor3", Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80), "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", cpage})
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Name", "HitboxHeader", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 14), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 269), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", "Hitbox", "Parent", cpage})
	local hbox = false
	pcall(function() _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup or {}
		table.insert(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup, function() hbox = false end)
	end)
	local reach = 20

	local function stabtick()
		local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
		if not lp then return end
		local ch = lp.Character
		if not ch then return end
		local stab1, ev = select(2, _G.__MM2_Knife(false, nil, true))
		if not ev then return end
		if stab1 then pcall(function() stab1:FireServer() end) end
		local rt = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local rp = rt and rt.Position
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not cam then return end
		local vs = cam.ViewportSize
		local tly = 0
		pcall(function() local gs = game:GetService("GuiService")
			local ins = gs:GetGuiInset()
			if ins then tly = ins.Y end
		end)
		local cx, cy = vs.X / 2, tly + (vs.Y - tly) / 2
		local cmult = math.max(1, (reach or 20) * 2)
		local cdist = 1.5 * (1 + (cmult - 1) * 0.1)
		pcall(function() for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if p ~= lp and p.Character then
					local tr = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local hu = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if tr and hu and hu.Health > 0 then
						local hit = false
						if rp and (tr.Position - rp).Magnitude <= cdist then
							hit = true
						else
							local sp, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(tr.Position)
							if onScreen then
								local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - Vector2.new(cx, cy)).Magnitude
								if d <= reach then hit = true end
							end
						end
						if hit then
							ev:FireServer(tr)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	local hrow = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "HitboxRow", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 315), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", cpage})
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Knife hitbox extender", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", hrow})
	local htrack, hknob = __sw(hrow, 0, 0)
	local hclick = false
	local kfire = 0
	local shtoggle
	local showon, shtrack, shknob
	local shrow, shLbl

	local function htoggle(state)
		hbox = state
		shtoggle(state and showon)
		if state and not hclick then
			hclick = true
			_G.__hclick = _G.__MM2_Conn(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gpe) if ded then return end
				if gpe then return end
				if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
					and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
				if not hbox then return end
				if os.clock() - kfire < 0.9 then return end
				kfire = os.clock()
				stabtick()
			end)
		end
		__knob(htrack, hknob, state)
	end
	_G.__MM2_Tg("hitbox", htoggle, function() return hbox end, htrack, hknob)
	htrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() htoggle(not hbox) end)
	showon = false
	shrow = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "ShowKnifeRow", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 343), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", cpage})
	shLbl = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Show knife hitbox", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", shrow})
	shtrack = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 0), "BackgroundColor3", OFF_STATE, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", shrow})
	__c(shtrack, UDim.new(1, 0))
	shknob = __mk("Frame", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), "BackgroundColor3", Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100), "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", shtrack})
	__c(shknob, UDim.new(1, 0))
	shtoggle = function(state, instant)
		showon = state and hbox
		pcall(function() if _G.__showc then _G.__showc:Disconnect() end
			_G.__showc = nil
			pcall(function() if _G.__showb then _G.__showb:Destroy() end end)
			_G.__showb = nil
			pcall(function() local function sweep(p)
					if not p then return end
					for _, o in ipairs(p:GetChildren()) do
						if o.Name == "HitboxBall" then pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
					end
				end
				sweep(workspace.CurrentCamera)
				sweep(workspace)
			end)
		end)
		if state and hbox then
			pcall(function() local cam0 = workspace.CurrentCamera
				if not cam0 then return end
				local part = __mk("Part", {"Name", "HitboxBall", "Shape", Enum.PartType.Ball, "Anchored", true, "CanCollide", false, "CanTouch", false, "CanQuery", false, "CastShadow", false, "Material", Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, "Color", Color3.fromRGB(0, 122, 255), "Transparency", 0.85, "Size", Vector3.one * 3, "Parent", cam0})
				_G.__showb = part
				_G.__showc = _G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.RenderStepped, function() if not showon then return end
					pcall(function() if not part.Parent then
							if showon then shtoggle(true) end
							return
						end
						local sm = math.max(1, (reach or 20) * 2)
						local rr = 1.5 * (1 + (sm - 1) * 0.1)
						local character = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer.Character
						if not character then return end
						local position = nil
						local fromThrow = false
						local heldk = false
						local cs0 = game:GetService("CollectionService")
						if cs0 then
							for _, item29 in ipairs(cs0:GetTagged("ThrowingKnife")) do
								local hl0 = item29:FindFirstChild("HandleLink")
								local fg0 = hl0 and hl0.Value
								local pr0 = fg0 and fg0.Parent
								if pr0 and pr0:IsDescendantOf(character) then
									if item29:IsA("BasePart") then position = item29.Position end
									if not position and item29:IsA("Model") then position = item29:GetPivot().Position end
									fromThrow = true
									if position then break end
								end
							end
						end
						if not position then
							local kn0 = character:FindFirstChild("Knife")
							if not kn0 then
								for _, t in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
									if t:IsA("Tool") then
										local okIt, it = pcall(function() return t:GetAttribute("ItemType") end)
										if okIt and it == "Knife" then kn0 = t break end
									end
								end
							end
							kn0 = kn0 and kn0:FindFirstChild("Handle")
							position = kn0 and kn0.Position
							heldk = position ~= nil
						end
						if not position then
							local hr0 = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							position = hr0 and hr0.Position
						end
						if position then
							if fromThrow then
								part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 122, 255)
							else
								part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
							end
							part.Size = Vector3.one * rr * 2
							part.CFrame = CFrame.new(position)
							if _G.__hideb then
								part.Transparency = 1
							elseif fromThrow or heldk then
								part.Transparency = 0.85
							else
								part.Transparency = 1
							end
						end
					end)
				end)
			end)
			TweenService:Create(shtrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(true) }):Play()
			TweenService:Create(shknob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(true) }):Play()
		else
			if not hbox then
				__MM2_BlockPaint(shtrack, shknob, shLbl, true, false, instant)
			else
				TweenService:Create(shtrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(false) }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(shknob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(false) }):Play()
			end
		end
	end
	_G.__MM2_Tg("showhitbox", shtoggle, function() return showon end, shtrack, shknob)
	_G.__MM2_BlockedSyncs["showHitbox"] = function() pcall(shtoggle, showon) end
	shtoggle(showon, true)
	shtrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if not hbox then
			__MM2_BlockPaint(shtrack, shknob, shLbl, true, false)
			return
		end
		shtoggle(not showon)
	end)
	local drag = false
	local rrow = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "ReachRow", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 368), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", cpage})
	local rbar, rfill, rknob = __sl(rrow, 7, 0.2, LIME)
	local rval = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 12), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 4), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "20/100", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 10, "TextColor3", LIME, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, "Parent", rrow})

	local function reachApply(v)
		local n = tonumber(v) or 20
		if n ~= n then n = 20 end
		reach = math.clamp(math.floor(n + 0.5), 0, 100)
		local pct = reach / 100
		rfill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
		rknob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, -6, 0.5, -6)
		rval.Text = tostring(reach) .. "/100"
	end
	_G.__MM2_Val("reach", reachApply, function() return reach end, 0, 100)

	local function reachset(x)
		local absSize = rbar.AbsoluteSize.X
		if absSize <= 0 then return end
		local pct = math.clamp((x - rbar.AbsolutePosition.X) / absSize, 0, 1)
		reachApply(math.floor(pct * 100 + 0.5))
	end
	rbar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			drag = true
			reachset(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	__live(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
		if drag
			and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			reachset(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	__live(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			drag = false
		end
	end)
	pcall(function() if _G.__throwc then _G.__throwc:Disconnect() end end)
	_G.__throwc = game:GetService("CollectionService"):GetInstanceAddedSignal("ThrowingKnife"):Connect(function(part8) if not hbox then return end
		task.spawn(function() local linkTool = nil
			pcall(function() local hl = part8:WaitForChild("HandleLink", 3)
				local hv = hl and hl.Value
				local lt = hv and hv.Parent
				local chx = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer.Character
				if lt and lt:IsA("Tool") and chx and lt:IsDescendantOf(chx) then linkTool = lt end
			end)
			if not linkTool then return end
			local stabbed, handleTouched = select(2, _G.__MM2_Knife(false, linkTool, false, true))
			if not stabbed or not handleTouched then return end
			local tbl42 = {}
			while part8.Parent and hbox do
				local pm = math.max(1, (reach or 20) * 2)
				local radius = 1.5 * (1 + (pm - 1) * 0.1)
				pcall(function() local position = nil
					if part8:IsA("BasePart") then position = part8.Position end
					if not position and part8:IsA("Model") then position = part8:GetPivot().Position end
					if position then
						local lp0 = Players.LocalPlayer
						for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
							if player ~= lp0 and tbl42[player] ~= player.Character and player.Character then
								local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
								if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and (hrp.Position - position).Magnitude <= radius then
									tbl42[player] = player.Character
									stabbed:FireServer()
									handleTouched:FireServer(hrp)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
				task.wait(0.05)
			end
		end)
	end)
end
_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].gunBeamOn = false
do
	local ghrow = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "ShowGunHitboxRow", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 393), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", cpage})
