	__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Show gun hitbox", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", ghrow})
	local ghtrack, ghknob = __sw(ghrow, 0, 0)

	local function ghtoggle(state)
		_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].gunBeamOn = state
		__knob(ghtrack, ghknob, state)
	end
	_G.__MM2_Tg("gunbeam", ghtoggle, function() return _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].gunBeamOn end, ghtrack, ghknob)
	ghtrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() ghtoggle(not _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].gunBeamOn) end)

	local function gunToPos(v)
		if typeof(v) == "Vector3" then return v end
		if typeof(v) == "Instance" then
			if v:IsA("BasePart") then return v.Position end
			if v:IsA("Attachment") then return v.WorldPosition end
		end
		return nil
	end
	local GUNBEAM_GREEN = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
	local GUNBEAM_RED = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)

	local function isPlayerInst(o)
		if typeof(o) ~= "Instance" then return false end
		local m = o:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
		if not m then return false end
		return Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m) ~= nil
	end

	local function gunRayHitsPlayer(a, b)
		local dir = b - a
		if dir.Magnitude < 0.05 then return false end
		local ok, res = pcall(function() local prm = RaycastParams.new()
			prm.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			prm.RespectCanCollide = true
			local me = Players.LocalPlayer
			if me and me.Character then
				prm.FilterDescendantsInstances = { me.Character }
			end
			return workspace:Raycast(a, dir, prm)
		end)
		if not ok or not res then return false end
		local inst = res.Instance
		if not inst then return false end
		local m = inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
		if not m then return false end
		return Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m) ~= nil
	end
	_G.__gunbeamdraw = function(a, b, hit)
		local gbrt = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN]
		if not gbrt or not gbrt.gunBeamOn then return end
		if typeof(a) ~= "Vector3" or typeof(b) ~= "Vector3" then return end
		local dv = b - a
		local len = dv.Magnitude
		if len < 0.05 then return end
		local hitRed = false
		if hit ~= nil then
			if hit == true or isPlayerInst(hit) then
				hitRed = true
			end
		end
		if not hitRed then
			if gunRayHitsPlayer(a, b) then
				hitRed = true
			end
		end
		local col = hitRed and GUNBEAM_RED or GUNBEAM_GREEN
		local dbg = game:GetService("Debris")
		local mid = a + dv / 2
		local cyl = __mk("Part", {"Name", "GunBeam", "Shape", Enum.PartType.Cylinder, "Anchored", true, "CanCollide", false, "CanTouch", false, "CanQuery", false, "CastShadow", false, "Material", Enum.Material.Neon, "Color", col, "Transparency", 0.25, "Size", Vector3.new(len, 0.3, 0.3), "CFrame", CFrame.lookAt(mid, b) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi / 2, 0), "Parent", workspace})
		local mk = __mk("Part", {"Name", "GunBeamMark", "Shape", Enum.PartType.Ball, "Anchored", true, "CanCollide", false, "CanTouch", false, "CanQuery", false, "CastShadow", false, "Material", Enum.Material.Neon, "Color", col, "Transparency", 0.35, "Size", Vector3.one * 1.2, "CFrame", CFrame.new(b), "Parent", workspace})
		local fin
		fin = TweenService:Create(cyl, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0.85), { Size = Vector3.new(len, 0, 0) })
		fin.Completed:Connect(function() pcall(function() cyl:Destroy() end)
			pcall(function() mk:Destroy() end)
		end)
		fin:Play()
		TweenService:Create(mk, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0.8), { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
		dbg:AddItem(cyl, 1.3)
		dbg:AddItem(mk, 1.3)
	end
	pcall(function() if _G.__gunbeamconn then pcall(function() _G.__gunbeamconn:Disconnect() end) end
		_G.__gunbeamconn = nil
	end)
	task.spawn(function() local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
		local cs = rs:WaitForChild("ClientServices", 30)
		local ws = cs and cs:WaitForChild("WeaponService", 30)
		local gf = ws and ws:WaitForChild("GunFired", 30)
		if not gf then return end
		if not alive() then return end
		_G.__gunbeamconn = _G.__MM2_Conn(gf.OnClientEvent, function(tool, a, b, hit) local me = Players.LocalPlayer
			local ch = me and me.Character
			local bp = me and me:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
			local ap = gunToPos(a)
			local mine = false
			if typeof(tool) == "Instance" then
				mine = (ch and tool:IsDescendantOf(ch)) or (bp and tool:IsDescendantOf(bp))
			else
				local hrp = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if hrp and typeof(ap) == "Vector3" then
					mine = (ap - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= 6
				end
			end
			if not mine then return end
			pcall(function() _G.__gunbeamdraw(ap, gunToPos(b), hit) end)
		end)
	end)
	pcall(function() _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup or {}
		table.insert(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup, function() _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].gunBeamOn = false
			pcall(function() for _, o in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
					if o.Name == "GunBeam" or o.Name == "GunBeamMark" then
						pcall(function() o:Destroy() end)
					end
				end
			end)
		end)
	end)
end
do
	local function wbbuild()
		local wbRow = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "GunWallbangRow", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 418), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", cpage})
		local wbLbl = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Gun wallbang", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", wbRow})
		local wbTrack = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 0), "BackgroundColor3", OFF_STATE, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", wbRow})
		__c(wbTrack, UDim.new(1, 0))
		local wbKnob = __mk("Frame", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), "BackgroundColor3", Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100), "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", wbTrack})
		__c(wbKnob, UDim.new(1, 0))

		local function wtoggle(state)
			if state and _G.__MM2_Wallon then
				pcall(function() if _G.__wbsync then _G.__wbsync() end end)
				pcall(function() if _G.__wcsync then _G.__wcsync() end end)
				return
			end
			wb = state
			_G.__MM2_Wbon = state
			if state and not _G.__silent then
				wb = false
				_G.__MM2_Wbon = false
				pcall(function() if _G.__wbsync then _G.__wbsync() end end)
				pcall(function() if _G.__wcsync then _G.__wcsync() end end)
				return
			end
			pcall(function() if _G.__wbsync then _G.__wbsync() end end)
			pcall(function() if _G.__wcsync then _G.__wcsync() end end)
		end
		_G.__wbsync = function(instant)
			if _G.__MM2_Wallon or not _G.__silent then
				wbTrack.Active = false
				__MM2_BlockPaint(wbTrack, wbKnob, wbLbl, true, false, instant)
			elseif wb then
				wbTrack.Active = true
				if wbLbl then wbLbl.TextColor3 = __MM2_BaseTxt() end
				TweenService:Create(wbTrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(true) }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(wbKnob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(true) }):Play()
			else
				wbTrack.Active = true
				if wbLbl then wbLbl.TextColor3 = __MM2_BaseTxt() end
				TweenService:Create(wbTrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(false) }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(wbKnob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(false) }):Play()
			end
		end
		_G.__MM2_BlockedSyncs["wallbang"] = _G.__wbsync
		pcall(_G.__wbsync, true)
		_G.__MM2_Tg("wb", wtoggle, function() return wb end, wbTrack, wbKnob)
		wbTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() wtoggle(not wb) end)
	end
	wbbuild()
	local aimbotRow = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "AimbotRow", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 97), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "LayoutOrder", 5, "Parent", cpage})
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Aimbot", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", aimbotRow})
	local aimbotTrack, aimbotKnob = __sw(aimbotRow, 0, 0)
	local aimbotOn = false
	local lockedHead = nil
	local aimbotPress
	local wallon = false

	local function combatHasTool(p, ...)
		return hasTool(p, ...)
	end
	local srvModCache = nil
	local srvModCacheT = 0

	local function serverRoleOf(p)
		local role = nil
		pcall(function() if not p then return end
			if not srvModCache or os.clock() - srvModCacheT > 5 then
				srvModCache = nil
				for _, d in ipairs(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):GetDescendants()) do
					if d.Name == "CurrentRoundClient" and d:IsA("ModuleScript") then
						srvModCache = d
						break
					end
				end
				srvModCacheT = os.clock()
			end
			if srvModCache then
				local tbl = require(srvModCache)
				if type(tbl) == "table" then
					local datas = { tbl.PlayerData, tbl.Roles, tbl.Data, tbl.players }
					local keys = { p.Name }
					pcall(function() keys[#keys + 1] = p.DisplayName end)
					pcall(function() keys[#keys + 1] = tostring(p.UserId) end)
					for _, t in ipairs(datas) do
						if type(t) == "table" then
							for _, k in ipairs(keys) do
								local e = t[k]
								if type(e) == "string" then role = e break end
								if type(e) == "table" then
									if type(e.Role) == "string" then role = e.Role break end
									if type(e.role) == "string" then role = e.role break end
								end
							end
						end
						if role then break end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		if type(role) == "string" then
			local ok, rn = pcall(function() return role:lower():gsub("%s+", "") end)
			if ok and rn and rn:find("murder", 1, true) then return "murderer" end
			if ok then return rn end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function anyknife()
		local found = false
		pcall(function() for _, q in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				local ch = q.Character
				if ch then
					for _, c in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do
						if c:IsA("Tool") and c.Name == "Knife" then found = true return end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		return found
	end
	_G.__MM2_Murd = nil
	_G.__MM2_MurdT = 0
	_G.__MM2_Murderer = function(p)
		if p then
			if p ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
				_G.__MM2_Murd = p
				_G.__MM2_MurdT = os.clock()
			end
			return _G.__MM2_Murd
		end
		local m = _G.__MM2_Murd
		if not m then return nil end
		if m == Players.LocalPlayer then _G.__MM2_Murd = nil return nil end
		if m.Parent ~= Players then _G.__MM2_Murd = nil return nil end
		local ch = m.Character
		if not ch or not ch.Parent then _G.__MM2_Murd = nil return nil end
		local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum and hum.Health <= 0 then _G.__MM2_Murd = nil return nil end
		local armed = false
		pcall(function() for _, c in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("Tool") and (c.Name == "Gun" or c.Name == "Revolver") then armed = true break end
			end
		end)
		if armed then _G.__MM2_Murd = nil return nil end
		if os.clock() - _G.__MM2_MurdT > 25 then _G.__MM2_Murd = nil return nil end
		return m
	end

	local function combatRole(p, noCache)
		local ch0 = p.Character
		if ch0 then
			for _, c in ipairs(ch0:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("Tool") and c.Name == "Knife" then
					if not noCache then _G.__MM2_Murderer(p) end
					return "Murderer"
				end
			end
		end
		local bp0 = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
		if bp0 then
			for _, c in ipairs(bp0:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("Tool") and c.Name == "Knife" then
					if not noCache then _G.__MM2_Murderer(p) end
					return "Murderer"
				end
			end
		end
		if combatHasTool(p, "Gun", "Revolver") then return "Sheriff" end
		local sr = nil
		pcall(function() sr = serverRoleOf(p) end)
		if sr == "murderer" then
			if noCache then return "Murderer" end
			_G.__MM2_Murderer(p)
			if not anyknife() then return "Murderer" end
		elseif sr == "sheriff" or sr == "hero" then
			return "Sheriff"
		end
		if noCache then return nil end
		local cmr = nil
		pcall(function() cmr = _G.__MM2_Murderer() end)
		if cmr == p then return "Murderer" end
		return nil
	end

	local function setAimbotOn(state)
		aimbotOn = state
		if not state then lockedHead = nil end
		__knob(aimbotTrack, aimbotKnob, state)
	end
	_G.__MM2_Tg("aimbot", setAimbotOn, function() return aimbotOn end, aimbotTrack, aimbotKnob)
	aimbotTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setAimbotOn(not aimbotOn) end)
	local aimbotKey = nil
	local capturingAimbotKey = false
	local aimbotKeybind = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "AimbotKeybind", "Size", UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 24), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -79, 0, 0), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "AutoButtonColor", false, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 10, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Text", "...", "TextTruncate", Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, "ClipsDescendants", true, "Parent", aimbotRow})
	__c(aimbotKeybind, UDim.new(0, 8))
	__s(aimbotKeybind, Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50), 1, true)
	aimbotKeybind.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() capturingAimbotKey = true
		aimbotKeybind.Text = "..."
	end)
	local getAimTarget
	aimbotPress = function()
		if aimbotOn then
			if lockedHead then
				lockedHead = nil
			else
				lockedHead = getAimTarget()
			end
		end
	end
	_G.__MM2_UIS(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gpe) if not alive() then return end
		if ded then return end
		if gpe then return end
		if capturingAimbotKey then
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
				aimbotKey = input.KeyCode
				local nm = tostring(input.KeyCode):gsub("Enum.KeyCode.", "")
				local short = { LeftControl = "LCtrl", RightControl = "RCtrl", LeftShift = "LShift", RightShift = "RShift", LeftAlt = "LAlt", RightAlt = "RAlt", CapsLock = "Caps", Space = "Spc", Backspace = "Bspc" }
				nm = short[nm] or nm
				if #nm > 5 then nm = nm:sub(1, 5) end
				aimbotKeybind.Text = nm
				_G.aimbotKeyName = nm
				if watermarkAimKeyLabel then
					pcall(function() watermarkAimKeyLabel.Text = "[<font size=\"13\">" .. nm .. "</font>]" end)
				end
				aimbotKeybind.TextColor3 = __MM2_BaseTxt()
			end
			capturingAimbotKey = false
			return
		end
		if aimbotKey and input.KeyCode == aimbotKey then
			aimbotPress()
		end
	end)
	getAimTarget = function()
		local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not camera then return nil end
		local mousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
		pcall(function() if UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.MouseEnabled then
				local vs = camera.ViewportSize
				mousePos = Vector2.new(vs.X / 2, vs.Y / 2)
			end
		end)
		local best, bestDist = nil, 50
		for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= Players.LocalPlayer and p.Character then
				local head = nil
				if _G.aimTarget == "Head" then
					head = p.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
				else
					head = p.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
					or p.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
					or p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				end
				local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if head and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
					local sp, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position)
					if onScreen then
						local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - mousePos).Magnitude
						if d < bestDist then
							local blocked = false
							if wallon then
								local params = RaycastParams.new()
								params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
								params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { Players.LocalPlayer.Character, p.Character }
								local dir = (head.Position - camera.CFrame.Position)
								local aHit = workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, dir.Unit * dir.Magnitude, params)
								blocked = aHit ~= nil
							end
							if not blocked then
								best, bestDist = head, d
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return best
	end
	local silent = false
	pcall(function() workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom end)
	pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("Aimbot") end)
	_G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.RenderStepped, function() if not alive() then return end
		pcall(function() local g = aimbotOn and lockedHead ~= nil
			if _G.watermarkAimNameLabel then
				_G.watermarkAimNameLabel.TextColor3 = g and acc or __MM2_BaseTxt()
				if watermarkAimKeyLabel then watermarkAimKeyLabel.TextColor3 = g and acc or __MM2_WhiteTxt() end
			end
		end)
		if aimbotOn and lockedHead then
			local head = lockedHead
			if head then
				local hn = head.Name
				if hn ~= "Head"
					and hn ~= "Torso"
					and hn ~= "UpperTorso"
					and hn ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then lockedHead = nil head = nil end
			end
			local char = head and head.Parent
			local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if head and head.Parent and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
				local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
				local cur = cam.CFrame
				cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cur.Position, head.Position)
				pcall(function() local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
					local ch = lp and lp.Character
					local hrp = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if hrp then
						local tp = head.Position
						hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(tp.X, hrp.Position.Y, tp.Z))
					end
				end)
			else
				lockedHead = nil
			end
		end
	end)
	local hideCircleOn = false
	local hideRow = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "HideCircleRow", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 38), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 157), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "LayoutOrder", 6, "Parent", cpage})
	local hideLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Hide FOV circle", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", hideRow})
	local hideTrack, hideKnob = __sw(hideRow, 0, 0)

	local function setHideCircleOn(state)
		hideCircleOn = state
		__knob(hideTrack, hideKnob, state)
	end
	_G.__MM2_Tg("hideCircle", setHideCircleOn, function() return hideCircleOn end, hideTrack, hideKnob)
	hideTrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setHideCircleOn(not hideCircleOn) end)
	__mk("Frame", {"Name", "CombatGap", "Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 283), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "LayoutOrder", 49, "Parent", cpage})
	local wrow = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "WallCheckRow", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 201), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "LayoutOrder", 8, "Parent", cpage})
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Wall check", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", wrow})
	local wtrack, wknob = __sw(wrow, 0, 0)
	_G.__wcsync = function()
		if _G.__MM2_Wbon then
			wtrack.Active = false
			TweenService:Create(wtrack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(false) }):Play()
			TweenService:Create(wknob, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(false) }):Play()
		else
			wtrack.Active = true
			__knob(wtrack, wknob, wallon)
		end
	end

	local function setWallCheckOn(state)
		if state and _G.__MM2_Wbon then
			pcall(function() if _G.__wcsync then _G.__wcsync() end end)
			pcall(function() if _G.__wbsync then _G.__wbsync() end end)
			return
		end
		wallon = state
		_G.__MM2_Wallon = state
		pcall(function() if _G.__wcsync then _G.__wcsync() end end)
		pcall(function() if _G.__wbsync then _G.__wbsync() end end)
	end
	_G.__MM2_Tg("wallcheck", setWallCheckOn, function() return wallon end, wtrack, wknob)
	wtrack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setWallCheckOn(not wallon) end)
	pcall(function() if _G.__wbsync then _G.__wbsync() end end)
	pcall(function() if _G.__wcsync then _G.__wcsync() end end)
	local throwev = nil
	local srad = 150
	local knifecf
	local kspeed = 700
	_G.__MM2_VisParams = _G.__MM2_VisParams or RaycastParams.new()
	_G.__MM2_TargetVisible = function(tchar, origin)
		if not tchar then return true end
		local lpV = Players.LocalPlayer
		local chV = lpV and lpV.Character
		local camV = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not chV or not camV then return true end
		local vp = _G.__MM2_VisParams
		vp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		vp.FilterDescendantsInstances = { chV }
		vp.RespectCanCollide = true
		local eye = origin or camV.CFrame.Position
		local pts = {}
		for _, nm in ipairs({ "HumanoidRootPart", "Head", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Torso" }) do
			local pt = tchar:FindFirstChild(nm)
			if pt and pt:IsA("BasePart") then pts[#pts + 1] = pt end
		end
		if #pts == 0 then
			local pp = tchar.PrimaryPart
			if pp and pp:IsA("BasePart") then pts[#pts + 1] = pp end
		end
		if #pts == 0 then return true end
		local seen = 0
		for _, pt in ipairs(pts) do
			local dir = pt.Position - eye
			if dir.Magnitude < 0.001 then
				seen = seen + 1
			else
				local hit = workspace:Raycast(eye, dir.Unit * dir.Magnitude, vp)
				if not hit then
					seen = seen + 1
				else
					local hi = hit.Instance
					local pass = false
					if hi and hi:IsA("BasePart") then
						pass = hi:IsDescendantOf(tchar) or hi.Transparency > 0.5 or hi.CanCollide == false
						if not pass then
							local anc = hi
							while anc and anc ~= workspace do
								if anc.Name == "Truss" or anc.Name == "Beam" then pass = true break end
								anc = anc.Parent
							end
						end
					end
					if pass then seen = seen + 1 end
				end
			end
		end
		return seen >= math.max(1, math.ceil(#pts / 2))
	end
	local boost = 1
	local oldping = {}
	local oldlead = {}
	local oldpos = {}

	local function guess(part, tchar, travel)
		local base = part.CFrame
		if boost <= 0 then return base end
		local hrpT = tchar and tchar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local vel = nil
		pcall(function() if hrpT then vel = hrpT.AssemblyLinearVelocity end end)
		pcall(function() local hum0 = tchar and tchar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if hum0 and hum0.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.1 then
				local grounded = true
				pcall(function() local st0 = hum0:GetState()
					grounded = st0 ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping and st0 ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall and st0 ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
				end)
				if grounded then
					local ws = hum0.WalkSpeed
					if ws and ws > 1 then vel = hum0.MoveDirection * ws end
				end
			end
		end)
		pcall(function() local humS = tchar and tchar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if humS and humS.MoveDirection.Magnitude <= 0.1 and hrpT then
				local sv = hrpT.AssemblyLinearVelocity
				if sv and sv.Magnitude < 2 then vel = nil end
			end
		end)
		local tkey0 = nil
		if tchar then tkey0 = "k:" .. tchar.Name end
		if tkey0 then
			local nowT = os.clock()
			local curP = part.Position
			local ph = oldpos[tkey0]
			if ph and nowT > ph.t then
				local dt = nowT - ph.t
				if dt > 0.01 and dt < 0.25 then
					local mv = (curP - ph.pos) / dt
					if mv.Magnitude > 0.5 and (curP - ph.pos).Magnitude >= 0.5 and (not vel or vel.Magnitude < 0.5) then vel = mv end
				end
			end
			oldpos[tkey0] = { pos = curP, t = nowT }
			if nowT - (_G.__MM2_PruneT or 0) > 1 then
				_G.__MM2_PruneT = nowT
				for pk, pv in pairs(oldpos) do
					if type(pv) == "table" and type(pv.t) == "number" and nowT - pv.t > 30 then oldpos[pk] = nil end
				end
			end
		end
		if not vel or vel.Magnitude < 0.5 then return base end
		if tkey0 then
			_G.__vfit = _G.__vfit or {}
			_G.__vspoof = _G.__vspoof or {}
			_G.__vturn = _G.__vturn or {}
			_G.__vring = _G.__vring or {}
			local fit = _G.__vfit[tkey0]
			_G.__vsign = _G.__vsign or {}
			local vsign = _G.__vsign[tkey0] or 0
			local cs = (vel.Y > 2 and 1) or (vel.Y < -2 and -1) or 0
			local flip = (cs ~= 0 and vsign ~= 0 and cs ~= vsign)
			if fit then
				fit = fit:Lerp(vel, 0.3)
				if flip then fit = Vector3.new(fit.X, vel.Y, fit.Z) end
			else
				fit = vel
			end
			if cs ~= 0 then _G.__vsign[tkey0] = cs end
			_G.__vfit[tkey0] = fit
			local shv = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z)
			local fhv = Vector3.new(fit.X, 0, fit.Z)
			local turn = 0
			if shv.Magnitude > 1 and fhv.Magnitude > 1 then
				turn = math.acos(math.clamp(shv.Unit:Dot(fhv.Unit), -1, 1)) / math.pi
			end
			local fused = fit:Lerp(vel, 0.4 + 0.6 * math.clamp(turn * 2.2, 0, 1))
			local trust = 0
			local liveE = nil
			pcall(function() if hrpT then liveE = hrpT.AssemblyLinearVelocity end end)
			if liveE then
				local ph2 = Vector3.new(fused.X, 0, fused.Z)
				local eh2 = Vector3.new(liveE.X, 0, liveE.Z)
				local pm, em = ph2.Magnitude, eh2.Magnitude
				if pm < 1 and em < 1 then
					trust = 1
				elseif pm >= 1 and em >= 1 then
					local ratio = em / pm
					if ratio <= 1.5 and ratio >= 0.6 then
						local align = ph2.Unit:Dot(eh2.Unit)
						if align >= 0.7 then
							trust = math.clamp((align - 0.7) / 0.25, 0, 1) * (1 - math.clamp(math.abs(ratio - 1) / 0.4, 0, 1))
						end
					end
				end
			end
			if liveE and trust <= 0 then
				local sp = _G.__vspoof[tkey0] or 0
				if sp < 20 then _G.__vspoof[tkey0] = sp + 1 end
				vel = liveE
			else
				local sp = _G.__vspoof[tkey0] or 0
				if sp > 0 then _G.__vspoof[tkey0] = sp - 1 end
				vel = fused
			end
			_G.__vturn[tkey0] = turn
			local rg = _G.__vring[tkey0] or { n = 0, i = 0, t = {}, x = {}, z = {} }
			rg.i = rg.i % 8 + 1
			rg.t[rg.i] = os.clock()
			rg.x[rg.i] = vel.X
			rg.z[rg.i] = vel.Z
			if rg.n < 8 then rg.n = rg.n + 1 end
			_G.__vring[tkey0] = rg
		end
		local steady = 1
		local tkey = tchar and tchar.Name or nil
		if tkey then
			local nowH = os.clock()
			for k, e in pairs(oldlead) do
				if type(e) ~= "table" or type(e.t) ~= "number" or nowH - e.t > 60 then oldlead[k] = nil end
			end
			local pv = oldlead[tkey]
			local prv = (type(pv) == "table" and pv.v) or pv
			if prv then
				local hv = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z)
				local hp = Vector3.new(prv.X, 0, prv.Z)
				if hv.Magnitude > 0.5 and hp.Magnitude > 0.5 then
					local dp = (hv.Unit:Dot(hp.Unit) + 1) / 2
					steady = 0.45 + 0.55 * math.clamp(dp, 0, 1)
				end
			end
			oldlead[tkey] = { v = vel, t = os.clock() }
		end
		local ping = 0
		pcall(function() ping = Players.LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() end)
		if type(ping) ~= "number" then ping = 0 end
		table.insert(oldping, ping)
		if #oldping > 10 then table.remove(oldping, 1) end
		local psum = 0
		for _, v in ipairs(oldping) do psum = psum + v end
		ping = psum / #oldping
		_G.__jit = _G.__jit or { rtt = nil, jit = 0 }
		_G.__lshot = _G.__lshot or {}
		local jr = _G.__jit
		if jr.rtt == nil then jr.rtt = ping jr.jit = 0 else jr.jit = jr.jit * 0.9 + math.abs(ping - jr.rtt) * 0.1 jr.rtt = jr.rtt * 0.82 + ping * 0.18 end
		local nowS = os.clock()
		local stale = 0
		if tkey and _G.__lshot[tkey] then stale = math.clamp(nowS - _G.__lshot[tkey], 0, 0.04) end
		if tkey then _G.__lshot[tkey] = nowS end
		local lscale = 1.7
		local ld = math.min((ping * 0.5 + jr.jit * 0.25 + stale + travel + 0.02) * boost * math.max(steady, 0.72) * lscale, 1)
		if ld <= 0 then return base end
		local np
		do
			local hx, hz = vel.X, vel.Z
			local hspan = ld
			local prevAir = tkey and _G.__apex and _G.__apex[tkey] and _G.__apex[tkey].air or false
			if ld > 0 and not prevAir and (hx * hx + hz * hz) > 0 then
				local coh, om = 1, 0
				pcall(function() local rg = tkey0 and _G.__vring and _G.__vring[tkey0] or nil
					if rg and (rg.n or 0) >= 4 then
						local nowR = os.clock()
						local sx, sz, n = 0, 0, 0
						local prevA, turn, tn, oldest = nil, 0, 0, nowR
						for j = 0, (rg.n or 0) - 1 do
							local idx = ((rg.i - j - 1) % 8) + 1
							local t = rg.t[idx]
							if not t or nowR - t > 0.5 then break end
							local hx0, hz0 = rg.x[idx], rg.z[idx]
							local m = math.sqrt(hx0 * hx0 + hz0 * hz0)
							if m > 0 then
								sx = sx + hx0 / m
								sz = sz + hz0 / m
								n = n + 1
								local ang = math.atan2(hz0, hx0)
								if prevA then
									local d = ang - prevA
									while d > math.pi do d = d - 6.2831853 end
									while d < -math.pi do d = d + 6.2831853 end
									turn = turn + d
									tn = tn + 1
								end
								prevA = ang
								oldest = t
							end
						end
						if n >= 2 then
							coh = math.clamp(math.sqrt(sx * sx + sz * sz) / n, 0, 1)
							local elapsed = nowR - oldest
							if tn >= 1 and elapsed > 1e-3 then om = -turn / elapsed end
						end
					end
				end)
				local vturn = (_G.__vturn and tkey0 and _G.__vturn[tkey0]) or 0
				local conf = math.clamp(coh, 0, 1) * (1 - math.clamp(vturn, 0, 1) * 0.5)
				if om ~= 0 then
					local ang = math.clamp(om * ld * 0.5 * conf, -0.6, 0.6)
					local c, s = math.cos(ang), math.sin(ang)
					hx, hz = hx * c - hz * s, hx * s + hz * c
				end
				hspan = ld * math.clamp(0.2 + 0.8 * conf, 0.2, 1)
			end
			local vY0 = vel.Y * ld
			local vcu = 1.5
			local vcd = 5
			if vY0 > vcu then vY0 = vcu end
			if vY0 < -vcd then vY0 = -vcd end
			np = Vector3.new(base.Position.X + hx * hspan, base.Position.Y + vY0, base.Position.Z + hz * hspan)
		end
		local apext = _G.__apex or {}
		_G.__apex = apext
		local vyA = vel.Y
		local nowA = os.clock()
		local ae = tkey and apext[tkey] or nil
		local airA = false
		pcall(function() local gp = part and part.Position or nil
			if not gp then return end
			_G.__gflr = _G.__gflr or {}
			local fkey = (tkey or "?") .. ":" .. (part and part.Name or "?")
			local rp0 = RaycastParams.new()
			rp0.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			local fl0 = {}
			if tchar and tchar.Parent then table.insert(fl0, tchar) end
			rp0.FilterDescendantsInstances = fl0
			rp0.RespectCanCollide = true
			local reach = 8 + 6 + math.abs(vel.Y) * 0.2
			if reach < 8 then reach = 8 end
			if reach > 60 then reach = 60 end
			local hit0 = workspace:Raycast(gp, Vector3.new(0, -1, 0) * reach, rp0)
			if not hit0 then airA = true return end
			local clr = gp.Y - hit0.Position.Y
			local base0 = _G.__gflr[fkey]
			if math.abs(vel.Y) < 1 then
				if base0 then
					if clr < base0 then base0 = base0 * 0.7 + clr * 0.3 else base0 = base0 * 0.98 + clr * 0.02 end
				else
					base0 = clr
				end
				_G.__gflr[fkey] = base0
			end
			local floor0 = base0 or 4
			local tol = math.max(floor0 * 0.35, 1)
			airA = clr > floor0 + tol
			if not airA then
				local acc0 = nil
				if ae and ae.py ~= nil and ae.t then
					local dtA = nowA - (ae.t or nowA)
					if dtA > 0.01 then acc0 = (vel.Y - ae.py) / dtA end
				end
				if acc0 and acc0 < -98 and math.abs(vel.Y) > 4 and clr > floor0 + 0.35 then airA = true end
			end
		end)
		if tkey and ae then
			local takeoff = (ae.air ~= nil and ae.air ~= airA)
			local flipped = (ae.py ~= nil and ae.py > 8 and vyA < -8)
			if takeoff or flipped then ae = nil end
		end
		local gA = 196.2
		if airA then pcall(function() gA = workspace.Gravity end) end
		if type(gA) ~= "number" or gA <= 0 then gA = 196.2 end
		if tkey then
			apext[tkey] = { py = vyA, fy = np.Y, t = nowA, air = airA }
			for k, e in pairs(apext) do if nowA - (e.t or 0) > 30 then apext[k] = nil end end
			_G.__jpair = _G.__jpair or {}
			_G.__jumpV = _G.__jumpV or {}
			_G.__jtoff = _G.__jtoff or {}
			local wasAir = _G.__jpair[tkey]
			if airA and not wasAir then
				_G.__jtoff[tkey] = nowA
			end
			if airA and vyA > 8 then
				local tof2 = _G.__jtoff[tkey]
				if tof2 then
					local est = vyA + gA * math.max(0, nowA - tof2)
					if est > 0 and est < 200 then
						local pj = _G.__jumpV[tkey]
						_G.__jumpV[tkey] = (pj and pj * 0.7 + est * 0.3) or est
					end
				end
			end
			if not airA then
				_G.__jtoff[tkey] = nil
			end
			_G.__jpair[tkey] = airA
		end
		if not airA and tkey then apext[tkey] = nil end
		if airA then
			local vyE = vyA
			if tkey then
				local jv = _G.__jumpV[tkey]
				local tof = _G.__jtoff[tkey]
				if jv and jv > 0 and tof and (nowA - tof) <= jv / gA + 0.1 then
					local modeled = jv - gA * math.max(0, nowA - tof)
					if modeled > vyE then vyE = modeled end
				end
			end
			local drop = 0
			local vyUse = vyE * ld
			local vcu2 = 1.5
			local vcd2 = 5
			if vyUse > vcu2 then vyUse = vcu2 end
			if vyUse < -vcd2 then vyUse = -vcd2 end
			np = Vector3.new(np.X, base.Position.Y + vyUse - drop, np.Z)
			pcall(function() local rp = RaycastParams.new()
				rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
				local filt = {}
				if tchar and tchar.Parent then table.insert(filt, tchar) end
				local olp = Players.LocalPlayer
				local och = olp and olp.Character
				if och then table.insert(filt, och) end
				rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = filt
				local hit = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(np.X, base.Position.Y + 2, np.Z), Vector3.new(0, -1, 0) * 40, rp)
				if hit then
					local floor = hit.Position.Y + 2
					if np.Y < floor then np = Vector3.new(np.X, floor, np.Z) end
				end
			end)
		end
		local aimDrop = 2
		if aimDrop ~= 0 then
			np = Vector3.new(np.X, np.Y - aimDrop, np.Z)
		end
		pcall(function() local rp3 = RaycastParams.new()
			rp3.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			local fl3 = {}
			if tchar and tchar.Parent then table.insert(fl3, tchar) end
			local lp3 = Players.LocalPlayer
			local c3 = lp3 and lp3.Character
			if c3 then table.insert(fl3, c3) end
			rp3.FilterDescendantsInstances = fl3
			rp3.RespectCanCollide = true
			local gh3 = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(np.X, base.Position.Y + 2, np.Z), Vector3.new(0, -1, 0) * 60, rp3)
			if gh3 then
				local floor3 = gh3.Position.Y + 1.5
				if np.Y < floor3 then np = Vector3.new(np.X, floor3, np.Z) end
			end
		end)
		if np.X ~= np.X or np.Y ~= np.Y or np.Z ~= np.Z then return base end
		return CFrame.new(np) * (base - base.Position)
	end
	local scircle = nil
	pcall(function() if typeof(Drawing) == "table" and typeof(Drawing.new) == "function" then
			scircle = Drawing.new("Circle")
			scircle.Visible = false
			scircle.Radius = srad
			scircle.Thickness = 2
			scircle.Color = PURE_WHITE
			scircle.Transparency = 0
			scircle.Filled = false
			scircle.NumSides = 128
			pcall(function() _G.__draws = _G.__draws or {} table.insert(_G.__draws, scircle) end)
		end
	end)
	local fovCircle = nil
	pcall(function() local fg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		fg.Name = "FovGui"
		fg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
		fg.ResetOnSpawn = false
		fg.DisplayOrder = 999
		fg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
		fg.Parent = _G.pgui
		local fr = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "FovRing", "AnchorPoint", Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Active", false, "Size", UDim2.fromOffset(srad * 2, srad * 2), "Parent", fg})
		__c(fr, UDim.new(1, 0))
		__mk("UIStroke", {"Color", PURE_WHITE, "Thickness", 2, "Transparency", 0, "ApplyStrokeMode", Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, "Parent", fr})
		fovCircle = fg
		pcall(function() _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup or {}
			table.insert(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup, function() pcall(function() fg:Destroy() end) end)
		end)
	end)
	local srow = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "SilentAimRow", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 127), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "LayoutOrder", 5, "Parent", cpage})
	__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "Silent aim", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", TEXT_PRIMARY, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Parent", srow})
	local strack, sknob = __sw(srow, 0, 0)
	local fov = 150
	local FOV_MIN, FOV_MAX = 50, 500
	local draggingFov = false
	local fovBar, fovFill, fovKnob = __sl(hideRow, 28, 0.222, LIME)
	local fovValueLabel = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 12), "Position", UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 25), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "150/500", "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 10, "TextColor3", LIME, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, "Parent", hideRow})

	local function fovApply(v)
		local n = tonumber(v) or 150
		if n ~= n then n = 150 end
		fov = math.clamp(math.floor(n + 0.5), FOV_MIN, FOV_MAX)
		local pct = (fov - FOV_MIN) / (FOV_MAX - FOV_MIN)
		srad = fov
		pcall(function() if scircle then scircle.Radius = fov end end)
		fovFill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
		fovKnob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, -6, 0.5, -6)
		fovValueLabel.Text = tostring(fov) .. "/500"
	end
	_G.__MM2_Val("fov", fovApply, function() return fov end, FOV_MIN, FOV_MAX)

	local function fovset(x)
		local absSize = fovBar.AbsoluteSize.X
		if absSize <= 0 then return end
		local pct = math.clamp((x - fovBar.AbsolutePosition.X) / absSize, 0, 1)
		fovApply(math.floor(FOV_MIN + pct * (FOV_MAX - FOV_MIN) + 0.5))
	end
	fovBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			draggingFov = true
			fovset(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	__live(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
		if draggingFov
			and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			fovset(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	__live(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			draggingFov = false
		end
	end)
	pcall(function() local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
		local ch = lp and lp.Character
		if ch then
			for _, t in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do
				if t:IsA("Tool") and (t.Name == "Gun" or t.Name == "Revolver") then
					pcall(function() t.Enabled = true end)
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	_G.__FVL_SUPPRESS = function()
		local held = _G.__gsupp
		_G.__gsupp = {}
		if type(held) == "table" then
			for _, c in ipairs(held) do pcall(function() c:Enable() end) end
			held = nil
		end
		if not _G.__silent then return end
		if typeof(getconnections) ~= "function" then return end
		local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
		if not lp then return end
		local ch = lp.Character
		if not ch then return end
		local gS = ch:FindFirstChild("Gun") or ch:FindFirstChild("Revolver")
		local bpk = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
		if not gS and bpk then gS = bpk:FindFirstChild("Gun") or bpk:FindFirstChild("Revolver") end
		if not gS then return end
		for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(gS.Activated)) do
			pcall(function() c:Disable() end)
			table.insert(_G.__gsupp, c)
		end
	end
	_G.__FVL_UNSUPPRESS = function()
		if type(_G.__gsupp) == "table" then
			for _, c in ipairs(_G.__gsupp) do pcall(function() c:Enable() end) end
		end
		_G.__gsupp = {}
		task.spawn(function() for _, d in ipairs({ 0.4, 1.2, 2.5 }) do
				task.wait(d)
				pcall(function() if ded then return end
					if _G.__silent then return end
					local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
					if not lp then return end
					local ch = lp.Character
					local gS = nil
					if ch then gS = ch:FindFirstChild("Gun") or ch:FindFirstChild("Revolver") end
					if not gS then
						local bp = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
						if bp then gS = bp:FindFirstChild("Gun") or bp:FindFirstChild("Revolver") end
					end
					if not gS then return end
					if typeof(getconnections) ~= "function" then return end
					gS.Enabled = true
					for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(gS.Activated)) do
						if c and c.Enabled == false then pcall(function() c:Enable() end) end
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
		pcall(function() if typeof(getconnections) ~= "function" then return end
			if _G.__silent and not ded then return end
			local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
			if not lp then return end
			local ch = lp.Character
			local gS = nil
			if ch then gS = ch:FindFirstChild("Gun") or ch:FindFirstChild("Revolver") end
			if not gS then
				local bp = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
				if bp then gS = bp:FindFirstChild("Gun") or bp:FindFirstChild("Revolver") end
			end
			if not gS then return end
			for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(gS.Activated)) do
				if c and c.Enabled == false then pcall(function() c:Enable() end) end
			end
		end)
	end

	local function stoggle(state)
		if state and typeof(getconnections) ~= "function" then
			silent = false
			_G.__silent = false
			TweenService:Create(strack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(false) }):Play()
			TweenService:Create(sknob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(false) }):Play()
			return
		end
		silent = state
		_G.__silent = state
		if state then
			TweenService:Create(strack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(true) }):Play()
			pcall(function() if _G.__FVL_SUPPRESS then _G.__FVL_SUPPRESS() end end)
			pcall(function() if _G.__wbsync then _G.__wbsync() end end)
			pcall(function() if _G.__FVL_W1 then pcall(function() _G.__FVL_W1:Disconnect() end) _G.__FVL_W1 = nil end
				if _G.__FVL_W2 then pcall(function() _G.__FVL_W2:Disconnect() end) _G.__FVL_W2 = nil end
				local meW = Players.LocalPlayer
				if not meW then return end

				local function arm(chW)
					if not chW then return end

					local function supNow()
						if not _G.__silent then return end
						pcall(function() if _G.__FVL_SUPPRESS then _G.__FVL_SUPPRESS() end end)
					end

					local function hookTool(t)
						if t:IsA("Tool") and (t.Name == "Gun" or t.Name == "Revolver") then
							supNow()
							pcall(function() t.Equipped:Connect(function() supNow() end) end)
						end
					end
					_G.__FVL_W1 = chW.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child) if not _G.__silent then return end
						hookTool(child)
					end)
					for _, ex in ipairs(chW:GetChildren()) do
						if _G.__silent then hookTool(ex) end
					end
				end
				arm(meW.Character)
				_G.__FVL_W2 = meW.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(chW) if not _G.__silent then return end
					task.wait(0.1)
					pcall(function() if _G.__FVL_SUPPRESS then _G.__FVL_SUPPRESS() end end)
					arm(chW)
				end)
			end)
			TweenService:Create(sknob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(true) }):Play()
		else
			TweenService:Create(strack, TWEEN_FAST, { BackgroundColor3 = __swCol(false) }):Play()
			pcall(function() if _G.__FVL_UNSUPPRESS then _G.__FVL_UNSUPPRESS() end end)
			pcall(function() if _G.__FVL_W1 then pcall(function() _G.__FVL_W1:Disconnect() end) _G.__FVL_W1 = nil end
				if _G.__FVL_W2 then pcall(function() _G.__FVL_W2:Disconnect() end) _G.__FVL_W2 = nil end
			end)
			wb = false
			_G.__MM2_Wbon = false
			pcall(function() if _G.__wbsync then _G.__wbsync() end end)
			pcall(function() if _G.__wcsync then _G.__wcsync() end end)
			TweenService:Create(sknob, TWEEN_FAST, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = __knobCol(false) }):Play()
		end
	end
	_G.__MM2_Tg("silent", stoggle, function() return silent end, strack, sknob)
	strack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() stoggle(not silent) end)
	task.spawn(function() local cx, cy = 0, 0
		local cvw, cvh = -1, -1
		pcall(function() local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if not cam then return end
			local vs = cam.ViewportSize
			cvw, cvh = vs.X, vs.Y
			local tly = 0
			pcall(function() local gs = game:GetService("GuiService")
				local ins = gs:GetGuiInset()
				if ins then tly = ins.Y end
			end)
			cx = vs.X / 2
			cy = tly + (vs.Y - tly) / 2
			_G.__FVL_CIRCLE = { cx = cx, cy = cy, r = srad }
		end)
		_G.__FVL_CIRCLECONN = _G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.RenderStepped, function() if not alive() then return end
			pcall(function() local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
				if not cam then return end
				local vs = cam.ViewportSize
				if vs.X ~= cvw or vs.Y ~= cvh then
					cvw, cvh = vs.X, vs.Y
					local tly = 0
					pcall(function() local gs = game:GetService("GuiService")
						local ins = gs:GetGuiInset()
						if ins then tly = ins.Y end
					end)
					cx = vs.X / 2
					cy = tly + (vs.Y - tly) / 2
				end
				if scircle then
					scircle.Position = Vector2.new(cx, cy)
					scircle.Visible = silent and not hideCircleOn
					scircle.Radius = srad
				end
				if fovCircle then
					local frF = fovCircle:FindFirstChild("FovRing")
					if frF then
						frF.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy)
						frF.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(srad * 2, srad * 2)
						frF.Visible = silent and not hideCircleOn
					end
				end
				local tbl = _G.__FVL_CIRCLE
				if type(tbl) == "table" then
					tbl.cx = cx
					tbl.cy = cy
					tbl.r = srad
				else
					_G.__FVL_CIRCLE = { cx = cx, cy = cy, r = srad }
				end
			end)
		end)
	end)
	knifecf = function()
		local tgtCF = nil
		pcall(function() local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
			local ch = lp and lp.Character
			if not ch then return end
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if not cam then return end
			_G.cirK = _G.__FVL_CIRCLE
			local ccx, ccy, krad
			if _G.cirK then
				ccx, ccy, krad = _G.cirK.cx, _G.cirK.cy, _G.cirK.r
			else
				local vs = cam.ViewportSize
				ccx, ccy = vs.X / 2, vs.Y / 2
				krad = srad
			end
			local mx, my = ccx, ccy
			pcall(function() local ml = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
				mx = ml.X
				my = ml.Y
			end)
			local amMurder = false
			pcall(function() amMurder = (combatRole(lp, true) == "Murderer") end)
			local bd = 100000
			for _, q in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if q ~= lp and q.Character then
					local skip = false
					if amMurder then
						if combatRole(q) == "Murderer" then skip = true end
					else
						if combatRole(q) ~= "Murderer" then skip = true end
					end
					if not skip then
						local part = nil
						if _G.aimTarget == "Head" then
							part = q.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
						else
							part = q.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
							or q.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
						end
						local hum = q.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						if part and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
							local sp, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
							if onScreen then
								_G.dxm = sp.X - mx
								local dym = sp.Y - my
								local d = math.sqrt(_G.dxm * _G.dxm + dym * dym)
								local dxr = sp.X - ccx
								local dyr = sp.Y - ccy
								local dr2 = dxr * dxr + dyr * dyr
								if d < bd and ((not krad) or dr2 <= krad * krad) then
									local blocked = false
									if wallon then
										local okV2, visV2 = pcall(function() return _G.__MM2_TargetVisible(q.Character) end)
										if okV2 and visV2 == false then blocked = true end
									end
									if not blocked then
										bd = d
										local kdTravel = 0
										pcall(function() local hrpK = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
											if hrpK then kdTravel = (part.Position - hrpK.Position).Magnitude / kspeed end
										end)
										tgtCF = guess(part, q.Character, kdTravel)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		return tgtCF
	end
	local kgate = 0

	local function fireknife()
		pcall(function() local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
			local ch = lp and lp.Character
			if not ch then return end
			local ktool = nil
			for _, t in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do
				if t:IsA("Tool") and t.Name == "Knife" then ktool = t break end
			end
			if not ktool then
				local bp2 = lp:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
				if bp2 then ktool = bp2:FindFirstChild("Knife") end
			end
			local hd = ktool and ktool:FindFirstChild("Handle") or nil
			local hrp = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local handleCF = nil
			if hd then handleCF = hd.CFrame end
			if not handleCF and hrp then handleCF = hrp.CFrame end
			if not handleCF then return end
			local ev = nil
			if not ev then
				local ef = ktool and ktool:FindFirstChild("Events") or nil
				if ef then ef = ef:FindFirstChild("KnifeThrown") end
				if ef and ef:IsA("RemoteEvent") then ev = ef end
			end
			if not ev then
				for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
					if d.Name == "KnifeThrown" and d:IsA("RemoteEvent") then ev = d break end
				end
			end
			if not ev then
				local rsT = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
				for _, d in ipairs(rsT:GetDescendants()) do
					if d.Name == "KnifeThrown" and d:IsA("RemoteEvent") then ev = d break end
				end
			end
			if not ev then ev = throwev end
			if not ev then return end
			local tgtCF = knifecf()
			if not tgtCF then return end
			local okFire = pcall(function() ev:FireServer(handleCF, tgtCF) end)
			if not okFire then throwev = nil end
		end)
	end
	local lastguide = 0
	local guidecf = nil
	pcall(function() if _G.__guiders then _G.__guiders:Disconnect() end end)
	_G.__guiders = _G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.RenderStepped, function() if not silent or ded then guidecf = nil return end
		if os.clock() - lastguide > 0.1 then
			lastguide = os.clock()
			guidecf = knifecf()
		end
		if not guidecf then return end
		pcall(function() local cs = game:GetService("CollectionService")
			if not cs then return end
			for _, v in ipairs(cs:GetTagged("ThrowingKnife")) do
				local bp = nil
				if v:IsA("BasePart") then bp = v end
				if not bp and v:IsA("Model") then bp = v.PrimaryPart or v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) end
				if bp then
					pcall(function() bp.CanCollide = false end)
				end
			end
		end)
	end)
	local throwwas = false
	local throwfired = false
	local throwid = "1957618848"
	pcall(function() if _G.__animrs then _G.__animrs:Disconnect() end end)
	_G.__animrs = _G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.RenderStepped, function() if not silent or ded then throwwas = false throwfired = false return end
		local animatorNow = nil
		pcall(function() local lpA = Players.LocalPlayer
			local chA = lpA and lpA.Character
			local humA = chA and chA:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if humA then animatorNow = humA:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") end
		end)
		if not animatorNow then return end
		local trk = nil
		pcall(function() for _, tr in ipairs(animatorNow:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
				local okId, aid = pcall(function() return tr.Animation.AnimationId end)
				if okId and aid and tostring(aid):find(throwid, 1, true) then trk = tr break end
			end
		end)
		local playingNow = trk ~= nil
		if playingNow and not throwwas then
			throwfired = false
		end
		if playingNow and not throwfired then
			local okT, pos, len = pcall(function() return trk.TimePosition, trk.Length end)
			if okT and pos and len and len > 0 and pos >= len * 0.95 then
				if os.clock() - kgate >= 1 then
					kgate = os.clock()
					throwfired = true
					fireknife()
				end
			end
		end
		if throwwas and not playingNow and not throwfired then
			if os.clock() - kgate >= 1 then
				kgate = os.clock()
				throwfired = true
				fireknife()
			end
		end
		throwwas = playingNow
	end)
	local predBallFn = nil

	local function predBall(part)
		if not predBallFn then return nil end
		return predBallFn(part)
	end
	_G.__FVL_VEL = _G.__FVL_VEL or {}
	pcall(function() if _G.__FVL_VELCONN then _G.__FVL_VELCONN:Disconnect() end end)

	local function fitVel(hb, tau, minN, now)
		if type(hb) ~= "table" or type(tau) ~= "number" or tau <= 0 then return nil end
		local n = #hb
		if type(minN) ~= "number" or minN < 2 then minN = 2 end
		if n < minN then return nil end
		local newest = hb[n]
		if not newest or not newest.p or (now - newest.t) > 0.15 then return nil end
		local t0 = newest.t - tau * 3
		local sw, swx, swxx = 0, 0, 0
		local swy, swxy = 0, 0
		local swz, swxz = 0, 0
		local cnt = 0
		for i = 1, n do
			local s = hb[i]
			if s.p and s.t >= t0 then
				local x = s.t - newest.t
				local w = math.exp(x / tau)
				cnt = cnt + 1
				sw = sw + w
				swx = swx + w * x
				swxx = swxx + w * x * x
				swy = swy + w * s.p.X
				swxy = swxy + w * x * s.p.X
				swz = swz + w * s.p.Z
				swxz = swxz + w * x * s.p.Z
			end
		end
		if cnt < minN or sw <= 0 then return nil end
		local den = sw * swxx - swx * swx
		if den == 0 then return nil end
		local vx = (sw * swxy - swx * swy) / den
		local vz = (sw * swxz - swx * swz) / den
		if vx ~= vx or vz ~= vz then return nil end
		local v = Vector3.new(vx, 0, vz)
		if v.Magnitude > 120 then v = v.Unit * 120 end
		if v.Magnitude < 0.15 then v = Vector3.zero end
		return v
	end
	pcall(function() _G.__FVL_VELCONN = _G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if ded then return end
			if not alive() then return end
			local vt = _G.__FVL_VEL
			if not vt then return end
			local now = os.clock()
			local seen = {}
			for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				local chr = pl.Character
				local hrp = chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not hrp then
					if chr then vt[chr] = nil end
				else
					seen[chr] = true
					local e = vt[chr]
					local pos = hrp.Position
					if not e then
						e = { p = pos, t = now, v = Vector3.zero, vs = nil, vf = nil, h = {}, rep = nil, lastMove = now }
						vt[chr] = e
					elseif now - e.t >= 0.03 then
						local dtx = now - e.t
						local dp = pos - e.p
						local moved = dp.Magnitude > 0.02
						if moved then
							e.v = dp / dtx
							local hb = e.h
							hb[#hb + 1] = { t = now, p = pos }
							if #hb > 10 then table.remove(hb, 1) end
							local gap = now - (e.lastMove or now)
							if gap > 0.005 and gap < 0.5 then
								if type(e.rep) == "number" and e.rep > 0 then
									e.rep = e.rep * 0.75 + gap * 0.25
								else
									e.rep = gap
								end
								local rg = _G.__FVL_REPG
								if type(rg) ~= "number" or rg <= 0 then rg = gap end
								_G.__FVL_REPG = rg * 0.8 + gap * 0.2
							end
							e.lastMove = now
						elseif (now - (e.lastMove or now)) > 0.15 then
							e.v = Vector3.zero
							e.vs = nil
							e.vf = nil
						end
						e.p = pos
						e.t = now
						e.vs = fitVel(e.h, 0.20, 3, now)
						e.vf = fitVel(e.h, 0.05, 2, now)
					end
					if predBallFn then
						_G.tq = chr:FindFirstChild("Torso")
						if not _G.tq then _G.tq = chr:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") end
						if _G.tq then pcall(function() predBallFn(_G.tq) end) end
					end
				end
			end
			for k in pairs(vt) do
				if not seen[k] then vt[k] = nil end
			end
		end)
	end)
	if tonumber(_G.__FVL_WBUP) == nil then _G.__FVL_WBUP = 4 end
	if tonumber(_G.__FVL_YOFF) == nil then _G.__FVL_YOFF = 0 end
	if tonumber(_G.__FVL_WBLEAD) == nil then _G.__FVL_WBLEAD = 0.05 end
	if tonumber(_G.__FVL_AIMLEAD) == nil then _G.__FVL_AIMLEAD = 0.10 end
	if tonumber(_G.__FVL_BALLSHOW) == nil then _G.__FVL_BALLSHOW = 0 end
	if tonumber(_G.__FVL_VDAMP) == nil then _G.__FVL_VDAMP = 0.45 end
	if tonumber(_G.__FVL_AIRH) == nil then _G.__FVL_AIRH = 1.0 end
	if tonumber(_G.__FVL_TURNTH) == nil then _G.__FVL_TURNTH = 0.96 end
	if tonumber(_G.__FVL_TURNCUT) == nil then _G.__FVL_TURNCUT = 0.30 end
	if tonumber(_G.__FVL_OFIX) == nil then _G.__FVL_OFIX = 1 end
	if _G.__FVL_AIR == nil then _G.__FVL_AIR = false end
	_G.__FVL_PING = function()
		local v = nil
		local ok, raw = pcall(function() local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
			if lp and typeof(lp.GetNetworkPing) == "function" then return lp:GetNetworkPing() end
			return nil
		end)
		if ok and type(raw) == "number" and raw > 0 and raw < 1 then v = raw end
		if not v then
			pcall(function() local st = game:GetService("Stats")
				local it = st and st.Network and st.Network.ServerStatsItem and st.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]
				if it then v = it:GetValue() / 2000 end
			end)
		end
		if type(v) ~= "number" or v <= 0 or v >= 1 then return _G.__FVL_PINGS or 0.04 end
		local s = _G.__FVL_PINGS
		if type(s) ~= "number" or s <= 0 then s = v end
		s = s + (v - s) * 0.25
		_G.__FVL_PINGS = s
		return s
	end
	_G.__FVL_LEADTIME = function(wbMode, chr)
		local al = tonumber(_G.__FVL_AIMLEAD)
		if type(al) ~= "number" or al < 0 or al > 0.5 then al = 0.10 end
		if al > 0.25 then al = 0.25 end
		local p = _G.__FVL_PING()
		if p ~= p or p < 0 or p > 1 then p = 0.04 end
		local rl
		local e = chr and _G.__FVL_VEL and _G.__FVL_VEL[chr]
		if e and type(e.rep) == "number" and e.rep > 0 then rl = e.rep end
		if type(rl) ~= "number" or rl <= 0 then rl = _G.__FVL_REPG end
		if type(rl) ~= "number" or rl ~= rl then rl = 0.05 end
		if rl < 0.02 then rl = 0.02 end
		if rl > 0.12 then rl = 0.12 end
		local ld = p + al + rl
		if ld < 0 then ld = 0 end
		if ld > 0.3 then ld = 0.3 end
		if wbMode then
			local wl = tonumber(_G.__FVL_WBLEAD)
			if type(wl) ~= "number" or wl < 0 or wl > 0.3 then wl = 0.05 end
			ld = ld + wl
			if ld > 0.4 then ld = 0.4 end
		end
		return ld
	end
	predBallFn = function(part)
		if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return nil end
		local okb, ball = pcall(function() local b = part:FindFirstChild("PredBall")
			if b and b:IsA("BasePart") then return b end
			b = nil
			b = __mk("Part", {"Name", "PredBall", "Shape", Enum.PartType.Ball, "Size", Vector3.one, "Anchored", false, "CanCollide", false, "CanTouch", false, "CanQuery", false, "CastShadow", false, "Material", Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, "Color", Color3.fromRGB(0, 122, 255), "Transparency", 1, "Massless", true, "CFrame", part.CFrame, "Parent", part})
			__mk("WeldConstraint", {"Part0", part, "Part1", b, "Parent", b})
			_G.__FVL_BALLS = _G.__FVL_BALLS or {}
			_G.__FVL_BALLS[#_G.__FVL_BALLS + 1] = b
			return b
		end)
		if not okb or not ball then return nil end
		pcall(function() local show = tonumber(_G.__FVL_BALLSHOW)
			if type(show) == "number" and show > 0 then ball.Transparency = 0.35 end
		end)
		return ball
	end
	_G.__FVL_AIM = function(obj, part, wbMode)
		local hum = obj.Character and obj.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local vel = nil
		local now = os.clock()
		local hrp = obj.Character and obj.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local trk0 = _G.__FVL_VEL and _G.__FVL_VEL[obj.Character] or nil
		if hum and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.1 then
			local ws = hum.WalkSpeed
			if type(ws) ~= "number" or ws <= 0 then ws = 16 end
			vel = hum.MoveDirection.Unit * ws
			pcall(function() local av = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
				if av then
					local am = math.sqrt(av.X * av.X + av.Z * av.Z)
					if am > 0.5 and am < ws then vel = Vector3.new(av.X, av.Y, av.Z) end
				end
			end)
		else
			vel = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
			if vel.Magnitude < 0.5 then
				local trk = trk0
				if trk and trk.v and trk.v.Magnitude >= 0.5 then vel = trk.v end
				if trk and hrp and vel.Magnitude < 0.5 and trk.p and type(trk.t) == "number" then
					local dtx = now - trk.t
					if dtx > 0.005 then
						local dp = hrp.Position - trk.p
						if dp.Magnitude > 0.0001 and dp.Magnitude < 60 then vel = dp / dtx end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if vel ~= vel then vel = Vector3.zero end
		local conf = 1
		if trk0 then
			local vs = trk0.vs
			if vs and vs.X == vs.X and vs.Z == vs.Z then
				local vm = math.sqrt(vs.X * vs.X + vs.Z * vs.Z)
				if vm >= 0.2 and vm < 100 then
					local use = vs
					local vf = trk0.vf
					if vf and vf.X == vf.X and vf.Z == vf.Z then
						local fm = math.sqrt(vf.X * vf.X + vf.Z * vf.Z)
						if fm >= 0.2 and fm >= vm * 0.35 then
							local thrh = tonumber(_G.__FVL_TURNTH)
							if type(thrh) ~= "number" or thrh < 0 or thrh > 1 then thrh = 0.96 end
							local cosT = (vf.X * vs.X + vf.Z * vs.Z) / (fm * vm)
							if cosT > 1 then cosT = 1 elseif cosT < -1 then cosT = -1 end
							if cosT < 0 then conf = 0 else conf = cosT end
							if cosT < thrh then use = vf end
						end
					end
					vel = Vector3.new(use.X, vel.Y, use.Z)
				end
			end
		end
		local cut = tonumber(_G.__FVL_TURNCUT)
		if type(cut) ~= "number" or cut < 0 or cut > 1 then cut = 0.30 end
		local ldScale = cut + (1 - cut) * conf
		_G.__FVL_AIR = false
		local isFall = false
		local stH = nil
		pcall(function() local h2 = obj.Character and obj.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if h2 then
				stH = h2:GetState()
				_G.__FVL_AIR = stH == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping
				or stH == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
				or stH == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
			end
		end)
		local rise = false
		pcall(function() local av = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			if av and av.Y ~= av.Y then return end
			if av and av.Y > 0.5 then rise = true end
			if stH == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping and av and av.Y <= 0.5 then rise = true end
			if stH == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then isFall = true end
		end)
		if _G.__FVL_AIR then
			pcall(function() local av = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
				if av and av.Y < -0.5 then isFall = true end
			end)
			if stH == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then isFall = true end
		end
		if rise then isFall = false end
		local yo = tonumber(_G.__FVL_YOFF) or 0
		if type(yo) ~= "number" or yo < -2 or yo > 2 then yo = 0 end
		local base = part.Position
		local me = Players.LocalPlayer
		local mc = me and me.Character
		local mr = mc and mc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local dst = 0
		if mr then dst = (base - mr.Position).Magnitude end
		if not wbMode and _G.__FVL_AIR then
			local hsp = nil
			if trk0 and trk0.v and trk0.v.X == trk0.v.X and trk0.v.Z == trk0.v.Z then
				hsp = math.sqrt(trk0.v.X * trk0.v.X + trk0.v.Z * trk0.v.Z)
			elseif trk0 and trk0.vs and trk0.vs.X == trk0.vs.X and trk0.vs.Z == trk0.vs.Z then
				hsp = math.sqrt(trk0.vs.X * trk0.vs.X + trk0.vs.Z * trk0.vs.Z)
			else
				pcall(function() local av = hrp and hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
					if av then hsp = math.sqrt(av.X * av.X + av.Z * av.Z) end
				end)
			end
			if (hsp and hsp <= 4) or (dst <= 45 and (not hsp or hsp <= 6)) then
				local sc = hrp and hrp.Position or base
				return Vector3.new(sc.X, sc.Y + yo, sc.Z)
			end
		end
		local ld = _G.__FVL_LEADTIME(wbMode, obj.Character)
		if not _G.__FVL_AIR then ld = ld * ldScale end
		local hv = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z)
		if hv.Magnitude < 0.0001 and _G.__FVL_AIR then
			pcall(function() local trk2 = _G.__FVL_VEL and _G.__FVL_VEL[obj.Character] or nil
				if trk2 and trk2.v then
					local t2 = Vector3.new(trk2.v.X, 0, trk2.v.Z)
					if t2.Magnitude > 0.0001 then hv = t2 end
				end
			end)
		end
		local flatDir = hv
		if flatDir.Magnitude < 0.0001 then flatDir = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) end
		if _G.__FVL_AIR then
			local G = 196.2
			pcall(function() local g0 = workspace.Gravity
				if type(g0) == "number" and g0 >= 50 and g0 <= 400 then G = g0 end
			end)
			local ball = predBall(part)
			local bpos = base
			local bvx, bvz = flatDir.X, flatDir.Z
			local bvy = 0
			local gotVel = false
			if ball then
				local okp, wp = pcall(function() return ball.WorldPosition end)
				if okp and wp and wp == wp then bpos = wp end
				pcall(function() local av = ball.AssemblyLinearVelocity
					if av and av.Magnitude >= 0.5 then
						if av.X == av.X then bvx = av.X end
						if av.Z == av.Z then bvz = av.Z end
						if av.Y == av.Y then bvy = av.Y end
						gotVel = true
					end
				end)
			end
			if not gotVel then
				pcall(function() local av = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
					if av then
						if av.X == av.X then bvx = av.X end
						if av.Z == av.Z then bvz = av.Z end
						if av.Y == av.Y then bvy = av.Y end
					end
				end)
			end
			local vd = tonumber(_G.__FVL_VDAMP)
			if type(vd) ~= "number" or vd < 0 or vd > 1 then vd = 0.45 end
			local vyTrue = bvy
			local vyTrk = nil
			pcall(function() local trkV = _G.__FVL_VEL and _G.__FVL_VEL[obj.Character] or nil
				if trkV and trkV.v and trkV.v.Magnitude >= 0.5 and trkV.v.Y == trkV.v.Y then
					vyTrk = trkV.v.Y
				end
			end)
			if vyTrk then
				local disagree = (vyTrue > 0) ~= (vyTrk > 0)
				local bigGap = math.abs(vyTrue - vyTrk) > 18
				if disagree or bigGap then
					vyTrue = vyTrue + (vyTrk - vyTrue) * vd
				end
			end
			bvy = vyTrue
			local apx = 6.4
			pcall(function() local th = obj.Character and obj.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if th then
					if th.UseJumpPower and type(th.JumpPower) == "number" and th.JumpPower > 0 then
						apx = th.JumpPower * th.JumpPower / (2 * G)
					elseif type(th.JumpHeight) == "number" and th.JumpHeight > 0 then
						apx = th.JumpHeight
					end
				end
			end)
			if apx < 0.5 or apx > 200 then apx = 6.4 end
			local gnd = bpos.Y - 150
			pcall(function() local rp2 = RaycastParams.new()
				rp2.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
				rp2.FilterDescendantsInstances = { obj.Character, me.Character }
				local gh = workspace:Raycast(bpos, Vector3.new(0, -150, 0), rp2)
				if gh and gh.Instance and gh.Instance.CanCollide and gh.Instance.Transparency <= 0.5 then
					gnd = gh.Position.Y
				end
			end)
			local ldA = _G.__FVL_LEADTIME(wbMode, obj.Character)
			local ah = tonumber(_G.__FVL_AIRH)
			if type(ah) ~= "number" or ah < 0 or ah > 1 then ah = 0.7 end
			local ldH = ldA * ah * ldScale
			local pred = bpos + Vector3.new(bvx * ldH, bvy * ldA, bvz * ldH)
			pred = pred + Vector3.new(0, -0.5 * G * ldA * ldA, 0)
			local ceil = bpos.Y + 40
			pcall(function() local rp3 = RaycastParams.new()
				rp3.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
				rp3.FilterDescendantsInstances = { obj.Character, me.Character }
				local ch2 = workspace:Raycast(bpos, Vector3.new(0, 40, 0), rp3)
				if ch2 and ch2.Instance and ch2.Instance.CanCollide and ch2.Instance.Transparency <= 0.5 then
					ceil = ch2.Position.Y
				end
			end)
			local ceilCap = ceil - 1
			if bvy > 0 then
				local riseCap = bpos.Y + (bvy * bvy) / (2 * G)
				if riseCap > ceilCap then riseCap = ceilCap end
				if pred.Y > riseCap then
					pred.Y = riseCap
				end
			end
			local hardCap = bpos.Y + apx
			if hardCap > ceilCap then
				hardCap = ceilCap
			end
			if pred.Y > hardCap then
				pred.Y = hardCap
			end
			if pred.Y > ceilCap then
				pred.Y = ceilCap
			end
			if bvy <= 0 and isFall then
				local floorY = gnd + 2.5
				if pred.Y < floorY then
					pred.Y = floorY
				end
			end
			local py = pred.Y + yo
			if pred.X ~= pred.X or py ~= py or pred.Z ~= pred.Z then
				return Vector3.new(base.X, base.Y + yo, base.Z)
			end
			return Vector3.new(pred.X, py, pred.Z)
		end
		if vel.Magnitude < 0.5 then
			return Vector3.new(base.X, base.Y + yo, base.Z)
		end
		local aim = base + hv * ld
		local off = aim - base
		local maxLead = dst * 0.5
		if maxLead < 4 then maxLead = 4 end
		if off.Magnitude > maxLead then aim = base + off.Unit * maxLead end
		local py = aim.Y + yo
		if aim.X ~= aim.X or py ~= py or aim.Z ~= aim.Z then
			return Vector3.new(base.X, base.Y + yo, base.Z)
		end
		return Vector3.new(aim.X, py, aim.Z)
	end
	_G.__FVL_PICK = function()
		local me = Players.LocalPlayer
		if not me then return nil end
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not cam then return nil end
		local myCh = me.Character
		local myHRP = myCh and myCh:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not myHRP then return nil end
		local myPos = myHRP.Position
		local cir = _G.__FVL_CIRCLE
		local ccx, ccy, rad = 0, 0, 0
		if cir then
			ccx, ccy, rad = cir.cx, cir.cy, cir.r
		else
			local vs = cam.ViewportSize
			ccx, ccy = vs.X / 2, vs.Y / 2
		end
		if not rad or rad <= 0 then rad = 150 end
		local plist = Players:GetPlayers()
		local ranked = {}
		for _, pl in ipairs(plist) do
			if pl ~= me and _G.__FVL_VALID(pl) then
				local ch = pl.Character
				local hrp = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if hrp then
					local sp, on = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
					if on then
						local gx = sp.X - ccx
						local gy = sp.Y - ccy
						if gx * gx + gy * gy <= rad * rad then
							local d = hrp.Position - myPos
							ranked[#ranked + 1] = { pl = pl, d2 = d.X * d.X + d.Y * d.Y + d.Z * d.Z }
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if #ranked == 0 then return nil end
		table.sort(ranked, function(a, b) return a.d2 < b.d2 end)
		for _, entry in ipairs(ranked) do
			local pl = entry.pl
			local isM = false
			local bpk = pl:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
			if bpk and bpk:FindFirstChild("Knife") then isM = true end
			if not isM and pl.Character and pl.Character:FindFirstChild("Knife") then isM = true end
			if not isM then
				local okr, r = pcall(function() return combatRole(pl, true) end)
				if okr and r == "Murderer" then isM = true end
			end
			if isM then return pl end
		end
		return nil
	end
	_G.__FVL_VALID = function(pl)
		if not pl or not pl.Character then return false end
		local hum = pl.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		return hum ~= nil and hum.Health > 0
	end
	_G.__FVL_MOUSE = function()
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not cam then return nil end
		local ok, res = pcall(function() local ml = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
			local ray = cam:ViewportPointToRay(ml.X, ml.Y)
			local rp = RaycastParams.new()
			rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = { Players.LocalPlayer.Character }
			local hit = workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 300, rp)
			return hit and hit.Position or ray.Origin + ray.Direction * 300
		end)
		return ok and res or nil
	end
	_G.__FVL_JUKE = function(tgt)
		local ch = tgt and tgt.Character
		local trk = ch and _G.__FVL_VEL and _G.__FVL_VEL[ch]
		if not trk then return false end
		local vs = trk.vs
		local vf = trk.vf
		if not vs or not vf then return false end
		local sx, sz = vs.X, vs.Z
		local fx, fz = vf.X, vf.Z
		if sx ~= sx or sz ~= sz or fx ~= fx or fz ~= fz then return false end
		local sm = math.sqrt(sx * sx + sz * sz)
		local fm = math.sqrt(fx * fx + fz * fz)
		if sm < 4 or fm < 4 then return false end
		local cos = (sx * fx + sz * fz) / (sm * fm)
		if cos ~= cos then return false end
		return cos < 0.35
	end
	_G.__FVL_JMARK = function(tgt)
		local ch = tgt and tgt.Character
		local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not hum then return end
		local hp = hum.Health
		if type(hp) ~= "number" or hp ~= hp then return end
		if hp <= 0 then _G.__FVL_JMISS = false return end
		local seq = (_G.__FVL_JSEQ or 0) + 1
		_G.__FVL_JSEQ = seq
		local p = tonumber(_G.__FVL_PING and _G.__FVL_PING()) or 0.1
		if p ~= p or p < 0 or p > 1 then p = 0.1 end
		task.delay(0.28 + p, function()
			if _G.__FVL_JSEQ ~= seq then return end
			if not hum then return end
			if hum.Health < hp then
				_G.__FVL_JMISS = false
			elseif not hum.Parent then return
			elseif hum.Health > 0 then
				_G.__FVL_JMISS = true
			end
		end)
	end
	pcall(function() if _G.__FVL_AIMCONN then _G.__FVL_AIMCONN:Disconnect() end end)
	pcall(function() _G.__FVL_AIMCONN = player:GetMouse().Button1Down:Connect(function() _G.__FVL_SKIP = "entered"
			_G.__FVL_JTOKEN = (_G.__FVL_JTOKEN or 0) + 1
			if ded then _G.__FVL_SKIP = "ded" return end
			if not silent then _G.__FVL_SKIP = "notsilent" return end
			pcall(function() local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
				local ch = lp and lp.Character
				if not ch then _G.__FVL_SKIP = "nochar" return end
				local gun = ch:FindFirstChild("Gun") or ch:FindFirstChild("Revolver")
				if not gun or not gun.Parent then _G.__FVL_SKIP = "nogun" return end
				local hrp = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not hrp then _G.__FVL_SKIP = "nohrp" return end
				local shoot = gun:FindFirstChild("Shoot")
				if not (shoot and shoot:IsA("RemoteEvent")) then
					shoot = nil
					for _, d in ipairs(gun:GetDescendants()) do
						if d.Name == "Shoot" and d:IsA("RemoteEvent") then shoot = d break end
					end
				end
				if not shoot then _G.__FVL_SKIP = "noshoot" return end
				local orgCF = nil
				local haveMuz = false
				local hrpPart = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local mz = hrpPart and hrpPart:FindFirstChild("GunRaycastAttachment")
				if not mz then mz = ch:FindFirstChild("GunRaycastAttachment") end
				if not mz then mz = gun:FindFirstChild("GunRaycastAttachment") end
				if mz then orgCF = mz.WorldCFrame haveMuz = true end
				if not orgCF then
					local hd = gun:FindFirstChild("Handle")
					if hd and hd:IsA("BasePart") then orgCF = hd.CFrame end
				end
				if not orgCF then orgCF = CFrame.new(hrp.Position) end
				_G.__FVL_WBREADY = haveMuz
				local tgt = _G.__FVL_PICK()
				local valid = (tgt and tgt ~= lp and _G.__FVL_VALID(tgt)) or false
				if not tgt then _G.__FVL_SKIP = "notarget" end
				local aimPos, aimPart = nil, nil
				local blocked = false
				local useWb = false
				local noAssist = false
				if not valid then _G.__FVL_SKIP = "invalid" end
				if silent and valid then
					local thrp = tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
					or tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
					if thrp then
						aimPart = thrp
						useWb = wb
						if _G.__FVL_WBREADY == false then useWb = false end
						if _G.__MM2_Wallon or useWb then
							local camW = workspace.CurrentCamera
							local eyeW = camW and camW.CFrame.Position or orgCF.Position
							local okv, vis = pcall(function() return _G.__MM2_TargetVisible(tgt.Character, eyeW)
							end)
							if okv and vis == false then blocked = true end
						end
						local open = true
						if _G.__MM2_Wallon and blocked and not useWb then
							open = false
							noAssist = true
							_G.__FVL_SKIP = "blocked"
						end
						if useWb and valid and not blocked then
							pcall(function() local fpM = tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
								or tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
								or tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								if fpM then
									local rpM = RaycastParams.new()
									rpM.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
									rpM.FilterDescendantsInstances = { ch, tgt.Character }
									local dM = fpM.Position - orgCF.Position
									if dM.Magnitude > 0.01 then
										if workspace:Raycast(orgCF.Position, dM.Unit * dM.Magnitude, rpM) then
											blocked = true
										end
									end
								end
							end)
						end
						if open and (hrp.Position - thrp.Position).Magnitude <= 300 then
							pcall(function() aimPos = _G.__FVL_AIM(tgt, thrp, useWb and blocked or nil)
							end)
						end
					end
				end
				if _G.__FVL_SKIP == "entered" then
					if aimPos then _G.__FVL_SKIP = "FIRED" end
				elseif aimPos then
					_G.__FVL_SKIP = _G.__FVL_SKIP .. "+mousefallback"
				end
				if not aimPos and not noAssist and valid and tgt and tgt.Character then
					pcall(function() local fp = tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
						or tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
						or tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if fp then
							aimPos = fp.Position
							_G.__FVL_SKIP = _G.__FVL_SKIP .. "+livepos"
						end
					end)
				end
				aimPos = aimPos or _G.__FVL_MOUSE()
				if not aimPos then _G.__FVL_SKIP = "noaim" return end
				pcall(function() if valid and tgt and tgt.Character and not noAssist then
						local rp4 = RaycastParams.new()
						rp4.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
						rp4.FilterDescendantsInstances = { ch, tgt.Character }
						local o4 = orgCF.Position
						local d4 = aimPos - o4
						if d4.Magnitude > 0.01 then
							local h4 = workspace:Raycast(o4, d4.Unit * d4.Magnitude, rp4)
							if h4 then
								blocked = true
								if not useWb then
									local fp2 = tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
									or tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
									or tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									if fp2 then
										aimPos = fp2.Position
										_G.__FVL_SKIP = _G.__FVL_SKIP .. "+unblocked"
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
				if tonumber(_G.__FVL_OFIX) ~= 0 then
					pcall(function() local rpO = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local p = rpO and rpO.Position or orgCF.Position
						local d = aimPos - p
						if d.Magnitude > 0.0001 then
							orgCF = CFrame.lookAt(p, p + d.Unit)
						end
					end)
				end
				local wbPending = (useWb and blocked) and true or false
				local jukeNow = false
				if valid and tgt and tgt.Character then
					pcall(function() jukeNow = _G.__FVL_JUKE(tgt) end)
				end
				local jdelay = 0
				if jukeNow and aimPos and not noAssist then
					jdelay = tonumber(_G.__FVL_JDELAY) or 0.09
					if type(jdelay) ~= "number" or jdelay < 0.04 or jdelay > 0.25 then jdelay = 0.09 end
					if _G.__FVL_JMISS then jdelay = jdelay + 0.05 end
				end
				if jdelay > 0 then
					local tok = _G.__FVL_JTOKEN
					task.delay(jdelay, function()
						if _G.__FVL_JTOKEN ~= tok then return end
						if ded or not _G.__silent then return end
						if not _G.__FVL_VALID(tgt) or not aimPart then return end
						local tcD = tgt.Character
						if not tcD or not aimPart:IsDescendantOf(tcD) then return end
						local chD = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
						local gunD = chD and (chD:FindFirstChild("Gun") or chD:FindFirstChild("Revolver"))
						if not gunD or gunD ~= shoot.Parent then return end
						local p = nil
						if wbPending then
							pcall(function() p = _G.__FVL_AIM(tgt, aimPart, true) end)
						else
							pcall(function() p = _G.__FVL_AIM(tgt, aimPart, nil) end)
						end
						if not p then p = aimPart.Position end
						local orgD = orgCF
						local hrD = chD and chD:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local attD = hrD and hrD:FindFirstChild("GunRaycastAttachment")
						if not attD then attD = chD and chD:FindFirstChild("GunRaycastAttachment") end
						if not attD then attD = gunD:FindFirstChild("GunRaycastAttachment") end
						if attD then orgD = attD.WorldCFrame end
						if wbPending then
							local back = Vector3.new(p.X - orgD.Position.X, 0, p.Z - orgD.Position.Z)
							if back.Magnitude > 0.05 then
								back = back.Unit
								pcall(function() shoot:FireServer(CFrame.new(p + back * 2), CFrame.new(p - back * 2)) end)
							else
								local wbUp = tonumber(_G.__FVL_WBUP) or 4
								if type(wbUp) ~= "number" or wbUp < 1.5 or wbUp > 12 then wbUp = 4 end
								pcall(function() shoot:FireServer(CFrame.new(p + Vector3.new(0, wbUp, 0)), CFrame.new(p)) end)
							end
						else
							local okv, vis = pcall(function() return _G.__MM2_TargetVisible(tcD, orgD.Position) end)
							if okv and vis == false then return end
							pcall(function() shoot:FireServer(orgD, CFrame.new(p)) end)
						end
						pcall(function() _G.__FVL_JMARK(tgt) end)
					end)
					return
				end
				if silent and useWb and valid and blocked then
					if aimPart then
						pcall(function() local wbAim = _G.__FVL_AIM(tgt, aimPart, true)
							if wbAim then aimPos = wbAim end
						end)
					end
					local back = Vector3.new(aimPos.X - orgCF.Position.X, 0, aimPos.Z - orgCF.Position.Z)
					if back.Magnitude > 0.05 then
						back = back.Unit
						shoot:FireServer(CFrame.new(aimPos + back * 2), CFrame.new(aimPos - back * 2))
					else
						local wbUp = tonumber(_G.__FVL_WBUP) or 4
						if type(wbUp) ~= "number" or wbUp < 1.5 or wbUp > 12 then wbUp = 4 end
						shoot:FireServer(CFrame.new(aimPos + Vector3.new(0, wbUp, 0)), CFrame.new(aimPos))
					end
				else
					shoot:FireServer(orgCF, CFrame.new(aimPos))
				end
				if valid and tgt then pcall(function() _G.__FVL_JMARK(tgt) end) end
			end)
		end)
	end)
	if not scircle then hideLabel.Text = "Hide FOV circle: N/A" end
end
do
	_G.__infoTint = function()
		local a = acc
		if _G.__infoJob then _G.__infoJob.Text = game.JobId end
		if _G.__infoGame then _G.__infoGame.Text = tostring(game.PlaceId) end
		if _G.__infoJobLabel then _G.__infoJobLabel.TextColor3 = a end
		if _G.__infoGameLabel then _G.__infoGameLabel.TextColor3 = a end
		if _G.__infoPingV then _G.__infoPingV.Text = tostring((_G.__infoPing and _G.__infoPing()) or 0) .. " ms" end
		if _G.__infoFpsV then _G.__infoFpsV.Text = tostring(_G.__infoFps or 60) end
		if _G.__infoPlV then _G.__infoPlV.Text = tostring(#Players:GetPlayers()) end
		if _G.__infoBar then _G.__infoBar.BackgroundColor3 = a end
		if _G.__infoAvStroke then _G.__infoAvStroke.Color = a end
	end
	_G.__infoFps = 60
	_G.__infoPing = function()
		local lpI = Players.LocalPlayer
		local v = nil
		if lpI and typeof(lpI.GetNetworkPing) == "function" then
			pcall(function() v = lpI:GetNetworkPing() * 1000 end)
		end
		if type(v) ~= "number" or v <= 0 then
			v = 0
			pcall(function() v = game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
			end)
		end
		if type(v) ~= "number" then return 0 end
		return math.floor(v * 10 + 0.5) / 10
	end
	pcall(function() if not __MM2_OtherGui(sgui) and _G.__infostat then _G.__infostat:Disconnect() _G.__infostat = nil end end)
