	pcall(function() _G.__infostat = _G.__MM2_ConnOwn(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN], game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat, function(dt) local irt = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN]
			if not irt then return end
			local fN = (irt.infoFN or 0) + 1
			local fT = (irt.infoFT or 0) + (dt or 0)
			if fT >= 0.05 then
				local fps = math.floor(fN / fT + 0.5)
				irt.infoFN = 0
				irt.infoFT = 0
				irt.infoFps = fps
				local h = irt.infoFpsH or {}
				irt.infoFpsH = h
				table.insert(h, fps)
				while #h > 5 do table.remove(h, 1) end
				local s = {}
				for i, v in ipairs(h) do s[i] = v end
				table.sort(s)
				local med = s[math.ceil(#s / 2)] or fps
				if irt.infoPingV then irt.infoPingV.Text = tostring((_G.__infoPing and _G.__infoPing()) or 0) .. " ms" end
				if irt.infoFpsV then irt.infoFpsV.Text = tostring(med) end
				if irt.infoPlV then irt.infoPlV.Text = tostring(#Players:GetPlayers()) end
			else
				irt.infoFN = fN
				irt.infoFT = fT
			end
		end)
	end)
	if mpage then pgs["Info"] = mpage:Clone() end
	if pgs["Info"] then

				{ set = "swimidle", ids = { 112237056566091 } }, { set = "climb", ids = { 135793463019488 } }, }, }
			local curSel = nil
			_G.__MM2_AnimChoiceApply = function(name)
				for _, p in ipairs(packs) do
					if p.n == name then
						curSel = p
						_G.__MM2_AnimChoiceName = name
						adrlabel.Text = "Animation: " .. p.n
						break
					end
				end
			end
			local toolTr = nil
			local toolFor = nil
			local anFor = nil
			local savedAnims = {}
			local ourInjected = {}
			local toolConns = {}

			local function toolStop()
				if toolTr then pcall(function() toolTr:Stop(0.1) end) end
			end

			local function toolPlay(ch)
				if not curSel or not curSel.tool then return end
				if not ch then return end
				if toolFor ~= ch then toolTr = nil toolFor = ch end
				local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local an = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
				if not an then return end
				local has = false
				for _, c in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do
					if c:IsA("Tool") then has = true break end
				end
				if not has then return end
				if toolTr then
					if not toolTr.IsPlaying then pcall(function() toolTr:Play(0.12) end) end
					return
				end
				local ok, tr = pcall(function() local a = Instance.new("Animation")
					a.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(curSel.tool)
					local x = an:LoadAnimation(a)
					x.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle
					x.Looped = true
					return x
				end)
				if ok and tr then
					toolTr = tr
					pcall(function() tr:Play(0.12) end)
				end
			end

			local function restore(target)
				toolStop()
				for i = #ourInjected, 1, -1 do
					local a = ourInjected[i]
					pcall(function() if a and a.Parent then a:Destroy() end end)
					table.remove(ourInjected, i)
				end
				local ch = target or (Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer.Character)
				local af = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("Animate")
				if not af then return end
				for setName, keep in pairs(savedAnims) do
					local set = af:FindFirstChild(setName)
					if set then
						local has = false
						for _, c in ipairs(set:GetChildren()) do
							if c:IsA("Animation") then has = true break end
						end
						if not has then
							for _, idv in ipairs(keep) do
								__mk("Animation", {"AnimationId", idv, "Parent", set})
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local function setsReady(af)
				if not af then return false end
				for _, nm in ipairs({ "idle", "Idle", "walk", "run", "jump", "fall", "swim", "climb", "jumpLand" }) do
					local st = af:FindFirstChild(nm)
					if st then
						for _, c in ipairs(st:GetChildren()) do
							if c:IsA("Animation") then return true end
						end
					end
				end
				return false
			end

			local function stopTracksFrom(ch, set)
				local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local an = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
				if not an then return end
				for _, tr in ipairs(an:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
					local a = tr.Animation
					if a and a.Parent == set then pcall(function() tr:Stop(0.05) end) end
				end
			end

			local function inject(ch)
				if not curSel or not curSel.an then return end
				local af = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("Animate")
				if not setsReady(af) then return end
				for _, entry in ipairs(curSel.an) do
					local set = af:FindFirstChild(entry.set)
					if set then
						if anFor ~= ch then anFor = ch savedAnims = {} end
						if not savedAnims[entry.set] then
							local keep = {}
							for _, c in ipairs(set:GetChildren()) do
								if c:IsA("Animation") then keep[#keep+1] = c.AnimationId end
							end
							savedAnims[entry.set] = keep
						end
						stopTracksFrom(ch, set)
						for _, c in ipairs(set:GetChildren()) do
							if c:IsA("Animation") then c:Destroy() end
						end
						for _, id in ipairs(entry.ids) do
							local a = __mk("Animation", {"AnimationId", "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id), "Parent", set})
							table.insert(ourInjected, a)
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local function waitInject(ch)
				if not curSel or not curSel.an then return end
				for i = 1, 24 do
					task.wait(0.25)
					if not alive() then return end
					local af = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("Animate")
					if setsReady(af) then
						inject(ch)
						return
					end
				end
			end

			local function applySel(ch)
				if not curSel then return end
				restore()
				local target = ch or (Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer.Character)
				if not target then return end
				for _, c in ipairs(toolConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
				table.clear(toolConns)
				pcall(function() table.insert(toolConns, target.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c) if not alive() then return end
						if c:IsA("Tool") then task.defer(function() toolPlay(target) end) end
						if c.Name == "Animate" then task.defer(function() inject(target) end) end
					end))
					table.insert(toolConns, target.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(c) if not alive() then return end
						if c:IsA("Tool") then toolStop() end
					end))
				end)
				task.defer(function() waitInject(target) end)
				toolPlay(target)
			end
			pcall(function() local lpA = Players.LocalPlayer
				if lpA then
					_G.__MM2_Conn(lpA.CharacterAdded, function(c) if not alive() then return end
						task.wait(0.5)
						applySel(c)
					end)
				end
			end)
			local aopen = false
			local alist = __mk("ScrollingFrame", {"Name", "AnimationList", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 92), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "ScrollBarThickness", 0, "Visible", false, "ZIndex", 10, "Parent", pgs["Warp"]})
			local warpPage = pgs and pgs["Warp"] or nil
			local warpScrollDefault = (warpPage and warpPage.ScrollingEnabled) or true

			local function setWarpLock(locked)
				if warpPage then warpPage.ScrollingEnabled = (not locked) and warpScrollDefault end
			end

			local function animListScrollable()
				return aopen and alist.Visible and (alist.CanvasSize.Y.Offset > alist.AbsoluteSize.Y + 0.5)
			end
			alist.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if animListScrollable() then setWarpLock(true) end
			end)
			alist.MouseLeave:Connect(function() setWarpLock(false)
			end)
			alist.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel and animListScrollable() then
					setWarpLock(true)
				end
			end)
			alist.InputEnded:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
					setWarpLock(false)
				end
			end)
			local astroke = __s(alist, BORDER, 1, true)
			local open
			local emoDiv, emoHead
			local emoDivTw, emoHeadTw
			local emoIdBox, emoAddBtn
			local emoBox = nil
			local spotDiv, spotHead
			local spotDivTw, spotHeadTw
			local spotMusLbl, spotMusTrack
			local spotBox = nil
			local spotCopyBtn = nil
			local spotUnlinkBtn = nil
			local copySpotAuthLink, unlinkSpotify, spotifyReq
			local spotToken, spotTokenOk = nil, false
			local pollSpotBackendOnce = nil
			local SPOTIFY_BACKEND = "http://127.0.0.1:8000"
			local spotLastHwid = nil
			local spotHwid, pollSpotBackendLoop, showSpotLinkModal, spotFormEnc
			local setMusOn = nil
			local fadeMusFrame = nil
			local musTitleLbl, musArtistLbl, musCoverImg, musSeekFill, musSeekKnob
			local musTimeCur, musTimeRem

			local function abrebuild()
				for _, c in ipairs(alist:GetChildren()) do
					if c:IsA("TextButton") then pcall(function() c:Destroy() end) end
				end
				local y = 0
				for _, pk in ipairs(packs) do
					local b = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", alist})
					local lbl = __mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), "BackgroundTransparency", 1})
					local lblText = pk.n
					lbl.Text = lblText
					lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
					lbl.TextSize = 12
					lbl.TextColor3 = (curSel and curSel.n == pk.n) and acc or __MM2_SecTxt()
					lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
					lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
					lbl.Parent = b
					local p = pk
					b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() open(false)
						adrlabel.Text = "Animation: " .. p.n
						curSel = p
						task.spawn(function() if _G.__MM2_Toast then pcall(_G.__MM2_Toast, "Animation", "Reset your character", "AnimWarn") end end)
						_G.__MM2_AnimChoiceName = p.n
						abrebuild()
					end)
					y = y + 26
				end
				alist.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y)
				return y
			end

			local function agohost(g)
				local t = g and 1 or 0
				alist.BackgroundTransparency = t
				astroke.Transparency = t
				__optFade(alist, t)
			end
			open = function(o)
				aopen = o
				adrarrow.Rotation = o and 180 or 0
				if o then
					local yy = abrebuild() or 0
					local show = yy
					if show > 130 then show = 130 end
					alist.Visible = true
					alist.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
					agohost(true)
					TweenService:Create(alist, TWEEN_POP, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, show), BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
					TweenService:Create(warpBox, TWEEN_POP, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 69 + show) }):Play()
					if spotMusLbl then
						TweenService:Create(spotMusLbl, TWEEN_POP, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 331 + show) }):Play()
					end
					if spotMusTrack then
						TweenService:Create(spotMusTrack, TWEEN_POP, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 385 + show) }):Play()
					end
					if spotBox then
						TweenService:Create(spotBox, TWEEN_POP, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 372 + show) }):Play()
					end
					if spotCopyBtn then
						TweenService:Create(spotCopyBtn, TWEEN_POP, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 378 + show) }):Play()
					end
					if spotUnlinkBtn then
						TweenService:Create(spotUnlinkBtn, TWEEN_POP, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 415 + show) }):Play()

					pcall(function() if spotDivTw then spotDivTw:Cancel() end end)
					pcall(function() if spotHeadTw then spotHeadTw:Cancel() end end)

					end

					if emoDiv then
						emoDivTw = TweenService:Create(emoDiv, TWEEN_POP, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 125 + show) })
						emoDivTw:Play()
					end
					if emoHead then
						emoHeadTw = TweenService:Create(emoHead, TWEEN_POP, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 133 + show) })
						emoHeadTw:Play()
					end
					if emoIdBox then
						TweenService:Create(emoIdBox, TWEEN_POP, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 177 + show) }):Play()

					end
					if emoBox then

					end
					TweenService:Create(astroke, TWEEN_POP, { Transparency = 0 }):Play()
					__optFade(alist, 0, TWEEN_POP)
				else
					setWarpLock(false)
					TweenService:Create(alist, TWEEN_EASE, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
					TweenService:Create(warpBox, TWEEN_EASE, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 107) }):Play()
					if spotMusLbl then
						TweenService:Create(spotMusLbl, TWEEN_EASE, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 385) }):Play()
					end
					if spotMusTrack then
						TweenService:Create(spotMusTrack, TWEEN_EASE, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 385) }):Play()
					end
					if spotBox then
						TweenService:Create(spotBox, TWEEN_EASE, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 372) }):Play()
					end
					if spotCopyBtn then
						TweenService:Create(spotCopyBtn, TWEEN_EASE, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 416) }):Play()
					end
					if spotUnlinkBtn then
						TweenService:Create(spotUnlinkBtn, TWEEN_EASE, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 453) }):Play()
					end
					pcall(function() if spotDivTw then spotDivTw:Cancel() end end)

					if spotDiv then
						spotDivTw = TweenService:Create(spotDiv, TWEEN_EASE, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 327) })
						spotDivTw:Play()
					end

						spotHeadTw = TweenService:Create(spotHead, TWEEN_EASE, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 335) })
						spotHeadTw:Play()

					pcall(function() if emoHeadTw then emoHeadTw:Cancel() end end)

						emoDivTw = TweenService:Create(emoDiv, TWEEN_EASE, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 163) })
						emoDivTw:Play()
					end

						emoHeadTw = TweenService:Create(emoHead, TWEEN_EASE, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 171) })

					end

					task.delay(0.22, function() if not aopen then alist.Visible = false end end)
				end
			end
			adrbtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() open(not aopen) end)
			local aresetbtn = __mk("TextButton", {"Name", "AnimationResetButton", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 36), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 92), "BackgroundColor3", BG_DARKER, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Text", "", "AutoButtonColor", false, "Parent", pgs["Warp"]})

				open(false)
				curSel = nil
				_G.__MM2_AnimChoiceName = nil
				adrlabel.Text = "Animation: ..."
				restore()
				abrebuild()
			end)

			emoHead = __mk("TextLabel", {"Name", "EmoteHeader", "Size", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 14), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 171), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "TextColor3", TEXT_SECONDARY, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 11, "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", "Emote", "Parent", pgs["Warp"]})

			__c(emoBox, UDim.new(0, 8))

			pcall(function() if readfile and isfile and isfile("AeonHub/AeonHubBin/emotes.json") then
					local d = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(readfile("AeonHub/AeonHubBin/emotes.json"))
					if type(d) == "table" then emoCache = d end
				end
			end)

			local function saveEmoCache()
				pcall(function() if writefile then writefile("AeonHub/AeonHubBin/emotes.json", game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(emoCache)) end
				end)
			end
			local emoCleanupDone = false

			local function stopEmoTrack()
				if emoTrack then pcall(function() emoTrack:Stop() end) pcall(function() emoTrack:Destroy() end) emoTrack = nil end
			end
			if not emoCleanupDone then
				pcall(function() _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup = _G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup or {}
					table.insert(_G.__MM2_RT[MY_GEN].cleanup, function() pcall(function() stopEmoTrack() end)
						pcall(function() restore() end)
					end)
					emoCleanupDone = true
				end)
			end

			local function emoTrackReady(tr, timeout)
				if tr == nil then return false, 0 end
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while os.clock() - t0 < (timeout or 10) do
					local ok, len = pcall(function() return tr.Length or 0 end)
					if ok and len and len > 0 then return true, len end
					task.wait(0.15)
				end
				return false, 0
			end

			local function playEmoAnim(id)
				stopEmoTrack()
				local char = player and player.Character
				local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local ator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
				if not ator then
					return false
				end
				if not string.find(id, "rbxassetid") then id = "rbxassetid://" .. id end
				local anim = __mk("Animation", {"AnimationId", id})
				local tr = nil
				pcall(function() tr = ator:LoadAnimation(anim) end)
				pcall(function() anim:Destroy() end)
				local okL = false
				if tr then okL = emoTrackReady(tr, 10) end
				if tr and okL then
					emoTrack = tr
					pcall(function() tr.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
						tr.Looped = true
						tr:Play()
					end)
				else
					if tr then pcall(function() tr:Destroy() end) end
				end
				return tr ~= nil and okL or false
			end

			local function resolveEmoteAnims(input)
				local idText = tostring(input or ""):match("(%d+)")
				if not idText then return nil end
				if type(emoCache) == "table" and emoCache[idText] and #emoCache[idText] > 0 then return emoCache[idText] end
				local ok, objs = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. idText) end)
				local found, visited = {}, 0
				if ok and objs then
					local function walk(p, depth)
						if depth > 4 or visited > 2000 or #found >= 25 then return end
						for _, c in ipairs(p:GetChildren()) do
							visited = visited + 1
							if visited % 200 == 0 then task.wait() end
							if visited > 2000 or #found >= 25 then return end
							if c:IsA("Animation") then
								local aid = tostring(c.AnimationId or "")
								if aid ~= "" then table.insert(found, { name = tostring(c.Name or aid), id = aid }) end
							end
							walk(c, depth + 1)
						end
					end
					for _, ob in ipairs(objs) do
						if ob:IsA("Animation") then
							local aid = tostring(ob.AnimationId or "")
							if aid ~= "" then table.insert(found, { name = tostring(ob.Name or aid), id = aid }) end
						end
						walk(ob, 0)
					end
					for _, ob in ipairs(objs) do pcall(function() ob:Destroy() end) end
				end
				if #found == 0 then
					local aid = "rbxassetid://" .. idText
					local char = player and player.Character
					local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					local ator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
					if ator then
						local t = __mk("Animation", {"AnimationId", aid})
						local tr = nil
						pcall(function() tr = ator:LoadAnimation(t) end)
						local okL = false
						if tr then okL = emoTrackReady(tr, 0.8) pcall(function() tr:Destroy() end) end
						pcall(function() t:Destroy() end)
						if okL then table.insert(found, { name = idText, id = aid }) end
					end
				end
				if #found == 0 then return nil end
				if type(emoCache) == "table" then emoCache[idText] = found pcall(saveEmoCache) end
				return found
			end
			local emoBusy = false

			local function addEmote()
				TweenService:Create(emoAddLabel, TWEEN_FAST, { TextColor3 = __headCol() }):Play()
				task.delay(1.5, function() pcall(function() TweenService:Create(emoAddLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.3, EASE_Q), { TextColor3 = __MM2_LightTxt() }):Play() end) end)
				local txt = emoIdBox and tostring(emoIdBox.Text or "") or ""
				txt = txt:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
				if txt == "" then
					stopEmoTrack()
					return
				end
				if emoBusy then return end
				emoBusy = true
				emoIdBox.Text = ""
				task.spawn(function() local list, done = nil, false
					local probeOk, probeDone, idText0 = nil, false, tostring(txt):match("(%d+)")
					task.spawn(function() pcall(function()
							if idText0 then
								local char = player and player.Character
								local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
								local ator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
								if ator then
									local t = __mk("Animation", {"AnimationId", "rbxassetid://" .. idText0})
									local tr = nil
									pcall(function() tr = ator:LoadAnimation(t) end)
									if tr then probeOk = emoTrackReady(tr, 1.2) pcall(function() tr:Destroy() end) end
									pcall(function() t:Destroy() end)
								end
							end
						end)
						probeDone = true
					end)
					task.spawn(function() list = resolveEmoteAnims(txt)
						done = true
					end)
					local t0 = os.clock()
					while os.clock() - t0 < 30 do
						if done then break end
						if probeDone and probeOk and os.clock() - t0 > 0.3 then break end
						if probeDone and probeOk == false and os.clock() - t0 > 0.5 then break end
						task.wait(0.05)
					end
					local tw0 = os.clock()
					while not probeDone and os.clock() - tw0 < 2 do task.wait(0.05) end
					emoBusy = false
					local listOK = type(list) == "table" and #list > 0
					local okPlay = false
					if listOK then
						local first = list[1]
						okPlay = first and playEmoAnim(first.id)
					end
					if not okPlay and probeOk == true and idText0 then
						okPlay = playEmoAnim("rbxassetid://" .. idText0)
					end
					if okPlay then
						task.spawn(function() if _G.__MM2_Toast then pcall(_G.__MM2_Toast, "Emote", "Playing", "EmoWarn") end end)
					else
						task.spawn(function() if _G.__MM2_Toast then pcall(_G.__MM2_Toast, "Emote", "Invalid ID", "EmoWarn") end end)
					end
				end)
			end

			end)

			local function spotB64encode(data)
				local b64 = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
				local out = {}
				for i = 1, #data, 3 do
					local a, b, c = data:byte(i, i + 2)
					local n = (a or 0) * 65536 + (b or 0) * 256 + (c or 0)
					local rem = #data - i + 1
					local chars = rem >= 3 and 4 or (rem == 2 and 3 or 2)
					for j = 1, chars do
						local idx = math.floor(n / (64 ^ (4 - j))) % 64 + 1
						out[#out + 1] = b64:sub(idx, idx)
					end
					for j = chars + 1, 4 do out[#out + 1] = "=" end
				end
				return table.concat(out)
			end

			local function spotB64url(s)
				return (spotB64encode(s):gsub("%+", "-"):gsub("/", "_"):gsub("=", ""))
			end

			local function spotSha256(msg)
				local K = { 0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
					0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174, 0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
					0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967, 0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
					0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070, 0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
					0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2 }
				local h0, h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7 = 0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a, 0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19
				local ml = #msg * 8
				msg = msg .. "\128"
				while (#msg % 64) ~= 56 do msg = msg .. "\0" end
				local hi = math.floor(ml / 4294967296)
				local lo = ml % 4294967296
				for _, wv in ipairs({ hi, lo }) do
					for i = 1, 4 do msg = msg .. string.char(bit32.extract(wv, 32 - i * 8, 8)) end
				end
				local w = {}
				for chunk = 1, #msg, 64 do
					for i = 0, 15 do
						local b1, b2, b3, b4 = msg:byte(chunk + i * 4, chunk + i * 4 + 3)
						w[i + 1] = ((b1 or 0) * 16777216 + (b2 or 0) * 65536 + (b3 or 0) * 256 + (b4 or 0)) % 4294967296
					end
					for i = 17, 64 do
						local s0 = bit32.bxor(bit32.rrotate(w[i - 15], 7), bit32.rrotate(w[i - 15], 18), bit32.rshift(w[i - 15], 3))
						local s1 = bit32.bxor(bit32.rrotate(w[i - 2], 17), bit32.rrotate(w[i - 2], 19), bit32.rshift(w[i - 2], 10))
						w[i] = (w[i - 16] + s0 + w[i - 7] + s1) % 4294967296
					end
					local a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h = h0, h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7
					for i = 1, 64 do
						local S1 = bit32.bxor(bit32.rrotate(e, 6), bit32.rrotate(e, 11), bit32.rrotate(e, 25))
						local ch = bit32.bxor(bit32.band(e, f), bit32.band(bit32.bnot(e), g))
						local t1 = (h + S1 + ch + K[i] + w[i]) % 4294967296
						local S0 = bit32.bxor(bit32.rrotate(a, 2), bit32.rrotate(a, 13), bit32.rrotate(a, 22))
						local maj = bit32.bxor(bit32.band(a, b), bit32.band(a, c), bit32.band(b, c))
						local t2 = (S0 + maj) % 4294967296
						h = g g = f f = e e = (d + t1) % 4294967296
						d = c c = b b = a a = (t1 + t2) % 4294967296
					end
					h0 = (h0 + a) % 4294967296
					h1 = (h1 + b) % 4294967296
					h2 = (h2 + c) % 4294967296
					h3 = (h3 + d) % 4294967296
					h4 = (h4 + e) % 4294967296
					h5 = (h5 + f) % 4294967296
					h6 = (h6 + g) % 4294967296
					h7 = (h7 + h) % 4294967296
				end
				local out = {}
				for _, v in ipairs({ h0, h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7 }) do
					for i = 1, 4 do out[#out + 1] = string.char(bit32.extract(v, 32 - i * 8, 8)) end
				end
				return table.concat(out)
			end
			spotFormEnc = function(s)
				return (tostring(s or "")):gsub("([^%w%-%.%_%~])", function(c) return string.format("%%%02X", string.byte(c)) end)
			end

			local function spotNewVerifier()
				local cs = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789-._~"
				local t = {}
				for i = 1, 64 do local k = math.random(1, #cs) t[i] = cs:sub(k, k) end
				return table.concat(t)
			end
			spotifyReq = function(method, url, body)
				local req = request or (syn and syn.request) or http_request
				if not req then return nil end
				if not spotToken or spotToken == "" then return nil end
				local ok, res = pcall(function()
					return req({ Url = url, Method = method, Headers = { ["Authorization"] = "Bearer " .. spotToken, ["Accept"] = "application/json", ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" }, Body = body or "" })
				end)
				if ok and res then return res end
				return nil
			end

			local function spotTokenPost(url, body)
				local req = request or (syn and syn.request) or http_request
				if not req then return nil end
				local ok, res = pcall(function() return req({ Url = url, Method = "POST", Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" }, Body = body })
				end)
				if ok and res then return res end
				return nil
			end

			local function spotSaveTokens()
				pcall(function() if writefile and spotToken then
						writefile(spotTokenFile, game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode({ access = spotToken, refresh = spotRefreshToken }))
					end
				end)
			end

			local function spotRefreshNow()
				if SPOTIFY_BACKEND ~= "" and spotLastHwid then
					local tok = pollSpotBackendOnce(spotLastHwid)
					if tok then
						spotToken = tok
						spotTokenOk = true
						return true
					end
				end
				if not spotRefreshToken or spotRefreshToken == "" then return false end
				local r = spotTokenPost("https://accounts.spotify.com/api/token", "grant_type=refresh_token&refresh_token=" .. spotFormEnc(spotRefreshToken) .. "&client_id=" .. SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID)
				if r and r.Body and #r.Body > 0 then
					local ok, d = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(r.Body) end)
					if ok and type(d) == "table" and d.access_token then
						spotToken = d.access_token
						if d.refresh_token then spotRefreshToken = d.refresh_token end
						spotTokenOk = true
						spotSaveTokens()
						return true
					end
				end
				return false
			end
			spotRemoteToast401 = function()
				spotTokenOk = false
			end
			showSpotLinkModal = function(url)
				pcall(function() local anchor = (pgs and pgs["Warp"])
					if not anchor then return end
					local root = anchor
					while root and not root:IsA("ScreenGui") do root = root.Parent end
					if not root then root = anchor end
					local old = root:FindFirstChild("SpotLinkModal")
					if old then old:Destroy() end
					local lm = (THEMES[selectedTheme] and THEMES[selectedTheme].light)
					local m = __mk("Frame", {"Name", "SpotLinkModal", "AnchorPoint", Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), "Position", UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), "Size", UDim2.new(0, 360, 0, 156), "BackgroundColor3", lm and C_L_CARD or Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 18), "BorderSizePixel", 1, "BorderColor3", lm and C_L_BORDER or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 75), "Parent", root})
					__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 22), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 10), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 14, "TextColor3", __MM2_BaseTxt(), "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "Text", "Clipboard blocked - copy manually", "Parent", m})
					__mk("TextBox", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 32), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 38), "BackgroundColor3", lm and C_L_PANEL or Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 30), "BorderSizePixel", 1, "BorderColor3", lm and C_L_DIVIDER or Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 65), "Font", Enum.Font.Code, "TextSize", 12, "TextColor3", __MM2_LightTxt(), "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "ClearTextOnFocus", false, "Text", url, "Parent", m})
					__mk("TextLabel", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 30), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 76), "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "Font", Enum.Font.Gotham, "TextSize", 12, "TextColor3", lm and C_T_SEC or Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 160), "TextXAlignment", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, "TextWrapped", true, "Text", "Tap the field, select all, copy, then open it in your browser.", "Parent", m})
					local c = __mk("TextButton", {"Size", UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 28), "Position", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 112), "BackgroundColor3", lm and C_L_HOVER or Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 34), "BorderSizePixel", 1, "BorderColor3", lm and C_L_BORDER or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 75), "Font", Enum.Font.GothamBold, "TextSize", 13, "TextColor3", __MM2_BaseTxt(), "Text", "Close", "Parent", m})
					c.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() pcall(function() m:Destroy() end) end)
				end)
			end
			copySpotAuthLink = function()
				spotPKCEVerifier = spotNewVerifier()
				local ch = spotB64url(spotSha256(spotPKCEVerifier))
				local url = "https://accounts.spotify.com/authorize?client_id=" .. SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID .. "&response_type=code&redirect_uri=" .. spotFormEnc(SPOTIFY_REDIRECT) .. "&scope=" .. spotFormEnc("user-read-currently-playing user-read-playback-state user-modify-playback-state user-read-recently-played") .. "&code_challenge=" .. ch .. "&code_challenge_method=S256"
				local copied = false
				pcall(function() if setclipboard then setclipboard(url) copied = true end end)
				if not copied then showSpotLinkModal(url) end
			end
			unlinkSpotify = function()
				spotToken, spotTokenOk, spotRefreshToken, spotRemoteTrackId = nil, false, nil, nil
				spotRemoteGen = spotRemoteGen + 1
				pcall(function() if delfile and isfile and isfile(spotTokenFile) then delfile(spotTokenFile) end end)
				if musTitleLbl then musTitleLbl.Text = "Nothing playing" end
				if musArtistLbl then musArtistLbl.Text = "" end
				if musCoverImg then musCoverImg.Image = "" musCoverImg.ImageTransparency = 1 end
				if musCoverNote then musCoverNote.TextTransparency = 0 end
			end

			local function spotFmtMs(ms)
				ms = math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(ms) or 0) / 1000)
				return string.format("%d:%02d", math.floor(ms / 60), ms % 60)
			end

			local function spotBackendNow()
				if SPOTIFY_BACKEND == "" or not spotLastHwid then return nil end
				local ok, body = pcall(function() local u = SPOTIFY_BACKEND .. "/spotify/now?hwid=" .. spotFormEnc(spotLastHwid)
					local req = request or (syn and syn.request) or http_request
					if req then
						local res = req({ Url = u, Method = "GET" })
						return res and res.Body or nil
					end
					return game:HttpGet(u)
				end)
				if not ok or not body then return nil end
				local ok2, d = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(body) end)
				if ok2 and type(d) == "table" and d.ok then
					return { StatusCode = tonumber(d.status) or 0, Body = d.body }
				end
				return nil
			end

			local function spotNowReq()
				if SPOTIFY_BACKEND ~= "" and spotLastHwid then return spotBackendNow() end
				return spotifyReq("GET", "https://api.spotify.com/v1/me/player/currently-playing?market=from_token")
			end

			local function spotTickUI()
				local nowU = os.clock()
				if nowU - spotLastUI < 0.25 then return end
				spotLastUI = nowU
				pcall(function() if not spotMasterOn or not spotTokenOk or not spotPlaying then return end
					if spotLastDur <= 0 then return end
					local pg = math.min(spotLastProg + math.max(0, os.clock() - spotLastTick) * 1000, spotLastDur)
					if musSeekFill then musSeekFill.Size = UDim2.new(pg / spotLastDur, 0, 1, 0) end
					if musSeekKnob then musSeekKnob.Position = UDim2.new(pg / spotLastDur, -6, 0.5, -6) end
					if musTimeCur then musTimeCur.Text = spotFmtMs(pg) end
					if musTimeRem then musTimeRem.Text = "-" .. spotFmtMs(spotLastDur - pg) end
				end)
			end

			local function startSpotRemote()
				spotRemoteGen = spotRemoteGen + 1
				local gen = spotRemoteGen
				if setMusOn then setMusOn(true) end
				if musTitleLbl then musTitleLbl.Text = "Connecting..." end
				pcall(function() if _G.__spotTick then _G.__spotTick:Disconnect() end end)
				_G.__spotTick = _G.__MM2_Conn(RunService.Heartbeat, function() if not alive() then return end
					spotTickUI()
				end)
				task.spawn(function() while gen == spotRemoteGen and spotTokenOk do
						if not alive() then return end
						if spotMasterOn then
							local r = spotNowReq()
							if r then
								if r.StatusCode == 401 then
									if not spotRefreshNow() then spotRemoteToast401() break end
								elseif r.StatusCode == 204 then
									if musTitleLbl then musTitleLbl.Text = "Nothing playing" end
									if musArtistLbl then musArtistLbl.Text = "" end
									if musTimeCur then musTimeCur.Text = "0:00" end
									if musTimeRem then musTimeRem.Text = "-0:00" end
									spotPlaying = false
									if musCoverImg then musCoverImg.Image = "" musCoverImg.ImageTransparency = 1 end
									if musCoverNote then musCoverNote.TextTransparency = 0 end
								elseif r.StatusCode == 200 and r.Body and #r.Body > 0 then
									local ok, data = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(r.Body) end)
									if ok and type(data) == "table" and type(data.item) == "table" and data.item.id then
										if data.item.id ~= spotRemoteTrackId then
											spotRemoteTrackId = data.item.id
											local nm = {}
											if type(data.item.artists) == "table" then
												for _, a in ipairs(data.item.artists) do
													if type(a) == "table" and a.name then table.insert(nm, a.name) end
												end
											end
											if musTitleLbl then musTitleLbl.Text = data.item.name or "Unknown" end
											if musArtistLbl then musArtistLbl.Text = table.concat(nm, ", ") end
											if musCoverImg then musCoverImg.Image = "" musCoverImg.ImageTransparency = 1 end
											if musCoverNote then musCoverNote.TextTransparency = 0 end
											local th = data.item.album and data.item.album.images and data.item.album.images[1] and data.item.album.images[1].url or nil
											if th then
												spotCovN = spotCovN + 1
												local tag = spotCovN
												local tid = data.item.id
												task.spawn(function() local img = nil
													pcall(function() local req = request or (syn and syn.request) or http_request
														if req then
															local res = req({ Url = th, Method = "GET" })
															if res and res.Body and #res.Body > 1000 then
																writefile("AeonHub/AeonHubBin/spotcov_" .. tostring(tag) .. ".png", res.Body)
																img = getcustomasset("AeonHub/AeonHubBin/spotcov_" .. tostring(tag) .. ".png")
															end
														end
													end)
													if img and spotRemoteTrackId == tid then
														if musCoverImg then musCoverImg.Image = img TweenService:Create(musCoverImg, TweenInfo.new(0.3, EASE_Q), { ImageTransparency = 0 }):Play() end
														if musCoverNote then musCoverNote.TextTransparency = 1 end
														pcall(function() local old = "AeonHub/AeonHubBin/spotcov_" .. tostring(tag - 2) .. ".png"
															if delfile and isfile and isfile(old) then delfile(old) end
														end)
													end
												end)
											end
										end
										if type(data.item.duration_ms) == "number" and data.item.duration_ms > 500 then
											spotLastDur = data.item.duration_ms
										end
										spotLastProg = tonumber(data.progress_ms) or 0
										spotLastTick = os.clock()
										spotPlaying = data.is_playing ~= false
										if musSeekFill and musSeekKnob and spotLastDur > 0 then
											local pct = math.clamp(spotLastProg / spotLastDur, 0, 1)
											musSeekFill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
											musSeekKnob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, -6, 0.5, -6)
											if musTimeCur then musTimeCur.Text = spotFmtMs(spotLastProg) end
											if musTimeRem then musTimeRem.Text = "-" .. spotFmtMs(spotLastDur - spotLastProg) end
										end
									else
										spotRemoteTrackId = nil
										spotPlaying = false
										if musCoverImg then musCoverImg.Image = "" musCoverImg.ImageTransparency = 1 end
										if musCoverNote then musCoverNote.TextTransparency = 0 end
										if musTitleLbl then musTitleLbl.Text = "Nothing playing" end
										if musArtistLbl then musArtistLbl.Text = "" end
										if musTimeCur then musTimeCur.Text = "0:00" end
										if musTimeRem then musTimeRem.Text = "-0:00" end
									end
								else
									spotRemoteTrackId = nil
									local rs = tostring(r.StatusCode or "?")
									local rwhy = ""
									pcall(function() local e = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(r.Body or "")
										if type(e) == "table" and type(e.error) == "table" then
											rwhy = tostring(e.error.reason or e.error.message or "")
										end
									end)
									if musTitleLbl then musTitleLbl.Text = "Spotify error " .. rs .. (rwhy ~= "" and " (" .. rwhy .. ")" or "") end
									if musArtistLbl then musArtistLbl.Text = "" end
								end
							end
						end
						task.wait(2)
					end
				end)
			end
			pollSpotBackendOnce = function(hwid)
				local ok, body = pcall(function() local u = SPOTIFY_BACKEND .. "/spotify/token?hwid=" .. spotFormEnc(hwid)
					local req = request or (syn and syn.request) or http_request
					if req then
						local res = req({ Url = u, Method = "GET" })
						return res and res.Body or nil
					end
					return game:HttpGet(u)
				end)
				if not ok or not body then return nil end
				local ok2, d = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(body) end)
				if ok2 and type(d) == "table" and d.ok and d.access then return d.access end
				return nil
			end
			spotHwid = function()
				local ok, v = pcall(function() return gethwid() end)
				if ok and v and #tostring(v) > 0 then return tostring(v) end
				return nil
			end
			pollSpotBackendLoop = function(hwid)
				task.spawn(function() local t0 = os.clock()
					while os.clock() - t0 < 300 do
						if not alive() then return end
						local tok = pollSpotBackendOnce(hwid)
						if tok then
							spotLastHwid = hwid
							spotToken = tok
							spotTokenOk = true
							startSpotRemote()
							return
						end
						task.wait(3)
					end
				end)
			end
			pcall(function() if readfile and isfile and isfile(spotTokenFile) then
					local d = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(readfile(spotTokenFile))
					if type(d) == "table" and d.refresh then
						spotRefreshToken = d.refresh
						task.spawn(function() if spotRefreshNow() then
								startSpotRemote()
							end
						end)
					end
				end
			end)
			fadeMusFrame = function(show)
				local t = show and 0 or 1
				local info = show and TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Circular, Enum.EasingDirection.Out) or TWEEN_EASE
				if show then musFrame.Visible = true end
				TweenService:Create(musFrame, info, { BackgroundTransparency = t }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(musScale, info, { Scale = show and 1 or 0 }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(musStroke, info, { Transparency = t }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(musCover, info, { BackgroundTransparency = t }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(musCoverStroke, info, { Transparency = t }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(musCoverNote, info, { TextTransparency = t }):Play()
				if musCoverImg then
					TweenService:Create(musCoverImg, info, { ImageTransparency = t }):Play()
				end
				TweenService:Create(musTitleLbl, info, { TextTransparency = t }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(musArtistLbl, info, { TextTransparency = t }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(musSeekBar, info, { BackgroundTransparency = t }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(musSeekFill, info, { BackgroundTransparency = t }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(musSeekKnob, info, { BackgroundTransparency = t }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(musCaption, info, { TextTransparency = t }):Play()
				if musTimeCur then
					TweenService:Create(musTimeCur, info, { TextTransparency = t }):Play()
				end
				if musTimeRem then
					TweenService:Create(musTimeRem, info, { TextTransparency = t }):Play()
				end
				if not show then task.delay(0.22, function() if not spotMasterOn then musFrame.Visible = false end end) end
			end
			do
				local draggingMus, dragStartMus, startPosMus, dragTwMus = false, nil, nil, nil
				musFrame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
						draggingMus = true
						dragStartMus = input.Position
						startPosMus = musFrame.Position
					end
				end)
				__live(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input) if draggingMus
						and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
						local delta = input.Position - dragStartMus
						local target = UDim2.new(startPosMus.X.Scale, startPosMus.X.Offset + delta.X, startPosMus.Y.Scale, startPosMus.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
						pcall(function() if dragTwMus then dragTwMus:Cancel() end end)
						dragTwMus = TweenService:Create(musFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.1, EASE_Q, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = target })
						dragTwMus:Play()
					end
				end)
				__live(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input) if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
						draggingMus = false
					end
				end)
			end
		end)()
	end
end
setActiveTab(pgs["Info"] and "Info" or "Main")
_G.__MM2_BlockedSyncs["face"] = function()
	local light = (THEMES[selectedTheme] and THEMES[selectedTheme].light)
	if _MM2_FACE then _MM2_FACE.ImageTransparency = (light and 0.4 or 1) end
end
_MM2_FACE = __mk("ImageLabel", {"Name", "FaceBackdrop", "Image", "rbxassetid://116139183245656", "Size", UDim2.new(0, 240, 0, 240), "Position", UDim2.new(1, 9, 1, 24), "AnchorPoint", Vector2.new(1, 1), "BackgroundColor3", C_L_WHITE, "BackgroundTransparency", 1, "ScaleType", Enum.ScaleType.Crop, "ZIndex", 0, "ImageTransparency", ((THEMES[selectedTheme] and THEMES[selectedTheme].light) and 0.4 or 1), "Active", false, "BorderSizePixel", 0, "Parent", inner})
if selectedTheme ~= "Lime" then
	pcall(function() applyTheme(selectedTheme) end)
end
task.defer(function() local name = _G.__MM2_CfgAutoGet and _G.__MM2_CfgAutoGet()
	local loader = _G.__MM2_CfgAutoLoad or _G.__MM2_CfgLoad
	if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" and loader then pcall(loader, name) end
end)
local themeDirty = true
_G.__MM2_ThemeMarkDirty = function() themeDirty = true end
sgui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function() themeDirty = true end)
task.spawn(function() while alive() and not ded do
		task.wait(0.3)
		if themeDirty and THEMES[selectedTheme] and THEMES[selectedTheme].light then
			themeDirty = false
			pcall(applyTheme, selectedTheme)
		end
	end
end)
return sgui
